# files-documents normative contract clauses

A section is binding only when the matching requirement unconditionally references
it. A proposed or historical section does not become accepted by residing here.
The reference, requirement identity, scenarios, and normal source-authority rules
form one contract. Preserve all clauses; see openspec/contracts/README.md.

## REQ29-C23 — Request a bounded originals ZIP with honest completeness

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"00037cf42155ec42d7ef","name":"REQ29-C23 — Request a bounded originals ZIP with honest completeness"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

This is an expressly qualified ordinary staff original-download/export purpose, not authority inferred from Read, preview, acceptance, folder/view or participant access. One Tenant/environment/source envelope preserves every applicable Legal Entity, classification, origin, purpose, residency and egress restriction, including Q28 origin constraints. No universal accepted/preview-ready/unfinished condition or compulsory new scan is invented. Official artifacts, D26 records packages and external shares remain separate.

Story: US29-C23. Source: organization-and-review-tools.md. Decision basis: Q44 paragraphs 1–3; REQ29-P01/P02/P04–P06.

## REQ29-C73 — Preserve staff evidence disclosure, filing and existing review notices

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"023f5b31f33f129f67ca","name":"REQ29-C73 — Preserve staff evidence disclosure, filing and existing review notices"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Supply grants no participant own-submission/read/preview/history/completed access, nor staff all-evidence reading. Each status/name/origin/count/result follows current source projection; independent staff work after recipient expiry does not renew that recipient. ## REQ29-C42 — Offer the same retained contribution again as new supply

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"043a9ede01735ec7d91b","name":"REQ29-C42 — Offer the same retained contribution again as new supply"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Offer this file again is a new deliberate current resupply admission for the exact withdrawn contribution and lawful retained version in the same source/request/item/purpose/recipient lineage. It is not Restore, reversal of withdrawal or revival of an older predecessor. Current resupply/proof/access/target rights are independently required; withdrawal-only Off does not itself remove them.

## REQ29-B09 — Paired exact-version reading

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"072f7a696adaa294b07e","name":"REQ29-B09 — Paired exact-version reading"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Scope and owners.** Provide the optional generic staff paired-reading capability for two exact versions whose relationship/purpose is admitted by the source. A SHALL NOT add automated difference detection, change counts, Changed/Unchanged/equivalence claims or a new comparison artifact. Preserve P18/P22/P23 and other existing source-owned comparison requirements; no global ban on existing/future qualified comparison. Opening/closing/swapping/retrying SHALL NOT accept evidence, merge/edit/restore/adopt/publish, change source membership, complete work or create a P6/P17 notice/task. Reuse existing source and shared-reader owners; no new Pair aggregate/workflow/policy engine is required.

**Joint current authority and selection.** Derive tenant/environment/actor and validated active context from trusted server authority; validated caller IDs are selectors, not claims. Prove current source-purpose authority for each exact version/representation and their joint presentation before eligible enumeration, metadata/counts/order/search, generation and applicable reads/composition. Do not union assignments, staff/donor/missionary hats, classifications or purposes. Source-proved defaults or deliberate choices SHALL identify a real permitted relation, not nearest timestamp/name/hash. No arbitrary cross-source picker, automatic original-context discovery or new global Compare capability. Unknown proof is not allowed or a proved empty version history.

**Stable inputs, current status and source actions.** Bind each side to exact source occurrence/use, immutable version and qualified representation/profile/options. Keep displayed identities stable while lawful; status and current source basis SHALL be refreshed or marked unresolved when required, never silently asserted from old labels. Show version/time/provenance/status only as permitted; older/newer does not mean accepted/current. Repeated identical source-version selection can be explained without extra processing; equal bytes in distinct versions do not collapse provenance. Q03/Q21/Q28 source commands SHALL retain their own current evidence-set/capability checks; pane focus/order, swap, find or telemetry cannot choose a review target or rebase a stale action. No mandatory compare/scroll/dwell gate.

**Qualified representations and coverage.** Use each input's actual qualified reader lane, exact immutable output and coverage manifest. Preserve the full D32 format/processing contract and all omissions; no implicit requirement that both versions have the same file format and no quiet PDF/image-only completion. Supported source-family/pair composition must be explicit and actually qualified; staged coverage is not completion of omitted declared lanes. P18 uses its canonical exact object/generation/length/digest serving, not an alternate authoritative re-render. Preserve available native text/semantics; unavailable or omitted content is not absent, empty or unchanged. No OCR/AI/external refresh, original mutation, automatic original download or public/third-party viewer fallback.

**Disclosure and stale-response fences.** Re-prove current authority at every inherited source/generation/promotion/full/range/conditional-read boundary and relevant reconnect/visibility/context transition. Denied or unknown required current proof SHALL withhold affected content and clear its protected DOM/canvas, snippets, thumbnails, find results, accessibility text and dependent UI state; late callbacks cannot repopulate it. Fence responses by current actor/tenant/purpose/pair/side/input/representation options, not URL or request completion order. Technical failure alone is not revocation; retain already delivered context only while the existing qualified disclosure conditions permit it. If joint paired use is denied, stop the composed pair; independent standalone access must be separately valid, not an automatic purpose switch. Already delivered information cannot be remotely recalled.

**Per-input generation, retry and custody.** A SHALL compose existing input generations rather than introduce combined output or a durable pair lifecycle. Coalesce only under the exact qualified source/version/profile/projection/restriction key; content digest or two open panels grants no sharing authority. Recover original semantic intent/attempt outcomes under current disclosure. Changed input conflicts or creates its own qualified intent, never retargets an old result. D32 fixed anchors, at most two attempts and classified operator recovery remain; ordinary reopening/swapping cannot reset exhausted work or renew custody/access. Closing this view may stop its own fetch/subscription but SHALL NOT cancel/delete a shared job/output or prolong it through observer counts. Tentative promotion/deletion races retain one winner and immutable keys; provider work stays outside database locks.

**Flexible accessible interaction.** Use established shared Base UI/base-maia controls in the source context, with obvious identity/status per side, independent navigation and safe return to task/notes/focus. Provide comfortable adjacent reading where suitable and a fully usable single-column/version-switching equivalent; switching preserves each still-permitted position/zoom/find context. Preserve needed fixed-page/wide-content zoom/pan without a forced two-pane horizontal layout. Any splitter/sync control is optional, operable without mandatory dragging and accessible by keyboard/AT; linked scroll cannot assert semantic alignment, skip or clamp content unnoticed. Find is explicitly scoped to that version and its entire declared represented content, including unloaded pages/sheets; interrupted search is incomplete, not zero. No inaccessible chrome, color-only state, surprise auto-focus or extra account/name/address form. Qualify actual document content, supported language/RTL/locale/date semantics, touch and low bandwidth.

**Honest partial failure and recovery.** Track each input's selection/preparation/Ready/limited/unsupported/technical failure/expiry/authority condition distinctly; these are orthogonal facts, not one misleading overall Success flag. A valid partial reader may remain usable without pretending a complete pair exists. Preserve independently permitted source actions and sufficient inspection alternatives; failed comparison alone does not reject evidence, waive content review or require another upload. Missing pair candidates, unavailable capability and denied existence need safe truthful copy and an actually owned next action. Do not silently replace an unavailable version or grant its historical content. Recover known source/preview success after response/UI failure without creating another business act.

**Database and privileged-path safety.** No new pair table is required. For actual source/version/profile/generation/output/access records, require appropriate non-null identity, same-tenant/environment relationships, valid states/digests/lengths, immutable attribution/lineage and conditional uniqueness for live generation/semantic admission. No permissive defaults or cascades may retarget or destroy another use/history. Keep current mutable cross-row authority in qualified owning boundaries, not a CHECK over changing rows. Prove actual SELECT and mutation grants, applicable USING and resulting-row WITH CHECK behavior, policy combinations, views/functions/RPC/triggers, Storage and worker/service paths; a valid row must not be patchable into another source/tenant/actor/profile/Ready output. USING can imply WITH CHECK, and definer naming alone proves neither safety nor bypass. Derive timestamps and attribution from trusted context. No money precision or financial-state change follows from displaying saved values.

**Numeric limits and composed capacity.** Preserve every D32 per-file/profile/attempt/custody ceiling and its complete P01–P40/O01–O06. The numeric table below is a checked inheritance map, not new universal upload limits or observed capacity. Qualify finite aggregate per-pair/viewer/device/worker/network and tenant-fair scheduling separately before activation, including two maximal inputs and mixed warm/cold/failed states. No automatic doubled entitlement, halved valid input limit, unconditional eager loading of both whole files or all history, hidden truncation or invented 100-pair inference from 100 single opens. Backpressure may defer one side truthfully; any new supported workload promise must have named measured budgets/owners and explicit trace, not an ad hoc runtime guess.

**Audit, privacy and operations.** Preserve owner-required technical generation/access evidence with exact permitted actor/purpose/input/profile/outcome correlation; it is not business review/acceptance or human-reading proof. No new business comparison record/dashboard is required. Technical logs, analytics, error reports and queue envelopes SHALL exclude private filenames/content/cell values/find queries/raw provider links/credentials; source audit follows its own restricted disclosure/retention/redaction. No service-worker/offline private cache or exported combined file is implied. Use existing owned repair/cleanup/incident controls; one pane's problem cannot justify provider-console/SQL repair, public URLs or a retention reset.

**Rollout, proof and traceability.** Activate only exact source/reader/composition cohorts that pass P01–P24 and inherited D32 obligations. Inventory/fence legacy metadata/public/raw-URL routes; do not backfill provenance, byte availability or permission from old stubs. Mixed client/schema/profile/worker versions, stop/rollback/restore SHALL retain source restrictions, original data, current action fences and cleanup; no fallback to an old insecure reader or replay of source effects. Map the selected decision through full owner/incorporated OpenSpec sections, design/tasks, later authorized tickets and native release evidence. Keep all twenty-one definitions unchanged; paired reading is an interaction, not a new business identity. No whole File Manager/Workflow Studio dependency is justified beyond the actual admitted owner seams.

**Exact inherited profiles.** These values apply only to the named source/representation and units, never as generic intake limits:

- Original and expanded input; 20 MiB streamed original per file; 200 MiB cumulative expanded package; Both files retain their individual eligibility. Pair scheduling has no automatic aggregate allowance.
- Images; 40,000,000 pixels per image; 256 MiB aggregate decoded-image working data per attempt; Two panes need measured decoding/viewer bounds; no eager full-document raster load.
- Output; 50 MiB cumulative output-byte content per generation; No new merged comparison output or enlarged per-generation limit.
- Attempt; 60 elapsed seconds; 30 aggregate process-tree CPU seconds; 2 GiB memory; 512 MiB scratch; Per-input limits remain; total concurrent reservations/device load require measured scheduling.
- Healthy-load fixture; 100 simultaneous opens across 20 tenants: 80 repeats across five same-source files and 20 distinct cold files, each ≤2 MiB and ≤10 pages or 1,000 cells; Preserve this fixture/measurement; it is not proof of 100 paired sessions. Define and execute a representative composed-pair fixture separately.
- Mobile fixture; 400 kbps, 300 ms round trip, 320 CSS px viewport; maximal/overload cases separate; Pair controls and progressive reading must remain usable without all-file eager transfer.

Story: US29-B09. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q29, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

## REQ29-A21 — Issue an exact private document share

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"076a6c05e3e841ea31ba","name":"REQ29-A21 — Issue an exact private document share"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Allow a named recipient to obtain source-authorized private document reading access without compulsory enrollment as a donor, missionary, staff member or organization member. The first concrete Q41 cohort is exact currently eligible evidence durably received from the participant or supplied by staff under Q28 and held by the ordinary Private Document Request source. That source owns a distinct outbound disclosure/read admission for its continuing lawful purpose; P29 supplies custody and the shared file experience. An issued unfinished or fulfilled request may qualify; fulfillment alone neither grants nor ends outbound access. Actual cancellation or an applicable adverse source restriction blocks new shares and affected future reads. Retention, acceptance, staff file access and a source reference alone do not establish disclosure authority. No new ordinary share admits historical/superseded material by inference or widens an existing specialist source lane.

Each new Private Document Share binds one exact tenant/environment, source/request/use, immutable selected evidence version, intended recipient/address revision, stated source purpose, explicitly permitted reading actions and a finite end. The staff actor must have the current source/P12 authority to administer that exact disclosure within its assignable ceiling; being able to read is not equivalent. That single source command may designate the named recipient under its qualified rules; no pre-created Party, guest directory entry or routine second approval is required. Q28 material carries all applicable restrictions of its supplied use, including any required origin permission for onward disclosure. Equal bytes and other independent uses neither confer nor automatically merge rights. The new Q41 lane pins the selected version and does not follow later uploads; independently qualified other-source current-version rules remain unchanged. A later correction or supersession never retargets the share and ends affected reads wherever the source ends eligibility. Declare P12 owner-transfer and grantor dependencies explicitly, including restricted-grantor departure; current qualified source administrators can manage the share without depending on its original issuer's continued availability.

Provide a qualified reading rendition for the actual permitted material. Original download is a separate explicit source-permitted action, never a preview-failure fallback. Do not issue a purportedly usable share with no qualified reading path: prepare/recover the rendition through the existing bounded owner process before issuance, or truthfully leave the share unissued. This is a source/custody readiness check, not mandatory grantor body inspection or a guarantee of continuous transport availability. Staff see the actual recipient, material/version, permitted actions and end in the existing source-context Share review. An owner-qualified finite default may populate the end; otherwise staff choose it there within the exact source's finite maximum and earlier limits. There is no universal share duration, implicit permanent grant, auto-renewal, follow-latest mode or inherited request-access clock.

Adopt the exact ordinary document-share recipient authority, including its legitimate no-Party branch, through source/native identity, P17 and P6. Qualify the complete initial invitation, requested confirmation and supported resend/correction/recovery paths with exact recipient, purpose, safe content, protected entry, deadlines and retention. The staff review identifies the actual invitation consequence of issuance, and temporary delivery delay remains visible separately from the committed share. P17 prepares; P6 alone dispatches and records delivery outcomes. Native Auth hooks must integrate that ownership rather than send through an untracked second mailer. Preserve consent/contact/provider restrictions and minimal safe identification; no evidence attachment, private title/body leak, arbitrary address, alternate-channel fallback or inherited Q20/Q31/Q35/Q40 notice default follows. Every actually required entry/communication branch must be qualified before offering the journey. Communication-only suppression gates messaging, not otherwise valid current reading; actual recipient/contact/proof invalidation or source privacy loss still denies affected reads. When fresh proof needs a blocked delivery path, do not bypass that restriction or claim access has succeeded.

Commit issuance, immutable original terms, actor attribution, business history and required delivery handoff coherently before dependent external effects. Use permanent semantic operation identity and a fingerprint of the intended scope; a matching committed operation is reconciled before new-command eligibility under current result-disclosure rights, and a reused key with different intent conflicts. Recover ambiguous source commit before retrying communication; provider acceptance with a lost response is unknown, not permission to create another share or fresh-key resend. Use complete exact Live P6 release or a fully proved zero; Reserved/unknown creates no historical send. Preserve sealed original material and every-call eligibility. Carry P17's closed three-cause recovery and at most two identical follow-up provider calls after the initial call, total within the earliest applicable deadline/window; other ambiguous outcomes reconcile only. A deliberate new qualified proof challenge is a separate bounded source act, not a disguised rekey of the old delivery. Source issuance, proof delivery, confirmation, admission, bytes served and human understanding remain distinct outcomes; provider failure does not undo issuance or justify pretending the recipient has access.

Persist mandatory scope and immutable version/use/recipient terms with tenant-aware typed relationships, finite timestamp and state checks, durable operation uniqueness and supporting indexes. Derive actor, tenant, authority, proof outcome and audit attribution from trusted execution context and current source records; caller-selected opaque IDs are only requested targets. Prevent issued authority columns from being reparented or rewritten through general updates. Actual grants, RLS SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE policies, old-row USING and new-row WITH CHECK behavior, views/RPCs/functions, storage and privileged worker/service routes must preserve the same boundary. Restrict deletion that would erase source/evidence/audit relationships; apply owner-directed retention/anonymization without leaving an active dangling grant. No money or financial fact mutation is part of this share.

Keep source business history, restricted security/access evidence and operational telemetry separate. Record actual grantor/reader or delegated service, exact source/use/version/share, recipient/proof revision, original end, issue/revoke/correction and allowed/denied retrieval/transport outcome under their owner retention rules. Do not log secrets, codes, raw private content or unnecessary addresses; safe aggregate diagnosis must remain possible. Qualify the exact Party/no-Party resolver, authenticated-data material binding, dispatch/history and any current reveal branch. Auth/security credentials have no readable Recent copy; other optional copies need the exact compatible reveal contract and shorter-of tenant Off/7/30-day versus contract 0/7/30-day retention, with unknown classification zero. Closed protected delivery carriers retain only their qualified finite material; no fake Party or optional-copy prerequisite follows. Bound confirmation attempts/sends/reservations, active operations, response bytes/time, concurrency, storage and per-tenant/global resource use through qualified shared controls. Resend must not reset abuse budgets, outages must not silently remove them, and an attacker must not permanently lock another person's unrelated account. Reuse existing rendition and transport work; no zero-cost claim, general guest directory, chat, read receipt, signature, watermark/DRM system, reminder engine or new parser/search dependency follows.

Publish the exact source, native identity/P12, P17/P6 and custody changes through their canonical authoring sources and declared OpenSpec projections at the authorized specification stage. Expand schema/readers, fence incompatible writers and old signup/public-serving paths, prove the exact hosted roles/source/host/provider and finite operational budgets, then enable a bounded source/tenant cohort. Unknown profiles remain unavailable. Old email flags, public links, restored bytes or unproved migrated rows create no Q41 grant. Rollback can disable new issuance or affected delivery while preserving evidence, immutable terms and denial fences; it must never route limited readers into old donor enrollment or public storage. Native positive/negative, race/replay/restore, migration, accessibility and actual staff/recipient proof are required before activation. No entire future Workflow/application product is a prerequisite, and this grooming review proves none of those runtime outcomes.

Private sharing remains a separate outbound source use. Q65 ongoing-reference readers need their actual current reference-purpose authority, not copied collection/share grants or general access to the original request.

Story: US29-A21. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q41, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

## REQ29-E05 — Qualify policy application through every path

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"07cc09fec9434ad29782","name":"REQ29-E05 — Qualify policy application through every path"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q62-C13 — Database and every-path enforcement.** Use typed same-Tenant/environment/source relationships with actual foreign-key or equivalent validated reference integrity, null-complete scoped composite relationships, suitable uniqueness for semantic effects/current applicability and valid state/trigger combinations, deliberate nullability and delete rules, immutable admitted attribution/history and source-qualified lawful privacy correction. One current assignment per defined source-use scope is not one policy for every record class. Qualified platform-global capability or specialist-source definitions need not be duplicated into Tenant ownership. Q66 ordinary tenant business-policy definitions and versions are tenant-owned; their assignments, permitted variants and effects require actual source/Tenant compatibility. Actor/tenant/approval/policy provenance fields are never caller-write authority. Keep P12 fine-grained current source decisions and coarse Tenant RLS; browser table grants cannot bypass the command or expose forbidden fields. Verify effective table/column grants, policy composition, old `USING` and resulting `WITH CHECK` behavior, SELECT/RETURNING/upsert paths, views, function owner/search path/named and inherited EXECUTE, service/BYPASS roles, storage, direct API/RPC, imports, jobs and restore. An allowed update must not reparent or transform a permitted row into forbidden scope. Application and database Tenant/environment context must agree through actual supported transactions, with safe pooled cleanup on success/error/cancellation/retry; a callback of separate calls is not assumed atomic. Verify schema/sequence/default privileges, trigger transformations and any exposed GraphQL/MERGE/bulk paths as well. Zero affected rows is not automatically business success. Neither FORCE RLS nor a DEFINER keyword proves safety. No binaries/policy copies in Auth metadata, broad user-editable JSON or new schema/API names are mandated by this review.

**Q62-C14 — Safe projections and disclosure.** Authoritative operational facts belong to their source in Asym Postgres; summaries/options/current badges are permission-filtered projections. They must not become editable policy authorities. Register relevant binding/trigger/history fields through the existing shared static field classification and subtract-only projection contract; do not introduce a competing field-policy mechanism. Revalidate disclosure for every read/result recovery and reject late results after user/Tenant/environment/source changes; clear unsafe client caches. Key any permitted cache by the complete relevant scope and authority basis, and do not use stale cache as admission proof. Policy names, alternative existence, hold/case details, errors, history, counts, logs and exports require the same field/purpose protection. Participants receive only independently authorized understandable explanations, never internal records controls or a new consent/attestation duty. Avoid persistent sensitive device caching, tokens or private text in telemetry. Actual lawful audit/tombstone retention and privacy repair are source-defined, not retain-everything forever.

**Q62-C15 — Failure and capable operation.** Distinguish no applicable policy, required choice, temporary verification failure, relevant conflict, committed success and unknown outcome. Keep permitted input and show the next effective action. A policy-owner handoff must reach someone with the actual authority and usable correction/qualification path; routine repair cannot require raw SQL or provider-console edits. Reuse existing source/P9 work where operational follow-through is actually needed, without creating a new issue queue or a task for every valid request. No new email, bell, reminder, subscription or provider effect is implied; existing P6/P17/Q25–Q48 effects retain their own occurrence/current-utility rules. Durable business history, security audit and technical diagnostics remain separate; diagnostics use safe correlation identifiers and stage/outcome codes.

**Q62-C16 — Finite performance and minimal implementation.** Reuse existing source admission/policy/version, trusted context, request review, records correction and custody seams wherever they meet the contract. Q66 adds a narrow ordinary-source tenant policy editor and complete change lifecycle. Do not turn it into a universal rule/SQL language, AI classifier, content scanner, separate authorization engine, snapshot platform or per-file polling service. Before activation qualify explicit finite budgets for policy options/resolution, mutation/current checks, history/impact work and repair/cleanup under representative roles/Tenant sizes. Use set-wise bounded reads, appropriate scope/relation/operation indexes, stable paging and tenant fairness; avoid N+1 per file, full-history admission scans and unbounded fan-out. Retain actual P12 R6 logical-governance bounds and its physical-shard/restricted-tier distinctions; do not invent a global query number or import another feature's latency SLA. Numeric operating budgets are measured owner-approved qualification inputs, never guessed legal periods.

**Q62-C17 — Migration, rollout and rollback.** Introduce only additive compatible representations/commands where required. Inventory retained records, all writers/readers/imports/jobs/direct paths and actual policy/trigger evidence before mapping; never fabricate historic assignment, actor, date or approval from an upload timestamp. Unknown legacy records retain qualified containment/repair and cannot gain new destructive eligibility. Activate per supported source cohort only after policy, current authorization, correction, copy coverage and native tests pass. A kill switch stops new unsafe acts while preserving lawful existing custody/read/recovery and recorded obligations. Rollback must not restore bypassing old writers or reinterpret new data as ungoverned; keep compatible protections or roll forward. Reconciliation/restoration applies adverse facts before access. Old-code/new-schema and new-code/old-schema behavior and backfill resumes require explicit proof.

Story: US29-E05. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q62-C13, Q62-C14, Q62-C15, Q62-C16, Q62-C17; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

## REQ29-B19 — Commit a linked replacement handoff

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"0816415c2e1b31616201","name":"REQ29-B19 — Commit a linked replacement handoff"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

For the normal path, one authoritative source transaction SHALL co-commit the actual eligible Q34 cancellation and parent authority fence, the new request's Q25-qualified original issuance, the unique issued predecessor-to-successor relationship, mandatory business/audit history, durable semantic result and required local follow-through/outbox work. Keep providers, remote calls and long processing outside that transaction. Use the relevant source aggregate ordering or equivalent proved database isolation/fences across the affected local facts; multiple browser/API/RPC calls are not a transaction. A definite rejection or rollback commits neither new issuance nor the attempted old cancellation. A lost response after commit recovers the original result. Do not present Canceled plus an unissued draft as completed replacement. If the source cannot qualify this coherent ordinary boundary, that replacement path cannot activate; do not substitute a broad distributed saga or compensating reopening.

Order replacement against actual fulfillment, cancellation, new evidence/clarification, instruction/requirement changes and relevant authority changes. Fulfillment first prevents this unfinished-request cancellation and stale replacement; a mere review click or queued evaluation is not fulfillment. Evidence or a clarification admitted first remains an original fact and requires current relevant source review of the proposed remaining work; it does not transfer into the successor. Cancellation/handoff first fences new ordinary predecessor actions, while already admitted independent duties retain their own authority. A prior independent cancellation may continue only through its expressly qualified remedy branch, not by treating all terminal states alike. Current revocation, wrong context, missing required history or unresolved authority fails closed for the affected act without erasing independently known outcomes.

Enforce at most one ever-issued direct replacement successor per predecessor and at most one predecessor for that replacement, in the same tenant/environment/source. The relationship cannot point to itself, form a cycle, reparent either issued request or be cleared by later cancellation, retention cleanup or restoration to make its slot reusable. Any issuance of a bound replacement draft through a generic Issue action, import, service or alternate writer must enforce the same handoff or exact earlier-cancellation continuation; no unlinking or relabeling a bound draft bypasses it. A subsequent mistake after issuance is addressed through the current successor's qualified source remedy and, when appropriate, its own new replacement; the old predecessor is never revived or reused to issue a sibling replacement. Distinct genuinely independent source needs keep their ordinary authoring paths and cannot use this operation to manufacture duplicate obligations. Linkage is historical fact, not a separate status owner or an access grant.

Use permanent semantic effect identity for preparation creation, draft changes, independent stop and final issuance/handoff, with explicit material user intent separated from resolved qualification witnesses and generated IDs/times. Recover a matching committed effect under current safe disclosure before applying fresh eligibility; return its original request/terms plus truthful current status rather than replaying its writes or substituting the latest successor. Changed material intent conflicts. Canonical no-op saves change no business revision, event, clock or count; stale expected basis still conflicts. Concurrent different transport keys cannot issue two successors. Retry serialization failures as the complete intended transaction with current source checks; reconcile uncertain commitment rather than inventing a fresh operation key. Providers' temporary idempotency windows, client button disabling and worker leases are not permanent effect identity.

Preserve the predecessor's Q34 cancellation meaning in full: no new ordinary predecessor intake, correction, review decision, item change or clarification; no unacceptance, deletion, waived requirement or fabricated fulfillment. Preserve durable receipts, accepted evidence, conversation, actor attribution, independently lawful safety/review follow-through, retention, holds and other uses. Its optional finite canceled-outcome response may use only the exact then-valid unfinished admission and unchanged end/caps/current restrictions under Q34; no files, transcript, internal reason or new person's identity is disclosed, and no later root or canceled renewal is created. Otherwise show neutral unavailable/help. Existing independent shares/rights retain their actual source scope and any applicable restriction, not a blanket global revoke or transfer. Already delivered copies cannot be recalled by this operation.

The successor is a new original request with fresh item identities, its own real issuance occurrence, Q11/Q12 original finite access terms, Q25 responsibility and Q01 recipient proof/current-authority requirements. It is not Q13/Q15 fresh continuation or inherited Q06 completed reading. Resolve and review its Requested by target under Q09/Q46 rather than blindly carrying forward old terms or backdating new work; a still-appropriate source-valid value, including a permitted past date, may be deliberately reused with new current applicability. Receiving history and source-sufficient facts keep their original meaning; no universal rule that the target must precede the access end, and no silent access extension, is introduced. Do not copy old ends, auto-renew old grants, promise a reset through resend, force a full donor/missionary account or create a fake Party. Qualified existing proof for the new principal/address/request purpose may be reused only under Q01's real rules. Source issuance, notification delivery, proof exchange, receipt, safety, acceptance and fulfillment remain distinct.

For Q48, end old recipient-work reminder eligibility through the actual predecessor cancellation/source fence. Preserve all old plans, captured settings, stops, used counts, reservations, original recipient/material pins and possible-send/unknown history; do not reroute, refund or revive old occurrences. A genuinely different source-authorized person may receive a genuinely new original request and its own Q48 plan/count under the tenant's current enabled policy and permitted staff choice, with fresh issuance timing; this is a new request, not a reset of the old one. Display that new enrollment choice at review. Do not automatically copy the former person's Stop/allow or pause state to another person, or clear restrictions whose actual scope still covers the new action. Source/P3 eligibility must reject nominal identity changes, recreation or contact aliases used to evade an existing person's stop, limit or contact restriction. A shared mailbox proves neither new identity nor new permission. No Q48 event or numerical bound is otherwise amended.

Reuse exact existing communication purposes: one actual Q34 cancellation may produce its Q35 occurrence under current tenant/default/staff choice, recipient/contact/privacy and usefulness rules; a cancellation already committed earlier produces no second cancellation occurrence on replacement issuance. Do not notify a known-wrong former recipient. A formerly legitimate recipient whose responsibility changed may still receive a factual Q35 notice when its current independent disclosure/contact qualification holds; impose no blanket cancellation-email ban. The successor's actual original invitation/follow-through uses its qualified source/P17/P6 contract, with required local handoff durable at issuance; provider completion is not a prerequisite for source success. No new generic recipient-changed notice, extra channel, automatic staff broadcast or copied old message is introduced. Recheck actual purpose, identity, contact, source and disclosure at the existing required dispatch boundaries. Keep the exact closed P17 binding, complete all-before-any message-plan release, P6 sealed original recipient/material/provider identity and original recovery/retry windows; no direct provider fallback, partial plan release, fresh-key resend or false recall. A failed/unknown invitation is repaired as that original communication effect and never by issuing another request.

Keep source relationships, historical facts and serving boundaries consistent across staff, recipient, search, lists, counts, activity, export, notifications, result recovery, caches and retained copies. The staff-visible predecessor/successor relationship itself requires current disclosure permission for its permitted content; seeing the successor grants no predecessor read, and old-recipient status never reveals the new person. Scope every native reference to tenant/environment/source/request/item/recipient as appropriate. Derive tenant, actor, author, timestamps, admitted outcomes and audit attribution from trusted context; validate selected recipient and proposed content under current source rules. Make required scope non-null, prevent self/cross-scope relationships with native keys and constraints, preserve uniqueness and immutable issuance/actor facts, and forbid cascades that erase independent history or reopen the successor slot. No money type, precision, posted fact or financial-owner mutation is introduced.

Use the sole fine-grained source/P12 decision and prescribed coarse Tenant RLS, with exact least-privilege grants and real effective-role checks on every supported API, command, direct DML, RPC/function, view, worker/service, import and restoration path. Test old-row selection and resulting-row state for INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE, SELECT/RETURNING, UPSERT/ON CONFLICT and any supported MERGE; an allowed draft save cannot change scope, issued recipient, actor, cancellation truth or relationship identity. Account for combined policies, function EXECUTE, schemas/search_path, actual definer owner/BYPASS/RLS and view execution—not generic claims that all definers bypass or that omitted WITH CHECK always permits reassignment. The current named-grant lockdown does not qualify this new source command or justify a service-client fallback. Cache invalidation is not an authorization fence.

This path requires a different intended person. Q60 same-recipient corrective work has its own one-ever-issued remedial result, automatic reminders unavailable/Off and exact independently qualified manual/issuance notices. Same-request correction retains Q48 counts/stops. Both preserve unaffected evidence and original outcomes.

Story: US29-B19. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q49, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

## REQ29-B04 — Person or team review responsibility

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"090697158442e8e9a666","name":"REQ29-B04 — Person or team review responsibility"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Source-owned responsibility.** The ordinary source SHALL define the specified work, same-tenant/source/purpose destination, person/team qualification and usable work/recovery route. A qualified team may retain responsibility during an actual human review without mandatory Claim or personal reassignment. Preserve specialist reviewer/SoD constraints. A bare Team, contact, watcher list or queue row SHALL NOT qualify by itself. This does not freeze a new table, mandatory XOR field, one-primary portfolio model, per-item assignment designer or shared Team aggregate. The source must nevertheless resolve coherent accountable responsibility rather than contradictory destinations for the same work.

**Authority and trusted identity.** The source SHALL independently check authority to administer responsibility, eligibility of the selected destination and the actual human's authority to inspect/perform each substantive action. Assignment, team membership, administrative/read roles and task visibility SHALL NOT themselves grant these rights. Use trusted tenant/environment/actor/time and validated stable source/item/destination selectors; caller-chosen identifiers are not trusted authority. Current P12/source rules, including relevant membership/grant/clearance and specialist SoD, govern at the action boundary after waits. Preserve independently valid authority where the source permits it. Any separately permitted NHI coordination retains its required current human owner/ceiling; a team route is not an NHI and cannot replace the actual human reviewer.

**Defaults and genuine gaps.** The source SHALL reuse a valid responsibility and resolve defaults through its explicit policy. Do not silently overwrite a valid deliberate handoff on refresh, retry, creator departure, changed label or delayed default projection. Source-defined dynamic changes remain possible through their qualified transition; Q24 mandates neither universal snapshotting nor automatic inheritance. Distinguish a known unresolved/invalid destination, known lack of eligible actors, unknown required qualification and ordinary absence. Unknown required authority fails closed for its dependent operation without erasing known work or claiming no reviewer exists. Preserve a known route's identity/history where appropriate. Require a capable source-owned repair/response path; no first-row/all-admin/fake-person fallback, mandatory online presence/calendar or new grace period.

**Exact handoff and durable recovery.** Allowed responsibility changes SHALL use a qualified source mutation with current administration/target checks, material expected heads, old/new responsibility, actual actor and source-required reason/pending-work consequences. Do not add a mandatory essay where a concise existing reason suffices. Generic Tasks Hub assignee editing is not this authority. Coherently commit mandatory source facts, history and durable handoff; co-located source/outbox writes are atomic, providers outside locks. Serialize relevant competing handoffs/reviews and preserve Q03/Q21/Q23 exact fences without making unrelated coordination revisions invalidate still-authorized review. Durable business-effect identity survives transport/lease expiry. Recover known success under current disclosure, reconcile unknown commitment, conflict on changed material intent, and prevent stale defaults/replays/projections from overwriting a later successor. Preserve one logical source work identity; no per-member duplicate tasks or exclusive review/view lease.

**Preserve source lifecycle.** Responsibility change SHALL NOT reattribute earlier human decisions, delete received evidence, alter correction lineage, erase applicable concerns, unaccept items, rewrite fulfillment or reset recipient access/due/read clocks. Q22 completion still follows all actual source conditions: missing responsibility is not a new completion condition after those conditions are lawfully met. No automatic re-review follows mere reassignment/departure. Existing source issuance/intake/read rules remain independent; Q24 adds no universal intake gate. Staff may continue independently authorized work after recipient expiry without reviving recipient access. Real source policy changes and separately authorized corrections use their own contracts. Q20 remains fresh-access notification only; no assignment/review-result notice policy is selected.

**Clear, accessible work.** In the existing request/My Work context, the UI SHALL distinguish current person/team responsibility, actual recorded reviewer and participant contact/help. Reuse valid resolution without a repetitive picker ritual. Where changes are allowed, show sufficient permitted identity/type disambiguation, predictable deliberate commit/cancel and the actual source result. No extra confirmation/claim/second reviewer or automatic ownership transfer merely from viewing/replying. Preserve authorized context/focus and independent known success during ancillary failures; discard stale responses across user/tenant/request/purpose/authority changes. A saved responsibility does not mean notified, seen, reviewing or accepted. Show honest assignment-needed versus unavailable-check versus denied states with a real next route. Qualify composed keyboard, screen-reader, touch, narrow/zoomed, localization, long/duplicate-name and low-bandwidth behavior. Keep inherited document-content accessibility obligations.

**Data and disclosure integrity.** Qualification SHALL prove stable tenant-aware keys/references and valid destination/state combinations, scoped nullability for genuine unresolved state, trusted actor/history/effect attribution, and effective grants/RLS USING and WITH CHECK, views/functions/RPCs/storage/trigger transforms and privileged paths. An allowed update cannot move work to a forbidden tenant/source/destination or forge reviewer attribution. Rename does not change identity; archive/delete/recreate or source reparenting cannot silently preserve invalid eligibility or transfer old identity. Preserve historical acts under governed retention/redaction/disposition; no destructive cascade or indefinite-retention inference. Current purpose governs candidate/work lists, counts, search, errors, caches and replay before disclosure. Participants receive approved status/help, not internal routing/rosters/history by default. Keep private evidence, contacts and credentials out of ordinary logs. Bound indexed resolution, membership checks, current work, history and recovery without whole-history scans or per-member task fanout; establish finite supported workload/latency/recovery budgets before activation and retain existing applicable P12 limits.

**Qualification and compatible adoption.** Before activation, the native source owner SHALL qualify exact supported person/team/admin/reviewer paths and capable recovery against P12/P9 and applicable Workflow/GEN-06 contracts. Prove source commands and effective roles, relevant races/faults/replay, mixed versions/import/restore, production-shaped workload and composed intended-user/browser/AT outcomes. Legacy finance queue/assignee flags cannot fabricate native team eligibility, reviewer history or grants. Gate unsupported old writers/consumers; preserve later history/effect identity and restrictions during scoped stop, roll-forward and restore, reconciling restored state with outward effects suppressed before resumption. At authorized publication reconcile canonical owner requirements/design/tasks/glossary/ADRs and full incorporated OpenSpec projections, then later authorized tickets/tests/release evidence. Q24 requires neither full Workflow Studio nor another permission/queue/routing/notification engine.

Story: US29-B04. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q24, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

## REQ29-A12 — Sixty elapsed days for ordinary unfinished request access

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"0971a4fc6489d3b58446","name":"REQ29-A12 — Sixty elapsed days for ordinary unfinished request access"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Exact ordinary profile.** Newly admitted qualified ordinary requests SHALL use a nominal unfinished end of original Q11 source issuance plus 1,440 elapsed hours (5,184,000 seconds). This is not calendar months/days, business days, end-of-day, a requested-target buffer or first-entry time. Preserve the qualified source instant/precision model.

**Scoped earlier limits.** Resolve the actual whole-unfinished end no later than the nominal result and genuinely applicable earlier whole-stage limits. Every limit SHALL retain action/item/representation/stage/purpose/overall scope. Requested targets, upload cutoffs and proof/representation TTLs SHALL NOT automatically become whole-request or completed-stage caps.

**No discretionary interval.** “Up to” SHALL mean the selected profile under genuine restrictions and source transitions, not arbitrary sender/tenant choice from zero to sixty. No date editor, override, pause/grace, renewal, duration menu or new policy-builder capability is created.

**Original output and provenance.** Preserve original issuance, exact admitted policy/result, calculation meaning and relevant limit provenance. Same-intent recovery SHALL resolve the existing effect before new arithmetic/defaults. Transport keys, credentials, target/calendar/locale changes and current defaults SHALL NOT reset or recompute a later original end.

**Valid finite source arithmetic.** Required issued-state times/results SHALL be finite, correctly typed, unambiguous and within supported range/precision. Reject malformed/null/infinite/overflowed or already-ended live terms; no now-plus-default, local-midnight or truncation fallback. Authoritative comparisons SHALL preserve the exclusive boundary.

**Current source order.** Preserve Q11 exact issuer/recipient/context capability and currentness, after-wait checks, causal occurrence/physical-commit distinction and coherent durable effect. No browser/provider timestamp, early SQL time sample, stale lease or queued wake alone establishes current authority.

**Correct completed transition.** Eligible normal fulfillment SHALL stop ordinary writes and apply Q07/Q10's distinct completed end, which may be earlier or later than the unfinished end. Do not use max(U,F+720h), retain a minimum sixty-day entitlement or impose an old stage-only U as a completed cap.

**No revival or pause.** Delivery, first entry, proof refresh, activity, workflow pause, correction, upload start and source review SHALL NOT pause/replenish the source interval. Actual completion after unfinished expiry cannot create ordinary completed admission. Recover proved prior effects under original terms and current disclosure; no processing-time clock.

**Due-target independence.** Requested-by targets remain nonauthorizing under Q09. Missing/past/changed targets SHALL NOT silently change this period. A later target must be surfaced as a possible timing mismatch, not represented as permission beyond access or turned into a universal target-only rejection rule.

**Prospective exact adoption.** Apply the profile only to explicitly bound new ordinary effects. Preserve valid existing/specialized terms. Map Workflow's fourteen-day suggestion to its actual source/credential scope and explicitly amend any conflicting ordinary-source clause during authorized publication. Do not silently relabel or extend old rights, import renewal or change other-purpose settings.

**Short technical capabilities.** Entry credentials, proof/sessions, provider URLs, worker leases and caches SHALL keep their independently qualified shorter limits/current checks. Do not mint a sixty-day bearer/session capability or remove Q01 assurance because the source period is longer.

**Structural authorization.** Preserve sole P12 resolver, exact native participant integration, coarse tenant RLS and required grant mutation boundary. Trusted actor/tenant/recipient/source/policy/time/approval attribution SHALL be server-derived or validated at its authoritative boundary; same-scope constraints and effective old/new-row enforcement SHALL prevent forbidden state transformations on every path.

**Every private use.** Current source stage, exact recipient/action/representation authority and applicable end SHALL protect metadata/history/counts/search/replay and full/HEAD/range/conditional/resume/stream paths. A future nominal timestamp or stale successful cache SHALL NOT authorize access; preserve inherited tiered audit and stream/revocation rules.

**Durable expiry enforcement.** Source expiry SHALL not depend on a single long process/browser timer, cleanup sweep or provider TTL. Persisted ends/current permission remain authoritative. Durable wakes and projections SHALL be bounded, replay-safe and stage-aware; early/stale/late execution cannot revoke valid completed access, prolong ended rights or delete evidence.

**Truthful UX.** Show actual applicable end/zone/precision, separate Requested by, next actor, received/review state and permitted actions in the existing source context. Explain prospectively that completed access has its own end and can end sooner. No full-sixty-day promise, forced download, extra timer ceremony or recipient blame for review.

**Accessible recovery.** Qualify actual timing/authentication/status/mobile/AT journeys. Proof/observer outages fail closed for affected actions without false source-expiry/rejection/deletion claims. Preserve confirmed work and other independently authorized actions; no countdown/tooltip/color/toast-only essential information.

**Retention and minimization.** This access period SHALL not determine evidence retention, holds, deletion/anonymization, backups or independent file uses. Preserve required original/adverse provenance under owned records rules; general telemetry and support surfaces SHALL not expose credentials, private files or unnecessary sensitive identity details.

**Lifecycle and help authority.** Contact/recipient/host changes, issuer-dependency changes, new work, reopening, active-expiry recovery and Q08 fresh access SHALL follow their exact owner procedures. A selected count creates no new grant/edit power or email-attachment bypass. Help must be accountable without promising unselected remedies.

**Bounded costs and dependencies.** Preserve existing source/worker/storage interfaces and P12 R6 governance-read/mutation-fence contracts. Qualify query/page/lock/latency/egress/retry budgets and provider limits. No new global serialization beyond owner-required fences, unbounded scans, long-lived resident process/browser timer per request or second scheduling/permission platform. Bounded durable wakes in the existing qualified substrate remain allowed.

**Durable evidence.** Authorized history SHALL explain original profile/issuance/result, genuine restrictions, receipts, source completion and recovery separately from delivery/engagement logs. Do not infer reading, receipt, human intent or current access from provider events; source effect identity must survive provider dedupe expiry.

**Compatible migration and restore.** Version/bind profiles prospectively and prove mixed readers/writers/workers, policy changes, restore, stop and rollback/roll-forward. Do not backfill unknown issuance from tasks/emails, replace old ends with sixty days, lose later restrictions or run old calendar/stage semantics on new effects.

**Qualification and trace.** The sixty-day choice is product judgment. Before activation, prove actual remaining opportunity under caps for intended recipient/reviewer/help cohorts plus real-role/time/race/provider/browser/AT outcomes. Trace Q12 through existing glossary, ADR, authorized owner/generated OpenSpec, tasks/tickets and release evidence. Unknown numeric service budgets or unusable cohorts are gates, not “monitor later.”

Story: US29-A12. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q12, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

## REQ29-P06 — Commit authoritative effects coherently and preserve safety before background work

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"0cb3fad8134ffaad898e","name":"REQ29-P06 — Commit authoritative effects coherently and preserve safety before background work"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Use the actual source transaction or qualified equivalent ordering boundary to commit the authoritative result, required history, current controls and durable secondary intent coherently. Where a source explicitly requires an enclosing database transaction—especially Q52's all-or-none new selected acceptances—that requirement is not weakened into a compensating saga or independent SDK writes. Safety-critical restrictions must be effective at the controlling admission boundary before deferred projections or impact processing. Keep expensive scanning/conversion/provider IO outside short locks. A claim, callback, service credential, authenticated wrapper or handoff ledger is not transactionality. Follow-through is durable, bounded and retryable under the original effect, while current eligibility is rechecked wherever a fresh external submission would occur.

## REQ29-A19 — Sequential fresh completed-read access after expiry

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"0ed06b3b0d5dd372ef0b","name":"REQ29-A19 — Sequential fresh completed-read access after expiry"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Allow the exact currently source-authorized staff actor to approve another fresh completed-read period after eligible ordinary expiry of the relevant preceding period for the same intended recipient and lawful Q16 completed admission. Preserve original completed proof, relevant predecessor/current lineage, actual ending causes and current purpose/authority. An ancient expired root, a copied request, missing history or no active-row result cannot create eligibility or bypass later adverse facts. Restrictions retain their real scope.

Each new deliberate admission uses Q17/Q18's fixed source-issued period of up to 720 elapsed hours, with the actual earlier-limited end, including unused access. There is no preset lifetime grant-count or cumulative-time ceiling, mandatory waiting gap, automatic renewal, pre-expiry extension or future reservation. No overlapping fresh read periods may exist in the same tenant/ environment/source/request/recipient/original-completed-admission context, including disjoint subsets. Other recipients and independent rights retain their owners. Do not terminate a valid period merely to make room for another. Legitimate revocation/remediation remains source-owned; its adverse ending cannot be relabeled ordinary expiry. No uninterrupted access or later approval is promised.

Each freshly reviewed subset is the original Q16 completed member/submitted- original-version/action/Q05-context ceiling intersected with current eligibility and this review. Do not union previous grants or silently ratchet the ceiling to the last discretionary narrow subset. Genuine binding limits remain. The request stays completed and read-only; fulfillment, receipts, accepted/ frozen evidence, correction lineage, immutable originals, retention/holds/ disposal and independent purposes remain unchanged. Retained files alone are not a sufficient reason for another admission or a personal archive.

Recover a matching committed intent on its exact original lineage and terms, under current disclosure, before applying new-admission rules. A later grant does not replace its historical result. A failed response after admission is still a committed effect; unknown commitment requires reconciliation. Only a confirmed pre-admission failure/rollback establishes no effect. Changed payloads, new transport keys and uncertainty cannot manufacture another approval.

For a genuinely unadmitted intent, revalidate material reviewed predecessor/ source state and exact current issuer capability, administrative scope and live assignable ceiling at the existing source/P12 mutation fence. Atomically enforce the same-context nonoverlap rule across different staff keys, subsets and nonexistent-next-row cases. A stale intent cannot silently advance after an intervening period ends. Do not park an ineligible command for automatic later issuance. Qualified checks after ordinary waits remain; no new browser- click clock or artificial cooldown is introduced. Preserve coherent required effect/audit/follow-up evidence and keep provider work outside source locks.

Protect trusted relationships, required finite state, semantic uniqueness, historical outputs and actor attribution across effective database grants, USING/WITH CHECK, policy composition and direct/view/RPC/definer/service/job/ storage paths. An allowed update/upsert/parent change cannot retarget, widen or reset a period. Each use requires current recipient proof, purpose and scope plus the exact mapped continuing grantor/delegation dependency; do not invent blanket survival or revocation on every issuer role change. An old expired/revoked credential cannot become sufficient by resolving to a newer grant. Compatible proof reuse remains allowed only under its owner contract.

Reuse the existing **Grant read access** action and completed context. Staff review the safe eligible purpose/subset and standard timing terms once, with estimates labeled before confirmed issuance and actual result/end afterward. Material changes require meaningful refresh; ordinary time passing alone does not. Distinguish new issuance, current use, proof, representation, delivery and unknown outcomes. Keep useful safe help, partial/status-only access, localized actual dates/zones, shared Base UI/base-maia and accessible mobile/low-bandwidth recovery. Preserve neutral unqualified responses and protected diagnostics.

Flexibility adds no arbitrary quota, forced account, mandatory essay, routine second approval, date editor, new request queue, renewal dashboard or automatic notification. Existing genuine source requirements remain. Repair still-valid access or perform independently permitted staff work without another grant. Do not clone/reopen a request, force reupload or use an attachment fallback to evade the source. Discretionary later access does not replace applicable accessibility requirements for the current interaction.

Use bounded indexed source-owned current/predecessor/intent proof and authorized paginated history, retaining P12 budgets and documented exceptions. A coherent current relation is not another write authority. Required minimal history follows its owner retention policy; no indefinite private payload log or full chain reconstruction is needed. Mixed versions, import/export, deletion, compaction and restore must preserve original effects/ends, fixed ceiling and later restrictions. No invented root/date backfill or broad fallback. Existing controls can stop unsafe new issuance while preserving history and current serving authority; no rollback may erase a later grant or adverse fact.

## REQ29-B05 — Review readiness before original issuance

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"0ff84225a3bdc8d7a3b1","name":"REQ29-B05 — Review readiness before original issuance"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Exact prospective scope.** Before first original ordinary issuance, the source SHALL establish current Q24 review responsibility and usable eligible coverage for the declared source review work/purpose, including genuine declared specialist/item restrictions. One generic eligible reviewer for an unrelated portion is insufficient. Qualified team members may cover different scopes without permission union, mandatory per-item assignment UI, redundant reviewers or one-person-for-all requirements. This is prospective for unissued requests, including previously saved drafts. Q11's causal original issuance remains distinct from draft/task/credential/email/visit/receipt; existing fresh admissions and already-issued intake are not newly gated by implication.

**Minimal qualification and separate authority.** Reuse valid source resolution and defaults. The source/P12 boundary SHALL separately establish trusted current issuer/context/purpose/recipient authority, exact responsibility-change authority if used, destination/declared coverage eligibility and actual later review rights. Callers SHALL NOT forge tenant, actor, approval, issue time/end or readiness. Qualified minimal source evidence may prove a team path without disclosing/enumerating every member, simulating another user's session, persisting a roster snapshot or naming a claimant. A changed eligible witness within the same still-qualified team is not itself a new personal assignment or material user intent. A deliberately selected named person cannot silently become somebody else. No future-file-row grant, preview, byte inspection, online presence, availability calendar, workload/SLA guarantee or creator/all-admin fallback is required or inferred. Actual future evidence receives its own current checks. Any already-supported automated issuer retains its source/P12 NHI policy and current human-owner/ceiling requirements; no new automated issuance lane is selected.

**Authoritative admission and relevant races.** The readiness predicate SHALL be enforced through the qualified source issuance boundary for every supported UI/API/worker entry, with current material source/recipient/purpose/requirement/responsibility/authority facts after relevant waits. Client preflight is explanatory, not an authorization credential. Coherently persist issuance, mandatory history, original terms/minimal qualification basis and required durable handoff; co-located source/outbox facts commit atomically, providers outside locks. Order material eligibility loss, target/source edits and issuance through the source's proved currentness/concurrency contract; a request-row lock or early timestamp alone does not prove multi-row authority. Reevaluate still-valid same-team coverage without gratuitous personal handoff or conflicts on irrelevant roster/display changes. Do not silently or without source authority weaken purpose, declared restrictions or requiredness to bypass readiness. Genuine authorized source edits retain their own version/consequences and recompute coverage for the actual new declared work.

**Original effect recovery.** Current authorization to access the result still applies, but an authorized same original committed effect SHALL be recovered before treating a later readiness gap as a new-issuance failure. Separate immutable material command intent from server-resolved witness/provenance and generated original issuance/time/end. An explicit changed source/recipient/material selection conflicts; a fresh membership lookup result is not fabricated new intent. Preserve durable original effect identity beyond transport keys/worker leases and across concurrent initializers. Reconcile unknown commitment before new issuance or destructive compensation. Do not dismiss possibly committed source work or reset source time because a response/task refresh/provider result was lost. Late/replayed consumers cannot regress responsibility, duplicate source work or renew access. Known issued outcome remains true during independently failing delivery/projection.

**Lean actionable UX.** A valid existing person/team responsibility SHALL require no extra picker, Claim, reviewer acknowledgment or confirmation ritual. Present only permitted meaningful responsibility/readiness context, then use the existing deliberate issue action. When blocked, distinguish a repairable selection, missing coverage, unavailable service, denied operation and unknown issuance result; never label service/permission failures as user input mistakes. Preserve separately authorized preparation/focus/reading context and known success, but do not call it safely saved unless persistence succeeded. If the issuer cannot repair, expose a capable permitted source response without granting more access or an inert admin button. No disabled-button-only dead end, silent background issue on repair, automatic retry subscription, offline private cache or recipient workaround. Existing qualified durable intent may resume only under its own current source contract. Discard stale cross-user/tenant/source/purpose results. Qualify real keyboard/AT/touch/reflow/zoom/locale/long-name/low-bandwidth behavior using Core's shared design system.

**Clock, delivery and later lifecycle.** A definitely unissued preparation has not started Q11/Q12 access; an unknown result is not proof of that state. If a supported operation only schedules preparation, recheck at its actual later source issuance; if source admission occurs now with delayed delivery, check now and preserve its original clock. Do not introduce scheduling or infer its semantics from a button name. Once lawfully issued, later staffing loss does not by itself erase the act, recall evidence, reset time, block all independent intake, force reupload, unaccept items or add a Q22 completion predicate. Actual current source restrictions still govern each operation and qualified recovery remains required. Preserve Q01–Q24, including causal timely/pre-visit fulfillment, original/fresh scope and no automatic completed access after expiry. No new notice or bypass switch is selected.

**Data, privacy and bounded operation.** Qualification SHALL verify tenant-aware stable source/recipient/responsibility relationships; one original logical admission/effect; valid unissued/issued state and immutable original occurrence/terms/attribution; safe nullability for preparation versus required admitted evidence; meaningful constraints, delete/reparent behavior and indexes. Dynamic cross-row eligibility belongs in the single qualified mutation/currentness boundary, not a fake static CHECK or task flag. Exercise effective grants/RLS USING and WITH CHECK, views/functions/RPC/storage/trigger transforms and privileged/worker/import paths; a permitted update cannot turn a row into forbidden scope or issued authority. Existing timestamp/money semantics remain: no financial field/precision change. Minimize retained qualification evidence, protect current candidate/count/error/replay disclosure and private logs, and apply existing retention/redaction/disposition. Bound indexed current coverage, admitted-effect lookup, history and recovery under explicit finite measured workload/latency budgets and existing applicable P12 limits; no whole-roster/history scan or per-member task fanout on the admission path.

**Qualification, migration and trace.** Before activation, qualify the actual native source/P12/P9/custody and applicable Workflow binding for C01–C07, with real-role/direct/privileged authority, race/fault/replay, migration/restore/load and composed staff/recipient/help/AT evidence. Do not reuse financial sequencing or scanner fixtures as native proof. Existing lawful issuances remain historical acts; do not fabricate missing historical readiness evidence or reissue/revoke merely because they predate Q25. Existing unissued drafts still face the new gate when first issued after activation. All participating writers/consumers must be compatible or gated before enabling the policy; a scoped stop/rollback cannot silently restore an old ungated writer or erase lawful later acts. Reconcile restored source/effect/current restrictions with outward effects suppressed before resuming. At authorized publication trace the exact decision through existing glossary, ADR, native owner requirements/design/tasks, full incorporated OpenSpec projections and later authorized tickets/tests/release evidence. No new readiness aggregate, staffing engine or whole-Studio prerequisite.

Q49 replacement and Q60 fresh corrective-request issuance satisfy original-issuance readiness. Later staffing loss does not retroactively undo lawful issuance or impose this gate on independent intake or original-result recovery.

Story: US29-B05. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q25, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.
