<a id="contract-10da7283cb1fcbb63a4d"></a>

## REQ29-A10 — Separate finite unfinished and completed request access

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"10da7283cb1fcbb63a4d","name":"REQ29-A10 — Separate finite unfinished and completed request access"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Prospective stage scope.** The ordinary source policy SHALL declare finite unfinished access and its eligible Q06/Q07 transition before admission. The unfinished deadline SHALL NOT cap a validly admitted completed stage solely through its old stage role. Existing or unknown overall limits SHALL NOT be relabeled; specialized owners remain authoritative.

**Complete finite initial policy.** Activation SHALL require a finite applicable unfinished end and exact source/recipient/action scope. Q10 selects no initial duration/anchor or extension. Null, infinity, missing policy, absent visit or unfinished review SHALL NOT mean indefinite access.

**Qualified fulfillment.** Completed admission SHALL require exact durable normal fulfillment strictly before the applicable exclusive unfinished end with proved at-fulfillment policy and recipient eligibility. Receipt, scan, task flag, backdated caller time and processing time SHALL NOT substitute. Source completion may succeed independently without recipient admission.

**Preserve true caps.** Every limit SHALL retain its declared action/stage/purpose scope. Q07's original 720-hour maximum, admitted applicable overall caps and later genuine cross-stage shortening SHALL remain effective. Removing a cause SHALL NOT restore a longer end. A stage-only change cannot silently alter a different limit or create new editing power.

**Atomic source order.** Fulfillment/admission SHALL share the relevant serialized source order with correction/review-set, recipient, revocation, disposition and policy changes. Required facts/provenance/effect intent SHALL be durable atomically or through an equivalent recoverable fail-closed owner contract. Check current state/time after waits; never call providers under source locks.

**Historical recovery.** Delayed/retried processing SHALL prove the original fulfillment occurrence, policy, exact scope, eligibility and applicable caps, then apply current authority. Recover the original end; never processing-time plus 30 days, current-state fabrication or revival from actual late fulfillment.

**Fenced expiry and projections.** Expiry jobs, timers, caches and projections SHALL check current authoritative stage/effect/policy before consequences. A stale unfinished command SHALL NOT end a valid completed admission; lag SHALL NOT extend truly ended authority. A proven derived-expiry error requires attributed owner repair preserving genuine adverse history, not blanket disregard of Expired/Revoked state. Enforce current expiry without sweep dependence.

**One durable effect.** One source fulfillment occurrence and recipient-admission consequence SHALL converge across duplicate events, concurrent workers, lost responses and key expiry. Bind exact context/payload; conflicting reuse SHALL fail. Retries cannot reopen, widen scope, alter the original end or leak stored results without current disclosure authority.

**Read-only exact scope.** Normal fulfillment SHALL stop ordinary submission/correction. Preserve Q03's predecessor fence and relevant evidence-set ordering. Completed reads SHALL include only the exact eligible recipient/purpose/current own-submission scope and Q04/Q05 actions; no new siblings, historical bytes, recipients or independent uses are added.

**No lifecycle reset.** Later invalidation, new work, reopening requests or factual correction SHALL use the owning domain's explicit procedure with prior evidence retained. They SHALL NOT reset completion/end, revive ordinary intake, restore predecessors or automatically issue Q08 access. Canceled/rejected/withdrawn outcomes do not become normal fulfillment by shorthand.

**Proof and credentials.** Eligibility for the declared source transition SHALL NOT require the recipient browser to be open. Every protected use still requires current qualified proof and permission. Stage transition SHALL NOT extend invitation/session/provider credentials; reuse compatible valid proof or the existing qualified re-entry path without unselected enrollment or ceremony.

**Every private delivery.** Current authorization and exact representation eligibility SHALL protect metadata/history/search/counts, originals/previews, replay and all full/HEAD/range/conditional/resume/stream paths. Old tokens/URLs or cached stage state SHALL NOT grant access. Preserve inherited stream expiry, feasible revocation and honest copy limitations.

**Truthful independent outcomes.** Business completion, recipient admission, current access, proof/session, file readiness, notification and observer uncertainty SHALL remain distinct. Missing admission does not invalidate independently permitted source completion. Unknown outcome SHALL be reconciled before new effects; preserve independently proved work during partial failures, without fabricated success, expiry, rejection or empty history.

**Recipient and staff clarity.** Keep the same source task context, truthful Received/Awaiting review/Complete states, current relevant boundary and actual completed-read end. Staff delay SHALL NOT become recipient lateness. Surface the access consequence to the appropriately authorized source owner; do not invent extra approval, reminder cadence, forced download or urgency-driven false completion.

**Accessible temporal journey.** Preserve shared Base UI/base-maia, semantics, keyboard/focus, speech/AT, touch, reflow/RTL/localization and qualified timing behavior. No countdown-only/color-only/toast-only essential information or background focus theft. Actual session and opportunity timers require applicable accessibility proof, not a blanket security exemption.

**Database and role enforcement.** Enforce same-tenant/environment/source/recipient/evidence relationships, semantic uniqueness, required finite values, legal states, trusted attribution and deliberate delete behavior. Actual grants, effective USING/WITH CHECK, views/RPC/definer/service/storage paths SHALL prevent forged time/scope/stage and allowed-row updates into forbidden states.

**Custody and retention.** Stage transition/expiry SHALL NOT alter records retention, holds, independent source uses or disposal authority. Technical checks and qualified representation repair may continue under their own authority without new recipient rights, clock extension, substitute content or recall claims.

**Communication boundary.** Transition, notification and entry delivery SHALL remain separate effects. Q10 selects no automatic message or cadence. Any separately authorized communication SHALL use P17/P6, exact source event identity and current eligibility; resend/failure SHALL NOT reset time or attach private files as a bypass.

**Bounded architecture.** Reuse source/PDP/custody/tasks/worker infrastructure. Use bounded scoped queries, indexed effect/eligibility lookups, appropriate contention boundaries and measured budgets. No new permission engine, workflow service, complete-history scan per read, global lock or provider fan-out is justified.

**Durable minimal evidence.** Preserve original policy/limit scope, fulfillment, eligibility basis, admission/end, genuine restriction and remediation provenance under owned records rules. Separate durable business/security history from minimized technical diagnostics. No private filenames/content, recipient addresses, selectors/verifiers or sensitive policy reasons in general telemetry.

**Prospective compatible rollout.** Enable only qualified cohorts and compatible readers/writers/expiry jobs. Do not relabel legacy ends, backfill eligibility from task timestamps or revive access on restore. Kill/rollback/roll-forward SHALL preserve facts and deny unsafe fallback; they cannot reintroduce the obsolete stage cap or reopen submissions.

**Qualified usefulness and trace.** Before activation, prove useful source review timing, exact real-role/race/provider/recipient journeys and an accountable help route. Unfinished-expiry recovery and Q08 fresh policies remain explicit separate capabilities. Trace this choice through glossary/ADR, authorized owner/OpenSpec sources, tasks/tickets and release evidence; adjacent tests are not native qualification.

Story: US29-A10. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q10, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

## REQ29-P13 — Bound workload and preserve tenant fairness

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"188b0f04bf3de9c0a064","name":"REQ29-P13 — Bound workload and preserve tenant fairness"} -->

This is not a quota on every mutation SQL statement.

## REQ29-E03 — Preserve original assignment and current applicability

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"18c25729d472a483a8f0","name":"REQ29-E03 — Preserve original assignment and current applicability"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q62-C07 — Coherent authoritative commitment.** Commit the admitted request policy basis, required attributed source history and original business-operation result coherently with the actual issuance/evidence act; do not issue first and hope to attach a policy later. The retained basis identifies the exact applicable source policy/version/binding, material scope and whether the actual source default or a deliberate authorized choice supplied the binding, and trigger facts or truthful not-yet-triggered state, with trusted actor/time and necessary provenance. Reuse the source aggregate/transaction or a proved equivalent fence; no distributed framework is prescribed. Required secondary follow-through is durable and retryable, without holding short database locks across providers. A failed summary refresh does not erase a committed result or turn it into failed issuance.

**Q62-C08 — Races, relevant change and retries.** Order issuance/admission against relevant purpose, policy/default, choice authority and restriction changes on the actual authoritative basis, rechecking after waits. A material change to what staff reviewed requires safe explanation and a fresh deliberate final act; an unchanged effective result or unrelated global edit does not force ritual review. Preserve a still-valid deliberate choice when a default changes; identical policy names do not prove unchanged meaning or valid authority. A proposed assignment may be a verified no-op only after current authority, expected-basis and complete intended semantic-effect checks, with no new business history, trigger or downstream occurrence while retaining required security audit. A real factual assignment/trigger correction is not a no-op merely because the resulting effective policy or date is unchanged. Stale change-away-and-back intent is not rescued by equal labels, and a no-op does not prevent a later legitimate current-basis change. Bind idempotency to the original business effect and validated intent, not one transport call or a file ID alone. Recover a matching committed original before assessing a retry as new work, under current disclosure. A mismatched retry conflicts; another actor's result is not falsely attributed to this actor. Unknown commit remains unknown until reconciled. Retry/replay must not create duplicate issuance, reassign a newer policy, reset dates, revive access or superseded applicability. Preserve the source's stale-intent/expiry/cleanup/restore fences. After source-qualified recovery/control evidence expires, reject unverifiable stale intent rather than treating it as new; deleting readable result history cannot reopen an old business effect.

**Q62-C09 — Historical assignment and current applicability.** A changed default is prospective and does not silently rewrite admitted facts. A later policy successor may affect existing records only through the owning source's approved effectivity/impact rules and recorded attributable result. Preserve original policy/version/binding/trigger evidence while retaining actual current applicability and restrictions; a historical assignment is neither today's authority by itself nor immunity from later applicable rules. Late uploads, replacements, new source uses and restored records follow that source's explicit qualified current path; do not globally choose either newest-policy-for-everything or frozen-policy-forever. A required impact/reconciliation operation must identify affected records/use classes and commit the current governing result or an explicit restrictive pending state. Fence affected irreversible consequences while impact cannot safely be resolved; a stale permissive disposer cannot continue because only the summary changed. Track that work durably with a capable owner, finite bounds and recovery, and distinguish assignment success from pending physical consequences.

Story: US29-E03. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q62-C07, Q62-C08, Q62-C09; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

## REQ29-D11 — Prove complete references without disclosing hidden uses

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

The shared use/copy registry shall identify every registered owner/version family and its coverage/freshness, including business references, drafts, issued artifacts, live and retained releases, schedules, previews, shares, communication preparation, recovery and active intake/processing. Exact source admission and the corresponding protective reference must be coherent; a foreign key or asynchronous best-effort registration alone cannot prevent a concurrent physical delete. Registering a use grants no access to its source or bytes.

## REQ29-A14 — Thirty-day fresh unfinished-request access

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"1c68b4df1f4070972f80","name":"REQ29-A14 — Thirty-day fresh unfinished-request access"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

A newly admitted standard Fresh Unfinished Request Access MUST resolve its nominal unfinished end from the qualified fresh source issuance occurrence G plus exactly 720 elapsed hours. Successful source issuance MUST durably preserve its actual finite end and calculation/limit provenance with the source effect. Click, message, credential, first visit, mutable task date and observer time MUST NOT substitute for G.

The same end MUST bound never-entered access. This profile MUST NOT include an enter-by substage, first-entry business activation, pause, grace or activity reset. Retry MUST recover the same deliberate source effect and original outputs before any new calculation; changed output timestamps MUST NOT be treated as new material intent.

“Up to” MUST preserve genuine scoped earlier limits, binding shortening and current restrictions, not create a sender/tenant duration menu. Per-action/file/credential/retention dates MUST NOT become whole-stage limits by indiscriminate minimum calculation. Missing, invalid, nonfinite or already-ended live terms MUST NOT default to a new period.

Q13's eligible completion and declared read scope MUST remain separate: actual eligible F during valid authority stops writes and can admit an end no later than F plus Q07's independent 720 elapsed hours under genuine caps. No max(U,E), stale unfinished cap, retrospective eligibility or clock reset is permitted. The derived one-admission less-than-sixty-day result MUST NOT become a new overall/retention/repetition rule.

The existing request context MUST show the actual relevant end, source status and next actor after authorization, with Requested by separate. Successful issuance has a resolved end; inability to retrieve it is uncertainty/error, not ordinary awaiting-entry activation. Use one meaningful staff review/action, truthful proof/delivery/receipt/review states, accessible localized main-content timing and safe recovery; no unnecessary reupload or new account/confirmation/download step.

Reuse the Q13 source mutation/current-use and durable follow-up boundaries. Enforce supported instant/precision/range/unit semantics and qualified post-wait checks; do not use a client countdown, stale cache, a time-dependent CHECK alone, or one thirty-day resident timer as authority. Existing bounded durable wakes and owner-required fences remain allowed; no new timer service or numeric framework is required.

Adopt the standard profile prospectively through the exact source/owner binding and declared projections. Preserve existing admitted ends and specialized policies. Resolve Workflow fourteen-day wording at its actual credential/source scope, not by a global replacement. Required owner/design/test/release trace MUST distinguish selection, ratification, publication and qualification.

Q13's tenant/issuer/recipient, schema/RLS/service, immutable lineage, all-path private serving, audit, retention and rollout safeguards MUST remain binding without a parallel implementation. This profile resolves C09's timing/activation/unused reservation and specializes C19: successful source issuance resolves its end but does not establish recipient usability, proof or delivery. P13 MUST NOT require a first-entry substage; P19's shortening case MUST use actual narrower completed restrictions for this equal-duration profile. Qualify the focused and applicable inherited proofs; preserve dated Q13 records and trace this specialization. No duration editor, policy dashboard, additional state machine, reminder program or first-entry mechanism follows. Q15 governs unfinished repetition/nonoverlap; Q16–Q19 independently govern completed reading and Q20 its optional fresh-access notice. They are explicit later selections, not defaults inferred from this interval.

Story: US29-A14. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q14, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-1c814ee3d10bfd98da6b"></a>

## REQ29-A05 — Current files and a concise participant submission history

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"1c814ee3d10bfd98da6b","name":"REQ29-A05 — Current files and a concise participant submission history"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Ordinary scope.** Show currently authorized current members plus a concise participant Submission history for the exact request item. Superseded content gets no ordinary history read grant. Preserve independent current members and specialist/staff history rights.

**Exact lineage and currentness.** Derive earlier/current relationships from the source's admitted correction lineage, never filename, digest, timestamps or a universal latest pointer. Preserve Q03's predecessor fence and no silent revival; viewing history cannot mutate candidacy.

**Source truth and attribution.** Derive history from durable admitted source facts and trusted actor/context, preserving the meaning of the admitted request/item revision. A later label/instruction change cannot reinterpret the earlier act. No client-created receipt, raw audit feed or separate write authority. Distinguish source business history, participant projection, technical trace and security access evidence.

**Durable effect and causal order.** Preserve Q03's atomic source admission/state/history invariant. Projection retry/replay yields one visible fact per admitted source effect. Deterministic causal identity/order survives tied times, delayed events and replay; do not fabricate chronology from arrival order.

**Minimal participant metadata.** Release only current source-approved event wording, dates, actor/context and file descriptors. Filenames, counts, IDs, reasons and event existence are protected. Do not expose staff assessments, hidden requirements, audit payloads, old thumbnails, extracted text or provider references.

**Authorization before results.** Resolve trusted principal/tenant/request/item/purpose authority and filter safely before returned ordering, counts, facets, continuation and serialization. Do not union staff/audit privileges into the participant purpose. A mailbox match, uploader identity, current request membership or a browser-side filter cannot grant all historical metadata.

**Enforce A beyond the screen.** Deny ordinary recipient access to superseded content through APIs, preview/thumbnail/OCR/search/export paths, original/HEAD/range/conditional routes and stale URLs. Do not serialize old content references and merely hide controls. Preserve independently authorized source paths.

**Coherent current work.** Current files, status, actions and correction relationships reflect compatible relevant source state or explicit refreshing/unknown state. History never drives currentness. Revalidate privileged reads/mutations; preserve independent members, focus and the exact result of a committed correction.

**Honest failure and coverage.** Distinguish successful permitted empty history from unavailable, unauthorized, refreshing and known incomplete coverage. History failure cannot become No submissions, erase receipt, trigger re-upload or disable independently valid current work. Unknown current authority still fails closed.

**Data and privileged-path integrity.** Enforce required same-scope references, immutable admitted facts, trusted actor/time fields, valid lineage and semantic effect uniqueness. Prove actual grants/RLS/new-row checks/views/functions/RPC/storage/privileged paths; no client write or reassignment can turn a permitted row into forbidden history.

**Truthful time and actor.** Separate authoritative received/correction time from processing/display/import times; retain causal identity under ties. Localize readable dates/timezones without inferring location. Attribute You or a named actor only when current source projection and trusted evidence permit it.

**Small current-first UI.** Keep current status, confirmation, errors and next actions visible. Present short history as a readable list; use one clear secondary disclosure or bounded continuation only when useful. No full file explorer, mandatory extra modal, nested timeline system or disabled old-file button wall.

**Accessibility and field usability.** Reuse Core Base UI/base-maia, semantic lists/headings and existing tokens. Prove keyboard/speech/AT/touch/zoom/reflow/RTL/long-name behavior and focus stability. Announce material changes without repeatedly reading the whole history; keep low-bandwidth current work usable.

**Read lifecycle and side effects.** Reauthorize history reads/retries/continuations and clear unauthorized stale state through inherited contracts. Reading creates no new submission, acceptance, reminder, message, task, read receipt, session extension or historical-byte grant. Existing required access audit/metering still applies.

**Owned help without implicit B.** Before activation provide a working source-approved responder/route for earlier-submission questions. Clarification and any exceptional disclosure use independent current authority; no automatic old-file delivery, restore, new unlock toggle or guaranteed release. Give a truthful outcome when disclosure is not authorized.

**Retention and specialized ownership.** Participant omission neither deletes source history nor extends its retention. Preserve holds, lawful owner disposition, privacy suppression and official/source-specific historical rights. Restores apply current adverse history before serving. No access-duration or permanent archive promise.

**Finite retrieval and capacity.** Use bounded permission-safe queries/continuation and appropriate source-scope/order indexes. Declare and test finite page, response, query and concurrency budgets before activation. No all-history drain, unbounded client filtering, hidden-total leakage or needless binary/preview work.

**Cache, telemetry and audit.** Scope caches/late responses to current principal and source context; qualify switch/logout/revocation/restore paths. Private history must not enter public caches, offline stores or general logs; URLs may contain only approved opaque references, not sensitive descriptors or reusable authority. Apply inherited access-evidence rules without exposing the audit feed or fabricating business events.

**Migration and containment.** Reconcile source identities, coverage and old readers before cohort activation. Unknown legacy lineage stays explicit, not guessed from name/time/hash. Test mixed versions, alternate routes, safe disable/roll-forward and restore; rollback cannot re-enable old-byte access or erase required history.

**Proof and completion.** Qualify real-role metadata/content denial, source admission/races/replay, failure/coverage, actual browser/AT/mobile and owned help outcomes for every declared cohort. A decorative list, mocked suite or permanent unavailable placeholder is not completion. Trace this decision through later authorized owner/OpenSpec work.

This history projection grants no general archive, export or restore power. Q44, Q47 and Q56 retain their independently admitted later capabilities; an exclusion in the original history decision is not a global prohibition on them.

Story: US29-A05. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q05, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-1c82f3fa52ac42845620"></a>

## REQ29-A18 — Thirty elapsed days for fresh completed-read access

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"1c82f3fa52ac42845620","name":"REQ29-A18 — Thirty elapsed days for fresh completed-read access"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

For the standard Q08 Fresh Completed Request Access profile across both Q16 eligible origins, the nominal read end is Q17's qualified new source issuance occurrence plus exactly **720 elapsed hours (2,592,000 seconds)**. One elapsed day means twenty-four hours. Preserve the actual admitted end and material policy/limit provenance as original source outputs. The same end applies when access is never used. No calendar month, civil-day increment, local midnight, business day, first-entry interval or staff date menu substitutes for this rule.

Apply genuine earlier limits at their actual scope. “Up to” does not guarantee a minimum usable period or authorize arbitrary duration selection. Grant-wide limits can shorten the period; member/action-only restrictions cannot end unrelated permitted actions. Ordinary predecessor expiry is historical, not a fresh cap. Continuing purpose/overall prohibitions and later real shortening remain binding; clearing a shortening cause cannot silently extend this same admission. Technical proof or representation failure is not itself permanent shortening. One grant end does not promise that every file remains available.

Recover a matching committed semantic source effect before calculating another end. Preserve its original occurrence, end and material provenance through retries, changed defaults, new transport identifiers, late observations, delivery retries, proof/session renewal and representation repair. Missing or unreadable required timing calls for safe source reconciliation, not now-plus-thirty, guessed nonexistence or activation pending. A historical expired result remains history under current disclosure; it creates no usable period. Conflicting material intent cannot manufacture a replacement effect.

Reuse Q17 source/P12 currentness and causal ordering, including relevant waits, races and operations that cross an exclusive end. Validate trusted required finite instants, units, supported range and precision. Whole-second duration units do not permit truncating the source occurrence or silently changing the admitted end. Effective constraints, tenant-aware relationships, attribution, semantic uniqueness, old/resulting-row RLS and privileged paths must preserve exact scope and immutable original results. No allowed update, upsert or parent mutation can widen or retarget this permission.

Apply current authority and the actual end at every protected metadata and byte-serving boundary. Correctness must survive process restarts, delayed workers and failed invalidation; it cannot rely on a browser clock, provider URL lifetime, cleanup sweep or one thirty-day Node timer. Reuse bounded indexed checks and existing recovery mechanisms. Starting a download before expiry does not authorize unlimited future serving; bytes already received cannot be recalled by online expiry.

Keep the existing **Grant read access** action and completed read-only context. Staff review the standard and material known limits once; label estimates until the source returns the actual result. Then show **Read-only access ends [date, time and zone]**, with current permitted actions and material earlier scoped limits where disclosure is allowed. Late arrival gets the real remainder; unused access shares the same end. Distinguish source expiry, proof/session failure, unavailable content, delivery failure and unknown issuance. Provide clear recovery in the same context, retaining shared Base UI/base-maia, accessible status/focus/authentication and mobile/low-bandwidth behavior. Shorter actual grant, proof and session timers require their own accessibility qualification. Add no extra account, duration picker, routine confirmation, forced download/reupload, automatic message or promise of another grant.

Apply this profile prospectively to its exact qualified source cohort. Mixed versions, policy changes, migration and restore must preserve prior effects, ends and later restrictions. No invented date backfill or broad fallback is allowed. Preserve Q16's exact completed member/submitted-original-version/ action/context ceiling, original fulfillment/receipts/corrections, immutable originals, retention/holds/disposal and independent domain ownership. Equal thirty-day values in other policies do not make them the same policy or create another completed stage. Asym remains a contextual use/production system for relevant files, not a personal drive.

Q19 permits individually reviewed sequential completed-read periods without a preset lifetime count or cumulative-time ceiling, overlap or automatic renewal. Earlier unselected-repeat text is historical only.

**Exact inherited profiles.** These values apply only to the named source/representation and units, never as generic intake limits:

- Nominal duration; 720 hours = 2,592,000 seconds = 2,592,000,000 milliseconds.
- Ordinary Node timer; 2,592,000,000 ms exceeds the documented 2,147,483,647 ms bound; an ordinary oversized delay becomes 1 ms. A shorter fitting timer would still not be durable authorization.

Story: US29-A18. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q18, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Known valid source publication stays valid when mail is unavailable, declined, stopped, failed or unknown. Recovery uses trustworthy original facts; current settings cannot invent original On, authority or historical zero. If original mail authority is unproved, retain the update and block historical sending without falsely calling it declined.

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"1f85d98850bdc12019e1","name":"REQ29-C21 — Withdraw shared availability and recover privately"} -->

Audience removal blocks that binding's future access; matching email/new assignment cannot revive it. Stop sharing clears readers and leaves the definition private to the current lawful maintainer, not automatically its historical creator. With no lawful maintainer, transfer first or immediately Remove/retire; urgent withdrawal cannot wait for replacement ownership.

Maintain minimum semantic replay evidence or reject unverifiable stale work; required history cannot vanish through actor/parent cascade.

Parent/user/rendition deletion cannot cascade required history.

## REQ29-B15 — Tenant-controlled participant cancellation notices

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"2398f9b4da334bba0106","name":"REQ29-B15 — Tenant-controlled participant cancellation notices"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Exact meaning and scope.** Only one genuinely new durably committed authorized Q34 ordinary cancellation SHALL own this optional participant-awareness occurrence. Drafts, menu navigation, failed/unknown cancellation, expiry, Q22 fulfillment, Q33 removal, Q30 text, task flags and retries MUST NOT produce it. The message describes only the exact canceled request, never acceptance, deletion or an enclosing outcome. Preserve Q34 terminal state, history, finite web scope and remedy. Existing Q20/Q26/Q27/Q31/Q32 controls may be discoverable together without altering their contracts. No new notice channel, audience, cadence, bulk operation or glossary term.

**Real bounded tenant noise controls.** Qualify one optional email step with initial system/default configuration Enabled and initial staff selection On. Authorized tenant plan administrators SHALL be able to publish Enabled/Disabled and, while enabled, the initial staff selection On/Off using existing complete-plan P17 ownership. This is a narrow new Q35 contract-permitted default/condition control, not assumed existing behavior. No new general settings engine or prescribed widget/storage shape. Default-only edits affect genuinely new cancellation reviews, not an already initialized visible choice or pending occurrence; disable retains the configured seed for future re-enable without resurrecting mail. Present their distinct consequences and scope. Expose only already-supported presentation/locale/sender/reply choices, source-owned Who and email How. No arbitrary To/CC, recipient-picker authority, unsupported channel or override of mandatory/specialist notices.

**Authority, reviewed choice and current admission.** Separate source cancellation, plan administration, safe contact visibility, contact management, exact participant disclosure and delivery rights. Derive tenant/environment/actor/executor/time from trusted context and validate source/request/recipient relations. Q01's exact closed no-Party branch requires compatible communication qualification; never fabricate a Party/account or infer rights from address/role/grant. Staff may choose/request On only while Enabled and currently eligible; they may always decline this optional notice for an otherwise lawful cancellation. Final deliberate submission carries the visible per-act value; missing/null/legacy/malformed intent is not On. Preserve it across validation/recovery and never silently reset on render/default changes. At actual cancellation admission bind applicable policy in source/publication order, separately retaining needed seed provenance. Default-only change need not conflict with a still-permitted reviewed choice. Known material target/disclosure/availability change requires inline revalidation or deliberate cancellation-only action, never silent retarget/downgrade.

**Native independence and original handoff.** Co-located cancellation, mandatory history/fence, actual notice choice and minimum durable original-boundary handoff SHALL commit coherently; no provider calls or optional expensive lookup inside source locks. Lack of optional mail readiness cannot prevent a separately authorized cancellation-only path. If required native durability itself fails, do not claim cancellation succeeded. Once native success is known, notice/projection failure cannot erase it or require another business cancellation. An exact supported unresolved handoff preserves original occurrence/policy/choice and recoverable recipient proof without guessing zero/Off; recovery proves only original-boundary facts and current narrowing, never today's new eligibility. If original notice authority cannot be proved, preserve valid cancellation with a truthful blocked/no-send notice outcome; do not leave a promised eventual send, invent zero or adopt repaired current settings. No inline-best-effort-only handoff, fake historic authority, later-On/Live catch-up or unreadable-copy dependency.

**Permanent identity and complete release.** Prove exact top-level Live contract/generation/binding/plan context before any P6 parent, even for zero members. Reserved/unknown/unavailable binding does not create a zero/parent. Release the complete valid bounded candidate set or proved zero atomically via the owning P17/P6 compiler; no producer child loops, appended recipients, split logical occurrence or force-release. Preserve original source identity, choice, plan, recipient basis and material comparison across different/expired transport keys and restore. Exact replay returns original effect under current disclosure; changed immutable material conflicts. Refresh/import/backfill/retry cannot create another cancellation notice, new recipient or replacement request. Known staff Off/Disabled need not resolve unused contacts; unknown is not a proved zero.

**Explicit Disabled stop and no backlog revival.** Add a Q35-specific source/P17/P6 current-stop exception to general future-only plan publication. Order every actual sealed provider-attempt admission committing Submission may have begun before first byte against published notice availability and applicable durable Disabled history since the original occurrence, including unmaterialized handoffs and later same-key/provider retry calls. Queue/claim/prepared does not equal admission. Disabled-first stops; admission-first preserves that admitted operation's potential effect. An intervening Disabled denies subsequent sends for affected historical work even after Enabled returns or if no worker observed Disabled. Preserve immutable original pins; allow evidence-only reconciliation, not replacement/rekey/retarget or recall fiction. Default Off and per-act Off are distinct from Disabled. Default-only edits do not stop pending selected mail; administrators needing that stop use Disable. Before any stop, existing P17 recovery permits at most two identical-envelope follow-up HTTP calls total after the initial call, only for concurrent_idempotent_request, exact typed provider_5xx or exact sealed network_or_timeout without contradiction, before the earliest member/material deadline and pinned provider-window expiry with fresh fences. Other indeterminate causes reconcile only; acceptance or definite rejection stops that loop. A new attempt/key needs sufficient definite-rejection evidence and fresh contract authority; accepted/indeterminate or Disabled-affected work cannot rekey. Newly occurring eligible cancellations after re-enable may notify; historical Off/zero/stopped work cannot catch up.

**Current useful disclosure independent of portal.** At preparation, each provider admission and any protected use, prove exact current source purpose/recipient/privacy/contact/consent/suppression/sender/action authority. Require original-basis continuity, not removal/re-entry/recreated-ID equality. Never use a known-wrong old address or silently retarget old work. Absence/expiry of Q34 web admission alone neither grants nor universally forbids independently qualified factual email. Prefer an approved informational no-action variant; any optional protected return requires the exact qualified Q34 root/end/caps, with no renewal/archive/replacement redirect. Do not require a link just to inherit an existing material class. Define an explicit finite source-owned useful-notice deadline for the qualified cohort; dispatch is denied at or after it or any earlier applicable restriction. Actual committed cancellation is positive applicability for this notice, not a generic canceled-parent suppression reason. Permanent cancellation truth is not perpetual send utility. A later source remedy/new request is not an automatic stop, but may narrow usefulness; never rewrite sealed content or tell users unrelated work ended.

**Content, material and lifecycle privacy.** Every subject/preheader/text/HTML/preview/reference/tag/URL/error/log/export obeys the source fact wall. Safe stable identification must distinguish the canceled request without private title, reason, filename, evidence, text, location/care information or attachments; sender/relationship metadata also needs permission. If useful identification is impossible, no confusing broadcast. Approved variants are qualified before preparation, never improvised fallback or sealed rerender. Formally admit an exact optional external-participant material class, finite retention ceiling and source utility; freeze the earliest class/intent/source/action deadline, never borrow financial30d, optional-staff7d or web access. Reuse existing immediate decryption denial and primary/cache purge within24h of its governing trigger; that cleanup bound is not an extra sending window. Native source history and body-free communication history retain distinct owners. Optional Recent copy uses the shorter qualified policy/class ceiling and exact current reveal authority; unresolved/incompatible no-Party reveal uses no_readable_copy without fake identity or blocking otherwise lawful cancellation/email. Do not restore purged material from logs/provider/backups.

**Small accessible composed journeys.** Use existing System Messages and source cancellation surfaces with shared Base UI/base-maia. Tenant controls explain future-review default versus pending-send Disable and no recall/backlog. Staff see effective choice, safe destination and final consequence beside Cancel request, with a distinct back action. No routine extra modal/approver, new composer, typed-name ritual or mandatory notice reason. Preserve permitted input/focus/choice on recoverable failure, clear forbidden context across tenant/user/request changes, and prevent automatic reconnect sends. Give persistent separate Canceled, not requested, unavailable/blocked, queued, provider-accepted, delivered/failed/unknown results to authorized staff, without claiming human awareness. Participant notice says only what ended and any actual safe help; replies do not reopen Q30 or imply nonexistent monitoring. Verify keyboard/AT/names/states/focus/touch/reflow/zoom/contrast/reduced motion/RTL/IME/international names/locale/time-zone and slow-network/shared-device paths, including plain-text and images-off email with safe identification and stop-work meaning intact. No offline private archive.

**Structural and effective authorization.** Design tenant/environment/source/cancellation/recipient/plan/publication/occurrence/member/material relations with typed identifiers, same-scope constraints, safe nullability and explicit valid states. Required original facts/attribution/time/pins are immutable; defaults never infer historical On. Enforce permanent uniqueness, complete release cardinality and restrictive deletion/anonymization that preserves required evidence without perpetual private prose. Test effective grants/RLS USING and WITH CHECK, policy combinations, SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/UPSERT, views/RPC/functions/triggers, storage/caches/counts/export and privileged service/job/import/admin paths. A permitted old row cannot change into a forbidden tenant/recipient/plan/terminal state. Derive trusted fields server-side; an admin client is not source authority. No new money mutation or precision rule.

**Bounded operations and accountable evidence.** Use existing indexed bounded release/recovery/history and tenant fairness. No all-tenant/whole-history scan, per-attempt task, separate retry queue or duplicate notification ledger. Keep source business history, security audit, technical diagnostics and provider evidence distinct, with safe correlation/actor/choice/policy/original outcome and repair evidence, not bodies/secrets. Declare finite compiler recipient/payload limits and measured source/worker/lock/recovery budgets before native qualification; never chunk one logical plan or fabricate an instantaneous-delivery SLA. The seven controls below require actual accountable operators. Noise controls are product capability, not a substitute for correct dedupe, suppression or accessible comprehension.

**Exact publication, rollout and proof.** Adopt C01–C12/P01–P30 and reconcile Q34's notice reservation only for this exact purpose. Explicitly amend source/P12 disclosure and no-Party branch, P17 meaning/default control/Disabled exception/catalog generation/material class and P6 complete release/current admission/history/repair plus their full incorporated OpenSpec bodies. Preserve earlier contracts and regenerate declared views from canonical sources. Activate prospectively after exact Live qualification, real native tests, composed user/AT proof, finite budgets and named operators. Compatible schema/readers/commands/recovery precede enablement; old/null clients cannot infer selected mail. No imported/backfilled/historical cancellation mail or new identity after restore. Scoped disable/roll-forward preserves cancellation/history and later restrictions; rollback cannot discard required pins or revive old work. No whole future Studio dependency or new glossary term.

Story: US29-B15. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q35, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

## REQ29-P12 — Treat files and extracted material as hostile until qualified

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"23f3f48ef1850ada96dc","name":"REQ29-P12 — Treat files and extracted material as hostile until qualified"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Admission, scanning, isolation, decoding/conversion and derivative promotion must follow exact bounded source profiles. File extension, declared MIME, thumbnail, checksum, clean scan and preview success are separate facts and do not establish authenticity, authority or completeness. Guard archives/decompression, malformed/encrypted/oversized inputs, parser resource use and arbitrary external fetch. Treat embedded instructions and extracted/OCR/AI text as untrusted data, never tool or policy authority. Original preservation/discard follows its source; transformations create identified outputs with exact input/profile/options/coverage provenance. A missing saved spreadsheet value is unavailable rather than zero; readers do not execute macros, refresh external data, recalculate or silently accept revisions.

Story: US29-P12. Source: proof.md. Decision basis: Founder §§5/9/11/13/19; Q02/Q04/Q29/Q53/Q56; Q61-C05/C11; Phase 26 D32 and public-media owners.

## REQ29-P15 — Migrate actual retained data and block incompatible old writers

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"243b1e033e0761de6aeb","name":"REQ29-P15 — Migrate actual retained data and block incompatible old writers"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Inventory actual retained records, bytes, provenance, classifications, copies, callers, readers/writers, processors, scripts and customer reliance before cutover. Distinguish disposable fixtures from retained data. Preserve stable opaque business/byte identities and source references through exact supported mappings; do not infer policy/approval/event facts from filenames, old public URLs, upload dates, task flags or absence. Install compatible guards/readers before writers; version semantic schemas and profiles, not only table shape. Old clients/jobs that cannot interpret current restrictions must not write or resume effects. Define one authoritative cutover and source-safe coexistence, rollback or roll-forward with independent byte integrity and restrictions proven. No destructive fresh-build assumption or Twenty fallback is allowed.

## REQ29-B12 — Default-on staff notices for participant clarification

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"2790435a68d15660716b","name":"REQ29-B12 — Default-on staff notices for participant clarification"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Meaning and default.** The native source SHALL admit this notice purpose only for one genuinely new durably received participant question or reply under Q30, including permitted pre-upload and accepted-item clarification. The qualified initial tenant plan SHALL enable its optional staff-email step. Drafts, unadmitted/failed sends, duplicate transport calls, views, task changes, staff replies, scans, uploads and source assignment changes MUST NOT manufacture this occurrence. No text classifier, question-mark requirement, first-unanswered latch or new needs-response state. A genuine short acknowledgment may qualify under the same meaning; no hidden urgency judgment. Preserve Q27 evidence mail and Q31 participant mail.

**Real bounded tenant control.** Authorized tenant administrators SHALL disable/enable this exact optional step and manage only its contract-permitted content, locale, sender and reply settings through existing P17 complete-plan review/publication. Show effective policy, inherited/published state and readiness with synthetic safe examples. Who is the source-qualified clarification-handling role; show its authorized source management route without exposing private rosters/content. No raw To/CC/BCC, arbitrary audience or unsupported channel. Apply existing applicable recipient preferences/restrictions by their exact purpose, not a new global preference center or copied Support flag. Off affects this step, not native obligations or unrelated communications.

**Original qualified staff audience.** Trusted source/P12/P17 resolution SHALL order receipt against policy and relevant recipient-resolution changes and retain original source/actor/purpose/meaning/binding/plan basis plus the complete finite admitted staff generation/candidate basis, or trustworthy original provenance sufficient to resolve that same basis, when needed for enabled mail. Each resolved recipient requires actual tenant/source/disclosure/contact/egress authority through the exact P12/P6 Party/contact branch or a separately permitted staff branch. Q24 review responsibility, task assignment, current team name, earlier author, creator/admin roster and address equality MUST NOT substitute. Current restrictions can narrow; later roster, role, contact or handling changes MUST NOT widen or retarget old mail. Source-defined continuity must be proved: removal/re-entry or deletion/recreation with equal identifiers cannot impersonate original admitted authority. Source handoff remains independently mutable; this is communication identity, not a universal full-staff-roster snapshot or a new Q25 issuance/email-readiness gate. Do not reuse Q01's participant authority as a staff fallback or fabricate a Party/account.

**Native receipt and recoverable handoff.** Required co-located message/attribution/history/handling obligation and durable notification handoff SHALL commit atomically, with provider work outside source locks. No false Received without Q30's native minimum. Optional egress unavailability SHALL preserve independently lawful receipt and source work, using trustworthy original-boundary unresolved provenance where proof is unavailable; unknown must not be converted to Off/zero or today's eligibility. Required native route failure follows Q30's dependent admission and capable recovery, not an email fallback. Known native success survives projection/provider failures. Recover the same act without participant resend, duplicate task or a new source send merely to repair mail.

**Whole-plan identity and release.** Before a P6 parent exists, prove exact top-level Live contract/binding/plan authority, even for zero children. Known disabled-step proof may yield zero without unused recipient/contact lookup; unknown/partial/over-limit proof is not zero or permission for a subset. Reserved/unqualified binding produces no P6 header or later catch-up. P6 SHALL release one complete bounded atomic recipient-step set under permanent source occurrence and semantic identities; no independently committed producer child loop. Exact concurrent replay converges under current disclosure; changed immutable terms conflict. A released parent/zero cannot gain omitted children, a new parent token or another Notify act through rekey/repair. Distinct genuine equal text remains distinct.

**Explicit Tenant Off amendment.** General plan edits remain future-only with immutable pins except this explicit Q32 current-stop rule. Published Off SHALL be ordered against each actual sealed provider submission/attempt fence committing Submission may have begun before first byte, including delayed original handoffs, compilation and subsequent retry admissions. Off-first denies affected admission; admission-first retains that admitted operation's possible effect. A missed Off→On interval cannot revive older affected work: after Off wins no subsequent same-key call, replacement attempt, changed envelope or new admission is authorized; evidence-only reconciliation remains. Preserve exact parent/member/content/policy identity, unrelated purposes and body-free in-flight truth. No recall promise, backlog resend or new cancellation engine.

**Actual staff usefulness.** Re-prove current staff source-purpose/action/disclosure/privacy and delivery authority at preparation, actual submission and staff doorway use after relevant waits. Participant expiry or request fulfillment does not alone cancel admitted staff follow-through; conversely no permanently actionable/unanswered status follows from a received message. Reading, replying or handling work is neither a new Q32 occurrence nor an invented universal suppression rule. Factual copy SHALL describe received clarification without asserting an answer is still owed; show actual current source state on return. Deny genuinely inapplicable/unauthorized notice or unsafe context. Do not reopen work, add Resolve/read/response gates, grant access, reuse participant credentials or send stale instructions requiring an expired participant to reply.

**Minimal content and qualified material.** Subject/preheader/body/HTML/text, sender/reply display, preview, tags, URLs, exports, errors, queues, logs and history SHALL obey the source fact wall. Exclude participant bodies/excerpts, filenames/file bytes, private comments, sensitive person/location/item/request labels and attachments; neutral relationship information is not automatically safe. Use only the exact qualified staff protected-action carrier, no raw storage/public deep link or participant doorway. Native text retention belongs to its source; P6 durable history is body-free. Before Live declare a reviewed closed optional staff email-only prepared-material class, finite source/action utility and earliest non-extending purge deadline. Do not borrow the optional-staff-with-bell class, financial class, participant policy or access durations. Unknown or incompatible readable-copy/reveal authority requires no_readable_copy; never fabricate identity or block lawful source/email solely for an optional convenience copy. Preserve immediate terminal decryption denial, owner purge/restore and independent provider retention.

**Simple accessible journeys.** Participants SHALL use the existing deliberate message action and truthful native Received/unknown/not-received recovery; no Notify staff checkbox, roster picker, internal notice-recipient/cohort addresses, mail settings/provider diagnostics or delivery/read/SLA promise. Preserve independently authorized participant-facing source contacts and history; those are not internal notice diagnostics. Staff SHALL return through existing authorized contextual work, with received text and any current handling state distinct from notice requested/blocked/queued/accepted/delivered/failed/unknown. Admin settings SHALL explain exact scope, effective On/Off and actual pending/in-flight limits without a per-message ceremony. Use shared Base UI/base-maia, accessible labels/state/errors/status/focus, normal multiline/IME and permitted draft recovery. Test mobile/reflow/zoom/AT/keyboard/RTL/international names/locales/time zones/plain-text mail/low bandwidth. Approved copy and actual Reply-To agree: email replies do not enter Q30; no fabricated mailbox promise or unsafe offline/cross-audience persistence.

**Structural data and authorization.** Derive tenant/environment, actor/author/time/source and policy attribution from trusted server context, not caller values. Enforce typed same-scope source/request/item/message/handling/recipient-generation/communication relationships, non-null required facts, valid explicit states and permanent uniqueness through keys/constraints and owning commands. An allowed update MUST NOT retarget source, author, tenant, recipient, original policy, released membership or stop state. Test actual grants and policy combinations, SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/UPSERT, USING and WITH CHECK, views/RPC/functions, service/worker/import/admin/storage/cache paths. Mutable cross-row authority uses the authoritative mutation/admission boundary, not an unsafe cross-row CHECK or stale JWT alone. Keep lawful redaction/anonymization and independent audit; avoid cascades that erase required source/delivery facts. No money mutation or new money precision requirement.

**Bounded operation and audit.** Use existing source/P9/P17/P6 work, repair and fair queues, not a task per message/recipient/attempt or a second delivery queue. Bound complete cohort/step resolution, payloads, queries/history, publication impact, worker batches, concurrency and reconciliation with measured named release budgets. No silent truncation, partial fanout, tenant-wide locks or provider calls under source locks. Index actual tenant/source/occurrence/current-work access paths. Preserve source business history, security audit, technical logs and provider evidence as distinct minimal records with trusted attribution, original policy/cohort/stop and normalized outcomes. Transport batching preserves exact members and cannot create a digest, unclear combined recipients or new semantic coalescing.

**Exact qualification and prospective rollout.** Resolve Q30/Q31's reservation only for this staff clarification purpose and reconcile actual source/P12/P9/P17/P6/ADRs and full incorporated OpenSpec through canonical sources. Qualify exact key/generation/binding/default/publication/recipient/action/material and P01–P26 before prospective activation; no Reserved inheritance or history catch-up. Mixed clients/schema/workers cannot guess authority/On or relabel Support/finance rows as Q32. Kill switches stop unsafe optional mail while preserving lawful native handling. Rollback/restore reapplies source restrictions, Off history, expiry and erasure before dispatch/read, retains required new durable facts and uses roll-forward when downgrade is unsafe. Map named operators and finite budgets before activation. No new provider, personal preference platform, inbox/bell/digest/reminder/ingestion/staffing model or whole future Studio prerequisite.

**Exact inherited profiles.** These values apply only to the named source/representation and units, never as generic intake limits:

- Terminal erasure; Existing P17 terminal triggers deny decryption immediately; primary ciphertext/keys/plaintext-capable caches purge within 24 hours. Physical purge, backup/cryptographic erasure and provider retention are separate. No retention extension from batching, retries, views or restore.
- Capacity and accessibility; No measured Asym cohort/traffic/latency budget exists here. Declare finite budgets and test production-shaped load before activation. WCAG 2.2 reflow at 320 CSS pixels and target-size/spacing criteria apply with their actual exceptions; component selection alone is not composed-flow proof.

Story: US29-B12. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q32, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

## REQ29-P18 — Keep prerequisites precise and the complete contract traceable

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"27d0931ca6be5d1cc0f9","name":"REQ29-P18 — Keep prerequisites precise and the complete contract traceable"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

The canonical domain/proof volumes own stories, full requirements and acceptance criteria. Generated story indexes, traces and OpenSpec projections carry those exact contracts, including complete unconditional companion bodies, and are not independent authoring sources. Preserve all Q01–Q67 and each explicit supersession, the accepted glossary and every applicable owner contract. The GF drafts remain examples, not approved tenant policies or universal defaults. Implement settled generic capability behavior and exact prerequisite interfaces without pretending unresolved material source policy, actual native credentials/copy coverage, legacy mappings or user evidence are known. Each gate names the owner, missing artifact/meaning, affected action, safe unavailable/recovery behavior and closure proof. An unresolved source behavior blocks that affected implementation slice; a merely unexecuted runtime test remains a required acceptance gate. A published/Ready specification is not implemented, qualified or activated.

Story: US29-P18. Source: proof.md. Decision basis: Founder §§3/16/19; Q61-C01/C13–C16; Q64 reference; Q66-C22; Q67-C18–C19; explicit /to-spec authorization.

## REQ29-P17 — Prove outcomes at the confirmed highest practical seams

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"295a091269001b0033ce","name":"REQ29-P17 — Prove outcomes at the confirmed highest practical seams"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Use the confirmed primary seam: source-owned commands, queries and file-serving interfaces with durable results and current permitted readback. Reuse existing API/service test infrastructure and controlled adapter fault injection for business behavior. Complement it with real-role PostgreSQL/RLS/private Storage/provider/copy/restore qualification and complete Playwright plus axe/browser/manual keyboard/assistive-technology journeys. These are evidence families for the same source contract, not new product subsystems or a proliferation of per-helper seams. Component tests are justified only by a distinct shared invariant not proved at the higher seam. Mocks, demo sessions, skipped fixtures, source lint and structural docs checks do not establish native authorization or actual physical outcomes.

Story: US29-P17. Source: proof.md. Decision basis: Founder §19; Q61 proof register; Q62–Q67 proof groups; explicit 2026-10-29 testing-seam confirmation under /to-spec.

## REQ29-E16 — Version activate retire and reactivate policy meaning

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"29d3eb87da0a224e492a","name":"REQ29-E16 — Version activate retire and reactivate policy meaning"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q66-C08 — Policy lifecycle with truthful recovery.** Distinguish mutable draft, immutable activated meaning, current default/applicability, retired-for-new-use and disposal pause states. Changes to activated governing meaning require a successor draft; preserve the operative version until a qualified activation/change. A separately authorized, attributed non-operative metadata edit may change a label or description without a new semantic version only when the source proves no change to meaning, applicability, approval basis, instructions or the minimum safe consequence summary. Material or uncertain changes use a successor; a caller metadata-only flag is not proof. Preserve historical displays/provenance and current expected-basis/result checks. Retire stops new assignments within its admitted scope without deleting history, existing obligations, active-use protections or needed recovery. Permit deliberate Reactivate of an unchanged retired version under fresh current qualification, retaining retirement/reactivation history; changed meaning requires a successor. Do not force a duplicate version just to repair retirement, resurrect old command intent or silently reactivate by reading/restoring. Lawful unused-draft deletion is separate from retention of used policy evidence.

**Q66-C09 — Activation and temporal applicability.** Activate exact approved meaning/current authority and new-use scope through one deliberate reviewed source command. Prospectivity is the default for a discretionary default change, not immunity from a mandatory current duty. Preserve original assignments while applying actual lawful successor effectivity. Before affected irreversible consequences can proceed, establish the current governing result or a restrictive pending-impact fence for records whose obligations change; do not wait for optional UI cleanup while an old permissive disposer runs. Record actual governing versus recorded/activated dates truthfully, including qualified later discovery of an earlier effective duty. No arbitrary backdating or old-fact rewrite.

Story: US29-E16. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q66-C08, Q66-C09; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-2a1d5aecc6a03323a941"></a>

## REQ29-E19 — Make policy administration usable and evolvable

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"2a1d5aecc6a03323a941","name":"REQ29-E19 — Make policy administration usable and evolvable"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q66-C17 — Simple complete UI and accessibility.** Provide the contextual policy-manager journeys and fields above using shared Base UI/base-maia; keep routine request/reference work unchanged except truthful current summaries. Explain material changes and affected scope in the normal final review, with specific labels and preserved input; do not require a universal extra confirmation/essay/second approver or show every secondary action at once. Draft save, activation, existing-record progress, correction, pause and physical outcome are distinct statuses. Support keyboard/touch, visible focus, AT status without focus theft, inline errors, Unicode/RTL/translated labels, 320-CSS-pixel reflow, actual date/unit semantics and weak-network recovery. Passive render/GET/prefetch/blur/IME is inert; deliberate click/tap/focused Enter/Space uses origin/CSRF-protected commands. No persistent restricted offline queue or optimistic final success.

**Q66-C18 — Minimal private projections.** Apply existing static classification and subtract-only source projection to policy text/options, names, counts, impact samples, reasons, holds, history, command results, exports, logs, caches and late responses. Policy-management power does not imply agreement/body, source-conversation or other-tenant access. Provide sufficient safe summaries or a capable restricted review route without leaking hidden counts/cases. Key allowed caches to complete scope/current basis and reject late responses after account/tenant/source switches. Do not store policy authority in Auth metadata or publish GF/private policy text by default. Lawful deletion/anonymization and minimal control evidence remain separate from an eternal audit archive.

**Q66-C19 — Capable ownership and durable evidence.** Preserve actual tenant actor/approval basis, semantic version, reviewed scope, governing/recorded times, exact impact outcomes, original results and correction/retirement/reactivation/Stop history under actual retention. Distinguish business history, security audit and technical telemetry; keep secrets/private bodies out of logs. Missing policy, conflict, unknown coverage, partial impact, departed owner or possible external effect requires a real capable source/operator route with bounded durable work and supported retry/reconciliation, not a powerless queue or routine direct SQL/provider-console repair. No new notification/recipient purpose or task for every ordinary request is implied.

**Q66-C20 — Migration, schema meaning and rollout.** Inventory actual retained definitions/records, source reliance, approvals, all readers/writers/jobs and copies. Never infer historic approval or binding from GF text, JSON settings, names, upload dates or a demo tenant. Version the policy interpretation/source schema as well as immutable values; code changes must not silently reinterpret old rules. Unknown versions in old workers deny new effects while preserving history/original results and possible-effect fences. Establish compatible guards before new writers, use supported cohorts and explicit legacy mappings, prove both mixed-version directions and byte restoration separately. Stops/rollback cannot erase restrictive control facts or restore bypassing writers; use roll-forward when old code cannot safely interpret admitted state.

**Q66-C21 — Finite qualification and proof.** Require measured finite bounds for definitions/rules/options, payloads/units, resolution/preview calls, impact targets/chunks/locks/retries, copy fanout, history/control retention and operator recovery under representative tenant skew. Use bounded setwise authorization, stable unique current-page projection and tenant-fair work; preserve actual P12 R6 logical/physical-shard/restricted-tier distinctions. No arbitrary business cap, query number or speed claim is imported from a vendor. Prove the positive/negative/boundary/authorization/concurrency/replay/migration/accessibility and production-shaped outcomes below before affected activation. Mandatory actual policy/native gates are not monitor-only risks.

Story: US29-E19. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q66-C17, Q66-C18, Q66-C19, Q66-C20, Q66-C21; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-2c521792bf6df88ba4dd"></a>

## REQ29-E15 — Create and configure tenant-owned records policies

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"2c521792bf6df88ba4dd","name":"REQ29-E15 — Create and configure tenant-owned records policies"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q66-C01 — Selected example and actual tenant control.** Use the identified GF drafts as the worked mapping example, retaining their actual unestablished approval/applicability status. Ordinary document records policy definitions and supported business values SHALL be tenant-owned and self-service for currently authorized tenant actors, including custom creation and later change; not hardcoded GF/Asym defaults, preset-only selection or routine Asym developer/support intervention. No GF body, private legal value, organization identity or approval is automatically shipped, copied or activated for other tenants.

**Q66-C02 — Scope, ownership and prior-contract reconciliation.** The ordinary source owns its tenant policy definitions, effective versions, applicability/defaults, bindings, change results and business history; P29 owns custody/disposition execution and P12 current authorization. Preserve specialist source owners and all Q01–Q65 invariants. This explicit authoring requirement extends the earlier no-general-editor selection limits in Q62/Q63/Q65 through the exact consequential amendments; it does not create a universal policy/permission/workflow engine. Platform-owned stable capability/field/source semantics may remain code-owned; ordinary tenant business rules and values are not thereby code-owned. No P18, P21 or projection-policy approval model is imported as universal ordinary authority.

**Q66-C03 — Complete structured configuration.** Support tenant-defined ordinary policy names/summaries, relevant classes/purposes/applicability, compatible trusted start events/dates and precision, actual retention/review bounds and units, disposition mode, effective scope/defaults/variants and eligible responsibility/approval references. Allow valid shortening, lengthening and changing between supported finite or continuing/review-based treatment when actual authority permits; no arbitrary vendor cap or permanent mode lock. Validate typed combinations, ambiguity, calendar semantics, supported representation and finite technical bounds. Do not accept arbitrary SQL/scripts, caller-created event meaning, free-form timers, legal conclusions or cross-source applicability by label. Multiple compatible policies/classes can coexist with unambiguous source resolution and independent obligations.

**Q66-C04 — Editable policy versus independent facts and duties.** Tenant editability does not rewrite original receipt, acceptance, fulfillment, historical binding, actor/time, prior effect or a third party's obligation. Source-qualified corrections and lawful successor changes remain possible with actual evidence and history. A policy edit cannot itself remove an independent hold, supersede a signed obligation, override independently required protection, grant read/share rights, reset a trigger or certify missing bytes restored. Actual duty/applicability mistakes must have their capable authorized correction/release route; do not make an erroneous constraint permanently uncorrectable or offer an unqualified bypass switch.

**Q66-C05 — Drafts, copying and preparation.** Permit blank creation and copying of authorized reusable definitions into inert tenant drafts without inherited approval, effectivity, actors, record assignments, holds or operational results. The GF worked example does not authorize public template distribution. Editing/saving/discarding a draft changes no effective record policy or disposal eligibility. Draft data has its own lawful purpose, access and cleanup; do not persist private notes or bytes before admission. No mandatory preview, reupload, policy questionnaire on each request or forced provider copy is introduced.

**Q66-C06 — Tenant authority and delegation.** Authoring, approval/activation, default administration, existing-record application, correction, hold/Stop handling, history/impact viewing and byte reading remain distinct actual source/P12 powers. Authorized tenant records actors can perform supported policy work without an Asym approval ticket; a single actor may complete a change when the actual source permits, with no universal second approver. Tenant responsibility/delegation uses existing eligible roles/teams and real authority, not original-creator-only controls or broad admin/service-role assumptions. Approval evidence may be referenced but cannot manufacture the current actor's authority. Unsupported self-escalation or changes to another tenant remain forbidden.

**Q66-C07 — Trusted current identity and context.** Derive tenant/environment/source and actual actor/author/approver attribution from validated native identity and current source/P12 context. Caller identifiers are validated selectors. Preserve actual supported limited/no-Party and system/NHI paths without inventing profiles, memberships or demo tenants. User metadata, stale JWT role claims, Drive IDs, organization names, document access or a service key are not authority. Recheck relevant facts after waits and before effect/result disclosure; application/database context must agree and reset through pooled success/error/cancel/retry paths.

Story: US29-E15. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q66-C01, Q66-C02, Q66-C03, Q66-C04, Q66-C05, Q66-C06, Q66-C07; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

## REQ29-D15 — Recover bytes and adverse controls together

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"33bfd889fcf8c719c5de","name":"REQ29-D15 — Recover bytes and adverse controls together"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Before a source cohort activates, operations shall qualify backup and isolated restore of its actual required bytes, metadata, references/manifests, integrity, keys/configuration, processing identity and current policy/adverse evidence. Declare and measure the applicable recovery-point and recovery-time objectives using representative source families, sizes and copies. A database backup excludes any object bytes it does not actually contain; synthetic database transport checks are not product file recovery.

## REQ29-D08 — Authorize private delivery at the actual serving boundary

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"348a9107e2b69f70ceea","name":"REQ29-D08 — Authorize private delivery at the actual serving boundary"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

The serving interface shall resolve the exact source use/version, representation/action and current selected actor/context before any protected metadata, headers or bytes. Discovery, original read, preview, download, print, export and share remain distinct powers. Strict private purposes use current requester-authorized, non-public/non-cacheable application retrieval and forbid reusable provider bearer URLs; other source profiles require their independently qualified delivery behavior. A reference link still requiring authorization is not a bearer grant.

## REQ29-D14 — Fence final dispatch, late writers and cleanup recovery

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"348b118ecc30db5bd75f","name":"REQ29-D14 — Fence final dispatch, late writers and cleanup recovery"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

An initial work claim is not final destructive authority. After waits, the source/custody boundary shall serialize relevant current holds/uses/writers and record a durable exact attempt/fence before external I/O at the qualified last-preventable dispatch-admission boundary. Its permit, handoff validity and generation are finite; expired/superseded authority denies a fresh dispatch. There is no assumed global SQL-plus-provider transaction or long database lock around network calls.
