<a id="contract-3b3f564aadfb73f7ef24"></a>

## REQ29-E10 — Operate disposal with bounded private evidence

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"3b3f564aadfb73f7ef24","name":"REQ29-E10 — Operate disposal with bounded private evidence"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q63-C15 — Private projections and current disclosure.** Register relevant policy, targets, use/copy history, stop causes and outcomes through the existing shared static field classification/subtract-only projection. Source/P12 purpose and current actor rights govern labels, counts, comparisons, errors, body access, operator views, original-result recovery, search/export and logs. Disposal permission does not grant another source's evidence or legal/hold details. Reject late responses and invalidate unsafe caches after actor/Tenant/environment/source changes; cached lists are never admission proof. No cross-Tenant bulk union, public fallback, persistent restricted device queue or telemetry with bodies/tokens/paths/sensitive reasons. Participants and donors receive only their independently authorized truthful availability/history; this decision adds no consent task or disposition notification. Actual lawful minimized history/tombstone/control retention is separate from retaining full private content forever.

**Q63-C16 — Structural database and every-door safety.** Preserve typed same-scope source/use/record/copy/policy/attempt/result/stop relations, null-complete composite references, required state/time checks, exact current-generation/semantic-effect uniqueness and deliberate delete behavior. Platform-global capability and specialist-source definitions may remain with their owners. Q66 ordinary tenant business-policy definitions and versions are tenant-owned; all assignments and execution remain scoped. Protect immutable original attribution and append-only correction/outcome history under lawful privacy repair; prevent authorized updates from reparenting, forging policy approval, clearing independent stops/holds, swapping a locator or converting an allowed row into forbidden scope. Keep one source/P12 decision layer and coarse Tenant RLS. Qualify effective schema/table/column/sequence, named/PUBLIC/default/inherited EXECUTE, overload/search_path/owner/FORCE/BYPASS/service composition, old USING and resulting WITH CHECK, trigger transformations, SELECT/RETURNING/UPSERT/MERGE/bulk, views/materializations/REST/GraphQL/RPC, Storage, workers/imports/legacy/admin/operator paths and actual pooled context. Unsupported bypass doors must close. Zero affected rows, a service credential or a function keyword is not business success; explicit literal WITH CHECK omission alone is not a vulnerability where PostgreSQL inherits the predicate.

**Q63-C17 — Minimal capable operations and audit.** Reuse narrow source/custody commands, existing durable-work and actual P9 owner routes instead of creating a global deletion scheduler, approval platform or separate incident queue. Required eligibility exceptions, unknown outcomes, intervention causes and impact work must have a capable owner, durable result and supported retry/reconciliation path; routine recovery cannot require direct production SQL or provider-console edits. Distinguish durable business history and policy/actor evidence from security audit and technical logs. Record real automation/human attribution, exact intent/target lineage and known/unknown outcome without private snapshots in general telemetry. No new communication purpose, blanket subscriber, per-request task or borrowed recipient reminder follows. Auditable operational privilege and provider-account controls must support the same boundary; UI-only safety does not constrain privileged tools.

**Q63-C18 — Finite scale and dependency qualification.** Qualify finite discovery/option/history pages, exact operation targets and source groups, copy counts, query/lock/attempt/retry/reconciliation and control-evidence budgets with real workload units and tenant skew. Use indexed bounded set-wise reads, stable unique paging/current per-page rights, bounded durable continuation and fair per-Tenant/source work; no full-store cleanup per review or unbounded tenant-wide destructive query. Respect the smaller actual source budget and qualified provider ceiling; documented Supabase1000/remove and Eve1–500/default100 are not ordinary defaults or capacity promises. Preserve P12 R6's applicable logical governance bound, physical-shard carve-out and restricted unstriped pre-egress/uniformity rules. Verify installed SDK/protocol, error/per-item/version semantics, current provider limits, identity/region/copy obligations and real byte recovery. No vendor's S3 label, lifecycle feature, ETag, TTL or native SDK example supplies Core policy or a stronger atomic guarantee.

**Q63-C19 — Migration, activation and restoration.** Inventory exact retained families/copies, policy/trigger evidence, every writer/reader/job/operator and unknown in-flight effects before adoption. No legacy row, object age, folder, current default or backfill may fabricate historical automatic approval. Introduce compatible source/custody representations and enforcement before writers; activate only an explicitly supported cohort after policy, operators, all-door authorization, boundary/race/fault, copy/restore and actual user proof. A safe stop/kill switch fences affected new admissions while retaining lawful custody, current restrictions and original reconciliation. Rollback cannot restore bypassing old writers, erase unresolved attempts or reinterpret admitted data as ungoverned; keep compatible protections or roll forward. Restore adverse disposal/revocation/correction/stop/control facts before serving or restarting workers, and qualify real object bytes separately from metadata. No activation-induced catch-up purge of unqualified existing records.

Story: US29-E10. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q63-C15, Q63-C16, Q63-C17, Q63-C18, Q63-C19; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

Neither rank, assignment, policy administration, read access nor Q28 implies this power; no original-author monopoly.

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"43c764b2215b235f6504","name":"REQ29-D13 — Separate source disposition from physical-copy removal"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Removal from a view, unlinking, source withdrawal, Trash/recovery retention, expiry, eligibility, claimed deletion, possible physical effect, partial disposal and verified copy removal shall remain distinct facts. Source-use or record disposition may complete while bytes remain lawfully retained for another source; physical-copy deletion may be incomplete even after its metadata is hidden. Source-specific Trash and restoration do not become a universal binary recycle bin.

One deliberate Save shall create a coherent validated usable revision. Working input/autosave is distinct from the shared basis. ## REQ29-C01 — Find ordinary files by approved names and details

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"4bb0490a0da8e9fdbe5d","name":"REQ29-C01 — Find ordinary files by approved names and details"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

<a id="contract-4c07e6da1f8460e18e1e"></a>

## REQ29-A04 — Submitted-original download under current source authority

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"4c07e6da1f8460e18e1e","name":"REQ29-A04 — Submitted-original download under current source authority"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Narrow ordinary capability.** Add a distinct current source-authorized Download submitted file action for the exact own-submission occurrence/member in the ordinary native profile. Upload, preview, correction, mailbox equality or retained custody does not imply it. Ordinary eligible delivery does not require per-file staff unlocking.

**Original identity.** Return the immutable bytes received for the explicitly selected submission version. Original is not first attempt, newest filename, summary, preview, redaction, repaired file or official document. Establish the trusted binding among source occurrence, byte identity, verified length/digest and qualified immutable provider copy before emission; never silently substitute. An end-of-stream hash alone is not preventive identity proof.

**Whole-original disclosure.** Require current permission for the entire unchanged original, including content/metadata omitted by preview and applicable parent/classification/purpose/residency restrictions. If not allowed, deny this action; any permitted processed/accessible alternative is distinct and truthfully labelled.

**Independent delivery qualification.** Qualify actual allowed original file families/features, integrity, hygiene/disposition and delivery mode. Preview success or business acceptance alone is insufficient; preview failure alone neither grants nor forbids an independently qualified download. No unsafe/unverified original bypass for its uploader.

**Deliberate contextual UX.** Provide the visible exact-file action alongside View, reachable by keyboard/touch without viewer or drive entry. One deliberate ordinary activation suffices; no routine extra reason/confirmation/account. No automatic download from page entry, hover, prefetch, preview failure, sign-in return or a retry loop.

**Honest feedback and recovery.** Report only observed preparation/handoff/transfer/error facts. Do not claim Saved, Downloaded successfully, Read or Accepted from a click or server response. Preserve task context and distinguish pending checks, unsafe, unavailable, lost access, changed version and custody failure with safe recovery.

**Safe private response.** Use the qualified Asym-controlled delivery boundary with appropriate attachment/media/no-sniff/private-cache behavior, safe source-approved filename suggestions and no privileged-origin active rendering. Private no-store applies to applicable success/error/redirect/304/416 paths. Errors and login redirects cannot masquerade as file bodies. Never expose raw reusable provider grants or public fallback URLs.

**Current authority and stream fences.** Reauthorize metadata and every new full/HEAD/range/conditional/recovery request through current source/PDP rules. Enforce inherited per-chunk expiry on long streams. Restriction blocks new grants and terminates in-flight delivery where feasible; no generic realtime grace or recall claim.

**Exact range and copy integrity.** Bind each admitted delivery to one immutable representation; validate supported range/conditional outcomes and enforce limits. A partial range requires trusted whole-object/version or qualified chunk-integrity binding. Reconnect cannot splice versions or revive earlier permission. A separately verified identical storage copy may serve the same identity; missing/corrupt copies require repair, not fabricated reconstruction.

**Data and privileged-path safety.** Derive trusted actor/tenant/purpose from server context. Protect required same-scope source/version/copy references, authority fields and integrity/disposition transitions. Verify effective grants/RLS/views/functions/storage and privileged gateway behavior. A read handler cannot accept caller-supplied owner/clearance or mutate acceptance/currentness.

**Existing metering and access evidence.** Apply P12's current tier audit/budget/residency contract, including its required before-serve and durable outbox behavior. Logger construction or an awaited helper that suppresses failures is not durability proof. Access evidence records actual technical outcomes, not saving/reading/consent. Repeated real reads remain accountable; no new CRM interaction or P6 message is created.

**Cache and telemetry privacy.** Protect private bytes and metadata across route/CDN/browser/service-worker/conditional paths. Do not persist an offline private file cache or expose source/provider URLs, tokens, raw private filenames/content/addresses through ordinary telemetry. No-store is not a claim that an intentional saved copy disappears.

**Accessible truthful controls.** Use Core Base UI/base-maia, shared semantic tokens and native semantics; label actions with exact permitted context and useful type/size. Prove keyboard/speech/AT/touch/zoom/reflow/RTL behavior. Preserve document-content accessibility distinctions and safe alternatives; download does not magically make a document accessible.

**Bounded transfer.** Prefer the simplest qualified browser-managed download and bounded server streaming/backpressure. Do not buffer the entire original in page-owned memory merely for progress or a success toast. Any alternative has declared finite source limits, response validation, cancellation/resource cleanup and supported-device proof.

**Read retries and currentness.** A repeated download is a new accountable read attempt, not another submission or a globally one-use permission. Reauthorize retries/resume; suppress accidental repeated initiation only while appropriate. Correction before read admission rejects a stale ordinary target; an active stream stays version-bound and obeys C08 without creating historical rights.

**Source semantics and retention.** Delivery changes no requirement satisfaction, review/claim/application/signature/publication outcome, retention/hold/disposal authority or request/session deadline. Preserve other source uses and existing specialized downloads. No previous-version archive, post-closure duration, other-person files, bulk export, sharing or general drive is granted.

**Read versus disposal.** Admit no new read from restricted/deletion-claimed/ineligible custody. Coordinate bounded read/copy lifecycle with cleanup so referenced valid bytes are not destroyed mid-operation and no read pins custody indefinitely or overrides source disposition. Restoration applies adverse history before delivery.

**Cohort rollout and legacy containment.** Integrate the narrow action in the native owner and Q01-compatible identity/PDP source package. Qualify every reader, URL, header, cache and alternate path before activation. Unknown legacy copies/lineage are restricted/reconciled, not inferred from names or deleted. A new gateway alone does not contain old public/presigned paths.

**Operational ownership.** Supply finite source byte/time/concurrency/range/budget limits and named operational responsibility before activation. Diagnose access, safety, provider, client and integrity failures separately. Disable affected delivery safely without weakening preview/correction boundaries, changing evidence or falling back publicly.

**Proof and completion.** Prove exact original identity, full-content authorization, real-role/private serving, races, range/cache/header behavior, actual browser/AT/recovery and source-cohort migration/restore. Qualify all declared original-delivery profiles; a button or generic Unavailable placeholder is not completion of A.

Story: US29-A04. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q04, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-4e6b235a6308adf384a6"></a>

## REQ29-A11 — Fix unfinished access at original source issuance

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"4e6b235a6308adf384a6","name":"REQ29-A11 — Fix unfinished access at original source issuance"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Original source issuance.** The source SHALL define one original access-issuance effect for the exact tenant/environment, purpose, request and recipient revision. It SHALL establish access availability under approved policy, distinct from drafts, task creation, credentials, delivery, confirmation and fulfillment. Caller-controlled metadata SHALL NOT manufacture the act or attribution.

**Complete finite terms.** At issuance the source SHALL resolve and preserve a valid finite original unfinished end. Null, infinity, invalid arithmetic and already-ended live admissions SHALL be rejected. Initial count, calculation units/calendar convention and any editing power SHALL be explicitly selected and qualified before activation, not borrowed from credential or other-purpose defaults.

**Immutable admitted result.** The original policy/calculation result and applicable limit scopes SHALL be recoverable unchanged. Due-date/default/locale/template changes SHALL NOT recompute the end. Preflight estimates and actual results SHALL be distinguished; material actor-approval or command-precondition conflicts require safe reconciliation, not silent widening.

**Exact authority and entry.** Issuance SHALL prove the issuer's exact source capability and current source/recipient/host/policy, independently of task visibility or broad staff role. Required Q01 confirmation/current proof remains necessary for protected use. GET/HEAD/scanner activity, generic login and return visits SHALL neither issue access nor restart time.

**Business idempotency.** Duplicate/concurrent commands SHALL converge on one original semantic admission despite new transport keys, retries and crashes. Resolve an existing effect before computing new timing/defaults: original issuance time and end are first-admission outputs, not regenerated intent fields. Same-intent retry SHALL recover them; conflicting material intent SHALL fail. A new credential/message SHALL NOT create a new source period. Scope uniqueness to the actual owner lifecycle.

**Coherent durable effect.** Required issuance terms, actor/policy evidence and durable secondary-effect intent SHALL be atomic or equivalently recoverable under a proved fail-closed source contract. No orphan live admission or phantom send may arise from partial writes. Unknown commit SHALL be reconciled before new effects or destructive compensation.

**Time and concurrent changes.** Qualify issuance occurrence/linearization, authoritative current time and relevant source/permission order after waits, including repeated waits and physical commit across a boundary. Rollback creates no issuance. An old transaction timestamp, stale lease or provider timestamp SHALL NOT establish authority; provider calls SHALL NOT occur under source locks.

**Restrictions and nonextension.** Preserve the original end while honoring true scoped revocation/shortening and current action restrictions. Clearing a genuine shortening cause SHALL NOT restore a longer end. Reauth, delivery repair, activity, due edits, copying, reopening and replay SHALL NOT automatically extend/reissue source authority.

**Credentials and communication.** Preserve separate original admission, credential and prepared-message identities. Replacement credentials follow their owning contract, including new message identity where required, while retaining original source terms. Delivery retry/failure/open/click SHALL NOT start, pause or reset recipient time or widen scope.

**Fulfillment causality.** Ordinary completed admission SHALL require original source admission and exact historical eligibility at the qualified fulfillment decision, before its applicable unfinished end. First issuance after earlier completion SHALL NOT backfill that eligibility. Qualified pre-visit or atomic source fulfillment remains possible without an online browser or arbitrary strict timestamp separation. Q07/Q10 limits and no-revival rules remain binding.

**Honest outcome recovery.** Distinguish issued/not issued/unknown, communication outcomes, assurance, file readiness, source completion and current access. Temporary proof/observer failure SHALL NOT be presented as proved source-access expiry/revocation or a definitive not-issued outcome; protected use still fails closed until current proof and authority are established. Recover the same result only under current disclosure authority.

**Structural authorization.** Enforce same-scope relationships, required finite values, semantic uniqueness, immutable original facts, trusted attribution and deliberate delete rules. Actual grants, effective USING/WITH CHECK, views/RPCs, definer/service and storage paths SHALL reject forbidden state transitions. Preserve P12's sole capability resolver, coarse tenant RLS and owning mutation boundary; qualify Q01's exact participant successor, not a generic new context or profile-role fallback.

**Current private use.** Apply current exact source/recipient/action/representation authority on metadata, history, previews, originals, replay, full/HEAD/range/conditional/resume and stream paths. Cached dates or valid technical tokens SHALL not authorize access. Preserve Q04's expiry/revocation/stream contract and Q10's stage-aware expiry; no job-lag grace.

**Truthful task UI.** Keep one source context, separate Requested by and actual access end, clear next actor, received/review/current-file states and scoped staff delivery diagnostics. Do not blame recipients for review, require timer-start confirmation, force downloads, create duplicate requests for retry or promise unselected recovery.

**Accessible temporal UX.** Preserve Core's shared UI system and qualify actual keyboard/AT/mobile/reflow/RTL/localized date and authentication flows. Show a stable truthful instant and timezone; essential information SHALL not depend on color, countdown, toast or tooltip alone. Evaluate each actual timer against applicable accessibility requirements.

**Owned lifecycle changes.** Recipient/host/purpose changes, issuer dependency loss, factual correction, reopening, new work and active-expiry recovery SHALL use explicit owner procedures with prior facts preserved. No broad rule that staff departure always revokes or preserves access, no retargeting by email match, and no implied new extension capability.

**Retention and privacy.** Access expiry SHALL not determine evidence deletion, holds or independent file-use permissions. Retain minimal required original/effect/adverse provenance under owned retention/disposal rules. General logs, messages and caches SHALL not contain private file details, raw proof, sensitive reasons or durable bearer material. No download-recall claim.

**Provider and route readiness.** Activate only a qualified source entry/host/assurance/communication contract. A known unsupported path SHALL not be called usable. Post-issuance failures retain original history/end and trigger owned safe repair; provider success/failure/unknown SHALL not rewrite authority or authorize platform/Site/provider-host fallback.

**Bounded implementation.** Reuse source/PDP/custody/communication/recovery infrastructure with scoped indexed reads, bounded pages/retries and measured per-cohort budgets. Preserve existing P12 R6 governed-read and owner-required mutation-fence contracts. No new global serialization, full-history scan per use, provider call per authorization, second timer service or generic permission engine follows from Q11.

**Durable explainability.** Authorized history SHALL explain who issued what scope, under which original terms, with which outcome, restrictions and recoveries. Distinguish business/security evidence from operational telemetry. Do not infer actual receipt, reading, human intent or device saving from transport events.

**Compatible rollout and restore.** Use prospective qualified cohorts, explicit policy/type mappings and compatible readers/writers/workers. Never backfill issuance from email/task timestamps or replace unknown ends with defaults. Mixed versions, restore and rollback SHALL preserve original effects and adverse history; unsafe fallback stays closed until reconciled.

**Readiness and trace.** Prove an adequate complete finite policy, recipient/reviewer/help journeys and real-role/concurrency/provider/UI behavior before activation. Trace Q11 through glossary/ADR, authorized canonical owners/generated OpenSpec, tasks/tickets and release evidence. Numeric policy, active recovery, Q08 settings and notifications remain explicit decisions; selected documents and adjacent tests are not native qualification.

Story: US29-A11. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q11, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

Lawful preparation does not require an online recipient or already-satisfied first-issue readiness. Actual issuance validates the current adopted draft, source/purpose/recipient, restrictions, responsibility, clocks and communication rules. Issuance-first fences stale application; application/edit-first requires issuance to validate the changed basis.

<a id="contract-520590b4b5b3e397c58f"></a>

## REQ29-B20 — Contain and recover replacement work

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"520590b4b5b3e397c58f","name":"REQ29-B20 — Contain and recover replacement work"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Urgent containment remains independent. Currently authorized staff may use actual source/P12 restriction or Q34 cancellation without waiting for a replacement draft, new recipient readiness or delivery. A narrower access revocation is not automatically request cancellation. When a real Q34 cancellation was committed earlier, preserve it permanently and show the actual canceled predecessor with a prepared, blocked or not-yet-prepared replacement; later deliberate issuance must prove that exact source remedy, current authority/readiness and the still-unconsumed successor relationship, then atomically commit its new issuance/result/history/follow-through. Do not re-cancel, re-notify the cancellation, restore old access or require cancellation power to perform a separately authorized new issuance solely because an earlier actor performed the stop. Repairing readiness does not automatically issue. If preparation is discarded or no valid replacement can be issued, keep the old stop truthful and provide the actual source remedy; do not call it successful replacement or leave a powerless help link as the only path.

Keep the interface in the existing source request and shared Base UI/base-maia design. Provide one resumable preparation with editable safe items, reviewed recipient and current responsibility, then a clearly named final action matching its actual effects; a previously canceled predecessor needs issuance wording, not another Cancel claim. Make active predecessor, unissued replacement, independently stopped predecessor, blocked readiness, committing/unknown result, issued replacement and delivery problem understandable without exposing internal machinery. Authorized staff can navigate the protected relationship and distinguish old canceled work from new outstanding work without double-counting; old business/source reporting remains truthful. Use persistent actionable outcomes, preserved lawful input, correct focus/status/error association, keyboard/screen-reader/speech/touch support, narrow-screen reflow, international names/addresses and locale/time-zone clarity. Prevent IME accidental submission, silent offline sends, stale tenant/actor results and preview-dependent actions. Recipients see their actual permitted request or neutral ended/help outcome with no unnecessary signup or forced reupload. Do not add a generic clone wizard, transfer dashboard, bulk reassignment, second inbox, compulsory explanatory email or confirmation on every draft save.

Qualify capable normal recovery and finite operation. Known old stops stay stopped; unknown commitment is reconciled; draft conflict preserves lawful input; unauthorized or unavailable checks do not silently discard successful effects. A wrong issued successor is corrected through its current source action, not Undo, history editing or old-grant restoration. Preserve minimal protected business/security history separately from payload-free technical diagnostics and governed source retention; free-text drafts and provenance receive applicable minimization/disposition, not indefinite duplicate archives. Use bounded indexed current/predecessor/successor queries, pagination for history, source limits for item/prose preparation, short database transactions and set-based checks; no recursive whole-chain scan, per-file polling, byte copying, mandatory Realtime or new provider. Record measured workload/query/lock/recovery/cost bounds and actual operating owners before activation; do not claim unchanged Supabase/Vercel/email cost or invent an empirical maximum from the review.

Roll out prospectively with an explicit inventory of existing drafts, issued/canceled sources and authority/communication paths. Do not infer replacement relationships, original proof, unused successor slots or current permissions from similar emails, copied names or missing history. Validate native constraints and current writer compatibility before exposure; fence old paths that can issue an unlinked or duplicate successor, reparent records or ignore current stops. Restored data must incorporate later cancellation, issuance, restriction and possible-send evidence before dispatch resumes. A scoped disable stops new replacement admissions while preserving truthful original-result recovery, existing issued requests, required safety actions and qualified source repair. Roll forward when older code cannot honor written facts; never roll back by reopening a predecessor or deleting a successor. Require native role, concurrency, fault/replay, message-plan/provider, serving, restore and composed accessibility/intended-user proof. Monitoring has named signals, thresholds, owners and responses; identity, authorization, evidence integrity and unique issuance remain prevention/release gates.

Preserve the adopted Document Request Replacement meaning and all current domain definitions. Reconcile the explicit source/P12 preparation and linked-replacement command, Q34 remedy/atomic composition, Q25 original admission, Q39 narrow definition reuse, Q01/access/serving, Q21/Q22/Q28, Q35/Q48, P3/P9/P17/P6/custody and applicable Workflow compatibility amendments through canonical owners and regenerate their incorporated OpenSpec projections. Trace exact terms and obligations through design, authorized tasks/tickets, implementation, native tests and release evidence. Specification publication proves no implementation, native qualification or live effect.

This path requires a different intended person. Q60 same-recipient corrective work has its own one-ever-issued remedial result, automatic reminders unavailable/Off and exact independently qualified manual/issuance notices. Same-request correction retains Q48 counts/stops. Both preserve unaffected evidence and original outcomes.

Story: US29-B20. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q49, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<a id="contract-56174577fbab78d83a6e"></a>

## REQ29-A20 — Default-on fresh-access email with staff control

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"56174577fbab78d83a6e","name":"REQ29-A20 — Default-on fresh-access email with staff control"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Scope and retained choice.** Apply this optional email choice only to the ordinary native fresh unfinished and fresh completed-read source actions, first or subsequent periods. Keep their purposes distinct. For each genuinely new, known-eligible review initialize the visible choice selected; preserve the actual choice through validation, back navigation and safe draft recovery. Default initialization is not server authorization. Require an explicit valid reviewed choice under the command contract; missing, null, legacy or conflicting values must not default to true. A selected control need not be manually toggled to authorize its visible consequence at deliberate approval. Exclude original invitations, identity messages, reminders, review/fulfillment notices, bulk, other channels, staff alerts and specialist mandatory notices.

**Authority and eligibility.** Treat the choice as a source occurrence condition, never as a per-grant Delivery Plan override, recipient preference, message classification or access permission. Derive tenant/environment, actor, grant/source, intended recipient/address revision, permission and audit attribution from trusted context and qualified owner relationships. The exact Live contract, effective bounded plan, audience, recipient eligibility/suppression, sender/reply and source/action checks must permit the notice. Known unavailable/unknown paths must be explained safely; staff can deliberately choose an otherwise-lawful grant-only path. Do not silently downgrade a confirmed combined intent before admission if material eligibility/destination changes. A provider delay with a valid durable authorized path is distinct from missing authority. After known source commitment preserve that result and report the actual notice state. Q01's closed recipient amendment and specialist narrowing/mandatory rules remain binding.

**Durable effect and exact replay.** Preserve the committed grant, explicit reviewed choice and required communication handoff/recovery evidence coherently at the existing authoritative boundary before claiming the combined operation accepted. No notice can describe an uncommitted grant; a crash between source write and secondary processing must be recoverable from durable exact source evidence. Providers run outside database locks. Recover matching committed work before evaluating new admission. A replay with false must not add a notice after plan/default/readiness changes; changed true/false or other material under a committed identity conflicts. Recovery may complete the already-authorized true notice under its original meaning. P6 exact-set release includes valid zero-member results; unknown compilation is not a valid omission. No replay may append an omitted member. Use permanent source/purpose/recipient communication identity and material equality; do not mistake a transport request key for the business effect.

**Current source and immutable recovery.** Keep all access clocks anchored to their ratified source issuance, with earlier limits; selection, queueing, delivery, opening and credential repair never reset them. Recheck exact source/grant/recipient/contact revision, plan/suppression, sender/reply/connection and protected-action applicability at the governing preparation/submission/use boundaries. A queued unfinished notice after fulfillment needs an exact qualified source/contract applicability or variant; otherwise do not dispatch it. Never rewrite a sealed unfinished notice into completed-read mail. Expired/revoked/no-longer-applicable work cannot advertise current access merely because it was selected earlier. Material changes use only authorized immutable recovery. A new credential, where allowed by ADR-0025, has its required new communication/prepared identity and recovery lineage without a new grant or clock. Old credentials do not resolve authority by finding the newest period.

**Provider truth and safe evidence.** Reuse P6/ADR-0032's sealed payload, exact envelope, account/environment identity, bounded same-key recovery and reconciliation rules. The provider's 24-hour deduplication window is not Core's business-history TTL or permission to replay with a new key. Possible submission remains indeterminate until exact evidence resolves it; no blind resend, changed envelope, physical exactly-once promise or guaranteed recall. Authenticate callbacks and correlate them against the durable exact provider account/connection/environment/message/attempt; payload tags, address and signed metadata alone are not tenant authority. Duplicate/out-of-order events must not rewind delivery truth or confuse engagement with receipt/reading. Retain only approved body-free evidence; never raw protected URLs, credential verifiers, secret-bearing click payloads or private document facts in logs/history/queues.

**One clear, accessible interaction.** Reuse the existing source review and supported shared controls. Show the permitted recipient/destination and selected consequence in ordinary readable content, with a final action whose visible and accessible names accurately distinguish grant-plus-email from grant-only. Checkbox changes alone have no grant/send/navigation effect. Preserve state/focus, keyboard/touch/reflow, approved locale/direction and actual localized end with time-zone context; avoid countdown-only or transient-only results. Show the grant and this notice's real status separately: For a staff-off choice, report this access notice was not requested, not that every independent email was suppressed. For selected notices, preserve requested intent and report the actual blocked/suppressed/non-applicable/unknown outcome; a zero-child result does not rewrite the choice. Queued/accepted is not delivered/read; possible submission is not definitely not sent. Explain safe next action in context. Use only the approved minimal subject/preview/body/action; no arbitrary recipient/composer/attachment, new account, routine second confirmation, manual-toggle ritual, new queue/dashboard or direct storage URL fallback.

**Data, privacy and bounded operation.** Enforce the tenant/environment/source/recipient relationship and permanent effect identity through qualified database keys, constraints and one authoritative mutation boundary. Ordinary roles cannot rewrite attribution, scope, choice, release membership or frozen message facts; inspect SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE grants, RLS USING and WITH CHECK, RPC/view/storage and service-role paths, not only UI checks. No broadening by allowed updates, cross-tenant references or unchecked privileged lookup. Preserve immutable history and lawful retention/erasure; access expiry is not deletion and a hold is not access. Qualify the optional external-recipient prepared-material/utility class and Recent-copy posture explicitly; neither existing staff/financial classes nor Q14/Q18's 30-day access period may be inherited accidentally. Use bounded indexed source/intent lookup, paginated authorized history and existing per-tenant dispatch limits; no request-time historical scans, new mail queue, scheduler or dual truth.

**Publication, rollout and proof.** At authorized publication reconcile the exact listed Q08–Q19 notice reservations and current source/P4/P12/P17/P6 authors, then regenerate declared projections and link future design/tasks/tickets/tests/release evidence. Keep Q01 identity intent and all other ratified limits; do not copy unrelated purpose keys or require whole Phase34/41. Before Live activation qualify the exact contract/profile, finite material/utility posture, current authorization and all P01–P18 outcomes. Mixed-version/legacy data must never infer true, regenerate historical notices, revive credentials or replay provider-unknown work; restore preserves restrictions, immutable identities and original clocks. Provide a narrowly scoped dispatch stop and roll-forward recovery that preserve committed grants/history. Establish workload/queue/reconciliation budgets with the existing owner before activation, not invented numbers here. Treat GOV.UK's preselection counterevidence and actual staff comprehension as release evidence, not solved by source review. No new resend/automatic renewal/notification framework follows.

Story: US29-A20. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q20, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

## REQ29-B16 — Source-owned ordinary item additions

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"56aa1de8ce8630cae256","name":"REQ29-B16 — Source-owned ordinary item additions"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

For an eligible issued but genuinely unfinished ordinary Private Document Request, the requesting source SHALL permit currently authorized staff to deliberately add a new ordinary Document Request Item for the same continuing legitimate purpose and intended recipient. Adding is a distinct requirement-authoring act: reviewer, task, file-read, assignment, support or grant-management powers alone do not authorize it. Use the actual source/P12 capability and relevant current revisions; no new generic permission or workflow engine is required.

Preserve the original request, every prior item definition, receipt, immutable version/correction lineage, acceptance, question and historical access decision. Give the added item its own durable identity, true admission time, instructions, source-qualified requiredness and review basis. Do not rewrite an existing requirement, copy acceptance, impersonate participant assertions, edit other requests through a template, or add specialist obligations through this ordinary action. Require only information the source actually needs; do not add a compulsory essay or routine second approver.

Before authoritative publication, establish a capable lawful route for the declared new work. For participant-required work, coherently admit the new item and its exact prospective participant action/read scope under the same purpose and recipient, with applicable review coverage. During a valid original or fresh unfinished period, use a qualified scope successor retaining that period's exact end, narrower subsets, earlier caps and current restrictions. This expressly amends Q01/Q13-C06/Q15 only for prospectively approved added-item lineage; old grants, tokens, proofs and historical requirements never silently widen. If access has expired, actual participant demand needs a separately reviewed and authorized Q13–Q15 fresh admission for that amended lineage, preserving ordinary-expiry eligibility and sequential nonoverlap. One combined user action is permitted when all distinct authorities are qualified. An inert draft or a promise of a later grant is not published usable work.

Do not make recipient access a universal prerequisite for independently lawful staff work. Where the source permits Q28 exact existing evidence and Q21 review to satisfy the new item without a participant act or assertion, that capable route may be used without renewing recipient rights. Show actual staff responsibility and participant scope/availability, including absence of current access where applicable, without revoking or concealing independently valid old-item rights. If participant action later becomes necessary, qualify actual amended scope/access or a capable source correction before presenting an actionable demand; do not conceal an inaccessible participant obligation behind a staff-only label. If no capable lawful route is available, keep preparation uncommitted and use an independently qualified request or source remedy.

Reuse current Q24 person/team responsibility when it genuinely covers the added declared work; establish missing coverage before publication. This is Q25's relevant source-edit coverage, not a global rerun of first-issuance readiness, a full-roster or presence check, mandatory reassignment or a new approval ceremony. Reuse Q01 identity proof only when it is qualified for the amended exact context and required assurance. Mailbox equality, enrollment and possession of an old link are insufficient; a new challenge is not mandatory for every added row.

Admit the source item, qualified route/scope, required audit and durable follow-through coherently. Serialize relevant changes with the complete Q22 predicate and actual Q34 cancellation using the source aggregate boundary or an equivalent qualified version/fence protocol. Addition first makes the new genuine requirement part of the current predicate; actual durable fulfillment or cancellation first denies this addition. Final human acceptance, queued evaluation or a stale display is not fulfillment. Draft or planned additions never hold completion. Source-certified nonblocking optional work remains nonblocking. Every relevant admitted change evaluates or durably arranges reevaluation, without another routine Complete action.

Preserve unaffected existing work and its lawful continuation. Revalidate affected uncommitted operations and relevant Q30 context, without interpreting every aggregate revision as a universal invalidation, read acknowledgment or reply gate. Old unadmitted operations cannot borrow new scope. Durable existing receipts remain receipts; mere file transfer is not receipt. Lost responses recover the same semantic addition under current disclosure, without another item, authority period or business effect; changed material conflicts and genuinely distinct authorized additions remain distinct. Replay after a lawful removal or terminal outcome returns the original result with permitted current status and never restores the old demand or scope.

Explicitly carry the approved amended scope into any Q06/Q10/Q13 normal completed-view admission only when actual timely fulfillment qualifies it, subject to declared current-own-submission/member/version/action limits and existing ends/caps. Later Q08/Q16/Q19 fresh completed-reading remains bounded by that actual fulfillment-created ceiling and current eligibility. Never derive it from today's larger request definition or a union of historic grants. An individually narrower active, fresh or recovery admission remains narrow while in effect, but does not by itself impose a permanent global ceiling on later separately authorized admissions within their genuine source-approved or fulfillment-created universe. Independent staff processing after recipient expiry can finish the request without reviving participant access. Addition itself creates no new duration, earlier-period rewrite, overlap, automatic renewal or completed-request reopening.

In the existing accessible source/request UI, let staff review the exact new item, responsibility, actual route, completion consequence, requested-by target and access end before one meaningful Add item action. Show truthful published, uncommitted, blocked or outcome-unknown results with capable recovery. On authorized return, distinguish added work and remaining responsibilities from preserved prior progress. State the real added-at time; do not label new work overdue before it existed or imply earlier accepted work was rejected. Preserve genuine source-level Q09 target/lateness, show the actual remaining access window and any known incompatibility with the required work or target, and use actual authorized timing/access remedies rather than silent grace or reset. No work-duration estimator or sufficiency guarantee is implied. No forced account, reupload, download, Read/Resolve acknowledgment or new notification follows.

Correct a mistaken addition through Q33 only while its outstanding unaccepted-item eligibility holds. After acceptance or terminal closure, preserve historical facts and use an attributable source error/remediation record plus the actually qualified source/privacy restriction or disposition route where needed. A new request does not remedy unnecessary evidence already collected. Do not offer universal Undo, silent unaccept, history deletion, predecessor revival or a powerless contact. Qualify a capable correction path for each supported stage before activation.

Enforce tenant/environment/source/request/item/recipient/version relationships and current authorization at every writer and private reader, including alternate APIs, SQL/RLS, views/functions, storage, worker/service-role and replay paths. Derive tenant, acting human, author and audit attribution from trusted server context; validate selected targets and material inputs against current owner rules. Protect both old-row selection and resulting-row state, immutable history and semantic-effect uniqueness. Cache invalidation is not an authorization fence. Minimize private labels, evidence and identifiers in notifications, logs, URLs, exports and retained copies under existing source/custody policies.

Adopt this capability prospectively through exact source/P12/Q01/Q13/Q15/Q22/Q24/Q25/Q28 and completed-reading owner amendments and their declared OpenSpec projections. Require native structural/current-authority/race/replay/restore proof and composed participant/staff accessibility/usability evidence. Gate incompatible old writers/readers, preserve history after rollback, and disable new additions without undoing admitted work. Reuse existing infrastructure and source surfaces; do not add a generalized amendment engine, arbitrary rule designer, bulk workflow, new account/portal, added-item email/default beyond Q40's exact optional purpose, reminder, channel, repeated approval or whole future Studio dependency.

Q40 separately permits the exact optional update notice from a genuinely committed addition. Publication alone grants no contact/transport permission or other-purpose notice. Q60 applies when mistaken acceptance/terminal state makes ordinary removal ineligible.

**Exact inherited profiles.** These values apply only to the named source/representation and units, never as generic intake limits:

- Fresh unfinished maximum; Existing Q14 permits up to30 elapsed days =720hours =2,592,000seconds from actual source issuance, subject to stricter caps. Q36 does not choose a new duration or reset issuance.

## REQ29-E20 — Record supported actual external events

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"5bc60ed37aa25dd9303a","name":"REQ29-E20 — Record supported actual external events"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q67-C01 — Optional source-owned capture.** Allow currently authorized tenant staff to record that an already-defined, supported external event actually occurred for its exact source subject and purpose, through the owning source's deliberate command. Reuse a suitable existing native human command or authoritative fact when available; keep qualified native and integration producers. This adds an input route, not arbitrary event definitions, policy-form date overrides or a generic status editor. It cannot mint contract termination, financial settlement, receipt, acceptance, Q22 fulfillment or Q65 reference End facts owned elsewhere.

**Q67-C02 — Tenant flexibility and real authority.** Tenants may use their existing qualified roles/delegations and supported source evidence rules to permit or restrict this route; no all-staff/admin grant, new mandatory configuration wizard, Asym support ticket or universal second approver is required. Initial recording, correction/retraction, policy editing, reviewing history, body access and disposition remain distinct actual source/P12 capabilities. Use a single actor when the true source permits. A policy editor, record viewer, uploader, workflow assignee or actor with a service credential is not thereby an event authority. No new public, donor, missionary or anonymous write route is granted.

**Q67-C03 — Truthful source admission.** The owning source validates the exact event meaning, subject, purpose, currently permitted evidence and accepted assertion at commitment. Preserve human/native/integration provenance and any supported evidence qualification distinctly. A staff report accepted for its declared records purpose is not proof of independent verification, legal validity or unrelated business completion. Source-required approval is honored; a successful save, attachment, checkbox, filename or signature-looking image cannot replace it. Relevant credible source conflict requires scoped resolution; arbitrary caller allegations cannot place a global hold or manufacture a conflict.

**Q67-C04 — Exact subject and honest impact.** Bind the typed source/event version and exact stable subject/occurrence identity within trusted tenant/environment scope; an empty or unknown scope never means all records with the same label. Use friendly names for display without making them identities. Show the permitted event/date/evidence and material current consequences before commitment, including that a late occurrence can make a policy already due. Explain automatic/manual mode where material without revealing hidden records/counts/hold reasons. Revalidate changed authority, subject, source basis and consequential impact; require renewed deliberate review when the submitted intent or material consequence changed. A read/preview is not authorization.

**Q67-C05 — Actual date and recorded time.** Preserve source-defined occurrence precision, calendar and timezone separately from trusted server-recorded time and actor. Date-only values remain dates rather than fabricated midnight instants; instant-based events use the source's required offset/zone semantics and reject unresolved ambiguous/nonexistent local times. Accept truthful late reporting when allowed. A future planned event is not an occurrence; compare under the declared source time basis, not an arbitrary UTC date or browser clock. Unknown/partial dates use only supported source semantics and truthful unresolved handling. Never default a missing occurrence to today, upload/edit time, an invented boundary or a convenient earlier date.

**Q67-C06 — Proportionate evidence and small UX.** Start in the relevant authorized source record with the subject and supported event known. Ask “When did [event] happen?” and only for missing evidence/reference the source actually needs; use an existing sufficient reference when allowed. Show a concise consequence summary and one specific “Record [event]” action. Preserve entered values for recoverable errors within current access, offer clear correction and cancel before submission, and avoid mandatory attachments, duplicate context, essays, second approval, global dashboards or blanket confirmation modals. Give truthful source-safe results: recorded; recorded with required processing pending; not recorded; or outcome unknown. Do not show final success before authoritative acceptance or “nothing will be deleted” when qualified automatic disposal may follow.

Story: US29-E20. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q67-C01, Q67-C02, Q67-C03, Q67-C04, Q67-C05, Q67-C06; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

## REQ29-D17 — Migrate reached families through one authority cutover

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"5c31e9c1a04dd84c76fe","name":"REQ29-D17 — Migrate reached families through one authority cutover"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Before a reached cohort changes custody or access authority, census actual retained records/bytes, consumers, readers/writers, jobs/scripts, provider versions/copies, URLs and customer reliance. Classify source meaning, immutable identity, privacy/retention and supported profile; unknown mapping is not zero data. Preserve exact source IDs and verify bytes/lineage/references through a single qualified cutover. Historical migration or schema visibility declarations are evidence to inspect, not proof of effective deployed access.

Story: US29-D17. Source: custody-and-integration.md. Decision basis: Founder §§4,16,19; Q61 R-LEGACY/P13; source cutover contracts including P18 ADR0033 and P23 D27.

## REQ29-P02 — Resolve current native identity, tenant and exact capabilities

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"5de5d9791fad70fd43f9","name":"REQ29-P02 — Resolve current native identity, tenant and exact capabilities"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Every admission and disclosure shall resolve verified native identity and the actual current source/P12 Tenant Authorization Context. Staff, limited request/share recipients, donors, missionaries, public readers, qualified non-human identities and audited operators retain their actual source-supported paths; no fake Party, donor, membership, staff role or demo Tenant may be fabricated to satisfy an interface. Tenant, actor, author, reviewer, approver and authoritative recording time are derived from trusted context; caller identifiers are selectors validated against it. Capability names/meaning remain with the registry and source, while tenants allocate eligible grants through qualified controls. Sign-in, email equality, a relationship, link possession, role title, ownership of a provider key and stale JWT claims are not current action authority. User-editable Auth metadata never authorizes. Current native session/revocation guarantees must qualify independently of application denial.

Story: US29-P02. Source: proof.md. Decision basis: Founder §§3/7/10/11/16/19; Q01; Q04; Q21; Q41; Q52-C04; Q60-C02; Q63-C05; Q66-C06–C07; Q67-C02/C12; Phase 12 authority.

Shared custody shall preserve private hostile intake, discarded source names and bounded raw-intake cleanup, opaque IDs, no public original and independently verified output. <a id="contract-63e48b56061ffc51f0f9"></a>

## REQ29-B08 — Staff-supplied existing document evidence

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"63e48b56061ffc51f0f9","name":"REQ29-B08 — Staff-supplied existing document evidence"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Scope and explicit source authority.** The ordinary native source SHALL admit a deliberate staff-origin existing-evidence contribution to a currently eligible unaccepted item without fresh participant affirmation solely for reuse. Preserve source-required personal assertions, consent, current documents, certification/signatures and specialist rules. Existing P12/source administration SHALL determine who may exercise the admitted action; read, review, assignment, creator, admin or custody status alone SHALL NOT confer it. Do not force a new permission atom, tenant switch, confirmation engine or second actor. Staff may still request participant work through existing source-supported actions.

**Exact selection and current eligibility.** Authorize discovery, metadata, preview, reuse and destination admission independently, using trusted tenant/environment/actor context and validated exact source, subject, target item, requirement/purpose and version selectors. The authoritative boundary SHALL recheck required current eligibility after relevant waits. No client-supplied actor, approval, tenant binding, origin, accepted flag or arbitrary provider URL becomes truth. Authorize before search/count/facet/thumbnail/duplicate disclosure. A readable file may be ineligible for reuse; unknown proof is not permission or valid empty search.

**Evidence membership and provenance.** Addition SHALL create only an attributable typed target contribution referencing qualified immutable bytes, with original provenance distinct from actual new staff admission. Respect source cardinality and relevant evidence-set revision; preserve other members. An exact already-current equivalent membership, using the source-defined contribution role, version, purpose and lineage identity rather than digest alone, SHALL return its existing permitted outcome without new admission, acceptance or outward effects; do not globally collapse genuinely distinct later source acts by digest. No silent latest-version substitution, whole-set replacement, recipient impersonation, backdated original receipt or cross-use acceptance transfer.

**Qualification, continued use and disposition.** Reuse admission SHALL prove exact durable custody/integrity and actual source use rights. Only expressly supported bounded post-admission target checks may remain Checking; review/serving/acceptance SHALL meet their current qualified profiles. Preserve independently owned target use/retention and declared continuing origin restrictions; later actor role loss alone is not universal target revocation. Qualify adverse restriction scope and propagation at actual affected doors, without old-user-ACL coupling or detached-copy bypass. Target reference/retention protection SHALL order with deletion claim/disposal; unlink/withdrawal is not physical deletion. Holds and retained bytes confer no use/read authority. ADR-0165's Page/Article Trash duration does not become a P29 binary-retention rule.

**Review, responsibility and fulfillment.** Q21 acceptance SHALL evaluate the exact current new item/requirement/evidence basis and actual safety/inspection, independently of admission. Same-person supply/review is allowed only by actual powers/SoD; no universal second reviewer. Relevant membership changes fence stale decisions; source-defined remaining concerns are not silently resolved by attaching any file. Q22 alone evaluates complete requirements and records actual fulfillment. Q24 current responsibility/recovery remains; Q25 is not retroactively made a continuous admission gate. Staff work after participant expiry may be lawful without granting or resetting any recipient rights.

**Participant truth and privacy.** Native projections SHALL distinguish staff contribution from participant submission/confirmation. Q02/Q04/Q05 own-submission scope SHALL NOT automatically include staff evidence. Expose only currently target-authorized summary, actor label, status, file/version or history; do not infer preview/download permission or reveal protected originating records, care notes, third-party data or other uses. Where safe, show what staff added and whether participant work remains; otherwise give approved neutral status and an actually owned help route. A review concern SHALL name an actually authorized next actor/action: do not direct participants to Q03 own-submission correction for a staff contribution they cannot amend; staff recovery or genuine source-authorized new participant work remains distinct. Required participant affirmation SHALL use a separately qualified meaningful exact presentation and act. No new recipient-wide library or hidden disclosure grant.

**Mistake recovery.** Before activating staff reuse, provide a qualified source action to withdraw the exact current still-unaccepted staff-supplied contribution from an open eligible item. Record actual actor/time/cause and preserve original admission/history, other members and bytes; the deliberate action supplies the ordinary reason, with no compulsory essay. Recompute/fence the current evidence/review basis without waiver, acceptance or predecessor revival. Any offered replacement SHALL atomically withdraw/add the exact qualified successor and preserve lineage; failed successor admission/transaction SHALL commit neither withdrawal nor addition. If a qualified replacement commits with permitted Checking, a later check failure SHALL preserve that committed lineage and use source recovery, without automatically restoring the predecessor. No permission to alter participant contributions or accepted/frozen/closed work is implied. Those cases require existing qualified source remediation; urgent privacy suppression follows its actual owner.

**Atomicity and replay.** Co-located source membership, current basis, required history/reference protection and durable follow-up SHALL commit atomically; otherwise use the existing qualified owner protocol that prevents half-admission. Serialize actual overlapping admission, withdrawal, correction, acceptance/closure, rights and disposal changes with bounded source-level fences; no tenant-wide lock or provider call under lock. Use trusted actual source occurrence times after relevant waits, not browser/upload time or a stale transaction-start clock. Recover same durable intent with original actor/time/output under current disclosure before evaluating fresh-command gates. Different transport keys cannot duplicate the business effect; changed material conflicts. An uncertain result is reconciled, not assumed absent. Secondary projection/notice failure does not roll back known source success or delete shared bytes.

**Work and communication compatibility.** As an explicit extension of Q27's ordinary-source trigger binding, a newly admitted staff-origin contribution/replacement that first makes its new evidence basis actionable SHALL use Q24 work and Q27 opt-in staff email, preserving all original occurrence/policy/cohort/Off/material/replay rules. The meaningful item/evidence basis, not each selected file, owns the event. Removal alone, same-member no-op, retry, processing oscillation or role change SHALL NOT create new incoming-work mail. Current old-basis mail is suppressed by its existing rules. Q26 is only for an actual authorized Request changes outcome. No new participant email/default, bell, transport or reminder follows.

**Database and authorization proof.** Required identity/scope/actor/time/version/basis/use fields SHALL be non-null where meaningful; absence SHALL never default to approved/participant-origin/current permission. Use same-tenant/environment keys and foreign keys for source/item/version relationships, checks for valid combinations, constrained current membership/cardinality and durable operation uniqueness. Provenance/decisions are immutable except explicit governed redaction; no cascades through independent evidence, uses, holds or histories. Cross-row/state invariants SHALL use authoritative transactions/fences and appropriate constraints, not a CHECK querying mutable rows. Qualify effective grants, SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE USING/WITH CHECK semantics, DELETE, views, RPC/functions/triggers, Storage and privileged paths with actual roles; allowed mutations must not retarget source/tenant/subject, forge actor, revive a withdrawn use or become accepted. Index the qualified current lookups/references; do not freeze unnecessary table/column/library choices. No money field or precision change is introduced.

**Usable contextual flows.** In the existing Base UI/base-maia item context, show eligible exact versions with sufficient permitted subject/version/date/provenance and the actual target consequence. Use one deliberate add action; no extra confirm solely for reuse or preview/dwell requirement. Keep separate truthful Checking, Awaiting review, Accepted, Removed and unresolved outcomes as applicable, with persistent accessible status and safe next action. Preserve permitted focus/notes/context on errors; discard delayed results after user/tenant/item/purpose change and clear private material when authority ends. Qualify actual inspection content, keyboard/AT/reflow/touch/localization/time-zone and low-bandwidth journeys, not just controls. No forced download, re-upload, new identity fields/account, automatic match, private offline cache or generic source-admin form.

**Qualification, rollout and bounded operations.** Define the exact supported native source cohort and all affected owner bindings before activation; legacy metadata, old approvals or imported flags SHALL NOT backfill staff admission/participant affirmation. Require P01–P24, bounded indexed current proof and measured finite workload/query/latency/recovery budgets with responsible owners before release. Mixed clients/writers, prospective activation, scoped stop and restore/roll-forward SHALL preserve committed uses, adverse restrictions, historical truth and permanent effect identity; old code must not reinterpret staff origin as recipient origin. No historical catch-up notices or new retention period. Use existing owned recovery/telemetry, minimal protected audit and governed retention/export/backup treatment, not a new workflow or monitoring platform.

Q53 separately admits new staff-local files with truthful new receipt; Q54 adds only qualified optional pre-intent hints. Existing-version reuse creates no new original receipt, participant assertion, copied acceptance or general library grant.

Story: US29-B08. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q28, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-645e76bcce913cc09137"></a>

## REQ29-A13 — Source-authorized fresh unfinished-request access

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"645e76bcce913cc09137","name":"REQ29-A13 — Source-authorized fresh unfinished-request access"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Fresh unfinished access MUST be a deliberate prospective source admission for the same intended recipient, request and continuing original purpose; it MUST NOT edit the ended grant, reopen completed work, confer membership or authorize general storage.

The source MUST prove original lawful admission and eligible ordinary unfinished expiry; unknown, adverse, binding-shortened, cancelled or independently prohibited states MUST NOT be relabeled ordinary expiry, including after an adverse cause is removed. Each limit retains its actual action/stage/purpose scope.

Original issuance/results, expired ends, receipt/acceptance facts and adverse history MUST remain attributable and unchanged. Previously admitted outcomes MUST be recovered on their original lineage, with current disclosure checks.

Fresh issuance MUST use the exact current source capability and P12 administrative scope/live assignable ceiling, qualified grant type and continuing issuer/delegation dependencies through the sole owning mutation boundary; read/review/support authority or a role name MUST NOT substitute.

Tenant, principal, actor, intended recipient/revision, source and authoritative timestamps/status MUST derive from trusted context and source facts; client selectors MUST be independently resolved. Current qualified Q01 proof MUST precede recipient use and MUST NOT create broader membership or source authority.

Reviewed requirement/action scope MUST be bounded by original admitted purpose/requirement lineage and current eligibility. Eligible pending work MAY admit new first submissions or immutable Q03 successors; accepted/frozen writes, new requirements/purposes/recipients and historical-byte access MUST remain excluded from this lane.

Read, submission and correction rights MUST be explicit and distinct. The fresh terms MUST declare eligible completed-read scope; completion MUST resolve current own-submission members within that scope without deriving reading from writing, unioning expired grants or widening a reviewed subset. Independently permitted accepted-file reading MUST NOT be prohibited merely because writes are frozen.

Fresh admission MUST have proved prospective causal authority at its source effect boundary. Completion-before-admission and gap completion MUST NOT obtain backfilled eligibility. A source-qualified atomic admission then fulfillment MAY qualify; equal timestamps alone MUST establish neither permission nor rejection.

The owner MUST apply Q14's source-issued up-to720-elapsed-hour period, including unused access, and Q15's individually reviewed nonoverlapping sequential admissions without a preset lifetime/cumulative cap. Resolved first-admission outputs MUST be preserved on retries. Missing policy MUST NOT mean infinity, automatic sixty days, one-use lifetime or any unselected renewal rule.

One eligible normal fulfillment during valid fresh unfinished authority MUST stop ordinary writes and MAY admit the declared completed view under Q07: end no later than F plus 720 elapsed hours and genuine applicable caps. Stage-only ends MUST NOT become overall caps; observation/retry/reopen MUST NOT reset F/end. Further Q08 eligibility MUST NOT be widened without an explicit compatible owner decision.

Issuance, relevant grant-state changes, requirement/correction/acceptance and fulfillment MUST satisfy the owners' coherent serialization/fence contract with current authority/state/time rechecked after waits. Use required P12 grant mutation fencing; providers MUST NOT run under source locks, and scheduling concurrency MUST NOT substitute for database invariants.

One deliberate fresh effect MUST have a stable business identity, bound material reviewed input and immutable admitted outputs. Same-effect retries MUST converge; incompatible material input MUST conflict; distinct intentional issuances MUST satisfy adopted repetition/overlap atomically. Current private replay disclosure MUST be independently authorized.

Source effect, required attributable audit and durable follow-up intent MUST have a coherent commit/recovery outcome. Unknown commitment MUST be reconciled before another issuance; grant, usability, proof, delivery and entry facts MUST remain distinct. Closing a dispatched UI MUST NOT be represented as cancellation.

An unadmitted operation bound to ended authority MUST NOT silently acquire a fresh grant on retry, upload completion, callback or worker execution. A new currently authorized deliberate submission MAY reuse exact qualified custody only under the source contract, preserving real receipt timing, scan/acceptance state and immutable Q03 lineage. An ambiguous finalization response MUST NOT alone trigger deletion of potentially admitted bytes; use existing source reconciliation and custody/retention rules.

Data constraints and the sole source command MUST enforce same-tenant source/recipient/requirement/effect relationships, required provenance, finite resolved time ordering and valid state combinations. Historical grant/receipt/acceptance meaning MUST NOT be rewritten by general UPDATE/upsert/cascade. Unknown unresolved facts MUST be explicit, not permissive null/default values; owner retention/disposal remains separate.

Qualification MUST cover effective table/column/function grants, RLS old/new-row checks, composed policies, views, RPC/storage and privileged service/definer/job paths. A permitted mutation MUST NOT move a row into another tenant, recipient, scope or forbidden state. Coarse RLS MUST NOT replace P12/source checks, and omission of a literal WITH CHECK clause alone MUST NOT be called a vulnerability.

Every private metadata/byte door, including history, count/order, preview, original, HEAD/range/resume/cache/replay and active serving, MUST enforce Q04/Q05 current exact authority. Cache invalidation MUST NOT be the permission fence. An expired credential/provider URL MUST NOT revive or borrow fresh rights. Delivery/custody timestamps MUST NOT manufacture source admission; no claim may promise recall of disclosed bytes.

Staff MUST use one meaningful review of source-bound recipient, action/requirement/read scope and qualified timing/limits, with refreshed review for material changes. The existing request UI MUST preserve receipt truth and clear next actors; ordinary time advance MUST NOT cause another confirmation. No new arbitrary recipient/date picker, mandatory essay or universal extra approver is required.

Recipient entry/help/status MUST disclose only currently authorized facts. Successful qualified source issuance resolves its end under Q14; commitment does not establish current recipient usability, proof or notification. Use accessible localized actual state/terms, qualified proof reuse, keyboard/focus/status and mobile/low-bandwidth recovery; no forced account/download, unnecessary reupload, insecure attachment fallback or automatic communication follows.

Durable source/security history MUST distinguish original expiry, fresh review/admission, scope/timing provenance, issuer, actual outcome and later correction/revocation from technical logs and Q05 participant history. Correlation and diagnosis MUST use minimal protected identifiers; credentials, file bodies and sensitive location/care content MUST NOT leak to logs, analytics, URLs or unsupported export.

Use bounded indexed source work, pagination, tenant fairness and the owner's existing read/audit budgets; P12 R6's logical bound MUST apply at its governed-read scope. Declare and prove actual capacity/latency/reconciliation limits before activation. No unbounded history scan, resident long timer, new global lock beyond owner-required fences or second scheduler/permission platform is warranted.

Adopt prospectively with explicit old/new producer/reader/worker compatibility, dark outward backfill, exact activation lineage and gates before issuance. Old data MUST NOT be promoted from guessed history. Restore/rollback MUST preserve current adverse facts and prevent old authority replay; an old binary unable to honor new semantics MUST NOT serve the affected path.

Trace Q13 selection, reviewed amendments and eventual ratification separately through the glossary/ADR and later authorized owner/OpenSpec/design/task/issue/test/release artifacts. Activation MUST require the proof groups below; adjacent tests, static docs and comparator screenshots MUST NOT be presented as native feature qualification.

Implementation MUST reuse Core's source, Auth/P12, custody, serving, outbox and shared UI boundaries. No new policy engine, general drive, duplicate-request workaround, custom retention scheme, pre-expiry extension, automatic notification or silent Q08 expansion may be inferred. Open product policies MUST be resolved explicitly rather than frozen as incidental implementation defaults.

Q14/Q15 settle unfinished duration and repetition; Q16–Q19 separately settle later completed reading. Fresh unfinished permission never automatically issues a fresh completed grant.

Story: US29-A13. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q13, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-654f8c8a6e4591d18282"></a>

## REQ29-P09 — Reuse exact communication, task and workflow effects

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"654f8c8a6e4591d18282","name":"REQ29-P09 — Reuse exact communication, task and workflow effects"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Each notice, reminder, task and attention cue retains its exact owning purpose, occurrence, audience, current utility, preference, suppression and semantic dedupe contract. Native request/review work remains usable without configurable Workflow. Workflow coordinates source-accepted results and never turns task creation, receipt, a generic checkbox or transport success into fulfillment. No new automatic email/bell/notification cohort is inferred from correction, event recording, a hold, a read or a source error. Preserve ratified defaults and tenant controls, including the Q60 same-recipient corrective-request automatic-reminder exclusion; ordinary staff reminders/notices remain subject to their own rules. Historical/imported facts remain dark for unrelated outward effects without separately reviewed bounded backfill.

Story: US29-P09. Source: proof.md. Decision basis: Founder §§3/10/16; Q20/Q24–Q27/Q30–Q32/Q35/Q40/Q48/Q51/Q52/Q60; Q64; Q67-C10; Phase 6/17 and Workflow owners.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->
