The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

The ordinary source shall own one optional external-email reminder purpose through exact qualified P17/P6 binding. Automatic and deliberate staff reminders do not introduce bell/SMS/campaign/deadline keys or borrow permission-request/financial contracts. Unfinished/overdue/unaccepted/scan/review-backlog states alone are insufficient; genuine Request changes may require recipient action despite existing files.

## REQ29-C05 — Author reusable request definitions without copying a case

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"662539c95bc9da7d65ab","name":"REQ29-C05 — Author reusable request definitions without copying a case"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

The ordinary source shall own optional reusable sets of item definitions and instructions for independent never-issued drafts. Start from scratch remains equally available. Template use, maintenance, draft preparation and issuance are distinct current powers; adjacent email/CMS/workflow templates confer none of them.

<a id="contract-66e42c1df98b2bcc8065"></a>

## REQ29-P16 — Qualify physical custody, recovery and provider exit

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"66e42c1df98b2bcc8065","name":"REQ29-P16 — Qualify physical custody, recovery and provider exit"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Choose the smallest adapter set that satisfies actual source profiles; provider choice does not create another access authority. Qualify access/revocation, conditional/range serving, integrity, immutable version/copy behavior, residency/egress, processors, concurrency, rate limits, credentials/keys, copy inventory, backups, restore and exit with exact environment/protocol evidence. Provider names such as private, signed, versioned or deleted do not prove the product guarantee. Database backups do not prove stored-object recovery. Order uses/holds/writers/promotions/restore against final destructive admission; finite permits and worker generations restrict new dispatch, while possibly escaped effects remain fenced until qualified reconciliation. Record actual physical observations even when later policy forbids another destructive submission.

Story: US29-P16. Source: proof.md. Decision basis: Founder §§5/14/15/19; Q61 R-CUSTODY/R-RESTORE; Q63-C06–C12; Q65 continuing use; Q66-C15; Q67-C11.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<a id="contract-6bdc6af23f2c4b2486cd"></a>

## REQ29-B02 — Source-owned request fulfillment

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"6bdc6af23f2c4b2486cd","name":"REQ29-B02 — Source-owned request fulfillment"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Complete actual rule and useful flexibility.** The ordinary native source SHALL fulfill an eligible active request when its complete current source rule is true, with no separate routine finish action. The rule SHALL include Q21 exact current staff acceptance for required ordinary file evidence, required technical admissibility and all genuine remaining source obligations. Preserve existing authorized non-file/waived/not-required outcomes and specialist final judgments. Staff may use only already-qualified requirement-changing powers for their purpose/scope, with current capability and relevant revision checks; disclose when a lawful change can complete the request. Do not add waiver, arbitrary hold, reopen, auto/manual mode, or a workflow builder. Missing/failed/filtered/paginated facts, unknown applicability, drafts, withdrawn/canceled states and absent definitions SHALL NOT produce vacuous completion. A genuinely complete empty or non-file requirement set may qualify only when the explicit source lifecycle/predicate permits it; no universal minimum file count.

**One owner, lawful authority and exact basis.** Only the owning source's qualified progression SHALL record fulfillment. Declare the permitted consequence of its meaningful commands and the authority for any deferred execution, including existing P12 NHI/delegation rules where applicable. Item review, task assignment, reading or grant administration SHALL NOT independently authorize arbitrary whole-request completion. Derive tenant/environment, actor/executor, occurrence time and audit attribution from trusted context; preserve human decisions and automatic source causation without inventing a human Complete click. Bind the exact request, source/policy/requirement revisions and relevant current evidence/condition outcomes. Same bytes, another request, another tenant or a provider event SHALL NOT transfer satisfaction. Tasks, caches, workflows and custody are consumers or fact providers, never alternate fulfillment writers.

**Coherent progression, concurrency and recovery.** Every qualified condition-changing path SHALL evaluate or durably arrange reevaluation of the affected request, including relevant requirement edits, admitted corrections, permitted alternatives and restriction changes. Prove both safety and progress for concurrent final updates: no premature fulfillment and no lost final transition. Use one source aggregate boundary or equivalent qualified version/fence protocol across real dependencies; preserve independent review and avoid tenant-wide locking. Coherently persist fulfillment, required durable history/follow-up and historical access eligibility; source facts and outbox intent sharing a database SHALL commit atomically. If completion is not yet established across a genuine recovery seam, report it unresolved and drive existing owned recovery without another business approval. Final checks apply after relevant waits. Match durable business effect identity, not only transport keys; replay preserves original outcome/attribution under current disclosure, changed intent conflicts, and duplicate events/keys/staff actions do not duplicate fulfillment/effects. Keep providers and expensive content processing outside locks.

**Optional work, intake races and terminal history.** Classify nonblocking optional work from the exact source rule, not its UI label; real aggregate evidence/safety dependencies still count. Truly nonblocking work SHALL NOT impose an undeclared completion wait. Already durable optional receipts and required history SHALL remain truthful under existing retention/disclosure; completion does not accept, delete, or falsely reject them. Required-item correction admitted first SHALL fence predecessor evidence before processing and be evaluated under Q03; closure first SHALL deny new ordinary admission. Upload initiation, transfer completion or local draft alone SHALL NOT promise receipt. Reconcile unknown admission before claiming loss or success. Cancellation/withdrawal SHALL remain their own outcomes, not fulfillment through a now-empty set. After fulfillment, preserve original history and follow existing source remediation for later changes; no silent unaccept, predecessor revival, new post-completion review power or reopening is added.

**Actual occurrence and existing access consequences.** Record the actual durable source fulfillment occurrence F and its exact basis. F SHALL NOT be browser/click/upload/scan time, a mutable task timestamp, observer time substituted for an earlier outcome, a guessed last-acceptance time or stale transaction-start time after a wait. Recover an earlier F only from its proved durable occurrence; if fulfillment actually occurs later, record the later occurrence. Independent current source work may finish after recipient access expires without reviving access. Apply Q06/Q07/Q10 and Q11–Q19: the ordinary completed interval is at most 720 elapsed hours from eligible F with applicable earlier caps and current restrictions, not a new period on replay. At or after the exclusive unfinished end, later F cannot create ordinary completed admission; real earlier eligible F may be recovered subject to its original end/current authority. Fulfillment, historical admission, current reading, credentials/readiness and notification remain distinct.

**Clear, accessible request experience.** Show source-authorized receipt, awaiting-staff, satisfied-item, remaining-source-work, unresolved completion and known completion outcomes accurately in the existing request context. Do not call timely recipient work overdue because staff review remains, or say an enclosing application/payment is approved. Explain that the request completes when its requirements are met and ordinary submissions/corrections then end; disclose the final meaningful action's consequence before execution. Use combined action wording only where the exact consequence is currently qualified; otherwise keep the meaningful item action with a truthful conditional explanation. Never treat a stale preview as a guarantee. After execution show actual item/request results and next permitted action/help; no false Done, infinite spinner or demand to repeat known acceptance. Preserve reading position, input/notes and focus on conflicts; announce statuses without unnecessary focus movement, use text as well as color, accessible content/controls, mobile reflow and supported localized absolute dates with timezone. No new offline private cache, account/name/address burden, mandatory essay/second reviewer/routine confirm, export, reminder or completion email is selected.

**Database, privacy and bounded operation.** Native design SHALL prove tenant/environment-aware primary/foreign relationships, exact evidence membership, non-null authoritative facts when fulfilled, valid lifecycle combinations, immutable occurrence/attribution for application roles and durable uniqueness of the source fulfillment effect. Preserve governed disposition rather than destructive cascades or indefinite retention. Use constraints for row/relationship invariants and the single qualified source mutation boundary for aggregate truth. Verify effective grants, RLS USING and WITH CHECK, views/functions/RPC/storage and privileged service paths, including updates that could move a permitted row into forbidden scope/state. Untrusted callers SHALL NOT set tenant/actor/accepted-by/completed-by/occurrence or bypass fields. Current reading and counts/errors/cache projections remain purpose-authorized; sensitive document content, locations and credentials stay out of ordinary events/logs. Bound indexed current-proof queries and recovery batches to the affected source, not full tenant/history scans or provider polling per request. Establish supported per-request/item/file/tenant volumes, concurrency and latency/recovery budgets against actual measurements before activation; no untested performance claim or new arbitrary product ceiling.

**Rollout, proof and publication.** Before activation qualify Q22-P01–P18, source capability/predicate/closure mappings and owned recovery. Use prospective compatible cohorts and a bounded activation/stop/roll-forward plan; old clients/workers cannot bypass the new rule, erase fulfillment or replay effects. Legacy task/accepted flags and imported snapshots SHALL NOT backfill fulfillment or historical access eligibility without exact owner-qualified evidence. Restore/rollback SHALL preserve source outcomes, effect identity and later restrictions; reconcile in suppressed-outbound mode before resuming authorized effects. Stop new progression only through the scoped operational control, never by pretending committed work was not completed. Trace this decision through source/P12/P9 and relevant GEN-06 bindings, design/tasks, implementation, tests and release evidence at their authorized publication stages; regenerate declared projections and read full incorporated contract sections. Q20 remains fresh-access notice only. No completion mail, global rule editor, new reviewer routing/bulk/negative taxonomy or unrelated source power follows.

Q60 correction before fulfillment removes only the affected current acceptance and creates actual source review work. Fulfillment-first remains historical/terminal; later findings change their actual current-reliance consequences, and new same-recipient corrective work has its own original issuance.

Story: US29-B02. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q22, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

## REQ29-E02 — Explain policy without duplicating routine work

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"6d940e03edeb04d94c76","name":"REQ29-E02 — Explain policy without duplicating routine work"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q62-C04 — Current trusted context and one authority.** Derive authenticated actor, Tenant, environment, source identity and authoritative timestamps/attribution from trusted context and current source/P12 decisions. Verify actual current membership, scope, capability, field and restriction policy rather than trusting mutable user metadata, stale role claims, supplied actor/tenant fields, a stored label or access to the screen. Preserve existing supported actor types without fabricating a Party or granting a new role. Policy definition, default administration, selection, issuance, history viewing and correction remain distinct permissions. UI, API, worker and retry use one authoritative business boundary; no competing client or provider policy authority.

**Q62-C05 — Clear ordinary UX.** Display approved policy name, safe covered purpose/evidence and actual start meaning in normal review as readable text. Show relevant limits or a concise summary of several classes without a false single deletion date; details are available progressively. Keep Access ends, Requested by and Review due separate. If a minimum truthful safe summary cannot be provided, the cohort is not qualified for this flow; do not reveal protected details or require file-body access to fill the gap. Only show Change when a real authorized alternative exists; selection-required cases use an explicit labeled choice. Preserve other inputs and focus through details, changes, validation and return. The existing Base UI/base-maia system SHALL support keyboard/AT, touch, reflow/zoom, long/translated names, RTL, actual source date/time semantics, IME and weak networks. No essential hover/color-only meaning, auto-submit on change/blur, auto-issue after repair or optimistic final success. GET/render/prefetch/details/menu inspection are inert with respect to issuance, admitted correction, send and disposal; supported deliberate draft saves retain their own admission. Existing command-origin and CSRF/request-integrity protections apply to the actual cookie-authenticated commands.

**Q62-C06 — Each consequential stage is admitted.** Earlier durable draft data, allocation, upload/staging or expensive processing needs its own independently admitted Q02 purpose and applicable policy before that act. A later issuance summary cannot retro-authorize bytes or private prose. Resolve current final request policy before new issuance; each later evidence use/copy is governed by its actual source class and qualified binding path. Preserve and recover only independently lawful preparation when a later act cannot proceed. Reuse valid source facts and sufficient permitted evidence without a repeated governance form, forced reupload or making every staff member a records expert.

Story: US29-E02. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q62-C04, Q62-C05, Q62-C06; REQ29-P01–P18.

## REQ29-A06 — Bounded read-only access after normal request fulfillment

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"73ade6baf9ddd36559b6","name":"REQ29-A06 — Bounded read-only access after normal request fulfillment"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Exact normal scope.** Apply the completed read-only mode only to normal source-confirmed fulfillment of the ordinary native request. Upload/scan/task/worker success, deadline, cancellation, rejection, withdrawal or security revocation cannot stand in for that outcome.

**Source completion and races.** Bind fulfillment to exact source evidence, relevant revision/member set and trusted attribution. Serialize completion versus ordinary admission at that source boundary; preserve Q03's early correction fence. Commit required source outcome/fence/history/effect intent durably and replay the same effect without renewed access. Source work may complete/reconcile under its own current authority after recipient access ends, without renewing that grant or exposing results through expired access.

**Historical completion and remediation.** Preserve the historical source outcome separately from later safety/eligibility change. Follow explicit owner-linked remediation/correction; never auto-reopen predecessor work or silently include new evidence/recipients under old authority.

**Business read-only boundary.** Reject ordinary new submission/correction and ungranted business mutations through every entry path. Permit only narrowly qualified read-support/help operations under current authority, including necessary technical preparation/audit; no blanket GET-only rule or supporting-operation bypass.

**Finite source access contract.** Before activating completed reads, require a resolved finite source policy/effective end and truthful presentation. Missing/null/unknown policy denies affected reads. Q06 selects no day count, anchor or renewal; completion/login/read/retry/help cannot incidentally extend or revive authority.

**Current scoped authority.** Revalidate exact principal/recipient revision, tenant/environment, source request/purpose/action and required assurance for every protected recipient operation. Independently admitted source/service operations use their own current authority. No membership fabrication, mailbox inference, staff-role union, public-completed fallback or transferable old grant.

**Current exact members only.** Preserve Q05's informational superseded history and Q04's exact current originals. Read only currently eligible own members within the qualified completed-request scope; no moving latest pointer, superseded bytes or implicit new-file audience expansion.

**Independent representation eligibility.** Completion and a future access date do not qualify every original/preview. Apply current safety, whole-original disclosure, custody integrity and rendition rules independently; no automatic fallback, substitute bytes or older-file revival. Authorized source workers keep their separate current authority.

**Expiry at the operation boundary.** Server time/source authority govern the applicable end. Deny new affected metadata/history/full/HEAD/range/conditional/recovery reads at or after it, regardless of delayed jobs/client clocks. Preserve inherited per-chunk expiry and feasible restriction termination without recall or grace claims.

**Session and re-entry.** Distinguish session/proof expiry from ended source access. Use Q01's qualified entry/proof reuse and safe same-source return; successful sign-in cannot renew/revive a grant or reopen submission. No extra account or per-view ceremony without source assurance need.

**Data and privilege integrity.** Protect scoped source/evidence/recipient references, valid state combinations, immutable source effect identity and trusted actor/time/expiry/clearance. Prove real-role grants/RLS/new-row behavior, views/functions/RPC/storage and privileged paths; generic status/expiry writes cannot create authority.

**Truthful outcomes and failure isolation.** Recover authoritative completion/admission after lost responses. Distinguish unknown source state, temporary outage, ended access, unavailable file and history-only failure. No default open/not_started/complete, false rejection or rollback of a committed outcome due to secondary failure.

**Ended access and owned help.** At source access end show only the permitted neutral end-state/help projection, without indefinite private receipt/history fallback. Assistance, resent links and new authentication grant no automatic renewal, old-file release or reopening; any fresh authority/remediation follows its source.

**Calm completed UX.** Use the same task context, truthful completion/current-next-step wording, clear applicable date/time/timezone, current eligible file actions and concise history. Remove ordinary mutation controls; avoid disabled-control walls, forced downloads, new archives, countdown noise or unselected emails.

**Accessibility and timing.** Reuse Core shared Base UI/base-maia semantics and prove keyboard/AT/speech/touch/mobile/zoom/RTL/focus/re-entry. Assess WCAG 2.2.1 mechanisms/exceptions for actual content-set limits; security wording/reauth alone is not proof. Preserve source authority in qualified timing/accommodation paths; do not misstate AAA guidance as AA.

**Privacy, caching and access evidence.** Protect completed metadata/bytes across routes, caches, error/conditional paths, back navigation and late responses. Apply current invalidation and existing tiered audit/metering. Keep private content/identities/URLs/tokens out of general telemetry; no offline archive or inferred human-read/acceptance event.

**Bounded serving and processing.** Use the inherited qualified bounded reads/renditions plus finite source page/payload/time/concurrency/egress budgets. No all-file prefetch, repeated regeneration, per-request scheduler or unbounded replay; slow clients and expiry release resources without weakening gates.

**Retention and source independence.** Completion/access end/help do not delete, extend retention, discharge holds or change other uses. Apply source disposition and adverse history before restore. Preserve official/specialist documents and worker authority without borrowing their grant durations or treating retention as access.

**Cohort rollout and alternate-path containment.** Qualify exact source completion writers, grant resolvers, old links, metadata/byte routes and read-support operations before activation. Reconcile legacy unknowns without inferring completion/expiry from status/age; test mixed versions, safe disable/roll-forward and restore with no broader fallback.

**Proof and trace.** Prove exact completion/races/replay, real-role current access, finite expiry, session return, all representation paths, browser/AT/mobile and source help for every declared cohort. Use Q07's actual source-fulfillment anchor and at-most720-elapsed-hour original completed profile; qualify actual source binding rather than inventing a runtime default. A completed-looking page or mocked test is not completion.

Story: US29-A06. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q06, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

## REQ29-B14 — Source-owned cancellation of unfinished ordinary requests

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"759d3bdfde2fea1b8e7f","name":"REQ29-B14 — Source-owned cancellation of unfinished ordinary requests"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Exact ordinary outcome.** The requesting source SHALL allow deliberate cancellation of an issued ordinary request eligible for cancellation and not durably fulfilled, because it was issued in error or its legitimate collection purpose ended. A difficult/missing/poor-quality document alone does not cancel a still-binding obligation. The outcome MUST be distinct from Fulfilled, rejected evidence, file deletion, archive, expired access, participant withdrawal or cancellation of another process. Earlier received/accepted items remain true; do not import Q33's unaccepted-item limit into the aggregate. Preserve specialist/application/finance/consent/care authority. No automatic expiry cancellation, bulk operation or draft-discard policy is added.

**Current authority and decision basis.** Require exact current source/P12 cancellation capability, validated same-scope target and relevant source/context revision. Derive tenant/environment, actor/executor, occurrence and audit attribution from trusted context; caller identifiers require relationship validation. Reviewer/assignee/creator, generic staff/admin role, file access, service credentials or grant administration alone are insufficient. Ending a purpose may justify termination without authorizing continued collection; cancellation authority must be evaluated as that action. Retain concise cause/basis from trusted source facts or needed concise staff input. Sufficient independent source facts may justify cancellation without file inspection; unavailable optional preview/download MUST NOT block that decision. No universal essay, new role/policy matrix or routine second reviewer is required.

**One authoritative terminal order.** Cancellation, Q22 fulfillment, relevant source authority/purpose/requirement changes, evidence admission/review and relevant Q30 text MUST share the owning aggregate boundary or an equivalent proved ordering protocol. Actual durable F first rejects ordinary cancellation; cancellation first prevents a later Q22 worker from fulfilling the canceled source. Final human work or queued/deferred reevaluation alone is not F. Stale affected human decisions must deliberately revalidate new relevant facts, not silently rebase/drop expected revision; unrelated activity need not conflict. Record actual source occurrence after relevant waits; no backdating from task, click, upload, browser or transaction start. No tenant-wide locks, provider calls or expensive document work under source locks.

**Immediate admission fence, bounded cleanup.** Committed parent cancellation MUST immediately deny new ordinary upload/finalization/correction/promotion-for-this-demand, acceptance/Request changes/Q33 mutation and canceled-context Q30 shared clarification from either participant or staff through every applicable route, worker, RPC and storage/source handoff. A previously minted upload intent or session is not authority to bypass the parent fence. Bound secondary task/index/recipient bookkeeping and recover from durable intent; cancellation safety cannot wait for a loop over children. Receipt/message/decision admitted first remains recorded; transfer, client progress or unknown finalization is not receipt. Reconcile ambiguous effects without asking for duplicate submissions or deleting shared bytes. Independently authorized safety processing and required lawful follow-through remain governed.

**Preserve history and independent obligations.** Cancel only this request's ordinary continuation and dependent work. Retire obsolete request-dependent review/collection concerns without erasing their history; preserve genuine independent source, incident, safety, retention, hold and other-use obligations. Do not rewrite accepted items as rejected, all items as No longer required, or the whole request as successfully fulfilled. Staff may handle already admitted work under independent current authority, including outward source/help follow-through only when separately qualified; they cannot bypass closure through a new ordinary Q30 reply or promise continued transcript visibility. This request's ordinary-content stop covers full/HEAD/range/conditional/rendition/recovery doors and current cache checks, with inherited per-chunk authorization/feasible restriction termination where qualified under Q01/Q04/Q06. An old signed URL or in-flight reader is not broader canceled-request authority; no recall of already delivered device copies is promised. Independent serving purposes require their own authority. Parent status, safe projections and staff history must agree without making task state a writer.

**Bounded canceled-status response.** As an explicit source/P12/Q01 amendment, cancellation SHALL narrow only each then-current independently valid finite unfinished participant admission (original or already-qualified fresh) to the exact request's Canceled outcome plus safe source help. Its bound MUST remain the same existing end and all earlier caps; no current admission at cancellation means no new allowance. Cancellation MUST NOT wait for optional terminal-status/proof readiness. If original eligibility is unresolved, deny protected status without recording uncertainty as proved absence; later recovery may use only durable evidence of the exact admission/recipient/end and restrictions actually valid at that original cancellation, subject to current restrictions, never a later or newly chosen root. Preserve the exact original recipient/root/basis and authority continuity, never an address match or recreated identifier. This retained outcome-only scope survives cancellation itself, not independent revocation, identity/recipient/source privacy restriction, uncertainty or expiry. Q01 proof reuse/re-establishment is permitted only through the qualified exact existing-root path within that bound, never a new canceled grant, renewal or automatic proof email. Without current proof/disclosure, return neutral unavailable/help with no existence leak. No file/preview/download, receipt detail, transcript, internal reason, other participant, new timer, normal Q06/Q10 completed view, or Q13/Q16 canceled renewal follows. Qualify this narrow source action and protected-entry continuation explicitly before activation; proof alone is never authority.

**Lasting mistake remedy, no reopening.** Before commit provide review/correction/abandonment of the intended action. After commit keep the request terminal and retain the original cancellation; make a lasting contextual remedy available in authorized source history/work after reload or staff handoff. Record the mistake with trusted attribution. For a continuing legitimate ordinary need, provide deliberate preparation and issuance of a separately reviewed new request only under independent normal original-issuance authority/readiness, Q25/Q01–Q19 and trace linkage; use another effective qualified domain remedy where appropriate. A draft-only path cannot count as a capable remedy for obtaining still-needed evidence. Permitted Q01 proof and Q28 exact evidence reuse remain, without copying grants, actor assertions or acceptance, and without needless reupload/reconfirmation solely for repair. Source identity/privacy/correct-recipient checks precede reuse. Do not reactivate the old link, redirect it automatically to a replacement, use Q33's unfinished-item inverse, or create/send a new request through retry. A supported terminal remedy must be demonstrated before the affected journey activates; no decorative contact or routine direct-SQL fix.

**Durable effect and existing communications.** Commit cancellation, mandatory source/business history and same-database follow-through intent coherently, using an equivalent qualified durable protocol only where needed. Preserve one semantic outcome with original material/actor/time across repeats, fresh transport keys, workers and restore; exact replay recovers original evidence/current permitted state, changed material conflicts, and repeated cancellation adds no redundant decision or new effects. Known cancellation survives failed projections/optional egress; unknown commit is neither success nor no effect. Existing queued communication uses its exact occurrence/policy/recipient and current source-purpose/action rules: stale participant demands may stop while independently lawful staff receipt awareness can remain. Do not blanket-stop every email, rewrite sealed pins, manufacture Q26/Q27/Q31/Q32 events, recall admitted provider effects or add any notice except the separately qualified Q35 exact cancellation purpose. Provider calls stay outside source locks.

**Simple accessible staff and participant UX.** Use existing Core base-maia/Base UI request surfaces. Present Cancel request as a deliberate action with safe exact request/recipient context, end-of-work consequence, preserved-history meaning and actual mistake remedy reviewable before final submission. One purposeful review/submit interaction suffices; no accidental menu-selection mutation, ambiguous Yes/Cancel pair, universal extra modal/approver, typed-name ritual, timed-only Undo or new reason-code designer. Keep Canceled distinct from Fulfilled/Expired/Archived/Accepted. Participant outcome-only copy may say the request was canceled and no more work is needed for this request; neutral unavailable/help applies when protected status is denied. Do not expose private reasons or imply no data was received/deleted. Provide persistent known/unknown results, suitable AT announcements, stable keyboard/focus, touch/reflow/RTL/IME/localization and low-bandwidth recovery. Keep lawful context/input on conflicts; clear forbidden content and discard delayed responses across user/tenant/request/purpose changes.

**Structural authorization and privacy.** Enforce same-tenant/environment source/request/recipient/decision/revision/evidence relationships, immutable original attribution/occurrence, permanent effect uniqueness and mutually exclusive authoritative terminal outcomes. Prevent reparenting, trusted-field forgery, incompatible pointer/status changes and destructive cascade loss with appropriate keys/constraints and owning commands. Cross-row current authority needs the source boundary, not an unsafe cross-row CHECK. Execute effective grants, RLS USING/WITH CHECK and combined policies, SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/UPSERT, views/RPC/functions/triggers and privileged service/job/import/admin paths; allowed old rows cannot become forbidden new states. Secure storage, counts/search/caches/export independently. Govern retention/redaction/anonymization/restore; append-only business evidence is not perpetual private prose. Keep Q34 internal rationale/history/diagnostics out of unauthorized egress; preserve independently qualified contacts, envelopes and protected-entry carriers under their contracts. No new money mutation/precision rule, public originals or offline private archive.

**Bounded operations and audit.** Maintain separate durable source/business history, security audit, technical diagnostics and provider evidence. Authorized operators must distinguish committed cancellation from pending secondary work, denied/stale command, unknown outcome and unresolved remedy; record original source basis and repair linkage without private bodies/secrets. Use bounded indexed aggregate proof/currentness and fair batches for history/cleanup/recovery; no all-tenant scan, complete-child materialization gate, per-poll business events or duplicate task ledger. Declare and measure finite workload, latency, lock/statement, queue and recovery budgets before activation. Required cancellation truth/safety cannot depend on optional notice delivery or an overloaded optional queue.

**Exact owner publication and prospective qualification.** Reconcile Q22/Q23/Q33 cancellation reservations, Q01/Q04/Q06–Q19, Q30 closure, P12/P9 source work and full incorporated Workflow/OpenSpec through canonical sources; explicitly publish the C06 limited outcome-only action and C07 remedy. Preserve specialist owner authority and regenerate declared projections rather than editing generated meaning independently. Add Document Request Cancellation as one concise glossary definition, preserving the prior22. Require P01–P26, actual operators and finite budgets before prospective activation. Compatible schema/readers/commands/recovery precede enablement; old clients/imports/workers cannot infer cancellation from missing files, expired links or task deletion. Scoped stops preserve known facts and lawful independent work. Rollback cannot discard new history or reopen canceled/fulfilled work; use roll-forward where necessary. Restore reapplies later restrictions and permanent identities before serving/dispatch. No new provider, platform or whole future Studio prerequisite.

Q35 separately owns its optional exact cancellation notice. Q49 different-person replacement and Q60 same-person corrective work retain distinct source powers; cancellation itself creates neither successor nor another notice occurrence.

Story: US29-B14. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q34, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-77011e8d2720fb7fe04c"></a>

## REQ29-C20 — Maintain shared views without publishing personal experiments

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"77011e8d2720fb7fe04c","name":"REQ29-C20 — Maintain shared views without publishing personal experiments"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

A shared view has one current accountable maintainer, initially its publisher, plus actual source-scoped administrative continuity. Creator attribution stays historical. Viewing confers no maintenance; current save/audience/transfer commands compare exact expected lifecycle/definition/audience basis.

Story: US29-C20. Source: organization-and-review-tools.md. Decision basis: Q43 paragraphs 6–7 and 9–10; REQ29-P03/P05/P06.

## REQ29-A08 — Source-authorized fresh completed-request access

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"7a5e8ddb4e5cc57f4e6f","name":"REQ29-A08 — Source-authorized fresh completed-request access"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Eligible ordinary expiry.** Fresh Completed Request Access SHALL require a proved lawful Q16 completed-view root admission from either the original unfinished or a qualified fresh-unfinished route that ended by its ordinary timebox and a current permitted request purpose. Unknown historical admission, security revocation, genuine binding shortening, purpose/content-use expiry, disposal or other source prohibitions SHALL NOT be bypassed by a new identifier.

**Issuing authority.** The issuer SHALL have the exact current source capability to cause issuance within P12's live administrative scope, assignable ceiling and applicable assurance/SoD. A staff role, help assignment, read permission or service credential SHALL NOT substitute. The compatible grant type and continuing grantor/delegation dependencies SHALL be explicitly mapped and enforced.

**Recipient and proof separation.** The source SHALL prove current eligibility to issue for the same intended recipient/purpose without requiring an unexpired old read grant or an open recipient browser. Every protected use SHALL still require current Q01/P4 proof, compatible native admission and source/P12 authorization. Contact/principal recovery SHALL follow its owner; this command SHALL NOT retarget through an arbitrary email field.

**Original scope ceiling.** Each fresh scope SHALL be the reviewed intersection of the original ordinary completed-view admission, current eligible own-submission members/actions/context and the new permitted scope. It SHALL NOT derive from access logs, unions of fresh grants or later-added files. A narrower individual grant SHALL NOT silently redefine the original scope or a global restriction.

**Preserve prior truth.** Prior grant states/ends, fulfillment, submission lineage and history SHALL remain preserved. Fresh issuance SHALL NOT reset Q07, reopen intake/correction/review, restore superseded content, grant resharing/membership or clear a source restriction. Qualified temporary representation repair remains a separate operation.

**Complete finite policy.** The source SHALL apply Q16–Q19: both lawful origins, fresh source-issued up-to720 elapsed hours under actual earlier caps, unused time governed by that same end, and individually reviewed sequential periods without overlap or preset lifetime/cumulative caps. There is no deferred first-entry activation or automatic renewal. Unknown authority or source binding SHALL deny issuance; qualify exact native mapping rather than inventing staff dates or defaults.

**Meaningful bound review.** Staff SHALL review source-authorized recipient identification, exact scope/actions, qualified timing terms and controlling limits, including any exact end already determinable, before a deliberate grant command. The server SHALL bind those material facts and revalidate them at commit. Material changes require refreshed review; ordinary time advancing under unchanged terms does not. Show the actual end when the selected source rule resolves it; immediate denial SHALL NOT wait for confirmation.

**Atomic current issuance.** The owning source/P12 mutation boundary SHALL serialize issuance against relevant source, recipient, restriction, issuer, disposition and grant-history changes. Required grant/effect/provenance/audit and authorized secondary-effect intent SHALL commit durably together or through an equivalent fail-closed recovery contract. No parallel grant-state writer or provider call under the source lock is permitted.

**Semantic idempotency and disclosure.** One stable deliberate issuance identity SHALL converge on one effect across clicks, retries and transport IDs. Bind its exact context and immutable reviewed payload; conflicting reuse SHALL fail without mutation rather than generate a new intent. Separately permitted deliberate repetitions MAY have distinct identities even for identical scope and SHALL atomically satisfy the adopted repeat/overlap policy. Recovery SHALL preserve the original effect/end while private replay output requires current disclosure authority.

**Independent communications.** Issuance, current access, recipient proof, notification and file delivery SHALL have separate outcomes. Failed/lost delivery SHALL NOT create a grant, change recipient or reset time. Communications SHALL use P17/P6 only when separately authorized; Q08 selects no automatic email/reminder.

**Data and role invariants.** The implementation SHALL constrain same-tenant/environment/source/admission/recipient/member relationships, semantic uniqueness, required finite values, legal states and trusted attribution. Actual grants/RLS old-and-new-row behavior, views/RPCs/definer/storage/service paths SHALL prevent forged scope, issuer, approval, end and owner transitions.

**Current private reads.** Every protected metadata/history/original/preview/full/HEAD/range/conditional/resume/recovery path SHALL require current exact new authority and representation eligibility. Q04/P12 expiry, private caching and feasible revocation termination remain binding; no old URL or provider capability SHALL be sufficient authority.

**Small explicit staff UX.** Use existing source context and shared Core controls for one meaningful review plus Grant read access. Show only review metadata the issuer may inspect; do not require original-content reading as a proxy for administration. No generic sharing defaults, mandatory essay, arbitrary recipient picker, repeated generic confirmation, bulk issuance or new portal is implied.

**Truthful command states.** Distinguish not submitted, pending, committed, not committed, outcome unknown and current blocked/ended access. Do not optimistically announce a grant or message. Closing a dispatched UI SHALL NOT imply cancellation; recovery SHALL reconcile the same operation before proposing a new decision.

**Accessible coherent journey.** Preserve shared Base UI/base-maia, semantics, keyboard/focus, touch, zoom/reflow, speech/AT, localization and actual deadline precision. Display material errors/status without repetition or private leakage. Reauthentication SHALL preserve safe context without unrequested download, broader enrollment or a private offline archive.

**Independent retention.** Fresh access SHALL NOT extend retention, release holds, reverse disposal, reconstruct expired-content authority, change independent source uses or promise recall of received copies. Retained bytes and newly generated renditions SHALL NOT supply recipient permission.

**Accountable recipient handoff.** Every enabled profile SHALL have a real source-qualified neutral help and entry/handoff route. Help, email threading, sign-in and resend SHALL NOT authorize issuance. No private details, approval promise or external attachment workaround is allowed where the current caller lacks disclosure authority.

**Bounded operation.** Issuance and review SHALL use scoped bounded queries/pages and the existing grant/worker mechanisms, with measured policy/repetition/egress budgets. No unbounded graph/history traversal, sweep-based correctness, N+1 provider review or global cross-tenant lock is justified by this choice.

**Durable minimal history.** Preserve attributable issuance intent/effect, original linkage, reviewed scope, policy/end, current authority changes and separate communication outcomes under owned audit rules. Diagnostics SHALL be minimized; sensitive free text, tokens, filenames/content and recipient addresses SHALL NOT spill into general telemetry.

**Prospective qualified rollout.** Admit only explicitly qualified source cohorts through compatible owner contracts and reader/writer versions. No grant backfill from generic tasks/emails/old URLs. Restore must reconcile adverse/current authority before serving; kill/roll-forward must preserve source history and deny wider fallback or reopened intake.

**Correction and revocation.** The source SHALL provide a currently authorized way to revoke or correct an erroneous fresh issuance, preserving the historical act and inherited active-delivery fences. Revocation SHALL NOT imply recall or deletion of evidence, and technical retry SHALL NOT undo it.

**Proof and ownership.** C01–C21 SHALL be traceable into canonical owner sources, derived OpenSpec, later authorized tasks/tickets and release proof. Activation requires real-role/concurrency/provider/browser/accessibility and handoff evidence. Residual monitoring SHALL have a named signal, threshold, owner and response; public benchmarks or mock tests SHALL NOT stand in for that proof.

Q16–Q19 replace the earlier fresh-read reservations with both lawful origins, fixed issuance anchoring, the up-to720-hour profile and individually reviewed nonoverlapping later periods. All actual adverse facts, original completed-scope ceilings and current checks remain.

Story: US29-A08. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q08, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

## REQ29-B07 — Opt-in staff email for source-actionable review

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"7b253dd5e4a8a7d6bd79","name":"REQ29-B07 — Opt-in staff email for source-actionable review"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Scope, default and occurrence.** The native source SHALL define the exact ordinary item/evidence/review basis that first requires actionable human review after first submission or current correction. Its optional staff-email system/default plan SHALL be Off; only authorized tenant publication may enable it. Source work and Q03 warnings remain independent. The notification occurrence SHALL be durable and distinguish genuinely new evidence basis from retries, task rendering, check/preview outages, same-basis readiness oscillation and responsibility-only changes. Technical events may wake a qualified source recheck but cannot own the decision. No per-file email, whole-request-ready gate, new submit-for-review ceremony, reviewer-online condition, personal claim or human-review SLA follows.

**Real flexible configuration and distinct powers.** Provide this event's usable bounded P17 tenant configuration, showing effective Off/On, inheritance, source-resolved staff audience and permitted message/locale/sender/reply choices. Enabling applies to new actionability occurrences, not old pending work or upload age. Use existing publication, synthetic impact/comparison and restore-as-draft. Review/source responsibility, plan/content administration, source visibility and notice/contactability authority SHALL remain separate; a reviewer need not become a plan administrator. Apply actual existing recipient preferences/consent/suppression without inventing a personal opt-in center or borrowing another purpose's preference key. No arbitrary To/CC, shared-mailbox identity, all-admin/member fallback, hidden global mute or participant-controlled staff recipients.

**Original policy admission and durable handoff.** The source actionability occurrence and tenant plan publication SHALL have a trustworthy authoritative logical order. Bind policy eligibility through P17's server-owned resolver to the effective plan at that original source boundary, with immutable sufficient provenance for exact recovery before any delayed execution. Do not accept a caller/producer-selected plan or use worker/browser time, upload time, task materialization or a refreshed configuration lookup to choose old eligibility. Co-located source/history/admission/handoff writes SHALL be coherent and atomic; providers remain outside locks. If optional proof cannot be established, preserve independently lawful receipt/work/review with a durable unresolved notice handoff and original-boundary provenance. Recover those original terms or expose blocked/unavailable; never borrow future On or manufacture zero. Failure to persist required source/handoff evidence must not be reported as a completed transition.

**Exact bounded staff recipients.** For an enabled original occurrence, bind the native source's then-admitted review-work recipient generation and complete finite concrete candidate basis, independently qualified for this notice purpose and contactability. A team route needs a declared/proved resolver; responsibility/team membership/task row alone grants no disclosure. Preserve original eligibility and generation evidence for unresolved recovery, with current narrowing before dispatch. Later joins, handoffs, restored access or expanded settings SHALL NOT append or replace recipients in old work. Continuing entitlement must be proved through the source's actual continuity rules, not assumed from an equal ID. For a proved disabled step, do not enumerate unused rosters/contacts merely to create a zero result. Proved zero, partial/unknown and over-limit resolution are distinct; no fallback, truncation or recipient list leak. Task projection need not materialize before source-qualified mail. Q01's request-participant no-Party authority MUST NOT become a staff-recipient fallback; qualify the actual staff branch without fabricated accounts or grants.

**Full-plan identity and replay.** Reuse P6's single bounded whole-plan compilation/release with permanent source-occurrence identity, exact original plan/binding/context and complete validated candidate set. All children or a proved zero result commit atomically before any child is claimable. Unknown or incomplete required resolution releases nobody. Replay SHALL recover the original admitted terms and outcome, not re-evaluate today's tenant/roster defaults. Changed bound material conflicts; legitimate new source occurrence uses new authority, not merely a fresh transport key. Item-plus-pending-status, same bytes/address or provider-key age are insufficient identity. A failed optional notice never deletes evidence, creates another task/review or changes acceptance/fulfillment.

**Explicit Off stop and current usefulness.** As a Q27-specific amendment to general future-only plan edits, published Tenant Off SHALL narrow this exact step's current eligibility, serialized with P6 submission admission through the qualified source/delivery boundary. Off-first suppresses affected unsubmitted work; admission-first preserves potentially in-flight/unknown evidence. Retain immutable plan/content pins and unrelated purposes. An intervening Off SHALL permanently invalidate the older admission for further new submission admission even after On; no old/zero/stopped occurrence is revived. Relevant evidence, source, privacy and recipient changes SHALL fence affected unstarted work. Re-prove source applicability, recipient/destination/contact, suppression, sender/connection/reply and current action at required preparation/submission/use boundaries. No stale review promise, readdressing, channel substitution or sealed-body rewrite. Use existing exact provider reconciliation/recovery; Off cannot promise recall or authorize a blind retry.

**Minimal protected content and material.** Show only an approved minimal staff-purpose projection, with the current authorized staff doorway. Subject, preview, body, sender/reply, labels, counts, samples and history SHALL not expose restricted request existence, care/location/third-party facts, filenames, raw notes, attachments or storage links outside their exact permission. Synthetic setup preview cannot load private live evidence merely because the actor manages templates. Do not copy Q26 participant credentials or require a new account; any protected action material must use its own qualified carrier and stay out of generic facts/editor/history/logs. Qualify the native optional email-only closed material class, finite source-utility ceiling, absolute non-extending purge due and explicit Recent-copy policy; no accidental 7/30-day inheritance or fake in-product sibling. Minimize body-free history and follow existing export/redaction/erasure/backup owners.

**Complete lean journeys.** Preserve the existing source/request/My Work experience, truthful current state and Q03 correction warning without replacing the evidence under a reviewer. Make the staff-email setting discoverable and understandable without forcing setup on every request. Explain effective Off, future-only On, independent work visibility, unavailable checks and scoped repair in plain language; do not blame participants for staff delay or imply delivery proves reading. No per-upload toggle, new bell, extra recipient action, compulsory duplicate entry, artificial personal claim or routine second approval. Use Core base-maia/Base UI and actual keyboard/AT/zoom/reflow/touch/mobile/low-bandwidth/localized/RTL/long-name and images-blocked email proof. Preserve authorized input/focus/context on recovery and discard stale cross-tenant/user/source results; no private offline cache or automatic download.

**Structural data and bounded operations.** Enforce stable same-tenant/environment source/basis/recipient-generation/plan/intent/attempt relationships, trusted actor/system attribution and times, valid states/nullability/defaults, immutable original admission and permanent uniqueness. Prove effective grants, RLS USING and WITH CHECK, trigger transformations, views/RPC/functions/storage and service/worker paths; an allowed update cannot retarget tenant, actor, basis, purpose, recipient or admitted policy. Use source-appropriate delete/redaction rules, not destructive cascades into required effect history or indefinite body retention. Bound indexed current proof, history pagination, exact finite recipient/step compilation, retry/recovery and tenant fairness under measured owner budgets. Over-limit rejects before partial release; known Off avoids unnecessary cohort work. No new money/precision fields, general notification schema or per-team mailbox machinery.

**Prospective adoption and proof.** Activate only after the exact native source/actionability/recurrence/policy-boundary/cohort contract, P12/P9/Workflow seams, P17 meaning/profile/default/binding/content/material and P6 release/dispatch/history/recovery are qualified with P01–P26. No whole future Studio completion is required. Preserve Q01–Q26 and reconcile Q03-C17/Q24 reservations plus C06's explicit owner exception into canonical sources and complete incorporated OpenSpec, then regenerate projections at authorized publication. Gate mixed-version writers, retain legacy unknown/omitted history without guessed On, prohibit historical catch-up through import/backfill/restore, and reconcile outward effects before resumption. Scoped stop/rollback preserves source work and valid later acts. Bind named operators and finite measured limits before activation.

Q60 correction on retained evidence creates no new Q27 email cohort. Genuine later eligible submission/Submission Correction and qualified staff-intake producers retain their existing exact notice contracts; ended occurrences never revive.

Story: US29-B07. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q27, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

## REQ29-E21 — Reconcile event recordings across producers

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"7c90506d35ae720cce12","name":"REQ29-E21 — Reconcile event recordings across producers"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q67-C07 — Coherent commit and bounded follow-through.** At one real source transaction or proved equivalent fence, coherently accept the occurrence/current interpretation, necessary original outcome, attributed history and current safety/control effects, with durable required follow-through. Establish any necessary restrictive current-impact fence before old permissive downstream actions can proceed. Background impact may be bounded and partial; it cannot postpone a safety-critical restriction or pretend every record was processed. Keep short database locks out of external IO; a sequence of SDK calls, an authenticated wrapper or a dispatch ledger alone does not prove this boundary. Preserve current duty resolution and capable recovery when dependencies fail.

**Q67-C08 — Business identity, concurrency and retry.** Distinguish the actual business occurrence from caller operation identity and delivery attempt. Scope operation keys to trusted context/principal/action and complete semantic intent; a changed-intent retry conflicts. Recover an admitted matching original under current disclosure before reevaluating it as new work. Cross-actor/manual/native/integration reports of the same source occurrence reconcile to that fact without a duplicate clock or falsely attributing another actor's recording to the caller. A real repeated event remains possible under source-defined identity; subject/type/date alone is not a universal dedupe key, and a contradictory date is not automatically a new occurrence. Preserve provenance and source-defined precedence/equivalence; no last-arrival-wins, human-always-wins or new-key bypass. Expired unverifiable intent cannot be silently reaccepted. New commands, including apparent no-ops, require current authority and the relevant expected source basis; stale change-away-and-back intent does not become current because values match. A true complete semantic no-op creates no occurrence revision, new recording time or impact. New corroboration or changed evidence can be material despite the same date, without becoming a new occurrence. Original committed acceptance remains the original operation result; any current correction/retraction is a separately qualified current-state projection, never a rewritten original receipt or revival.

Story: US29-E21. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q67-C07, Q67-C08; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

## REQ29-A17 — Fix fresh completed-read deadline at source issuance

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"7d2c10cf1da3d19c93d3","name":"REQ29-A17 — Fix fresh completed-read deadline at source issuance"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

For the standard Q08 profile across both Q16 origins, qualified fresh source issuance MUST durably resolve and preserve one finite read end with its calculation/limit provenance. The same end MUST bound unused access; no separate first-entry activation/reading clock is selected. The event MUST be the new Q08 admission, not original issuance/fulfillment, a click, preliminary approval, credential, delivery, visit or observation. Q17 selects no numeric duration, units or formula.

Reviewed material input MUST remain bound to one stable deliberate issuance intent, distinct from source-generated occurrence/end outputs. Recover a matching committed effect before any fresh calculation. Retries, new transport keys, resends, proof/session renewal, visits and representation repair MUST NOT reset the end. Conflicting reuse MUST NOT silently replace intent or terms. Missing/unreadable required timing MUST trigger safe reconciliation, not a guessed end, open-ended success or first-entry-pending state.

Preserve the admitted end, original completion/submission history and genuine later limitations with their actual scope. Fixed expiry MUST NOT override current restrictions; a member/action-only limit MUST NOT automatically end unrelated permitted work. The predecessor's ordinary ended period MUST NOT be reused as the new grant's cap, while real continuing overall/purpose limits remain binding. Temporary proof/representation failure MUST NOT alone become permanent shortening, and removal of a genuine shortening cause MUST NOT silently extend the admission. Retention/disposal and delivered copies remain separately owned.

Use the existing source/P12 mutation and current-use boundaries. Qualify trusted occurrence, current issuer/recipient/purpose/scope/limit checks after relevant waits, exclusive-end behavior, deadline-spanning operations and concurrent adverse changes. Required admission/effect/provenance/audit and authorized follow-up MUST commit coherently or use the existing fail-closed recovery contract. No timestamp, early check, worker lease or provider outcome alone proves this contract; no provider call belongs under the source lock. Historical replay MUST require current disclosure and MUST NOT imply current usability or a new grant.

Effective data constraints/grants/RLS and every direct/RPC/view/function/definer/service/job/storage path MUST preserve exact tenant/environment/source/recipient/member/effect binding, semantic uniqueness, finite valid terms, trusted attribution and immutable historical meaning. An allowed mutation MUST NOT retarget, widen or extend a grant. Protected metadata and byte-serving paths MUST enforce current scope/end and inherited cache/range/stream fences; expiry MUST NOT depend on a browser, punctual sweep or signed URL lifetime.

Reuse one Grant read access action and the existing completed context. Review stable terms/known limits and label estimates before issuance; show the actual admitted end and relevant scoped earlier limits after a qualified result. Distinguish issuance, current usability, proof, representation, notification and uncertainty. A known unsupported handoff MUST NOT be called ready; late discovery consumes the fixed period. Preserve accessible, localized and mobile/low-bandwidth recovery without new account, timer/mode selector, routine second confirmation, reupload, automatic message or promise of another grant.

Apply the profile prospectively through qualified owners and compatible readers/writers/jobs. Preserve original effects and later restrictions through policy changes, migration and restore; do not backfill timing from tasks/emails or recompute missing historical ends using current defaults. Use bounded indexed current proof, authorized pagination and existing operational budgets. Diagnose timing failures through protected source/business/audit evidence without leaking credentials, file contents or sensitive recipient context. No new timer/permission service or permanent browser archive is required.

Explicitly specialize Q08-C06's anchor/activation/unused model, Q08-C07/P17's end-resolution behavior and the corresponding Q16-C06/C08 display/model references: successful new issuance resolves the finite end; observation failure does not create deferred activation. Preserve Q08-C14/P14 and all other applicable gates. Q18 supplies the exact numeric/calculation profile and Q19 the repeat/nonoverlap/cumulative rule; exact native qualification MUST be proved before activation. Q07/Q14/Q15 values/rules, arbitrary dates, future scheduling, pauses, grace, automatic renewal or another completed stage MUST NOT be inferred. Reconcile current owners and declared projections at authorized publication while preserving dated history.

Q18 resolves the numerical profile and Q19 the sequential/nonoverlap profile. Preserve exact precision and original source result versus current permission.

Story: US29-A17. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q17, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

## REQ29-E17 — Apply policy changes to existing records and pause independently

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"8070149b0bc58ffbd9a0","name":"REQ29-E17 — Apply policy changes to existing records and pause independently"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q66-C10 — Deliberate existing-record application.** Provide a real tenant-authorized Apply to existing records path with exact source/class/use scope, relevant expected basis and current compatible impact. Explain safe eligible/blocked/unknown scope and consequences before commitment; previews are not authority and may not leak hidden records. Never treat missing/denied coverage as zero, silently expand a cohort, skip incompatible records as success or let a label-only comparison hide changed meaning. Bounded durable processing may report partial completion with exact per-target outcomes and capable reconciliation; no global all-record transaction is required. Explicitly reviewed eligible subsets are allowed when the source permits and unresolved mandatory obligations remain safely fenced.

**Q66-C11 — Corrective changes without erasure.** Preserve Q62's source-authorized Correct policy assignment and actual trigger/applicability correction. Wrong versions, defaults or cohort changes are repaired through current attributed successor/applicability/correction operations, not history deletion or blanket Undo. Equal displayed policy/date can still be a real approval, scope, trigger or duty correction. Show current permitted facts and actual expected outcome; a new correction does not reset old clocks, release holds, alter Q65 evidence or pretend physically lost material recovered. Prior usable definitions may seed a new qualified change; prior operational authority never revives merely by copying them.

**Q66-C12 — Pause, resume and urgent containment.** Authorized tenants can pause new automatic-disposal admissions in the affected scope independently of invalid or unsaved policy drafts, impact jobs or replacement readiness. Commit the actual Stop boundary and distinguish acknowledged pause from earlier possibly dispatched work still requiring reconciliation. Pause grants no indefinite retention exemption and cannot satisfy a due privacy obligation; route that conflict to the capable owner. Resume requires current policy, authority, coverage, unresolved-effect checks and a deliberate current act; stale workers/commands cannot resume or dispatch through an old generation. No instant provider recall or physical restoration is promised.

Story: US29-E17. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q66-C10, Q66-C11, Q66-C12; REQ29-P01–P18.

## REQ29-A15 — Sequential fresh unfinished-request access

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"837972dd82cb156cf591","name":"REQ29-A15 — Sequential fresh unfinished-request access"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

The standard lane MAY admit further separately reviewed fresh periods after eligible ordinary expiry. Every period MUST retain Q14's fixed up-to-720-hour source-issued terms. No preset lifetime grant-count or cumulative-day ceiling is added; genuine source-specific limits remain binding. No automatic renewal, pre-expiry extension, future reservation or mandatory cooldown follows. Finite periods MUST NOT be represented as a finite aggregate lifetime or a right to future approval.

First fresh admission MUST retain original lawful-admission/ordinary-expiry proof. Every later one MUST also prove its relevant preceding fresh outcome, actual ending cause and current source lineage; an ancient original expiry, technical failure or incomplete history MUST NOT suffice. Current original purpose and a legitimate need for recipient access MUST exist. Source completion, adverse endings and genuine limits MUST retain their meaning and scope, including coincident nominal ends and removed adverse causes.

The same tenant/request/intended-recipient/original-admission lineage MUST have no overlapping valid unfinished fresh admissions, including disjoint subsets. The owning source/P12 mutation boundary MUST atomically qualify predecessor/material review state, absence of overlapping authority and current predicates after relevant waits. A stale unadmitted intent MUST NOT silently advance to an intervening predecessor; material changes require current review. Ordinary time advance alone MUST NOT cause a second confirmation. Existing qualified fences or equivalent constrained transactions MUST cover no-row and distinct-intent races; a disabled button, per-key lock, lease or sweep alone does not suffice.

An already committed matching deliberate intent MUST recover its exact original effect and immutable outputs under current disclosure before its old predecessor is treated as a new-action conflict. Recovery MUST NOT return the latest grant as the original result or present expired historical success as current permission. Unknown commitment/lookup MUST be reconciled, not treated as no effect. Failed delivery or post-commit error MUST NOT erase an admission. Unadmitted old commands MUST NOT borrow later authority; source effect, required audit and durable follow-up retain coherent recovery.

Each newly reviewed scope MUST remain within original admitted purpose/requirements and current eligibility, with separate write/current-read/completed-read scope. Expired scopes MUST NOT be unioned; a prior narrow subset alone MUST NOT become an invented permanent global restriction. Binding restrictions and independent rights retain their actual scope. Q13/Q07 completion, qualified atomic admission/fulfillment and delayed recovery MUST remain unchanged; no completed-source reopening, gap backfill, acceptance reversal or Q08 expansion follows.

Use the existing request context and one meaningful Grant access to continue review/action. Show authorized prior outcome, current need/subset, actual admitted end and next actor, keeping operation result, current usability, proof and delivery distinct. Independent staff review MUST NOT require recipient regrant or unnecessary reupload. Qualify the shared accessible mobile/low-bandwidth journey and truthful recovery. No new counter/date editor, extra account, mandatory essay, routine second approval, renewal hub or automatic reminder is required.

Preserve trusted same-tenant/source/recipient/predecessor relationships, semantic uniqueness, immutable attribution/outcomes and effective old/new-row and privileged-path authorization through Q13's existing data boundary. Use bounded indexed current-state and paginated historical access; no full-lineage scan on every issue/read or second write authority. Required historical/replay evidence, source audit and later adverse facts MUST survive supported recovery/restore; retention/holds remain separately owned and MUST NOT reset on regrant. Missing proof MUST NOT default to empty/new lineage.

Adopt prospectively with explicit trace: specialize Q13-C02/P01 for subsequent expiry and resolve Q13-C09/C12/P11 plus Q14-C08/P16's repeat-policy reservation for this standard lane. Preserve the remaining Q13/Q14 controls, source-specific policies, compatible old/new behavior and native qualification gates. Update authorized source-owner text and declared projections together; keep dated records as provenance. No quota, cooldown, scheduled approval, new framework, retention rule, duplicate-request bypass or silent Q08 decision may be introduced as an implementation default.

Story: US29-A15. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q15, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

## REQ29-P14 — Provide durable business evidence and accountable operations

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"84689ab1f193f1a0f08c","name":"REQ29-P14 — Provide durable business evidence and accountable operations"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Separate durable business history, security/access audit and technical telemetry. Preserve permitted actor/source/operation/version/basis/outcome correlation and exact provider observations without copying private bodies, tokens, reasons or raw locators into general logs. A signed URL grant is not a completed download, transport completion is not human reading and a print dialog is not physical custody. Required incomplete/conflict/unknown/partial work has a currently capable source/operator owner, finite deadline, supported reconciliation and escalation. Use existing qualified work surfaces and alerts; do not create a new generic console or task per normal event. Holds and privacy conflicts require real source/records authority rather than a blanket longest/shortest rule.

Story: US29-P14. Source: proof.md. Decision basis: Founder §§11/14/15/19; Q61-C13–C16; Q63-C13/C17; Q66-C19; Q67-C16/C18.

Source commands atomically admit effect, history and result through validated current context. Same-scope publication/version/organization relationships, valid date-zone pairs, known-none versus unknown, immutable authorship and allowed transitions must hold through every effective database/Storage/service door.

It is not a personal alias, label, item/request title, global asset name or new identity. Naming is never required for receipt/discovery/review or another lawful source act; all specialist/public/resource names retain their owners.

Ordinary Q44 bulk download shall produce one bounded private immutable ZIP from a finite deliberate selection of exact eligible original-use/version bindings. Normalize exact duplicate selections, but do not merge equal bytes or names across distinct uses. There is no all-matching live view, implicit folder export or specialist-format substitution. Whole-command/source/export authority must pass before any permitted partial-member result; an unknown or denied whole gate cannot be evaded by shrinking the set.
