<a id="contract-8bdd308082e943ddeea8"></a>

## REQ29-E23 — Qualify external-event capture across private and recovery paths

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"8bdd308082e943ddeea8","name":"REQ29-E23 — Qualify external-event capture across private and recovery paths"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q67-C12 — Trusted Auth, scope and disclosure.** Derive actor/author/recorded time and trusted tenant/environment/source context from verified native identity and current P12/source authorization; permitted caller IDs are validated selectors. Check relevant current session/authority at consequential acts and after waits. A valid JWT, stale app metadata, user-editable metadata, UI role or demo profile does not prove current capability. Preserve qualified no-Party and NHI paths without inventing memberships; automation is not granted by this human route. Request-bound server clients and transaction-scoped database context must agree and reset on success/error/cancel/retry. The same decision controls reads, results, history, counts, exports, evidence, workers and recovery. Origin/CSRF protections apply to cookie-authenticated commands.

**Q67-C13 — Structural database and every-door safety.** Reuse the actual owning schema and enforce typed same-scope event/subject/evidence/current-interpretation/history/operation relationships, non-null required scope and null-complete composite references. Use tenant-aware referenced uniqueness/FKs or a proved equivalent where the actual placement requires it; arbitrary polymorphic IDs do not establish integrity. Enforce valid date-precision/state/revision combinations and required provenance. Original accepted facts/attribution are append-only for application actors; supported corrections change current interpretation through the owner. Prevent allowed updates from swapping tenant/subject/type, falsifying actor/approval, reopening stale state or clearing controls. Choose deliberate delete behavior so deleting an actor, policy or subject cannot cascade away required evidence/control history; lawful minimized disposition remains possible. Preserve the one P12/source decision layer and prescribed coarse Tenant RLS; missing, forged or mismatched application/database Tenant context fails closed. Qualify effective named/PUBLIC/default/inherited schema/table/column/sequence grants, column writes, policy composition, old `USING` and resulting `WITH CHECK`, SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/RETURNING/UPSERT/MERGE, trigger transformations, views/materializations, function owners/search paths/EXECUTE inheritance and overloads, service/BYPASS roles, REST/GraphQL/RPC, Storage, imports, workers and operator doors. No service key or function keyword is user authority. PostgreSQL's inherited predicate semantics apply; explicit-clause omission alone is not proof of a bypass. Zero affected rows is not accepted business success.

**Q67-C14 — Private evidence and projections.** Use existing source/P12 static classification and subtract-only projections for event names, source identities, dates, evidence/reasons, conflict details, impact, history and results. Recording permission does not grant unrestricted document access. Use only source-qualified evidence references; this feature does not add arbitrary URL fetching or private-body ingestion. Sanitize rendered input and allow only qualified link handling. Keep bodies, tokens, sensitive reasons, private URLs and locations out of technical telemetry. Prevent cross-tenant unions, public-cache fallback, cached authorization and late-response exposure after account/tenant/source changes. No persistent restricted offline command queue is introduced. Business history, source evidence, audit and technical logs have distinct lawful retention/minimization; append-only does not mean keep personal data forever. Search/export/backup/restore preserve the same restrictions and current controls.

**Q67-C15 — Accessible and resilient interaction.** Use shared Base UI/base-maia, specific visible labels and concise inline errors. Support keyboard/AT, visible unobscured focus, touch, 320-CSS-pixel ordinary reflow, zoom, Unicode/RTL/long names, localized unambiguous dates and low-bandwidth operation. Present source-required date precision/timezone clearly without unnecessary fields. Provide non-calendar entry for historical dates and announce meaningful recording/error/result status without focus theft or relying on color/hover. Passive render, GET, prefetch, blur, selection and IME composition do not submit; deliberate focused Enter/Space remains usable. On lost response recover the original operation; preserve safe input on validation/network errors, clear restricted data after loss of authority, and never blindly resubmit after sign-in or tenant switching. Closing the form after submission does not prove the command was canceled; preserve a safe route to the original result and reconcile a possibly accepted operation before new work. Canceling unsent input differs from correcting an accepted recording.

**Q67-C16 — Operations and finite scale.** Keep one owning source workflow and existing capable operator/source work surfaces. Required unresolved conflicts, partial impact, lost responses, missing capabilities and departed actors have authorized resolution/reassignment, with bounded durable processing and safe retry/reconciliation. Protect tenant fairness and source-qualified batch/lock/timeout/retry limits; use tenant/subject/operation indexes, bounded setwise authorization and stable paginated projections. Do not scan every file in a synchronous request or add a global queue/ledger when the qualified owner can supply the effect. Require measured finite input/evidence, impact/fanout, replay/control retention and recovery profiles before activation; no unmeasured vendor cap or “fast/scalable” claim. Preserve P12's logical/physical/restricted-tier placement distinctions and its governed-read R6 bound: at most one governance-row SELECT plus one ledger UPDATE logically, with the documented physical-shard carve-out. Restricted hard-deny budgets remain unstriped, conditional and enforced before egress; zero allowance denies and uniformity/safety takes precedence. This is not a cap on all mutation SQL. Current per-page/chunk projection is stable, unique and setwise.

**Q67-C17 — Migration, evolution and rollback.** Inventory existing authoritative facts and all readers/writers/consumers before enabling this route. Version event meaning/precision and source command schemas; labels and renamed fields do not define identity. Do not backfill occurrences from upload/edit time, task status, filenames, policy labels or missing rows. Qualify known legacy facts with provenance; unknown/conflicting legacy data remains explicit. Install compatible constraints/guards/readers before new writers; validate old-code/new-schema and new-code/old-schema behavior, unsupported versions and paused/retracted facts. Feature disable stops new capture while preserving safe reads, correction/reconciliation and required current restrictions. Rollback cannot delete accepted history, reopen old writers or revive historical effects; roll forward where the old runtime cannot interpret new accepted facts. Restored snapshots replay durable current controls before affected effects resume.

**Q67-C18 — Proof and traceability.** Require the positive/negative/boundary/authorization/concurrency/migration/accessibility and production-shaped proof below for the actual source profile, including one authentic staff journey and restricted-role denial. Analytical traces, static source reads and documentation checks are distinct from executable database, hosted/provider and observed-user proof. Trace Q67 clauses to its ADR, source-owned OpenSpec/design/task amendments and acceptance IDs during explicit publication, then actual ticket/implementation/test/release evidence; preserve source ownership and generated-view maintenance. No fake ticket ID, checked implementation task or legal/native qualification is supplied by this review. All mandatory gates remain gates, not monitoring substitutes.

Story: US29-E23. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q67-C12, Q67-C13, Q67-C14, Q67-C15, Q67-C16, Q67-C17, Q67-C18; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-8c5b70bb43ef05395ea6"></a>

## REQ29-E01 — Apply the actual approved ordinary policy

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"8c5b70bb43ef05395ea6","name":"REQ29-E01 — Apply the actual approved ordinary policy"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q62-C01 — Scope and chosen behavior.** For an eligible new ordinary Private Document Request, apply its actual approved source-purpose records policy automatically where that source supplies a qualified default, and show a clear authorized summary in the existing request review. The normal deliberate Issue action is sufficient; add no routine retention confirmation, second approver, reason essay or extra page. This governs ordinary request-evidence application only. Official/financial/Support/public/resource/import and other source policies retain their owners and independent qualification. No universal duration is selected. Q66 adds narrow tenant-controlled ordinary Document Records Policy authoring; routine issuance retains this simple flow and does not require editing a policy.

**Q62-C02 — Real source policy before application.** The responsible source/records/privacy owner SHALL supply the actual supported purpose/record classes, material applicability facts, approved effective policy/version and allowed tenant binding/variants, typed start event and date precision, preservation/privacy limits, hold authority, covered copies, successor/correction and disposition rules. Require only facts relevant to that source. Under Q66, currently authorized tenant source/records actors can create and revise supported ordinary business-policy definitions and values, not merely choose Asym presets. The source contract bounds capability meaning, actual authority and independent duties; it does not make all tenant business values code-owned. Filename, folder, Site, label, uploader role, browser locale, old template, digest, free text and a caller-provided policy reference do not establish it. A qualified policy may have a source-defined event that has not yet occurred; that is not missing policy. Missing or incompatible material policy blocks the affected new consequential act, not independently lawful unrelated work.

**Q62-C03 — Default, choice and uncertainty.** Distinguish a proved default, a source-defined deliberate-choice case, a missing/incompatible policy, and a temporarily unknown check result. Apply only a proved default. Offer alternatives only when the source has approved their compatibility and the current actor is authorized to choose. If that source requires individual determination or selection without a default, obtain it deliberately; A does not waive it. Never select first/longest/shortest/forever as a fallback, treat confirmation as policy approval, or turn unresolved applicability into a valid dropdown. A user may issue under a fixed default without acquiring policy-administration or alternative-selection power.

Story: US29-E01. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q62-C01, Q62-C02, Q62-C03; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-8cc8d114d8ab9e8264d2"></a>

## REQ29-B13 — Source-owned No longer required for ordinary requested items

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"8cc8d114d8ab9e8264d2","name":"REQ29-B13 — Source-owned No longer required for ordinary requested items"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Meaning and scope.** The ordinary requesting source SHALL support a deliberate No longer required decision for one exact outstanding, not-currently-accepted item in an eligible unfinished request when its demand was mistaken or no longer applies. It MUST preserve independent specialist/application/consent/financial/safety obligations and meaningful source judgments. Missing, inconvenient, unreadable or poor-quality evidence alone is not authority to remove a still-binding requirement. The action MUST NOT mean Accepted, deleted, hidden, canceled, waived everywhere or template edited. No additional glossary term is required; define this outcome under Document Request Item and the source decision contract.

**Actual source authority and basis.** The command MUST derive tenant/environment, actor/executor, source/item, current relevant definition and decision occurrence from trusted context and validated identifiers. Require actual current source/P12 requirement-change authority, independently of file reading, acceptance, Q24 responsibility, task assignment, broad admin status or grant administration. Reuse a qualified source command or implement the explicitly authorized ordinary-source capability; custody/UI cannot confer it. Retain the concise cause/basis from trusted context, with concise staff input only when required justification is otherwise absent. Preserve actual separation of duties without adding a routine second reviewer or permission/rule designer.

**Currentness and ordering.** Admission MUST bind the exact relevant source/requirement/evidence/context revision. Order removal and mistake correction against authority loss, requirement edits, durable participant/staff evidence admission/withdrawal, acceptance, Request changes, relevant Q30 clarification and Q22 fulfillment/closure. A stale affected decision MUST conflict with actionable current context, not auto-rebase or silently drop an expected revision. A newly relevant clarification fences the older affected human decision without a read/Reply/Resolve gate. Unrelated activity MUST NOT create needless conflicts. Use the owning aggregate boundary or equivalent proved fence; no tenant-wide lock or external call under it.

**Complete fulfillment and time.** Every qualified requirement decision MUST evaluate or durably arrange Q22 reevaluation using complete current authoritative facts and eligible lifecycle. Explicit lawful complete empty/non-file predicates remain possible; missing definitions, unknown applicability, filtered/paginated/failed reads, drafts or ended whole-request purpose cannot produce fulfillment by zero-count logic. Retain genuine final source judgments and independent aggregate safety dependencies, without a new routine Complete request action. Prove concurrent final changes cannot lose or prematurely create fulfillment. Record the actual durable occurrence after relevant waits, preserving original proved results and access eligibility; no click/scan/task/transaction-start approximation, backdating or new access clock.

**Evidence, intake and independent work.** A committed removal MUST stop the now-ended ordinary evidence demand/new submission or correction admission for that item under its current source. It MUST preserve already admitted receipts, exact versions, previous decisions and independent safety/review/retention/hold/use duties. Reconcile Q23 explicitly: end only unresolved review obligations that depended on this superseded demand, preserving their history; independently required concerns still bind and obsolete demand-specific concerns cannot remain hidden fulfillment blockers. Transfer alone is not receipt; uncertain finalization is reconciled before claiming received or lost. Do not require a needless preview/download or file-body access to decide applicability when the actual source basis is already sufficient. Do not equate item removal with whole-request closure, blanket access revocation or closed clarification; other eligible items and independently authorized Q30/history routes remain. Q06–Q19 access and Q26/Q27/Q31/Q32 occurrence meanings remain unchanged. Current source-purpose checks fence stale queued demands; no new email/bell/reminder or rewrite of old communication pins follows.

**Concrete mistake correction.** Before commit, staff MUST be able to review/correct/cancel their intended decision. After commit but before closure, the source SHALL provide a lasting contextual correction path from the item/history, usable after reload or by another currently authorized staff member, not only a transient toast. It records a new deliberate correction of the exact mistaken removal on the same original item/requirement lineage under current requirements, evidence, authority and lifecycle, retaining both acts. Reassess retained current evidence/Q03 lineage rather than restoring a pre-removal snapshot or introducing a different obligation. Make any renewed ordinary demand explicit; recheck applicable current recipient authority and permitted scope rather than reviving old grants, acceptance, Request changes text, deadlines, stale upload intents or delivery effects. A correction admitted first fences a pending stale fulfillment; closure admitted first denies this inverse. After closure, retain history and record the mistake through accountable source remediation. For a continuing legitimate ordinary need, offer a separately reviewed new request only through independently qualified original issuance, with a link to the prior mistake, current recipient/purpose/readiness and Q25/Q01–Q19 controls. Preserve independently permitted Q01 proof reuse and Q28 exact evidence reuse through their owners, without copying grants or evidence acceptance; no needless re-confirmation or reupload solely to repair state. Reuse another effective existing domain remedy where appropriate. No automatic new request, generic Reissue, status reset or decorative contact-only fallback. The affected journey MUST NOT activate without a proven capable remedy.

**Durability and replay.** Commit source decision/correction, mandatory durable history and same-database follow-through intent atomically, or qualify the equivalent durable protocol. Preserve original actor/time/material and one permanent semantic effect across double clicks, changed transport keys, replay windows, workers and restored data. Exact replay returns the original result under current disclosure; changed material conflicts. A replay of removal after its later correction MUST NOT remove again; repeating an already-removed current action cannot append redundant business decisions. Known removal survives failed projection/optional effect; unknown commit is not success or no-effect. Recover aggregate follow-through without a second business approval or participant resend. External work remains outside source locks.

**Simple accessible experience.** Use existing source request/item surfaces and Core's shared base-maia/Base UI design. Give the action an unambiguous name, exact safe item/context and current possible completion consequence before commitment; disclose the correction limitation when closure may follow. One deliberate submit suffices when review/correction is already possible; no unconditional modal, extra reviewer, mandatory long reason, timed Undo or per-file ritual. Show No longer required distinctly from Accepted, Changes requested, technical states and whole-request outcome. Keep a discoverable permitted historical item and direct attention to remaining work. Use persistent results plus appropriate AT announcements, stable focus, keyboard/touch/reflow/RTL/IME/localized dates and low-bandwidth recovery. Preserve lawful notes/reading context on conflicts; discard delayed results after user/tenant/request/purpose changes. Announce independently confirmed item success separately from unresolved fulfillment, notice or refresh.

**Structural database and authorization proof.** Use same-tenant/environment source/request/item/revision/evidence/decision relationships, required non-null immutable attribution and occurrence, valid state combinations and permanent effect uniqueness. FKs/constraints must prevent wrong-source links, forged current pointers, reparenting and accidental cascade loss; explicit command authority governs cross-row mutable facts rather than an unsafe cross-row CHECK. Verify effective grants, RLS SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/UPSERT with both USING and WITH CHECK behavior, all combined policies, trigger transforms, views/RPC/functions and service/job/import/admin paths. An allowed update MUST NOT become a forbidden new state. Omitted WITH CHECK is not by itself proof of a bypass; its effective semantics must be tested. Secure storage/read models/caches/search/counts independently. No money mutation, amount type or new money precision rule is introduced.

**Privacy and custody.** Preserve business decision/history under source retention with governed correction, redaction/anonymization and disposition; append-only business attribution does not require perpetual personal content. An internal reason is not automatically participant-visible. Expose only current purpose-authorized status, explanation, metadata and historical evidence; existing fulfilled-view rules and revoked/expired access remain. Q33 private rationale, history bodies, internal diagnostics and unauthorized filenames/recipient details/credentials MUST NOT enter routine logs/events, notices or URLs. Preserve independently qualified existing source contacts, recipient envelopes and protected-entry carriers under their own unchanged access/communication contracts; Q33 itself creates no mail or access purpose. Existing exports, backups, indexes and caches must reapply restrictions and lawful erasure on recovery. Removing a demand is not a byte deletion or removal of independent references/holds. No public sharing, cross-tenant copy or offline private cache is added.

**Bounded operations and evidence.** Use the existing source work/history/recovery owners. Record trusted business outcome/basis/supersession, currentness conflict and repair linkage separately from security audit and technical traces. Operators need safe diagnosis of committed item success versus pending Q22 progression. Index actual scoped lookups and bound history/proof/reevaluation/recovery without truncating authoritative sets. Declare and measure finite supported workload, latency, lock/statement and recovery budgets before activation; no invented universal item count or arbitrary time target. Avoid per-poll tasks/events, all-tenant scans, duplicate ledgers and indefinite repair loops. Source restrictions and unresolved repairs must be visible to authorized operators.

**Publication, rollout and proof.** Reconcile the exact ordinary source/Q21–Q23/Q28/Q30 meaning and P12/P9/Workflow integration through canonical owners and full incorporated OpenSpec. Do not weaken specialist owners or make a generated projection an authoring source. Qualify P01–P24, composed journeys, actual operators and finite budgets before prospective activation. Backfill/import must not interpret missing evidence or dismissed tasks as removal; old clients/workers cannot bypass currentness or overwrite new state. Add compatible readers/constraints/commands and recovery before activation; stop unsafe mutations while preserving lawful reads/history and independent work. Rollback cannot discard newly committed facts or reopen completed work; use roll-forward when downgrade is unsafe. Restore must reapply later corrections/restrictions and never replay stale effects. No new provider/platform or whole future Studio prerequisite.

Story: US29-B13. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q33, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"90785be425f5a58e3f0a","name":"REQ29-C18 — Share a reviewed view with named eligible staff"} -->

<a id="contract-97d0634313cc5b1fea9c"></a>

## REQ29-B11 — Default-on participant notices for staff clarification replies

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"97d0634313cc5b1fea9c","name":"REQ29-B11 — Default-on participant notices for staff clarification replies"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Meaning and deliberate choice.** The native source SHALL produce this optional notice only for one genuinely new authorized staff reply deliberately shared with its exact participant/item context. Private notes, drafts/autosave, participant questions, reads, work updates, evidence events and retries MUST NOT produce it. Eligible initial choice is On; explicit Off is supported. Missing/legacy/ambiguous intent MUST NOT be treated as On. Reopening a composer or changing eligibility MUST NOT erase a deliberate choice or silently restore On.

**Bounded tenant flexibility.** The exact qualified default plan SHALL enable one optional source request-recipient email step. Current authorized tenant administrators SHALL be able to disable/enable that step and manage only contract-permitted copy, locale, sender and reply settings through existing complete-plan publication. Show the source setting that determines Who and its authorized management route; do not expose arbitrary To/CC, raw-address overrides, fake team broadcasts or unsupported channels. Per-reply choice affects only this step. No new source mode or settings engine.

**Independent authority.** Source reply/write/share authority, safe recipient-contact visibility, recipient/contact management, plan administration, current recipient disclosure and delivery authority SHALL remain separate. Derive tenant/environment, actor and attribution from trusted context; resolve the participant and source purpose server-side. Current P12/source/consent/suppression/communication gates SHALL intersect at their actual boundaries. Source assignment, email match, another tenant membership, administrator label or service credential MUST NOT substitute for authority. Qualify Q01's closed recipient branch explicitly without a fake Party/account.

**Durable combined intent and honest recovery.** Required co-located native reply, attribution, explicit choice, safe history and durable handoff SHALL commit atomically; cross-owner delivery uses the existing recoverable boundary, never provider I/O inside source locks. Authoritatively order source admission with tenant publication and retain the resolver-issued original policy/binding/recipient basis; a delayed compiler MUST NOT select today's plan or guess an originally unknown authority. If minimum lawful durability cannot be established, do not acknowledge native success. A known material pre-admission mismatch SHALL preserve input and offer inline revalidation or deliberate lawful reply-only sharing. Where an exact original-boundary unresolved handoff is supported, unknown optional proof SHALL stay unresolved rather than become zero/Off. Once native success is known, no downstream failure may erase it; show separate notice outcome and resume the original operation without another reply.

**Permanent occurrence and complete release.** Bind one source reply occurrence, purpose, selected intent and exact recipient authority to one permanent communication occurrence. Before any P6 parent exists, prove exact top-level Live contract/binding/plan authority, even for zero children; Reserved/unknown/unavailable binding is not a P6 zero and later activation cannot catch up old replies. P6 SHALL atomically release the complete qualified bounded plan, including a proved zero result; it MUST NOT commit producer-owned child loops or partial enumeration. Duplicate replay returns the original business result under current disclosure rights. Conflicting immutable intent is rejected, not rekeyed. A released parent/zero is immutable: no appended omitted child, new parent token or separate Notify action on replay. Distinct legitimate equal-text replies remain distinct; never dedupe solely by text, item or status. Wakes, imports, backfills, plan changes and worker retries do not create new occurrences.

**Explicit Off and currentness amendment.** General plan edits remain future-only with immutable pins except this Q31-specific published Tenant Off narrowing. Order every affected provider-submission admission against current source/privacy/suppression and the durable Off history applicable since the original occurrence, including work not yet materialized from an unresolved handoff. The P6 admission is the sealed attempt/envelope fence committing Submission may have begun before first byte, not a queue/worker-claim label. Off-first denies admission; admission-first preserves the admitted call's possible effect. After Off wins, allow evidence reconciliation only, not another same-key send/new admission/replacement; later On cannot revive historical Off/zero/stopped work. New eligible replies after On may notify. Do not mutate pins or unrelated meanings.

**Honest useful action.** At preparation, provider admission and protected use, re-prove the governing source/recipient/action authority. Earlier native sharing does not prove present readability. Deny an inapplicable reply-available promise after expiry, closure, revocation, redaction/restriction, recipient replacement or other applicable source change. Do not substitute fresh access, newest contact/recipient, another request or completed-message history. A separately qualified useful help variant requires its own actual compatible contract; it is not an automatic fallback or a reason to rerender sealed material. Email failure does not change evidence, review, Q22 fulfillment or access clocks.

**Minimal content and lifecycle privacy.** Subject, preheader, body, text/HTML, tags, URLs, preview, logs, exports and errors SHALL obey the source fact wall. No reply body/excerpts, filenames, file bytes, private notes, sensitive item/request/person/location labels or attachments in this notice. Approved entry material belongs only in its exact protected carrier, never logs/history/Recent copy. Sender/relationship metadata is not automatically harmless. The native source owns clarification retention/redaction; P6 history stays body-free. Qualify a closed optional external prepared-material class and utility ceiling no later than action authority; freeze earliest purge deadline. Unknown Recent-copy classification is zero; restricted classes remain Off. Q01's no-Party recipient authority MUST NOT imply readable-copy reveal: use no_readable_copy unless its exact compatible current reveal contract is qualified. Do not fabricate a Party or block lawful native reply/email solely to obtain this optional support copy. Never restore purged material from provider/history/backups.

**Simple accessible composed UX.** Use the source composer and shared Base UI/base-maia controls. A proposed primary label is Share reply, with adjacent Email the request recipient about this reply, a safe destination summary and clear consequence; explain that the email links to the request and omits reply content. No routine modal, second composer, extra approval or hidden notification default. Explain unavailable email and offer a lawful next step. Preserve normal multiline/IME behavior, focus, explicit choice and permitted draft on recoverable failure; no automatic reconnect-send or unsafe revoked/cross-audience draft persistence. Announce important status without stealing focus or interrupting typing for every provider event; distinguish native receipt from not requested, blocked, queued, provider-accepted, delivered, failed and unknown mail. Do not infer human reading. Approved copy and actual Reply-To contract MUST agree that email replies do not enter this conversation; invent neither an unmonitored nor a monitored mailbox. Verify mobile, zoom/reflow, keyboard/AT, locale/RTL, international names, time zones, shared-device and low-bandwidth journeys.

**Structural authorization and bounded data.** Native design SHALL enforce same-tenant/environment/source/item/reply/recipient and communication relationships with typed keys, composite relationships or equivalent structural constraints, not an untyped address target. Required scope/actor/occurrence/choice fields are non-null with safe explicit state combinations and uniqueness. Authorized commands own transitions and immutable attribution/pins; historical business facts cannot be overwritten through generic CRUD. Actual grants, RLS USING and WITH CHECK, views/RPCs/functions, storage/cache and privileged paths SHALL preserve the same rights and prevent allowed-row-to-forbidden-state mutations. Delete/anonymize under owner retention without cascading away required evidence or retaining unnecessary private data. No money mutation or new monetary precision rule.

**Finite operation and recoverable evidence.** Use existing owner queues, repair surfaces and fairness limits; do not add a producer retry queue, a task per attempt or an unbounded recipient/history scan. Index actual tenant/source occurrence and eligible work paths; paginate history/configuration impacts and bound release/worker batches. Distinguish source business history, security audit, technical logs and provider evidence. Capture only safe identifiers, actor attribution, pinned choice/plan and normalized outcomes. Every critical gap has a named owner, signal, threshold and response. Define measured finite capacity, payload and latency budgets before activation; do not invent an unlimited-volume or instantaneous-mail guarantee.

**Qualification, rollout and traceability.** Activate prospectively only after exact native source/P12/P17/P6 key/generation/manifest/binding/default/publication/action/material qualification and the full P01–P24 pack. Default-On is not permission to activate a Reserved contract or backfill historic email. Mixed versions must reject ambiguous old writers without guessing On; mailbox/provider failure must preserve native work. Kill switches stop unsafe optional delivery without disabling lawful native replies. Restore/rollback reapplies stops, expiry and erasure before any dispatch/read; retain schema needed for new durable facts and roll forward unsafe downgrades. Reconcile actual owners and full incorporated OpenSpec bodies through their canonical generators; no tickets, implementation or full future Studio dependency is inferred.

**Exact inherited profiles.** These values apply only to the named source/representation and units, never as generic intake limits:

- Terminal material purge; P17 denies decryption at the terminal trigger and purges primary restricted ciphertext/keys/plaintext-capable caches within 24 hours. Immediate denial is not instantaneous physical deletion; backups and provider retention have separate owners. Acceptance can terminate retry material while body-free evidence remains.
- Recent readable copy; Existing per-contract ceiling is 0/7/30 days; effective retention is the shorter of tenant choice and contract ceiling. Unknown classification and restricted/high-risk classes are Off. This convenience copy is neither retry material nor native clarification history. No new Q31 entitlement is presumed.
- Performance/accessibility; No measured tenant volume or mail SLO is available. Require finite workload/budget records and production-shaped qualification. W3C 2.5.8 uses 24 by 24 CSS pixels or its defined spacing/exceptions; this is a floor, not proof the composed mobile control is good.

Story: US29-B11. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q31, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

## REQ29-P05 — Bind retries to complete business intent and recover original results

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"99a312b6f0ffb264780b","name":"REQ29-P05 — Bind retries to complete business intent and recover original results"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Every consequential command shall have a durable source-owned operation identity bound to trusted scope/principal, exact semantic inputs, target and relevant expected basis. Recover a matching committed original under current disclosure before treating it as a new command. A changed-intent retry conflicts. Distinguish a source business occurrence, accepted command and delivery/execution attempt. Different keys/callers/workflows do not duplicate the same semantic effect, while genuinely new source-qualified occurrences remain representable. Expected relevant revisions detect change-away-and-back; equality of displayed values, file digests or status is not complete intent equality. Original acceptance and currently applicable state remain separate. Bound replay evidence lawfully; unverifiable expired/stale intent is denied rather than accepted as new. A true complete no-op creates no false actor, time, revision, notification or clock reset.

Story: US29-P05. Source: proof.md. Decision basis: Founder §§10/14/16/19; Q03/Q08/Q13/Q16/Q21/Q41/Q49/Q52/Q60/Q63/Q66/Q67 semantic-operation clauses.

<a id="contract-a0ad6b23b018f8b34113"></a>

## REQ29-E07 — Order disposal against uses holds and writers

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"a0ad6b23b018f8b34113","name":"REQ29-E07 — Order disposal against uses holds and writers"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q63-C06 — Complete current obligations.** Before destructive admission, prove current source policy, required preservation/privacy handling, complete registered owner/version/use/hold/processor/copy coverage and closed/fenced writers for the actual target. Include still-valid signed grants, multipart/resumable uploads, processors and restore writes; credential expiry alone is not proof that admitted in-flight writes cannot later complete. Include live and retained source uses, exact delivery/recovery references, drafts/schedules/shares, upload finalization, rendition/assembly promotion and applicable backups/exports. Unknown adapter coverage, stale counts or an empty permitted-use view blocks the destructive conclusion and reaches its capable owner. Retention floor/hold release, withdrawn submission, completed/canceled request, expired access and an unlinked view are not sufficient by themselves. No operator override, automatic fallback or longer/shorter blanket winner may substitute for the source's real conflict decision.

**Q63-C07 — Final dispatch admission and durable evidence.** Qualify one authoritative source/custody dispatch-admission boundary for each exact target or admitted group. After waits, order the relevant current policy, stop, hold, reference and writer facts at this boundary and coherently persist the immutable attempt, target/basis, trusted actor/time, current fences and recoverable result/follow-through before external IO. A claim made earlier cannot skip this step. Admission must prevent new conflicting references, reuses, finalizations or promotions while the destructive outcome is pending. Every participating use/hold/reference/write/promotion/restore path must honor the same effective fence or proved equivalent ordering; a row lock or SKIP LOCKED candidate query alone proves neither complete coverage nor external exclusion. Use an actual short transaction or proved equivalent fence; multiple SDK calls are not one transaction. Do not keep a SQL transaction open across provider IO to pretend there is global atomicity. Only the exact admitted qualified attempt may dispatch within its finite qualified authorization/handoff validity and current worker generation. An expired or superseded permit/generation cannot authorize a fresh late call; it requires fresh current admission. That expiry never proves an earlier possible provider effect stopped or releases its protective fence.

**Q63-C08 — Honest irreversible boundary.** A conflicting hold/use/stop committed before final dispatch admission wins and prevents that admission. After that boundary, a request may already be capable of reaching the provider even if an acknowledgment or physical result is absent. Stop known not-yet-started/preventable remaining work where the qualified path can actually do so; treat affected possible attempts as may-have-started until reconciled. Do not promise a later hold always beats the network call or restores that copy. Preserve any still-protectable copies, record actual scope/timing/outcomes and route incident/source repair where required. If a source demands a stronger guarantee, that provider/path stays unqualified until it can prove it; this clause does not weaken the source rule or create a policy grace interval.

Story: US29-E07. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q63-C06, Q63-C07, Q63-C08; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-a0ae8c47c3a90f1f42a6"></a>

## REQ29-P11 — Deliver accessible, consistent and efficient task interfaces

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"a0ae8c47c3a90f1f42a6","name":"REQ29-P11 — Deliver accessible, consistent and efficient task interfaces"} -->

Use exact Base UI/base-maia, shared semantic tokens and shared component ownership. Reuse the qualified ReUI File Type, Table 13, Files/Drive Explorer and File Upload sources as directed, verifying actual source/license/compatibility and adapting to Mission Control's bounded layout. Never copy fixture permissions, simulated progress, fake storage meters or local-only completion. Embedded and narrow donor/missionary/recipient surfaces expose their task rather than a second staff drive. Keep known context, visible next actions and proportionate review; do not add universal uploads, reason essays, second approvals, preview clicks or confirmation dialogs. Preserve applicable source review requirements even when a document is not accessible through the default preview.

<a id="contract-a4458d2461017d5fa0b5"></a>

## REQ29-A16 — Fresh read recovery after either valid completed admission

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"a4458d2461017d5fa0b5","name":"REQ29-A16 — Fresh read recovery after either valid completed admission"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**/C03/C05–C22; applicable P01–P28.** Current issuance administration, recipient proof, read-only scope, coherent effects, all-path serving, rollout and qualification remain binding. C06 still requires a complete finite fresh timing/repeat policy before activation.

Q08 consideration MAY include eligible ordinary expiry of either the original ordinary completed admission or a completed admission actually established by lawful normal fulfillment under valid Q13/Q15 fresh unfinished authority. The source MUST prove the exact recipient/request lineage, causal admission, historical policy/scope/end and actual expiry. Generic completion, retained bytes, client origin flags, first-observed dates or what current policy would have allowed MUST NOT manufacture a historical admission.

Every new reviewed read scope MUST be bounded by the actual fulfillment-created completed admission's exact member/original-version/action/Q05-context ceiling, current eligibility and the approved subset. Original unfinished requirements, write rights, later files and unions of grants MUST NOT widen it. A later narrower Q08 subset alone MUST NOT redefine the ceiling; the narrow completed admission itself and genuine binding restrictions remain authoritative. The ceiling limits permission, not a frozen copy of current display facts.

Preserve original fulfillment, admitted ends, receipt/correction/acceptance facts and adverse history. Gap/no-admission cases MUST NOT acquire a first completed view retrospectively. Qualified recovery of a real timely admission MUST preserve its original facts, not reset time. Ordinary expiry MUST NOT hide revocation, binding shortening, purpose/content-use limits, disposal or coincident restrictions. An active grant with a proof/representation failure requires the appropriate existing recovery, not fabricated expiry.

Historical admission MUST NOT require prior visit, download or successful rendering. Deliberately admitted status/context-only scope MAY remain eligible only for those permitted actions; it MUST NOT become file access or a silent fallback success. Qualified representation repair MUST NOT add a member, substitute/add an authorized submitted-original version or grant a new read action; a new qualified rendition revision of the same admitted original remains allowed under its owner contract. Unknown root/expiry/current authority MUST close the affected action; independently proved eligible subsets or source work MUST NOT be blocked merely by an unrelated unavailable subview. Material scope reduction requires a truthful current review.

The existing Q08/source/P12 boundary MUST revalidate exact current issuer administration, recipient, purpose, reviewed scope, restrictions and qualified timing after relevant waits; recipient use requires current proof and every-door authorization. Bind stable intent to the exact historical admission and material terms, preserve coherent effect/audit/follow-up recovery and authorize private replay separately. Stale or changed root/scope input MUST NOT be repaired by widening, returning a different grant or creating a new intent automatically. Historical success MUST NOT imply present usability or notification.

Reuse the intended Grant read access action and completed-request experience for both origins. Show source-authorized scope, adopted finite terms, actual state/end when the chosen rule resolves it and a clear next action; administration MUST NOT require unauthorized file-body access. Preserve accessible proof/focus/status, localized and mobile/low-bandwidth recovery. No route picker, extra account, routine second confirmation, mandatory essay, reupload, intake/correction control, automatic message or new portal is required.

Preserve trusted same-scope root/member/effect relationships, immutable historical meaning and effective grants/RLS/privileged-path enforcement under Q08. Use bounded indexed baseline/member reads and authorized pagination; no full historical replay, inferred broad default or separate write authority. Prospective migration, mixed versions and restore MUST prove original eligibility and preserve later adverse facts; missing historic proof is not permission to backfill rights. Retention/holds/disposal and source completion MUST NOT reset on regrant.

Explicitly specialize Q08-C01/C04/P02/P05 and close only the Q13/Q14/Q15 reservation for this additional completed-admission origin. Apply Q17–Q19's fixed fresh source issuance, up-to720-elapsed-hour interval including unused access, and separately reviewed nonoverlapping sequential periods; exact authority qualification remains mandatory. Q07/Q14's thirty days and Q15's unfinished repetition policy MUST NOT become defaults. Missing policy MUST prevent issuance, not create a valid grant with terms invented later. Reconcile current owner text and declared projections at authorized integration; preserve dated history and applicable inherited proof. No new permission framework or other lane expansion follows.

The earlier timing/repetition reservations are resolved by Q17–Q19. Their exact current source-qualified terms apply to both lawful completed origins; no old grant is revived.

Story: US29-A16. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q16, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-a4d6671bc98e9a848cbd"></a>

## REQ29-E04 — Correct an assignment without erasing facts

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"a4d6671bc98e9a848cbd","name":"REQ29-E04 — Correct an assignment without erasing facts"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q62-C10 — Narrow mistaken-assignment correction.** Provide a real source/records-authorized **Correct policy assignment** route to correct an identified mistaken admitted assignment; ordinary issuance permission alone does not grant it. Target the exact current binding and relevant evidence scope with expected basis, truthful correction reason/basis and trusted actor/time. Show the permitted current assignment, proposed approved correction and its consequences before one deliberate Correct assignment action. Use trusted factual context or concise input genuinely required by the source; no blanket extra reason essay, second approver or blind correction. Preserve the original record and append the resulting current applicability and impact under the approved source rules. A wrong correction is itself corrected through the same current attributed path, never by deleting history. A source-proved trigger correction records its actual evidence and corrected current interpretation while preserving prior facts; it is not an arbitrary reset. Do not reset to edit time, clear independent holds, delete bytes, change recipient/purpose ownership, reopen/fulfill/cancel a request, reinstate acceptance or automatically issue replacement work. Where the required target policy or correction scope is unqualified, retain safe existing truth, prevent unsupported new/destructive effects and route to the capable owner; do not invent dates. Independently authorized urgent access/privacy containment need not wait for this correction. If required bytes are already gone, correction must not claim to restore them: use only independently lawful qualified recovery, and otherwise record irrecoverable loss and route actual incident/source remediation with truthful limits. The correction route and its rollback/restore semantics must be qualified before the affected cohort activates.

**Q62-C11 — Distinct classes, uses and clocks.** Preserve each admitted evidence original/version, selected assembly input/recipe/output, preview, request/history/audit, communication, export, staging/replay/provider/backup purpose and its actual obligations. Reused bytes do not merge permissions, provenance or retention; a new request does not erase the old use or reset its trigger. Receipt, Checking, acceptance, F, cancellation, withdrawal, removal from a view, access expiry, retention eligibility and verified disposition remain distinct. No download, policy inspection, default save, template copy or correction starts/restarts a period unless the actual source rule explicitly identifies that typed event. Show not-yet-started separately from unknown, and use the source's date precision/calendar/time-zone semantics rather than browser arithmetic.

**Q62-C12 — Holds, privacy limits and physical disposition.** Applying or changing a policy grants no read/share/publication right and releases no hold. Current complete registered use/hold/processor/copy coverage and actual owner disposition authority govern destructive actions; an empty visible list or expired period alone is insufficient. Resolve genuine preservation/privacy conflicts with the designated owner, not a universal longest/shortest rule. Fence disposition against new references, holds, finalizations and promotions, with current proof at destructive admission. Partial/unknown physical outcomes remain visible to qualified operators and retry the same intended operation; no metadata-only Deleted everywhere claim. Restoration applies current correction/revocation/disposal restrictions before serving and never revives expired access. Database backup alone is not byte backup.

Story: US29-E04. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q62-C10, Q62-C11, Q62-C12; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-a6ee4b973fd3c66b1171"></a>

## REQ29-C47 — Manage separate tenant, staff and recipient automatic-reminder controls

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"a6ee4b973fd3c66b1171","name":"REQ29-C47 — Manage separate tenant, staff and recipient automatic-reminder controls"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Eligible staff have actual Start, Pause, Resume, Use current tenant settings and Send reminder capabilities. Read/reviewer/task/rank is not that authority. Capture qualified defaults/revision in a plan. Use current settings creates a future revision preserving lifetime usage and every stop; active adoption starts a full waiting interval, paused/stopped adoption waits for permitted Resume. Superseded automatic occurrences stop without rewriting possible effects or unrelated manual work.

Story: US29-C47. Source: organization-and-review-tools.md. Decision basis: Q48 paragraphs 4–6; REQ29-P02/P05/P06/P09.

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"a9533e8d65b25db4d551","name":"REQ29-C24 — Seal exact originals with a readable integrity manifest"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Prepare privately and admit one immutable complete generation only after every included original, archive member and manifest fact passes current admission/integrity checks. Each normalized intended binding has one included/not-included outcome; each included binding has one distinct original member plus one contents manifest. Internal counts reconcile even when disclosure hides detail.

Do not invent a future commit timestamp or use click/comment/transaction-start/retry time.

The command binds trusted acting context/time, immutable target, expected current source/comment basis and durable semantic intent; current text/location/lifecycle, required history, affected review basis and result commit coherently.

Authorize existence, title, description, criteria/field values, maintainer, audience and history separately from matching records. Then authorize current source fields/rows before rank/count/facet/page/action. The publisher need not see all rows another qualified viewer sees; equal result sets and nonempty results are not prerequisites.

## REQ29-C32 — Withdraw and restore only the lawful current comment

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"b1786d77d333c3887972","name":"REQ29-C32 — Withdraw and restore only the lawful current comment"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Withdrawal removes active text/marker from normal presentation, retaining only currently lawful indicator/history. It is not physical deletion, evidence disposal or recall of past reading. Deliberate Restore of the exact current withdrawn revision uses the latest lawfully retained text/location as a new attributed revision; an Undo affordance may invoke this same durable command.

<a id="contract-b2a6a30f5d83b76f794f"></a>

## REQ29-E14 — Qualify partner reference usability and recovery

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"b2a6a30f5d83b76f794f","name":"REQ29-E14 — Qualify partner reference usability and recovery"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q65-C15 — Structural and database safety.** Enforce mandatory tenant/environment/source/target/use/generation/evidence/policy relationships with typed same-scope keys or proved equivalents, null-complete references, source-appropriate uniqueness, state/time constraints and indexed access. Platform-owned global capability or specialist-source definitions may remain global. Q66 ordinary tenant business-policy definitions and versions are tenant-owned while all assignments/effects stay scoped. Prevent general updates from reparenting uses, swapping immutable evidence, forging actors/policies or rewriting received/accepted/end history; reject unsafe partner/account/document cascades. Verify effective named/PUBLIC/default/inherited schema/table/column/sequence/EXECUTE grants, actual function owners/overloads/search paths, ENABLE/FORCE/BYPASS/service behavior, policies, trigger-adjusted rows, SELECT/RETURNING/INSERT/UPDATE old-and-resulting/DELETE/UPSERT/MERGE, views/materializations, REST/GraphQL/RPC/Storage and worker/import/legacy/operator paths. Preserve one source/P12 decision layer plus coarse Tenant RLS and coherent app/database context with pooled success/error/cancel/retry reset. Zero affected rows is not success; keywords alone are not security proof. No money, currency or posted financial facts change.

**Q65-C16 — Small, complete contextual UI.** Use existing source/request and Phase9 Files context with shared Base UI/base-maia components. Present Use for partner reference, exact current entries, deliberate Replace and End with concise permitted impact and truthful result. Explain that End stops this use while collection history remains. Show exact selected version/unit and sufficient safe provenance; never label a reference “legally valid,” “current agreement” or “partner approved” by inference. Reuse qualified held evidence without forced reupload or preview; no mandatory policy wizard, second approval queue, top-level agreement app or extra participant form. Display only current eligible actions and actually supported recovery; keep history and current operational entries distinguishable without duplicating a library. Optional bulk workflows are not newly selected by Q65.

**Q65-C17 — Accessibility, low bandwidth and failure UX.** Provide keyboard/touch equivalents, visible unobscured focus, meaningful status without focus theft, specific inline errors, preserved permitted input/selection, 320-CSS-pixel reflow, long/Unicode/RTL names and source-correct date/time-zone explanations. A changed list must not strand focus. Passive selection/blur/navigation/prefetch/IME confirmation is inert; deliberate click/tap and focused Enter/Space use the qualified command with existing origin/CSRF protection. Interrupted calls recover the exact original outcome before inviting another action; a lost response is not failure or success by guess. No persistent restricted offline queue, automatic download, public URL or external viewer fallback. Actual reading rendition accessibility remains separately qualified.

**Q65-C18 — Privacy across projections and purposes.** Apply the existing static classification and subtract-only source projection before discovery, option lists, counts, facets, metadata, names, provenance, history, results, thumbnails, full/HEAD/range/conditional reads, exports, logs and cache/late-response delivery. Target viewers receive only the reference-purpose projection; source reader rights do not automatically become target rights or vice versa. Do not inherit source-only display names, folders, internal comments or transcript merely by linking; obey their exact Q42/Q45/Q50 scopes. This internal reference creates no recipient invitation, Private Document Share, subscription, notice, renewal, public/donor exposure or reopened participant window. Source-required deletion/anonymization and minimal lawful history/control evidence remain distinct from a blanket permanent archive.

**Q65-C19 — Capable operations and evidence.** Record actual grantor/manager/system actor, immutable adopted basis, source target/purpose/policy, original result, successor/end links and current source consequences under their proper retention. Separate durable business history, protected security/access evidence and technical telemetry; do not log private agreement content, secrets or raw provider URLs. Missing bindings/policy, wrong-target incidents, unavailable bytes, source-correction reliance and ambiguous effects need a capable authorized source owner and supported reconciliation/containment path. A queue label or generic Contact admin is not recovery. No routine direct SQL/provider-console repair or unrequested notification purpose is introduced.

**Q65-C20 — Migration, rollout and restoration.** Inventory actual retained data, source/provenance/customer reliance, every reader/writer/job/cache and copy before the affected adoption. Existing accepted files, partner links, generic assets metadata or a matching name do not prove a historical ongoing use. Introduce compatible representations and guards before favorable writers; block incompatible direct/legacy paths. Activate only qualified target/source cohorts after actual policy, current all-door authority, use/disposition race, recovery, copy/restore and user proof. Disable new or affected favorable operations safely while preserving current denials, valid history and reconciliation. Rollback cannot bypass guards or erase unknown/end/replacement facts; restore adverse control history before serving or restarting, and prove object bytes separately from DB metadata. Roll forward when old readers/writers cannot preserve admitted state.

**Q65-C21 — Finite performance and proof.** Qualify bounded setwise source/permission checks, stable unique pagination with current per-page projection, use/evidence/copy fanout, payload/history/retention sizes, transaction/lock/retry and operational budgets under representative tenant skew. Preserve actual P12 R6 logical governance-read/ledger-write limits, written physical-shard carve-out and restricted unstriped pre-egress/uniformity requirements; do not copy example limits from assets, Tasks, Eve or Storage. Run the positive/negative/native concurrency/replay/migration/accessibility/production-shaped proof below. Code/source/model/blueprint checks are not hosted or real-user proof. Mandatory source/policy/native gates are not monitor-only risks.

Story: US29-E14. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q65-C15, Q65-C16, Q65-C17, Q65-C18, Q65-C19, Q65-C20, Q65-C21; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-b46437e412c206662e6f"></a>

## REQ29-E09 — Stop remaining work and repair without fictitious undo

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"b46437e412c206662e6f","name":"REQ29-E09 — Stop remaining work and repair without fictitious undo"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q63-C11 — Effective stop and safe reconsideration.** Provide a narrow actual source-authorized **Stop remaining work** action for the exact affected not-yet-irreversible scope, with a truthful source cause or concise necessary input and trusted attribution. It must establish a cause-owned intervention barrier that prevents rediscovery/re-admission of the same stopped targets through another scan, key, worker or manual route while unresolved; canceling one queue item is insufficient. This is operational containment, not a new legal hold, indefinite extension or authority to violate a required deadline. Use actual current source/records resolution and escalation. Distinguish reconciling possibly-started original attempts from reconsidering safely unstarted remaining targets. An authorized resolution may permit fresh current automatic authorization if the policy still allows; it never revives the stopped intent, drops independent holds or rekeys an uncertain prior effect. No universal Resume/Undo, arbitrary pause duration or new broad policy engine is created.

**Q63-C12 — Source-use end, copies and recoverability.** End only the source-use/record consequences the actual owner authorizes. A source use can end while bytes remain required by another use, class or hold; do not call that physical deletion. Originals, versions, selected assembly inputs/recipe/output, previews, request/history/audit, messages, staging, replay evidence, backups and external copies retain their own obligations. Show verified completion only for the stated target/copy scope. Policy correction or release-record correction is not restored bytes. For mistaken disposal, stop still-preventable work, preserve actual evidence and use only independently lawful qualified recovery; otherwise record actual loss and route capable incident/source remediation. Do not force recipient reupload, restore old access/acceptance or claim recovery from DB metadata alone. Restored bytes must meet current source admission, restrictions and adverse-history rules before serving.

**Q63-C13 — Required human cases and lawful timing.** Where the actual source requires human release, provide a finite reviewable proposal, sufficient permitted evidence and one deliberate qualified decision. Body-read permission is separate; no forced preview when safe metadata suffices and no blind approval when mandatory evidence is unavailable. Route genuine missing authority/evidence to a capable owner. Both automatic and manual paths need accountable coverage and source-compliant deadlines for holds/conflicts/required disposition/repair; an unserviceable queue or repeated retry does not authorize indefinite retention. Escalation resolves the actual source conflict, never chooses a blanket longest/shortest rule or approval by silence. No new reminder/email/approval hierarchy is selected; existing independently qualified P9/P6/P17 behavior retains its contract.

**Q63-C14 — Small truthful UX/UI.** Use the existing source Records/status context and a qualified existing source/P9 work view only for actual manual decisions or exceptions. Show safe policy/mode, exact affected scope, current known condition, next effective action and result history. Distinguish candidate/awaiting authorization, blocked/unknown eligibility, admitted or may-have-started work, stopped remaining scope, partial/unknown physical result and verified scoped completion. These are independent facts/views, not a mandatory giant status enum. Automatic routine work requires no per-file checkbox, routine modal, reason essay or new console. Stop/release/cause-resolution must be deliberate with understandable consequence, meaningful disabled states and current authority. Preserve lawful selection/focus and original-result recovery; no false optimistic Deleted or fictitious Undo. Base UI/base-maia controls must support keyboard/AT/touch,320 CSS pixel ordinary vertical reflow, zoom, RTL/long names, actual localized source dates and low-bandwidth status. Focused-button Enter/Space works; IME confirmation, select/blur, navigation or prefetch never initiates destruction, Stop or cause-resolution. Essential meaning is readable without hover/color alone. Keep keyboard focus visible and unobscured by authored sticky overlays; announce meaningful pending/partial/error/result changes to assistive technology without stealing focus or announcing every refresh. Existing command-origin/CSRF protections govern human cookie-authenticated commands.

Story: US29-E09. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q63-C11, Q63-C12, Q63-C13, Q63-C14; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-b6b457e3b1b516a8d112"></a>

## REQ29-D20 — Keep resources, references and conversations independently protected

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"b6b457e3b1b516a8d112","name":"REQ29-D20 — Keep resources, references and conversations independently protected"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Shared custody shall support exact independently admitted resource publications, partner-reference uses and conversation attachment occurrences through their source-owned identity, current audience/restrictions and records contracts. One byte object can serve several lawful uses without requiring a new physical copy or merging their authority. Publication/partner-reference admission and required copy/reference protection shall be coherent against disposition; a retained object or UI selection does not grant the new use.

Story: US29-D20. Source: custody-and-integration.md. Decision basis: Founder §§7,10–13; P26/P28; Q55/Q57–Q59/Q65 and the corresponding source volumes.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-b7f7ed07178820d770d6"></a>

## REQ29-D07 — Reuse the complete D32 read-only preview contract

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"b7f7ed07178820d770d6","name":"REQ29-D07 — Reuse the complete D32 read-only preview contract"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

The shared capability shall support the qualified D32 PNG/JPEG/WebP/GIF still representation, unencrypted static PDF, DOCX/PPTX static reading, XLSX saved-value view and literal TXT/CSV lanes without borrowing Support conversation authority. Animated GIF/APNG/WebP shows the first composited still with an animation-not-shown notice. Unlisted/legacy/macro-enabled/encrypted/archive/audio/video/SVG/HTML/ODF formats have no promised D32 preview. Every declared lane must qualify before claiming this capability complete; other source intake and public format profiles remain independent.

Story: US29-D07. Source: custody-and-integration.md. Decision basis: Founder §9; Q29/Q45/Q56/Q61-C06/P06; P26 D32-R01–R28/P01–P40/O01–O06 and its published source contract.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

## REQ29-C79 — Qualify small responsive matching and safe migration

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"b959fd122657ce3cab38","name":"REQ29-C79 — Qualify small responsive matching and safe migration"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Use bounded indexed compatible-held-fact lookup with set-wise current eligibility, stable unique continuation and per-page permission, through the source/API boundary. A rebuildable fingerprint projection is not authority. No browser whole-corpus download, candidate-byte fan-out, cross-use digest uniqueness, permission store or new matcher/provider/Realtime platform.
