<a id="contract-bae3d889167e799422f2"></a>

## REQ29-B10 — Source-owned item questions and replies

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"bae3d889167e799422f2","name":"REQ29-B10 — Source-owned item questions and replies"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Explicit scope and eligibility.** Admit optional text questions and replies for a currently confirmed intended participant within an eligible unfinished ordinary request/item, including before upload and explicitly source-qualified fresh access. Amend Q23's no-text-channel reservation only for this purpose. Accepted evidence SHALL NOT alone prohibit independently permitted clarification; actual source discussion restrictions remain. No new upload, complete account, prior staff invitation, tenant Off/replies-only menu, source policy designer or universal Compare/comment permission is required.

**Audience, authority and context.** Derive tenant/environment/actor/author/occurrence and current admission from trusted context; caller IDs are validated selectors. Prove exact source/item/recipient/purpose authority for send/read/recovery and relevant metadata, counts, pagination and projections. Reply-to references must be participant-safe and in the same permitted exchange. Bind a relevant exact version/instruction/review basis when one exists; a pre-upload item question needs no invented file/version. Do not union role assignments or infer rights from email, team/folder membership, link possession, file access or prior authorship.

**Shared text, stable meaning and correction.** Participant-visible clarification is distinct from internal review/task notes. Staff outward replies require a new deliberate content/audience-authorized act; no flag change, draft transfer or automatic publication of internal history. Expose only enough currently permitted shared text for the exchange. Sent identity, author/time, audience, intended item/basis and accepted meaning cannot be silently retargeted or overwritten by a new file, rename or ordinary PATCH. A currently permitted follow-up can correct an earlier statement; no ordinary edit/delete/unsend promise. Existing authorized restriction/redaction/disposition removes protected text as required, preserving only lawful minimal evidence and durable effect identity, without resurrecting text through caches, retries or backup restore.

**Source decisions and relevant new information.** A message SHALL NOT automatically change evidence-set identity, submit/correct a file, issue Request changes, waive or reinterpret instructions, accept/unaccept, reopen or fulfill. Order newly admitted relevant clarification with affected still-uncommitted human decisions using the existing source currentness protocol or equivalent. An older prepared decision cannot silently omit new context or auto-rebase; refresh/revalidation may let the same meaningful action proceed without a forced reply, resolved/read checkbox or repeated inspection of unaffected evidence. No semantic AI classifier or new schema counter is mandated. Scope to affected items and genuine dependencies, not all tenant chatter. Already committed human decisions remain historical truth. Later text during deferred Q22 completion is owned follow-through, not a new final gate; actual source-defined changes still alter the real predicate.

**Accountable work and receipt.** Before acknowledging Received, durably bind the exact message and required history to one existing source-work route with an authorized person/team or qualified protected recovery path. Responsibility/contact/read/respond authority remain separate. Reuse qualified defaults/handoffs; no all-admin/creator fallback or task per message. Current assignment loss or projection failure cannot drop admitted text; a durable obligation must converge to visible authorized work, even with external email off. Do not infer human reading, staff availability, response SLA, answer or requirement resolution from receipt, task closure or a view event. Missing/unknown route affects only dependent admission/handling and has capable recovery, not a global upload/completion gate.

**Atomic send and semantic replay.** Co-located message, trusted attribution, mandatory history/current context and required durable handoff/outbox commit coherently and atomically. External calls and expensive work remain outside locks. Preserve a durable identity for each deliberate send and its material body/item/purpose/audience; same intent recovers one effect, changed material conflicts, genuinely new equal text is not silently collapsed. A transport key, in-memory flag or attempt lease is not permanent effect identity. Reconcile ambiguous results before an accidental second send; known success survives secondary failure. Recover outcomes under current disclosure without redoing the act or using a fresh grant to execute an old unadmitted intent.

**Finite access, lifecycle and closure.** Qualify send/read/recovery against actual current scope/time after relevant waits, including ordinary/fresh periods, recipient change, revocation, source restriction, cancellation and fulfillment. Browser timestamps, loaded pages and pre-wait transaction time do not prove timely admission. Closure/expiry/revocation first denies new admission; send first retains the durable message and staff handling under independent authority. No activity grace, access reset, completed write or implicit completed-message archive. A late staff response cannot claim participant availability; preserve valid staff work and use an existing independently authorized help/communication route where needed. Fresh same-recipient shared context requires explicit current disclosure; old drafts never auto-send.

**Simple accessible interaction and honest recovery.** Use existing shared Base UI/base-maia source controls with an obvious item and audience, short labeled multiline field, deliberate Send and persistent outcome. Enter/IME composition must not accidentally send. Keep staff internal and outward composers/drafts unmistakable and separate; no arbitrary To/CC/directory or participant privacy settings. Preserve permitted draft/text/position/focus on validation or transient failures, distinguish unreceived/unknown/received and offer actual retry/reauth/help. Warn before deliberately discarding unsent permitted text, without a warning for an empty composer or routine Send. Do not promise instant staff response, show a known success as failed or auto-send after reconnect. Qualify 320-CSS-pixel reflow, text zoom, touch, keyboard/AT, non-Latin/RTL/localized names/times and slow networks; no forced third pane, mandatory essay, routine send confirmation or extra identity form. Apply real timing rules without extending source access by implication.

**Database and privileged-path invariants.** Use the smallest native source model with non-null trusted identities and tenant-aware request/item/recipient/reply relationships; version reference may be absent for a genuine pre-upload question, never a wildcard grant. Enforce valid content/state, immutable sent attribution/context, durable send uniqueness and source order. Prove actual grants, SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/UPSERT, effective USING and resulting-row WITH CHECK (including implicit checks), policy combinations, triggers, views/RPC/functions, service/worker/import and applicable storage routes. A permitted old/new tenant row cannot be transformed into another actor/item/audience or internal-to-shared content. Privileged execution is not source authority; mutable cross-row permission needs the qualified source boundary, not an unsafe CHECK or stale JWT alone. Prevent accidental cascades across independent uses/history; no money/financial precision change.

**Content, privacy and finite capacity.** Treat text as untrusted data, not markup/instructions, proof or policy. Use selected text-only rendering and safe supported links; no auto-fetch/unfurl, remote images, embedded content, attachments, external viewer or provider URL escape. Source links retain current target authorization. Keep bodies, drafts, sensitive names/locations, credentials and excerpts out of ordinary telemetry/URLs/queues; protected business history has its own policy. Before activation declare measured finite body/Unicode/byte, pagination/query, concurrency/rate, fairness, projection/recovery and latency budgets with units/boundaries. No copied vendor 500-character rule, arbitrary message-attempt cap, invented parser-specific policy or UI-only bound. Bound abuse without rejecting evidence, hiding prior success or inventing source expiry.

**Communication and ownership reuse.** Q30 creates a native source interaction and visible source work, not an automatic Support conversation, public task thread, CRM note, global inbox or new notification event. Q20/Q26/Q27 keep their exact occurrences and policies; none covers clarification by analogy. No email/SMS/bell default, read/typing receipt, reminder/escalation, email-reply ingestion or provider selection is added. Any separately authorized outward communication uses its actual P17/P6 source purpose/content/audience/delivery contract; native receipt does not depend on its delivery. Do not quietly create a new staffing/routing/configuration platform or whole Support/Workflow Studio prerequisite.

**Prospective qualification and trace.** Reconcile the selected source/P12 recipient operation, Q23 reservation, Q21/Q23 information-currentness, Q22 progression and P9 handling explicitly. Existing grants acquire no action merely from old upload/read capability; prospective source qualification must authorize any extension without resetting their clocks or requiring a new period by default. Legacy Support mail/notes/tasks cannot be relabeled as native clarification or accepted facts. Mixed clients/workers/schema, stop/rollback/restore must preserve received text, later restrictions/redactions, source outcomes and durable identity. Preserve the adopted Document Request Clarification meaning and every other current domain definition. Trace C01–C12/P01–P24 through complete incorporated owners, later authorized formal source/OpenSpec publication, design/tasks/tickets and actual native release evidence.

Q31 separately owns optional participant email from a genuinely new staff-shared reply; Q32 separately owns optional staff email from new participant clarification. Native text supplies no other mail, attachment, read-receipt or chat authority.

**Exact inherited profiles.** These values apply only to the named source/representation and units, never as generic intake limits:

- Original unfinished access; Q11/Q12 nominal end is original source issuance plus 1,440 elapsed hours (60 × 24 hours; 5,184,000 seconds), subject to actual earlier applicable restrictions. A question creates no new clock or minimum entitlement.
- Fresh unfinished access; Q13/Q14 standard nominal end is fresh source issuance plus 720 elapsed hours (30 × 24 hours). Only actual currently admitted clarification scope applies; replay cannot borrow new access.
- Accessibility fixtures; Qualify applicable 320 CSS px reflow and 200% text zoom, real keyboard/AT/IME/RTL and existing slow-network fixtures. Do not import accessibility timeout guidance as a twenty-hour data-retention/access policy.

Story: US29-B10. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q30, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-bf2e899d39ed246323e2"></a>

## REQ29-D05 — Bound hostile content and preserve truthful safety states

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"bf2e899d39ed246323e2","name":"REQ29-D05 — Bound hostile content and preserve truthful safety states"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Private intake shall validate actual content using the exact versioned source profile and bound encoded/expanded bytes, entries, nesting, XML behavior, pages/slides/sheets/cells, image pixels/frames, decoded memory, scratch, CPU, wall time, retries and concurrent work. Infected, suspicious, encrypted, unscannable, unsupported, malformed, timed-out and scanner-unavailable outcomes shall be distinguishable where disclosure permits. No generic override may expose known-malicious content or turn unknown into clean.

Story: US29-D05. Source: custody-and-integration.md. Decision basis: Founder §9; Q53/Q56/Q61-P06; P22/P23 hygiene and P26 D32.

Selection/receipt/scan/filename/thumbnail/OCR/AI/preview/dwell/download telemetry is not review. ## REQ29-E06 — Admit only expressly authorized automatic disposal

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"ca97fb09d936f3547ba6","name":"REQ29-E06 — Admit only expressly authorized automatic disposal"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q63-C01 — Bounded adoption.** Allow expressly policy-authorized automatic disposal of an eligible ordinary retained-record cohort without a new routine staff release for each already-authorized occurrence. The source policy must actually permit that mode and the complete path must be qualified. Keep all source-required human decisions and exact owner controls. Preserve P23/D27 explicit review/no-auto-purge, other specialist owners and independently qualified transient ZIP/preview/staging cleanup. No blanket age-based purge, Empty Trash, generalized drive delete, legal period, new consent duty or new glossary term is selected.

**Q63-C02 — Policy authority, flexibility and activation.** The responsible source/records/privacy owner supplies approved purpose/family, material applicability, version/effectivity, actual automatic/manual mode, typed event/date precision, preservation/privacy bounds, holds/conflicts, covered records/copies and correction/disposition/recovery obligations. Q66 permits currently authorized tenant actors to author and revise supported ordinary policy definitions, business values and disposition mode through its qualified lifecycle. Actual source semantics, independent obligations and current approval remain the boundary; choices are not limited to Asym-authored presets. Enabling or changing automation requires the actual source-authorized configuration/impact path with understandable affected scope; it cannot silently sweep existing or unmapped legacy records into disposal. Q62's historical assignments/current successors remain authoritative. A missing policy, unknown mode, shorter period, source closure or UI default is not permission. Policy approval, activation, execution and current exceptions are distinct facts. No arbitrary tenant DSL, free-form timer or universal grace is added.

**Q63-C03 — Discovery is inert; exact authority is real.** Candidate discovery, a preview count, a work claim and a worker lease do not authorize destruction. Discover only bounded permitted candidates; missing/stale source coverage is not proof of no use. Establish actual source authorization for each bounded operation under the approved policy or required human decision. Record the real policy/version/basis and execution mode; automatic authority is not fabricated fresh human approval. Details, GET/render/prefetch, filters and candidate review do not dispatch deletion. A required manual decision remains deliberate over sufficient permitted evidence; no timeout auto-approval or new routine approval ceremony is inferred.

**Q63-C04 — Finite exact targets and groups.** Bind each operation to immutable exact Tenant/environment/source record-use and physical-copy identities plus relevant policy/basis. A batch has finite fixed membership; discovery results or a broad prefix are never delete instructions. Newly eligible records cannot join an older authorization. Re-prove each independently actionable target/group; a blocked target does not falsely complete the whole set or necessarily block unrelated qualified targets. Preserve source-declared indivisible authorization groups and shared-copy constraints, while reporting actual physical partial outcomes rather than promising distributed all-or-none deletion. No caller-supplied locator or mutable filename identifies the target authoritatively. Provider target identity is resolved from trusted custody facts and the qualified adapter.

**Q63-C05 — Current actor, Tenant and execution context.** Use current source/P12 authority with trusted native Tenant/environment/source context. Distinguish policy administration, activation, automatic execution, required human release, stop/cause resolution, hold authority, view/body access and provider operation. A worker must have its actual qualified system/NHI authority for the exact operation; a service credential, queued message, generic admin label or the policy author's old session grants none. Attribute historical human approval and actual automated execution separately. A person's departure neither invents perpetual delegated access nor silently cancels a valid independently governed policy; current source delegation/authority decides. Application/database context must agree and reset safely across pooled success/error/cancel/retry. Caller IDs are validated selectors, not actor/tenant/approval facts.

Story: US29-E06. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q63-C01, Q63-C02, Q63-C03, Q63-C04, Q63-C05; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-caa67bedc0981b501971"></a>

## REQ29-D01 — Preserve document, byte, rendition, copy and use identity

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"caa67bedc0981b501971","name":"REQ29-D01 — Preserve document, byte, rendition, copy and use identity"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

The platform shall distinguish source business identity, immutable received or generated version, exact stored byte identity, processing rendition/output, provider copy and source use. A typed use binds its actual owner, Tenant/environment, source record and exact version or expressly qualified current-version resolution. A use is not an ownership transfer, byte copy or new reader grant. Source-designated originals shall retain their exact received bytes; source-required raw-discard rules remain separate. Provider locators, paths, names, folders, digests and Payload document versions shall not substitute for business identity.

Story: US29-D01. Source: custody-and-integration.md. Decision basis: Founder §§7–8,13; Q02/Q28/Q42/Q44/Q50/Q53/Q56/Q61-C02/C07; P18/P21/P22/P23 identity owners.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-cdb0ef2ce57e9de72e8a"></a>

## REQ29-A02 — Files for Asym work and exact submitted-file readback

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"cdb0ef2ce57e9de72e8a","name":"REQ29-A02 — Files for Asym work and exact submitted-file readback"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Purpose boundary.** Accept durable files only through an authorized Asym source operation or a narrow admitted resource/media purpose. Existing meaningful drafts, reusable assets, staging and retained obligations qualify before/after active placement. Do not offer arbitrary drive storage, sync or generic anyone-link authority.

**Context-first intake.** Derive tenant, source owner, purpose and profile from trusted server context; let ordinary task entry supply them. Where needed, choose among permitted source contexts before transfer. A folder, file extension, user-supplied owner/purpose string or AI classification is not admission. Unknown source/profile fails closed without fabricating a business record.

**Source and byte separation.** Preserve logical source document/occurrence, exact byte version, rendition generation, provider copy and individual typed use as different identities. One byte identity may serve multiple independently admitted same-scope uses; digest equality does not merge rights, provenance, permissions, currentness or retention. No universal file-current pointer or one semantic owner for all domains.

**Submission confirmation.** Confirm the exact durably received submission/version and only its currently permitted metadata. This is not a financial receipt or evidence acceptance. Selected locally, transferring, received, preparing/available preview and source review are different facts. Missing/failed preview never changes a received submission into not received or accepted.

**Own-submission read.** Add an explicit source-approved read of the exact admitted actor/recipient/request/submission occurrence/version. It is not a global search by uploader/email/Party/filename/hash. Reassignment, helpers and shared mailboxes follow Q01 and source authority; a replacement recipient inherits no old bytes automatically.

**Current access.** Reauthorize metadata, status, thumbnail, pages/tiles/grids, full/range/HEAD/conditional responses, retry/result recovery and alternative representations. Q01 confirmation alone is insufficient. Deny before serialization; no stale positive cache, browser return or alternate endpoint may bypass current source restrictions.

**Deliberate bounded viewer.** Keep readback in the same small request experience with exact version label, status, useful navigation and return context. Opening a list/request or receiving a filename does not eagerly convert/download every file. Reuse shared qualified controls and reading mechanics; no new office editor, global search/OCR/AI corpus or personal drive is implied.

**Exact rendition and honest coverage.** Bind a result to immutable input identity/digest, source restriction generation, profile/build/options and output digests/coverage. A new upload or profile creates distinct lineage. Do not mutate the original, declare completeness from exit0, silently replace a received version with latest, or describe partial conversion as the whole original.

**Hostile content isolation.** Validate actual allowed content and enforce inherited bounded profiles. Parser/converter has no ambient credentials, unapproved network or host-file access. Bound real streamed expansion, images, descendants, memory/CPU/scratch/time. Scripts/macros/formulas/external relationships remain inert. Browser isolation complements processor isolation; scanning alone is insufficient.

**Formats and truthful failure.** Reuse the accepted D32 reading profile and exact ceilings where consumed; independently qualify the new request audience and each promised format lane. Preserve PDF/Office/text/image coverage limits. No extension-based trust, raw active document embed, automatic original download, public URL or third-party viewer fallback. Accepted-for-custody and previewable are different.

**Accessible content and controls.** Preserve available native text, reading order, structure and exact values in qualified views. Test keyboard/AT/mobile/zoom/RTL plus content, not only dialog labels. Filename alt text is not a document equivalent; rasterized text is not sufficient. An inaccessible source needs honest limitation and a source-authorized accessible equivalent/assistance path; do not silently add OCR/AI, change originals or expand download access.

**Permission and purpose transitions.** Original download, replacement/deletion, previous versions, post-closure access, staff-added documents, other recipients and public/external delivery require their own source decisions. Q02 does not automatically grant them or remove already-qualified source rights. Reuse/link/publication uses exact source commands and cannot make a private item public or certify an expense/receipt/application.

**Atomic generation and recovery.** Keep the stable exact source/version/profile/projection/restriction input reuse key distinct from separately identified technical generations. Coalesce at most one current eligible live generation within that scope. Same operation/different intent conflicts; expiry may permit a new deliberate eligible generation without changing old command results/deadlines. Repeated opens cannot bypass an exhausted attempt budget. Current source/worker authority controls attempts/promotion; session closure does not cancel independently authorized work. Old workers cannot promote under a successor claim.

**Cleanup versus promotion.** Register tentative output before I/O. Conditional Ready promotion and deletion claim have one winner; deletion-claimed bytes cannot promote and stale tentative cleanup cannot delete promoted output. Immutable keys are never reused. Expiry, cancel, partial writes and lost provider responses reconcile exact copies with truthful failure/retry. No age-only purge of source originals.

**Data and SQL boundary.** Enforce required scope, same-tenant/source relationships, exact identity/version references, valid digests/nonnegative bounded sizes, semantic uniqueness and restricted state transitions. Caller cannot set actor, owner, classification, source, Ready, accepted or audit truth. Evaluate effective grants/RLS, old/new row predicates, functions/views and privileged worker paths; same-tenant UPDATE cannot reparent into a different authorized purpose.

**Organization and disposal.** Keep promised folders/tags/search/views/bulk management, consuming the source-specific contracts. Organization grants no access, retention or publication. Respect Phase23's separate Media folders and no-auto-purge floor. Visible or empty Used in is not complete use proof; holds, recovery, required disposition and concurrent new references are resolved by exact owners before physical deletion.

**Privacy and evidence.** Exclude raw private filenames, document text/cell values, addresses, source/provider URLs and tokens from ordinary logs, analytics, diagnostics and preview-executor payloads; use approved opaque references, states and bounded measurements. Required restricted source audit/provenance has its own exact access/retention rules and cannot preserve source-forbidden data. No private file upload to vendor issue/scanning services without authorization. Preview access evidence records actual actor/purpose/version/technical outcome under its owner, not human understanding or a new CRM/P6 interaction. Auth, original, rendition and audit retention remain separate.

**Migration and exit.** Inventory actual retained objects and references before migration. Preserve opaque identities and every independent use; admit legacy data only after owner/integrity/privacy/processing qualification. Unknown owner restricts and reconciles, not automatically deletes. Fence old public/mutable writers and mixed-version readers. Backup/restore/exit covers bytes plus metadata/references and applies adverse history before serving restored content.

**Operations and rollout.** Enforce finite source and shared-preview work budgets, fair tenant scheduling and bounded retries. Disable only affected unqualified profile/reader while preserving received evidence and safe submission/status where still qualified. Assign operational owners and exact signals before activation; no silent engine, public-storage or generic-download fallback.

**Definition of done.** Prove actual source admission, real-role SQL/permission boundaries, parser isolation/fidelity, every declared format, private delivery, races, migration/restore and accessible participant journeys. Preserve exact inherited obligations; configuration, filename chips, template previews, mocked tests and library dependencies are insufficient. No full future product becomes a prerequisite for its narrow shared file seam.

**Exact inherited profiles.** These values apply only to the named source/representation and units, never as generic intake limits:

- Original input; 20 MiB actual streamed bytes.
- Expansion/structure; 200 MiB cumulative actual expansion, 10,000 entries, XML depth 128, no DTD/entities/traversal/ambiguous package members.
- Pages/images; 200 output pages/visible slides; 40,000,000 pixels per image; 256 MiB aggregate decoded-image working data.
- Output; 50 MiB cumulative rendition content.
- Attempt; 60 elapsed seconds, 30 aggregate process-tree CPU seconds, 2 GiB memory, 512 MiB scratch.
- Healthy-load fixture; 100 simultaneous opens across 20 tenants: 80 repeated across 5 same-source files and 20 distinct cold files each ≤ 2 MiB and ≤ 10 pages or 1,000 cells. p95 server admission ≤ 1 s, cold Ready ≤ 60 s, warm first API content ≤ 2 s with declared worker capacity.
- Mobile fixture; 400 kbps, 300 ms round trip, 320 CSS-pixel viewport, plus maximum/overload/skew tests. Browser/render time is measured separately.

Story: US29-A02. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q02, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

## REQ29-B06 — Default-on review notices with tenant control

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"d068188dbb351b00c058","name":"REQ29-B06 — Default-on review notices with tenant control"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Meaning and eligibility.** The native source SHALL bind this optional notification to an actual authorized ordinary Q23 Request changes occurrence on the exact current review basis. It SHALL preserve Q03 correction, Q21 acceptance, Q22 fulfillment, Q24 responsibility and Q25 first issuance independently. The system default enables the initial Request recipient email step only through a qualified native profile; the current eligible review control starts on. Unsupported, missing, legacy, conflicting or unknown intent MUST NOT default to true. Technical viewer failure, generic task status, note autosave, a field edit or a receipt MUST NOT manufacture this occurrence. Repeated update calls are not new review decisions.

**Concrete tenant configuration.** Authorized tenant staff SHALL be able to configure this event in the existing P17 Delivery Plan surface: see the source-owned Request recipient audience, enable/disable its optional email, inspect the source-owned recipient basis and use the existing authorized source-management route where supported, and choose only exposed message/locale/sender/reply options. The first contract SHALL not masquerade as a multi-audience/channel product when it only declares Request recipient/email. Additional choices SHALL require an independently declared, qualified finite role/resolver/step for this meaning; Q24 assignment, a shared mailbox or a copied contact is insufficient. A plan SHALL be one complete version under existing inheritance, permission, synthetic comparison/test and publication rules; no mail-list editor, arbitrary To/CC/BCC, hidden override or new global preference is introduced.

**Authority and precedence.** Source review authority, source recipient/contact administration, plan publication, message authoring, notification eligibility and current recipient access SHALL be checked separately through their owners. The per-review choice is a source condition for its named optional recipient-email step, not a plan mutation or mandatory-message mute. Tenant Off and applicable recipient preference/consent/suppression/current privacy restrictions cannot be widened by staff or missing input. Protected required steps remain locked; any independently qualified sibling effect remains governed and visibly distinguished. Trusted context SHALL derive scope/environment/actor and sealed recipient authority; caller-supplied IDs are validated selectors, never proof. No new permission atom or all-purpose communication role is mandated.

**Review, intent and handoff.** The authoritative source command SHALL coherently persist exact review basis/outcome, required safe guidance/history, explicit notification choice and durable handoff identity. Co-located source facts/outbox SHALL commit atomically; provider I/O stays outside locks. Known ineligibility or indeterminate required admission proof SHALL be explained before a reviewed combined action can silently change meaning; allow an explicit lawful review-only choice where supported. A known committed review remains committed if later optional delivery becomes blocked, suppressed or uncertain. Reconcile unknown source commitment under current disclosure before retrying; never require another business review to repair mail. No notification may assert an uncommitted result.

**Exact occurrence and full-plan replay.** One genuine review occurrence SHALL retain one permanent source-authorized plan-occurrence identity and exact bounded recipient-step candidates through the sole P6 whole-plan entry point. The compiler SHALL resolve/pin the actual plan and immutable bindings through its owner boundary, prove and atomically release the complete set, or release none. Proved zero is durable; unknown/partial/over-limit resolution is not zero or permission to release a subset. Replay SHALL recover the original choice and original plan/recipient membership, not apply today's settings to old intent. Changed committed material conflicts. A later authorized review after correction uses a new occurrence; item-plus-status, email address, file hash and provider dedupe key are not sufficient business identity.

**Currentness, configuration changes and recovery.** Relevant source changes SHALL advance the exact source fence and invalidate older unstarted notice work through the existing P6 transition. Before external submission re-prove current source/recipient/destination/contact, applicable narrowing settings, suppression, connection/sender/reply and action validity. As an explicit Q26-specific amendment to general future-only plan-edit semantics, published Tenant Off SHALL narrow this exact optional step's current eligibility, ordered with P6 submission admission through the qualified source/delivery gate; Off-first stops affected unsubmitted work, admission-first preserves potentially in-flight truth. Immutable plan/content pins and unrelated notice behavior remain. Later On/configuration widening is future-only and MUST NOT revive stopped work or add a member to a released zero/off occurrence. No silent recipient/channel/content substitution or rewriting of a sealed notice. Preserve uncertainty and reconcile through the existing immutable provider-envelope rules rather than promise recall or blind resend. Changed recipient, correction, acceptance, fulfillment, expiry and erasure must not produce stale upload promises. Active upload access is not a universal email gate: a useful neutral variant requires its own exact source/contract qualification and never grants access.

**Safe surfaces and material.** Source participant guidance, internal review reasoning, notification content, recipient view, staff history and audit SHALL be separately allow-listed. Initial notice content SHALL be minimal and approved; no automatic raw guidance/notes, sensitive filenames, location/care/third-party facts, attachments or direct storage URL. Subject, preview, body, sender and reply can disclose existence and require the same purpose review. Use Q01's qualified closed recipient branch and protected doorway, with inert GET/HEAD and current proof/deliberate source action. No fake Party, portal membership or new account is required. The exact optional external step SHALL have a reviewed closed prepared-material/utility class and explicit Recent-copy posture before activation; do not inherit another purpose's 7/30-day class or an access period. Minimize body-free history, redact/erase through existing owners, and protect provider material, exports, logs, caches and backups.

**Lean accessible journeys.** Reuse Core's base-maia/Base UI components and existing review/request context. Put the visible optional control and permitted recipient/consequence beside the meaningful action; changing the control never sends. No routine extra confirmation, toggle ritual, per-file notice click, long compulsory message, composer or setup detour. Preserve authorized input, reading position, focus and exact choice on validation/lost reply; clear stale state on tenant/user/request/purpose switch. Distinguish Tenant Off, staff-off, recipient restriction, missing delivery setup, unknown check and actual delivery outcomes with safe actionable wording. Source guidance persists independently and uses the recipient's supported locale/direction; date/zone labels reflect actual source bounds. Qualify keyboard/AT/reflow/touch/mobile/slow-network, international names and email rendering with images blocked. No private offline cache, automatic download or permission-widening preview.

**Structural data and bounded operation.** Native storage SHALL enforce stable same-tenant/environment source/review/recipient/plan relationships, immutable admitted choice/binding/effect facts, exclusive authority arcs, valid nullability/defaults/states and durable uniqueness. Prove effective grants, RLS USING and WITH CHECK, update transforms, views/RPC/functions/storage and privileged paths; an allowed update cannot change tenant/recipient/purpose/author/plan into a forbidden state. History SHALL remain append-only where owner-required, with governed redaction/disposition rather than destructive cascades or permanent bodies. Use indexed scoped current checks, finite source/registry recipient limits, existing measured compiler ceiling, paginated history, bounded retry/reconciliation and tenant fairness. Over-limit/unknown resolution SHALL fail safely without truncation or arbitrary broadcast. No new money field or precision change follows.

**Adoption, proof and rollout.** Activation SHALL require exact native source/P12/Q01 recipient, P17 key/profile/binding/whole-plan/system-publication/fact/action/material and P6 dispatch/history/repair proof, including all P01–P24 outcomes below. Register only needed meaning-specific capabilities; full Workflow Studio or a second notice service is not a prerequisite. Publish prospectively with mixed-version gating: old payloads cannot acquire true, old reviews cannot send from backfill, and replay/restore cannot recreate delivered effects. Stop controls SHALL preserve review/source/history and allow lawful review-only operation; restored state must reconcile outward effects before resuming. Reconcile the exact Q23 reservation and current owners/OpenSpec projections at the authorized publication stage; retain explicit no-new-channel/staff-cohort/reminder boundaries. Name operational owners and finite measured limits before activation.

Story: US29-B06. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q26, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

## REQ29-P01 — Preserve one coherent experience and exact source ownership

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"d27e6ab5de153a31726b","name":"REQ29-P01 — Preserve one coherent experience and exact source ownership"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Phase 29 shall own shared immutable byte custody, intake, qualified processing/renditions, physical-copy inventory, authorized delivery, access evidence and owner-directed preservation/disposition. The ordinary request/share source owns its request, item, recipient-authority, review, fulfillment and permitted continuing-reference facts. The organization resource owner governs its publication and reader features. Existing official-document, finance, CMS, conversation, import, care and other source owners retain identity, accepted/current version, audience, legal/financial meaning and source policy. Mission Control and embedded controls consume those same source commands; a central explorer is not an alternative authority. All ordinary, existing specialist and individually named future consumers in the integration volume remain in scope with exact prerequisites. Native source work shall not depend on installing configurable Workflow Studio.

<a id="contract-d39f7b3eee94931cef82"></a>

## REQ29-E22 — Correct retract and apply external facts without historical replay

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"d39f7b3eee94931cef82","name":"REQ29-E22 — Correct retract and apply external facts without historical replay"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q67-C09 — Correction, retraction and mistaken retraction.** Provide real source-authorized correction and retraction of an exact admitted recording/current basis, with concise source-required reason/evidence, current impact and preserved original history. Correcting a date/evidence for the same occurrence preserves that identity. Retracting a recording removes reliance on that input; it does not assert the real event never happened, erase independent valid source evidence or automatically reset a clock to not-yet. Recompute current interpretation from the actual source evidence; disputed/unknown is distinct from false. A mistaken retraction can receive fresh current source acceptance for the same occurrence, linked to that history, without a new duplicate occurrence or replayed old command. Wrong subject/type requires correction of the mistaken scope and separate authorized admission to the correct scope, not reparenting immutable history. Required safe effects on both scopes must be accounted for. Retraction and required protective effects on the mistaken scope remain independently usable when unrelated correction or correct-target preparation is invalid, unavailable or unauthorized; do not require a successful replacement to stop mistaken reliance. Use sufficient permitted source basis without requiring unrelated body access or an extra upload. No generic Delete event/Undo or claimed restoration of lost bytes.

**Q67-C10 — Late facts, later records and historical workflows.** A truthful past occurrence can change current records applicability/eligibility through Q62/Q66 and Q63 safeguards. It must not silently enroll or replay historical workflows, send old notices, renew access, repeat collection or create unrelated outward effects. Preserve Workflow's explicit historical classification and separately authorized bounded backfill for such effects. Later matching records/uses resolve the existing real occurrence through their own current qualified applicability path; do not create a duplicate event to start them or blindly inherit a deadline from another class/use. Unknown event coverage is not proof of absence.

**Q67-C11 — Disposition and independent duties.** Event recording, correction, retraction, pending impact and verified physical disposition are separate facts. A reached deadline grants no disposal/hold-release authority. Preserve all Q62/Q63/Q66 current-policy, complete-use/copy/hold coverage, Stop, finite dispatch, possible-effect, recovery and restore rules, including races with already possible external deletion. An affected restriction must take effect at the controlling admission boundary; timeout or a new event revision cannot clear an unresolved possible effect. No universal grace period, blanket retain-forever or new per-file routine release is introduced. Missing producers, disputed facts and lawful privacy duties require capable resolution without inventing dates or bypassing obligations.

Story: US29-E22. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q67-C09, Q67-C10, Q67-C11; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"d53fd44d366b02473521","name":"REQ29-C89 — Bind one assembly to exact originals, recipe and output"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

One Document Assembly is one source contribution: selected immutable originals, ordered page occurrences/rotations/profile and separately identified immutable Assembled PDF. Participant inputs remain Submitted Originals; staff inputs/output retain staff origin. Internal pages are not separate request items or receipts.

<a id="contract-da8647a01e80f4f5ee7b"></a>

## REQ29-E18 — Preserve policy classes and coherent current effects

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"da8647a01e80f4f5ee7b","name":"REQ29-E18 — Preserve policy classes and coherent current effects"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q66-C13 — Distinct classes, triggers and representations.** Bind each actual ordinary record/use/copy class independently, including originals/assemblies, collection history, ongoing references, later corrections, messages, controls and required copies. Compatible shared definitions need not be duplicated, but reused bytes do not merge obligations or permission. Use trustworthy typed source events/dates, exact declared precision and approved calendar/time-zone semantics; distinguish not-yet-triggered, unknown, no applicable rule and legitimate continuing/review treatment. Views, copies, edits, policy publication, reference End and completion are not clocks unless the actual rule says so. Later records cannot inherit an elapsed old interval accidentally. No money/currency or posted financial facts change.

**Q66-C14 — Coherent effects, concurrency and idempotency.** Bind each consequential change to tenant/environment/source, policy/version/target generation, complete semantic intent and durable business-effect identity. Coherently commit authoritative facts, necessary control fences/history, original outcome and durable follow-through at a real source transaction or proved fence. Reconcile conflicting activation/default/impact/correction/Stop/hold/disposal operations and stale ABA intent at that boundary, rechecking after waits. Recover a matching committed original before new eligibility under current disclosure; changed intent conflicts, complete no-op preserves original attribution, and unverifiable expired intent fails safely. Repeat keys/events/chunks cannot duplicate effects, skip targets or reopen retired/paused state. No SDK callback, optimistic UI or long transaction spanning provider IO substitutes for proof.

**Q66-C15 — Shared custody and current disposal safety.** Policy activation or an elapsed interval is not a physical deletion command. Preserve complete current use/hold/processor/writer/copy coverage, Q63 finite dispatch permits, current generation, possible-effect fences, Stop, reconciliation and restore semantics. Order changes that affect eligibility with the same effective admission boundary used by references, holds, promotions and disposal. A policy change cannot recall an already possible external delete, release its fence by timeout or call unknown outcome safe. Independently lawful recovery is distinct from policy correction. No universal longest/shortest rule or read permission follows from retention.

**Q66-C16 — Structural and every-door database enforcement.** Enforce typed same-tenant/environment/source relations among policies, semantic schema/version, approvals, defaults, bindings, impacts, targets, commands and history with actual keys/equivalents, null-complete references, scoped uniqueness, valid states/date/unit checks and indexed access. Protect immutable meaning and trusted attribution; reject reparenting, arbitrary active-version edits, unsafe cascade deletion and allowed-to-forbidden row transformations. Keep platform-global capability definitions distinct from scoped tenant definitions. Verify effective named/PUBLIC/default/inherited schema/table/column/sequence/EXECUTE privileges; owner/overload/search_path/FORCE/BYPASS/service composition; USING and resulting WITH CHECK after triggers; SELECT/RETURNING/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/UPSERT/MERGE/bulk/COPY exposure; views/materializations, REST/GraphQL/RPC/Storage, workers/imports/legacy/operator and restore. Keep one source/P12 business decision layer plus coarse Tenant RLS. Zero affected rows and function keywords are not business success or security proof.

Story: US29-E18. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q66-C13, Q66-C14, Q66-C15, Q66-C16; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

## REQ29-D06 — Record exact transformations and whole assemblies

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"dbc033b0e65da5c513eb","name":"REQ29-D06 — Record exact transformations and whole assemblies"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Every rendition or derived output shall bind immutable source/input identities and digests, ordered input occurrences where applicable, profile/processor build, options, current restrictions, coverage and output integrity. Metadata stripping, crop, compression, redaction, OCR, conversion or accessibility repair creates a distinct output. Source-required originals remain intact; source-required discard applies only to its actual raw class. The browser's manifest or PDF is untrusted evidence of correspondence.

## REQ29-P04 — Protect every disclosure and invalidate stale access

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"de2e9681a7c846ea6414","name":"REQ29-P04 — Protect every disclosure and invalidate stale access"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Apply current source/P12 static classification and subtract-only projection before search, facets, counts, autocomplete, ordering, duplicate suggestions, pagination, metadata and result serialization. Titles, existence, thumbnails, use/hold associations, conflict reasons and counts can themselves be protected. Original, preview, derivative, manifest, OCR/text, range/conditional read, print/export and operation recovery each require their actual source-approved purpose. Current requester-authorized gateways remain required where bearer URLs are forbidden; private object-store authentication is not Asym authorization. Cache keys and invalidations preserve complete principal/Tenant/environment/source/purpose/version scope; tags are not isolation. Private responses and refreshed session cookies never enter shared/public caches. Reject late responses and clear protected client state after context change or revocation. Public caching belongs only to separately qualified released public outputs.

Story: US29-P04. Source: proof.md. Decision basis: Founder §§8/11/12/16/19; Q04/Q05/Q38/Q41/Q44/Q54/Q58/Q59; Q63-C15; Q66-C18; Q67-C14; source field projection contracts.

## REQ29-B01 — Staff acceptance of ordinary requested items

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"dff411bcc1f1292ca021","name":"REQ29-B01 — Staff acceptance of ordinary requested items"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Scope and necessary condition.** For the ordinary native file-evidence item cohort, require a deliberate source-recorded acceptance by authorized staff before that evidence can satisfy the item. This is necessary but not sufficient to bypass the source's other criteria. Preserve existing authorized non-file/waived/not-required outcomes and specialist policies; add no waiver/override power or collection-only fallback. Acceptance concerns the exact item and purpose, not general file correctness, legal authenticity, signature validity, application/financial approval, access, public release or whole-request completion. A qualified already-recorded acceptance on the same exact source/item/requirement/evidence/purpose basis may satisfy this gate without another human decision; prove current applicability and do not copy acceptance from equal bytes, another request or another use.

**Exact review basis and inspection.** Present and bind the complete source-relevant current evidence set and applicable requirement/instruction/review revisions. Include each relevant supporting occurrence/version, not a filename, latest-file pointer, file count or visible row alone. Preserve independent members/items/uses and constrain dependencies to those the actual invariant needs. Required byte/safety/integrity and source admissibility checks must hold at acceptance. Quarantined/unknown-required-check evidence cannot be accepted to bypass checks. Preview unavailable is not rejection or universal blockage: use a separately qualified, currently authorized inspection path only when its declared coverage is sufficient for the decision. No automatic original download, external viewer or provider URL fallback. Partial rendition, OCR/AI output and file-open telemetry cannot certify unexposed content or that review occurred.

**Current authority and data protection.** Use the exact source review capability through P12; task assignment, staff label, read/download permission and recipient-grant administration do not imply it. Derive tenant/environment, actor, accepted-by, decision time and attribution from trusted server context; caller selectors/expected revisions request scope only. Revalidate current authority, source eligibility and review dependencies at the authoritative mutation boundary after relevant waits. Enforce same-scope source/item/occurrence/decision relationships, valid required fields, permanent operation uniqueness and immutable decision facts through qualified keys/constraints and a single source mutation boundary. Inspect actual SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE grants, RLS USING and WITH CHECK, views/RPCs/storage and privileged service paths; an allowed row update must not rewrite actor, scope, evidence or acceptance. Preserve source-required separation of duties without inventing a universal second reviewer or self-review ban.

**Atomic decision, races and recovery.** Serialize acceptance with relevant correction admission, source revision/freeze/closure/waiver, current eligibility and adverse-authority changes through Q03's owning boundary. If correction admission wins, its predecessor cannot be accepted as current; if acceptance wins, ordinary recipient correction cannot supersede it. Do not silently rebase a stale decision to successor evidence. Commit the immutable decision, authoritative state changes and required durable history/follow-up evidence coherently; required secondary work has a qualified durable handoff. Providers and expensive processing stay outside locks. Recover the matching committed deliberate operation before treating the changed item state as a new failure, under current disclosure rights. Changed material under the same identity conflicts. Concurrent actors cannot create duplicate current acceptance/effects; reporting an existing decision must preserve its actual actor/time, never attribute it to the caller. Transport-key expiry, retries, refresh or projections cannot recreate the effect.

**History, current applicability and source consequences.** Retain exact decision provenance and its historical basis independently of current applicability, visibility and byte availability. Later reviewer departure/role loss does not alone erase a lawful historical decision or require duplicate review; apply any actual source-defined continuing dependency without inventing one. Source-qualified invalidation, discovered safety problems or changed requirements must update current eligibility/remediation through attributable source rules without mutating prior evidence/decision, silently restoring a predecessor or reopening historical fulfillment. Existing acceptance reuse must not rerun side effects or impersonate its original actor. Item acceptance is distinct from source fulfillment; required source consequences must be coherent/recoverable, while Q22 requires source-owned fulfillment of the complete current predicate without a routine final Complete button. Record actual source decision/fulfillment instants, not upload/scan/browser time or a stale transaction-start time. Staff work can continue under independent current authority after participant expiry; no backdating, access extension or new read admission follows.

**Minimal, truthful and accessible UX.** Use the existing source review with clear item/purpose/instructions, exact evidence and one deliberate clearly named acceptance action. No automatic acceptance on viewing, checking, inactivity or generic task completion; no mandatory per-file click, every-page dwell, download, essay, second approver or routine extra confirmation. Preserve current reading context/focus; announce material changes without silently swapping content or applying stale results after actor/tenant/purpose changes. Show received/checking/awaiting-review/accepted and remaining source work according to actual facts; a timely recipient waiting on staff is not missing or overdue because review is unfinished. Keep acceptance, task refresh, communication and fulfillment outcomes distinct. Recover a lost response; do not label known success failed because a secondary refresh failed. Explain current safe recovery in context. Qualify keyboard/AT/touch/reflow, localized labels/times and low-bandwidth behavior; no transient-only, hover-only or color-only consequential state. Preserve available document text/semantics and qualify the actual inspection path for supported reviewers; accessible viewer controls alone do not establish accessible document content. Disclose inaccessible/incomplete content and use only an existing source-qualified sufficient alternative when needed; no OCR/AI/translation or format expansion is promised.

**Privacy, bounded operations and rollout.** Keep content and sensitive associations behind current source disclosure and protected serving. Preserve minimal decision references/revision/actor/time/outcome evidence under owner retention; no full private-body duplication or secrets/filenames in general logs, analytics, queues or notifications. Historical integrity is not indefinite sensitive-content retention; hold/disposal and copies remain with their owners. Use bounded indexed current-set/operation checks, paginated authorized history and existing source/task operations; no full-history scan or tenant-wide lock where the invariant is narrower. Activate only qualified native paths; legacy uploaded/completed/safe booleans never backfill staff acceptance, and old clients/workers cannot bypass the new command. Restore and mixed versions preserve decision lineage, current restrictions, permanent effects and actual clocks. A scoped stop prevents new unsafe acceptance while preserving receipts/history and safe reads; rollback cannot erase downstream business effects. Recover through owned commands, not direct SQL or provider-console repair.

**Ownership, scope and proof.** Add the explicit ordinary-native staff-acceptance gate atop Q03-C05/C10/C11/C16 and the Q06/Q10 source-fulfillment boundary; preserve Q01–Q20 and all source-owned alternatives. At authorized publication reconcile native source/P12/P9 contracts, the applicable GEN-06 consumer binding and declared author-generated projections; no new acceptance engine, permission model, global policy editor, automatic collection mode or prerequisite of whole Phase34/41. Q24/Q25 govern current person/team review coverage, Q23 ordinary review recovery, Q33 no-longer-required demand, Q52 selected acceptance, Q22 source fulfillment and Q26/Q27 exact review notices. Q60 governs acceptance-error correction; these later selected capabilities retain their distinct source powers and actual qualification gates. Before activation prove P01–P18 at the real source/database/race/UI layers, including sufficient inspection coverage, durable handoff, authorized help/recovery and representative staff/participant comprehension. Record actual workload/latency/reconciliation budgets under existing owners; choose no unsupported numerical claim or universal staff SLA here.

Q52 permits optional finite selected-set acceptance: NEW decisions are all-or-none and exact independently existing acceptances keep original actor/time. Q60 supplies attributed acceptance-error correction, with original decision/F/access preserved. Neither is a generic inverse toggle.

Story: US29-B01. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q21, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-e29509bdbd95fe704332"></a>

## REQ29-A01 — Confirm recipients for external private document requests

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"e29509bdbd95fe704332","name":"REQ29-A01 — Confirm recipients for external private document requests"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Scope.** Apply this floor to external private-document collection requests. A request is one source-owned requirement and its relevant recipient/instruction revision, not an HTTP request. Preserve unrelated giving, receipts, newsletter and collaboration contracts unless expressly amended.

**Identity meaning.** Record the method and actual proof established. Label ordinary email proof **Email confirmed**, never **Identity verified**. A mailbox, native principal, person described, acting representative and business recipient are distinct. No first/last/newest matching Party inference or alias guessing.

**Shared admission.** Extend the existing Phase 4/native Auth and Phase 12 authenticated-human admission path narrowly for this exact request purpose without organization enrollment. The source grant constrains the authenticated principal and does not create a generic fifth role/context, anonymous private access, NHI impersonation or alternate JWT issuer. Existing member/public/NHI/operator semantics remain intact.

**Recipient authority.** Adopt a closed document-request-recipient authority for legitimate recipients without a Party. Bind tenant/environment, requesting source, request/revision, intended recipient/address revision and allowed purpose/actions. Use only this named producer branch; do not borrow Phase 28's newsletter-only exception or manufacture constituent records.

**Proof and reuse.** Invitation is insufficient. Fresh proof is delivered only to the fixed current source-approved address after deliberate eligible action. Reuse existing proof only under an explicit qualified request-purpose rule proving principal, exact address/revision, method, age, request binding and current source policy. Contact/provider email equality alone grants nothing. Stronger source assurance cannot be downgraded to email.

**Doorway and session.** Reuse ADR-0025/0037 selector-plus-fragment transport and deliberate protected POST. GET/HEAD, passive scanner visits and prefetch create no authorized session or send. The doorway is scanner-resistant, not scanner-proof: an advanced scanner can submit forms, and an exchange never proves a human actor. Initial landing state is non-authorizing. Preserve the exact frozen qualified Tenant Donor Portal Host and existing private headers; use only an explicitly qualified same-tenant continuation to the existing participant surface. No credential-bearing redirect or cross-host Domain cookie.

**Action separation.** Authorize instructions, initiate transfer, finalize, see status, preview, original download, replace and review separately. Upload permission supplies no directory, previous-submission, other-recipient, CRM, count/search or broader file read. A clean scan or confirmed recipient cannot accept evidence, complete a business requirement or sign anything.

**Recipient correction.** The source alone changes intended recipient or materially changes request instructions through a versioned successor. Fence obsolete proofs/grants and pending promotion, preserve attribution and accepted evidence. Do not transfer previous-submission access to a replacement recipient by default. Same address after a change is not the same revision.

**Recovery and representation.** Offer wrong-account switch, governed resend and source-owned correction/recovery. A user cannot type a replacement destination into the verification screen. Staff cannot set a verified flag or request the recipient's secret. Authorized helpers/representatives require existing source authority and separate actor attribution; Q01 neither universally permits nor bans shared mailboxes. A source without a qualified recipient/assurance policy cannot activate that purpose.

**Persistence and authorization.** Derive actor, tenant, source and admission from trusted server context and current authoritative records. Use opaque identities, non-null required scope, same-tenant/source foreign-key relationships, unique logical operations, and immutable recipient/request/byte references where issued. Prevent changing authority-bearing columns through general updates. RLS/grants/command validation must agree on old and new row states and actual execution roles; privileged workers re-prove their narrowly delegated source authority.

**Retry and races.** Consume the exact request-bound proof application/redemption and create/recover its one admitted result through an atomic authoritative command or equivalently proved recoverable owner protocol. Separately qualified reuse of parent native proof still requires a new independently authorized request binding. Scope idempotency to the intended effect and semantic fingerprint; same key/different intent conflicts. Co-commit local facts and required outbox work. Handle provider success/response loss as unknown until reconciled; never manufacture a fresh-key resend to hide uncertainty. Link/code siblings cannot both independently redeem.

**Currentness and clocks.** Recheck current actor/service identity and operation-appropriate source authority at every protected operation. Participant actions and reads additionally require their current exact recipient/request admission; independently admitted worker processing re-proves its own source/worker authorization and withdrawal fences, not the former browser session. Apply these checks to finalization, promotion, full/range/conditional reads and recovered results as appropriate. Keep request due date, invitation expiry, proof expiry, session expiry, upload-grant expiry and byte retention distinct. Use server time and exact qualified profiles; neither reads nor resend renew the business deadline. Define the atomic admission point for revocation races.

**Private custody.** Transfer only into server-reserved private staging through a finite purpose-bound grant. Verify exact object identity, type/size/integrity and current authority independently of browser/provider success. No overwrite, public URL or old bucket fallback. Transfers not durably received before their admission ends stay unavailable and follow owner cleanup/hold policy; late scan success cannot revive revoked source authority. Durably received intake can continue independently authorized source processing after browser-session expiry, without granting new participant access.

**Communication.** P4/request producer owns proof and occurrence; P17 prepares exact meaning-specific content; P6 alone dispatches and records outcomes. Qualify initial invitation, user-requested confirmation, resend, correction and any required security notice separately. Preserve consent/contact/provider restrictions; do not treat marketing unsubscribe, do-not-contact, hard bounce and complaint as one Boolean. No direct provider/SMTP or alternate-channel bypass when delivery is blocked.

**Abuse and privacy.** Bound guesses, sends, outstanding reservations, bytes and processing with shared qualified controls at request/recipient/tenant and suitable network/global scopes. Resend cannot reset attempt budgets. Avoid attacker-triggered permanent account/request lockout. Pre-confirmation errors and metadata remain non-enumerating; secrets/private filenames/addresses/bodies stay out of ordinary URLs, logs, analytics, queues, search and replay. Rate-limiter outage cannot silently remove protections.

**Usability.** One logical code input with full paste, autofill and leading-zero support; shared safe-link alternative where qualified. No forced password creation or profile completion merely to respond, default CAPTCHA puzzle, unnecessary repeated confirmation or staff File Manager shell. Preserve source-required stronger authentication or step-up. Preserve exact authorized return and accepted results across delay/retry. Use clear pending/received/processing/review labels, keyboard/screen-reader/mobile and low-bandwidth recovery, safe localization and non-lossy address handling.

**Audit and lifecycle.** Keep request creation, proof delivery, confirmation, admission, transfer, processing, review and business acceptance as separate evidence. Distinguish provider accepted from delivered, granted from bytes served, and served from human reading. Record opaque correlation, actual actor/proof provenance, source/recipient revisions, result and correction lineage under appropriate restricted retention; auth secrets are not file records. Restore applies revocation/disposal history before serving anything.

**Qualification and rollout.** Expand schema/readers first, fence incompatible writers, then qualify exact purpose/host/provider and enable a bounded cohort. Unknown versions fail closed. Imported or historical verified flags/links grant no new authority. Rollback may disable new admission while preserving evidence and denial fences; it must not return new recipients to old donor/signup/public-storage behavior. No whole Phase 34/41 dependency and no activation from mocked tests or configuration alone.

Story: US29-A01. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q01, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

Application shall consume the exact reviewed saved revision/availability and expected never-issued target basis under current template and target authority. Choosing a starter before typing is the simple default. Existing input is not silently appended to or overwritten; any supported replacement is deliberate, impact-visible and coherent, without requiring a generic merge system.

## REQ29-C37 — Preserve civil-date meaning and historical receipt timing

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"e2a5280f796edfe8e98e","name":"REQ29-C37 — Preserve civil-date meaning and historical receipt timing"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

For a qualified date-only target, bind the civil date to the source-declared IANA zone and compare the trusted receipt's local civil date: same day is not late, later date is late. A qualified instant retains declared precision and Q09 strictly-later/equality semantics. The editor does not convert kind/calendar/zone/precision, infer browser midnight or use an elapsed-day substitute.

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"e34fd22e9472358d203c","name":"REQ29-C49 — Coordinate counts and manual overlap at possible-send admission"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

One durable source lane coordinates active manual/automatic occurrences for the issued request/recipient purpose; future dates do not occupy it. Occurrence admission atomically reserves automatic capacity, exact intent and current source/plan/stop basis. First possible provider-call fence converts reservation to lifetime usage once.

## REQ29-C71 — Admit staff supply coherently and recover exact original work

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"e723c3600fa695f4fd6d","name":"REQ29-C71 — Admit staff supply coherently and recover exact original work"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

After required verified receipt, one real authoritative local transaction commits exact staff use/membership/evidence revision, custody reference/retention protection, truthful history/receipt, original result and required durable follow-through. Physical provider effects are not claimed transactionally atomic; no whole-Tenant lock or provider/scan I/O under source locks.

Story: US29-C71. Source: organization-and-review-tools.md. Decision basis: Q53-C06–C08; REQ29-P03/P05/P06/P10.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-e797d10a2ca62e7c5846"></a>

## REQ29-E11 — Admit an optional partner reference to exact accepted evidence

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"e797d10a2ca62e7c5846","name":"REQ29-E11 — Admit an optional partner reference to exact accepted evidence"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q65-C01 — Support A+B without compulsory expansion.** Preserve the complete collection/review/handoff record under its existing policy and allow optional, independently admitted Partner Document References for ongoing work with a particular partner. A remains a fully usable baseline; B is available when its real purpose and current authority qualify. No automatic reference follows from upload, acceptance, request fulfillment, a partner relationship or workflow completion. Preserve all Q01–Q64, other source families and legitimate existing uses.

**Q65-C02 — Name the narrow write authority.** The ordinary partner-document-reference source owns each reference's admission, exact adopted evidence basis, current lifecycle, ending/replacement, business history and records-policy binding, using a qualified Phase9 partner context. This declares the narrow source contract to implement/qualify, not an assertion that a native service already exists. Phase9 retains partner identity/relationship truth; the ordinary request source retains receipt, acceptance/correction and fulfillment; Phase29 owns shared custody/copies; Phase12 owns authorization decisions; GEN-05/Workflow coordinates only qualified source receipts. No new agreement-validity, signature, organizational-representation, membership, giving or partner-approval authority is created.

**Q65-C03 — Deliberate, contextual admission.** Provide a source-context Use for partner reference action after the exact relevant evidence has a qualifying source acceptance, at the supported handoff point or later. Do not require whole-request or partner-process completion unless the actual reference-purpose contract requires it; do not allow acceptance to be assumed from a partial workflow node. Optional preparation selects only permitted current evidence and a qualified target and creates no operative reference or custody authority. The normal command clearly identifies the additional use; do not silently bundle it with Accept or add a mandatory combined approval flow. Actual capability/policy and eligible destinations constrain tenant/staff choices. Q66 supplies separate tenant-controlled ordinary policy authoring; reference admission itself remains a simple contextual act and requires no policy editor or governance form.

**Q65-C04 — Exact evidence and acceptance basis.** Bind the admitted unit to immutable version/assembly/input-output identities and its actual source item/requirement revision, contribution/provenance and relevant acceptance basis. Q21 may accept a multi-file set and Q56 may admit an indivisible assembly; preserve that actual unit and coverage. A component cannot be presented as independently accepted or a complete agreement merely because it belongs to an accepted set. A source-qualified sufficient subset may be admitted when its actual acceptance basis and required dependencies remain intact; it is not a new per-file acceptance. A narrower representation is allowed only where the source expressly qualifies its meaning and disclosed coverage. Keep original participant versus staff-supplied acts truthful. Byte/digest/name equality is not equivalent purpose, acceptance or rights; a later acceptance of identical bytes is a different basis when its source facts differ.

Story: US29-E11. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q65-C01, Q65-C02, Q65-C03, Q65-C04; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->
