## REQ29-A09 — Requested-by targets preserve otherwise-authorized intake

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"e7d37092c71a8cb2b0b0","name":"REQ29-A09 — Requested-by targets preserve otherwise-authorized intake"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Ordinary target.** The ordinary request's due date SHALL be a requested-by target. Passing it alone SHALL NOT deny otherwise-authorized submission or Q03 correction, end reads, approve evidence, complete/cancel the request or create a late-approval gate. Specialized source policies remain authoritative.

**Current admission.** Every new admission SHALL enforce current tenant/environment/source/recipient/purpose/item/revision authority, finite access, binding intake limits, freeze/closure/completion and custody eligibility. Unknown authority SHALL deny the affected action. Q08 fresh access SHALL remain read-only.

**Trustworthy receipt timing.** The source SHALL durably preserve exact submission admission time and available immutable target-applicability evidence. If target-only failure prevents proof, timing SHALL remain explicitly unresolved; recovery must prove the admission-time terms, never substitute today's target or invent a verdict. Transfer start, client clock, provider dates, callback arrival, scanner completion, reviewer action and response receipt SHALL NOT replace receipt time.

**Qualified date meaning.** Target comparison SHALL use a qualified source interpretation, timezone/calendar where applicable and authoritative precision. For a resolved instant, strictly later is late and equality is not late. Date-only/absent/unknown states SHALL be distinct; no accidental UTC-midnight, browser-local or generic Studio default. Actual cutoff/expiry boundary rules remain separate.

**Historical integrity.** Authorized date changes SHALL preserve original receipt and applicable target evidence. Current outstanding-work projection MAY follow the source's new terms without silently rewriting historical timeliness. Erroneous facts require explicit attributed owner correction with prior evidence preserved. No generic task edit, backdate, retry, migration or derived flag SHALL grant new authority or erase a prior received act.

**Truthful outstanding work.** Recipient-overdue status SHALL require actual currently outstanding recipient work under its applicable target. Pending scanning/review or missing business acceptance alone SHALL NOT make timely submitted work recipient-overdue. Failed checks SHALL preserve receipt while accurately identifying any source-required next action.

**Correction and new obligations.** Each correction SHALL have its own receipt and exact Q03 relationship. Additional/clarification/replacement work SHALL use explicitly applicable source terms, without unrelated retroactive lateness or an automatic new interval. A correction SHALL NOT inherit predecessor approval or safety results.

**Exact review fencing.** Durable correction admission SHALL atomically apply Q03's predecessor fence and relevant evidence-set change. Stale review/completion SHALL NOT accept a superseded candidate; sibling material and independent uses remain intact. Failed successors SHALL NOT silently revive predecessors.

**Serialized source effects.** Admission SHALL serialize with relevant target/revision, recipient, hard-limit, freeze, acceptance, completion and revocation changes. Qualified compatible target-only changes need not force reupload or confirmation; material meaning/scope changes keep their actual guards. Required receipt/history/effect intent SHALL be durable together or use an equivalent fail-closed recovery contract. Actual time SHALL be reevaluated after waits; no provider operation under a source lock.

**Durable idempotency.** One deliberate submission identity SHALL converge on one exact durable effect across retry, double click, callback duplication and transport-key expiry. Bind submitted bytes and material recipient/item/purpose/correction/instruction scope; conflicting reuse SHALL fail. Server-resolved compatible target-only changes MAY be captured at first admission without replacing intent. Once admitted, replay retains its original time and captured terms/unresolved state. New corrections are separately identified; replay disclosure requires current permission.

**Unknown outcomes.** A transport/provider timeout SHALL NOT prove nonreceipt. Recover the same operation before duplicate submission or destructive cleanup; cleanup SHALL prove nonadmission/unreferenced custody under source policy. A missing response SHALL NOT move an already committed receipt after the target.

**Target-only failure.** Unavailable target information SHALL NOT be treated as no target, a late result or a hard cutoff. Independently proved actions SHALL remain usable under a qualified source recovery contract preserving the receipt, available immutable provenance and unresolved timing. Reconcile only trustworthy admission-time terms; otherwise retain unknown timing. If missing information is needed to prove authority or a binding cutoff, deny that action. Activation requires resolved timing semantics and this recovery policy.

**Trusted scope and attribution.** Tenant, actor, recipient, author/owner, source, review/approval facts and receipt time SHALL derive from trusted current context or validated source relationships, not caller declarations. Protected metadata, counts, history, cache keys, exports and recovery SHALL preserve exact role/tenant scope.

**Database enforcement.** The schema/mutation design SHALL enforce same-tenant relationships, semantic uniqueness, immutable provenance, legal states/nullability/delete behavior and bounded indexed access. Effective grants/RLS old-and-new-row behavior, views/RPC/definer/service/storage paths SHALL prevent authorized-row transitions into forbidden states.

**Private file boundaries.** Late intake SHALL retain file type/size/rate/quota, integrity, safety and representation gates. Original/preview/history reads remain independently authorized under Q04/Q05; lateness SHALL NOT publish files, revive superseded bytes, extend retention or bypass disposal/holds.

**Simple truthful UX.** Keep the existing request/item context, Requested by wording, current allowed action, exact correction target and durable receipt. Lateness alone SHALL NOT create a validation error, mandatory essay, repeated confirmation, new account/drive view or approval queue. Use action-specific blocked and unknown states.

**Accessible temporal UX.** Preserve Base UI/base-maia, shared semantics/tokens, keyboard/focus, speech/AT, touch, reflow, localization/RTL and time-zone clarity. Material progress/errors SHALL be perceivable without color-only/toast-only feedback or focus theft. Reauthentication/retry SHALL preserve safe context without unrequested replay or private offline storage.

**Workflow boundaries.** Target passage SHALL NOT let a task/timer complete, revoke or close the source through shorthand. Separately authorized reminders/escalations SHALL recheck exact current source work and governing dispatch rules. Q09 SHALL select no new message, cadence, escalation or generic execution platform.

**Bounded operation.** Use existing custody/command/task/worker paths, bounded scoped queries/pages, suitable indexes and measured request/tenant/byte/concurrency budgets. No complete-history scan per row, provider fan-out per timing label or cron dependency for authority correctness. Abuse limits SHALL preserve ordinary qualified retries.

**Minimal accountable history.** Preserve source-attributed receipt, target/revision applicability, correction and source decision history. Diagnostic telemetry SHALL minimize private names, addresses, filenames/content, tokens and deadline details. Staff correction/help SHALL use a real owner route; support messages do not grant rights.

**Safe adoption.** Adopt prospectively for qualified ordinary-source cohorts and compatible reader/writer versions. No blanket reinterpretation of legacy deadlines or guessed receipt targets. Backfill only from reliable source evidence; unknown stays unknown. Rollback/restore SHALL preserve new receipt history and current adverse authority without reopening intake.

**Trace and qualification.** Trace this selected answer and complete amendments into the authorized glossary/ADR, canonical owner requirements, generated OpenSpec, design/tasks, tickets, implementation and release proof at their proper stages. Native role/concurrency/provider/browser and representative-user evidence SHALL precede activation. Adjacent unit tests SHALL NOT be represented as that proof.

Story: US29-A09. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q09, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-e9111019175f9843bbbc"></a>

## REQ29-E08 — Reconcile actual copy outcomes

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"e9111019175f9843bbbc","name":"REQ29-E08 — Reconcile actual copy outcomes"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q63-C09 — Provider-specific execution and verified results.** Use the qualified Storage/provider API against the exact admitted immutable copy/generation, including a version token only where the actual installed protocol supports and qualifies it. Never delete via Storage metadata SQL, Empty bucket, wildcard/prefix or an inferred hash/filename. Parse each target outcome; one envelope success, object disappearance from a UI or generic404/empty response does not prove the intended copies were removed. Distinguish qualified verified absence, confirmed removal, failure, unknown and still-required copies, with truthful attribution. A not-found due to access, wrong scope or wrong locator is not deletion success. Provider acknowledgments are evidence under a proved verification profile, not blanket proof of backups, archives, caches or every external copy. Record qualified observed physical facts under actual reconciliation authority even if a later hold/policy forbids more deletion; do not suppress true loss because a finalizer now rejects further destructive work, or let late stale evidence overwrite a newer verified fact. Retired keys/locators must not be reused where a late old request could affect new bytes.

**Q63-C10 — Idempotency, leases and unknown outcomes.** Tie original authorization, exact target/generation, attempts, per-copy results and required follow-through to the durable semantic effect. Duplicate transport or competing workers cannot create another destructive effect or substitute today's broader policy. Recover matching committed original results under current disclosure before new-action eligibility; changed intent under the same key conflicts. Lease expiry, cancellation, timeout, API error or deleted readable history does not prove an old provider request cannot finish. Keep the possible-attempt/write fence until qualified reconciliation or authoritative negative evidence establishes the outcome and excludes unsafe late effects. A new worker may take over reconciliation without reopening source admission or blindly clearing the old attempt. Evidence-only reconciliation is distinct from a retry capable of another destructive submission: every such new submission needs fresh current dispatch admission for the exact original target. A repeated transport key does not make it read-only if the first request never arrived. A later hold/stop can forbid additional calls while original possibly-started effects still require reconciliation. After lawful control/replay evidence expires, reject unverifiable stale intent rather than accepting it as new. No-op handling compares complete intended effect/current basis, not merely equal status text.

Story: US29-E08. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q63-C09, Q63-C10; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

## REQ29-C48 — Schedule finite calendar opportunities independently of requested-by

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"ea3a21fa195199229dd0","name":"REQ29-C48 — Schedule finite calendar opportunities independently of requested-by"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Store captured IANA zone/local minute/interval/revision and resolved opportunity instants. First enrollment, permitted Resume, active profile adoption and actual no-recipient-work → recipient-work transition anchor at trusted transition time. Ongoing additions/edits/partial receipts/reads/target changes or event names alone do not reset; actual source predicate governs. No anchor/change resets lifetime usage.

<a id="contract-eae5f7a127bc2dc9d47d"></a>

## REQ29-A07 — Fixed source-owned completed-request access end

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"eae5f7a127bc2dc9d47d","name":"REQ29-A07 — Fixed source-owned completed-request access end"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Ordinary scope and adoption.** The 30-day policy SHALL apply only to prospectively admitted ordinary completed Private Document Requests explicitly bound to it. It SHALL NOT replace specialized document, CMS, staff, public or retention contracts.

**Exact anchor and unit.** The completed maximum SHALL be 720 elapsed hours from the one trustworthy source-recorded normal-fulfillment occurrence instant, bound to the exact effect and evidence scope. Client/effective business dates, processing time, notifications, first/last visit and mutable task timestamps SHALL NOT supply or reset that anchor.

**Eligibility and non-revival.** Completion SHALL resolve completed access only from trustworthy evidence of recipient eligibility, admitted policy and binding limits at fulfillment, followed by current authority checks. Today's state SHALL NOT manufacture historical admission. Missing read-admission evidence SHALL NOT block independently authorized source completion; such completion SHALL NOT restore ended recipient access or expose results through it.

**Preserved ceiling and earlier limits.** The source SHALL durably preserve the admitted end, no later than the completion maximum or any then-binding earlier end applicable to that authority and scope. A later source action that actually shortens authority SHALL preserve the narrowed end; removing/relaxing its cause SHALL NOT restore the prior later end without a separately qualified extension rule. Member-specific limits SHALL NOT accidentally terminate unrelated permitted members. Cap removal, policy relaxation/default change, replay, login, return, help, resend or retry SHALL NOT silently lengthen the admitted end.

**Current authority and recoverable conditions.** Every protected recipient operation SHALL revalidate current exact tenant/environment, principal/recipient revision, request, purpose, action, assurance and representation eligibility. Temporary failure or an explicitly reversible owner restriction MAY clear within unchanged valid authority; it SHALL NOT be confused with, or revive, expired/revoked authority.

**Durable completion and races.** Completion, relevant admission fences, policy/effect linkage and required durable history/effect intent SHALL agree through the source-owned atomic boundary or an equivalent fail-closed invariant. Native clock capture/checks SHALL account for relevant lock waits and current-state validation. Competing corrections, completion, reassignment and restrictions SHALL have a durable authoritative ordering; retries SHALL recover the same business effect/end while private replay responses still require current disclosure authority.

**Exclusive server boundary.** New affected protected operations SHALL be denied at or after the applicable end using qualified authoritative time at their enforcement boundary. Missing/nonfinite/invalid policy, provenance or time qualification SHALL deny affected access; no client clock, stale transaction time, delayed cleanup or unselected grace period SHALL extend it.

**All delivery paths.** Metadata/history, originals, previews, HEAD/range/conditional/resume and recovery paths SHALL preserve Q04/P12 current authorization, exact bytes, per-chunk expiry and feasible restriction termination. Provider links, caches and derived projections SHALL NOT create an alternate lasting grant.

**Durable data integrity.** Source relationships SHALL be tenant/environment consistent; trusted anchor/end/policy/effect/actor fields and legal state combinations SHALL be constrained at the authoritative mutation boundary. Preserve historical fulfillment and explicit correction/remediation evidence; unknown legacy facts or late completion SHALL NOT be repaired by inventing eligible access.

**Database and privilege proof.** Actual grants, column privileges, RLS USING/new-row behavior, composed policies, views, functions/RPCs, storage and service paths SHALL prevent scope forgery, attribution forgery, end extension and bypass. Client updates SHALL NOT turn an allowed row into an unauthorized state.

**Truthful deadline presentation.** The same request page SHALL show the actual effective end in readable localized date/time with clear timezone and sufficient precision near current status/actions. An earlier end SHALL replace the generic 30-day promise. Display, sorting, locale or timezone changes SHALL NOT change authority.

**Safe re-entry.** Session/proof expiry SHALL remain distinct from ended source access. Qualified same-request re-entry MAY restore a still-permitted view without new membership, repeated unnecessary enrollment or a reset deadline. Valid authentication SHALL NOT restore ended access or convert re-entry into an unrequested download.

**Honest failure and end states.** Unknown policy/outcome, technical outage, file unavailability, expired proof and ended access SHALL have truthful independently scoped states. After source access ends, only the permitted neutral help/end projection MAY remain; no permanent private receipt/history fallback or false deletion/rejection claim is allowed.

**Accessible shared UX.** The journey SHALL preserve Core's shared Base UI/base-maia system, clear names, keyboard/focus, touch, zoom/reflow, localization and meaningful non-repeating status. Each actual timing limit SHALL have documented applicable accessibility treatment; a 30-day maximum or security label alone SHALL NOT prove conformance.

**Independent custody and clocks.** Source access, intake due date, proof/session, delivery/worker/rendition and retention/hold/disposal clocks SHALL remain distinct. Expiry SHALL NOT delete evidence, extend retention, release a hold, recall downloaded copies or alter independent uses/rights.

**Delayed and partial effects.** Lost responses, delayed events/projections/notices and outages SHALL recover the original authoritative outcome/end without restarting or pausing the window. Required secondary effects SHALL use their existing durable owners; no email, reminder or renewal is implied by completion.

**Governed assistance.** The actual source help path SHALL explain or route the permitted problem without privileged bypass, private email attachment fallback, automatic extension or reopening. Any future fresh authority/accommodation SHALL be explicit, auditable and separately source-qualified.

**Bounded operation.** The implementation SHALL use existing owned service/worker paths, scoped bounded queries, explicit pagination and measured concurrency/egress budgets. Ordinary reads SHALL NOT require a global expiry sweep, provider call under a database lock, per-view deadline write or unbounded history/file fetch.

**Audit and privacy.** Durable evidence SHALL distinguish source completion, admitted policy/end, subsequent authority changes and actual access decisions from technical delivery outcomes. Diagnostics SHALL be correlation-safe and minimized; success SHALL NOT imply human reading, successful saving or evidence acceptance.

**Safe rollout and proof.** Rollout SHALL identify the exact prospective cohort, owner contract, real-role/provider/browser qualification and compatible reader/writer behavior. Unknown legacy requests SHALL NOT receive fabricated anchors or changed ends. Kill switch, restore and roll-forward SHALL preserve history and deny resurrected access; fallback SHALL NOT reopen intake or widen grants.

**Evidence-based policy ownership.** The source/product owner SHALL record 30 days as the chosen ordinary maximum and evaluate validated unmet needs or systematically earlier caps without silently changing grants. Cohort qualification SHALL identify source policies that leave no completed readback before claiming Q06/Q07 is delivered; no minimum guaranteed window or implicit cap relaxation follows. Monitoring SHALL use named signals, thresholds, owners and responses; it SHALL NOT substitute for authorization, accessibility or activation gates.

Story: US29-A07. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q07, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

## REQ29-D03 — Bind every transfer to narrow durable intake

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"eba0ff3a7a03909758c0","name":"REQ29-D03 — Bind every transfer to narrow durable intake"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Server-authorized intake shall bind current actor/context, source purpose and target, exact intended operation, permitted format/size/count, immutable generation-specific private target and finite transfer authority. Client names, MIME, classification, Tenant, visibility, digest or completion flags are untrusted inputs. Direct transfer is permitted only through a qualified narrow grant; it is not bucket access or source admission. The source owns upload/resume/cancel/retry semantics; browser navigation alone does not cancel durable accepted work.

No shared archive audience is created. Every status/manifest response reauthorizes its exposed facts, and every ZIP/header response authorizes all included originals and embedded manifest facts as one indivisible representation.

## REQ29-D09 — Preserve current stream, cache and access-evidence meaning

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"ee3707208259c5e9c526","name":"REQ29-D09 — Preserve current stream, cache and access-evidence meaning"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Full GET, HEAD, conditional/304/validator, byte-range/resume, print/export and recovery responses shall qualify current disclosure before returning protected names, lengths, hashes, status or bytes. Streaming uses the actual P12 tiered current checks, causal revocation, budgets and expiry/feasible termination boundaries; no per-member remote authorization loop or permanent grant follows from an initial request. Public release caching is separately source-qualified and cannot be reused for private results.

<a id="contract-ee74abf8d93784c3a859"></a>

## REQ29-E24 — Complete the representative native partner-document journey

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"ee74abf8d93784c3a859","name":"REQ29-E24 — Complete the representative native partner-document journey"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q64-C01 — Reference and evidence status.** Accept A with this complete amended review. Recommend the GEN-05 sending/supporting-partner agreement-document collection and staff-review leg, using the native ordinary GEN-06/BP-11 pattern, as the first concrete reference. Record GEN-05's context and BP-11's mechanics as documented intent; record their composition as this recommendation, not a shipped binding or observed tenant practice. The reference does not limit the agreed product or manufacture a retention class, legal period or new glossary term.

**Q64-C02 — Exact outcome and ownership.** The ordinary request source owns its issued requirement, received contribution, exact item/evidence decision, fulfillment and permitted remedies. The actual partner/onboarding source owns its own requirement meaning, agreement-related conclusions and handoff. The reference must identify and qualify the exact source record/operation and accountable records authority before production use. No uploaded file, clean scan, task, workflow finish or ordinary acceptance establishes signature/authenticity/legal validity, the contact's organizational authority, partnership approval, member access, giving authority or completed onboarding. Keep any specialist requirement with its real owner; never substitute a mobilization agreement contract.

**Q64-C03 — Native first, coordination second.** The complete native request/review/recovery journey must work with configurable Workflow Studio unavailable. Reuse qualified source commands, shared P9 work and P17/P6 communication purposes. Optional coordination consumes the same source receipts and permanent effect identities, with no duplicate request, task, reminder, acceptance or handoff. BP-11 capability names and synthetic bindings are not grants or existing services; `documents.requirement.accepted@1` is not evidence acceptance, and `private-clean-upload.received@1` cannot collapse receipt/processing/review. Its one-requirement outcome cannot independently complete a multi-item request or partner onboarding.

**Q64-C04 — Minimal preparation and approved policy.** Start the reference with one actual required document and one intended contact/reviewer for comprehension only; retain every ratified supported cardinality and option. Staff instructions stay within the current source purpose. Show Q62's actual approved policy summary within normal review and retain legitimate bounded source choices. Preparation/temporary bytes require their own prior-purpose authority. Neither a template, agreement title, folder, current default, GEN-05/06 generic clock nor a later issuance retroactively supplies policy. Preserve Q06–Q19 access, Q09/Q46 target and Q48 reminder meanings and exact approved values; adopt no generic three-business-day or two-reminder fallback here.

**Q64-C05 — Identity, recipient and source authority.** Bind verified native session/limited-participant admission, active Tenant/environment and current source/P12 permission to the actual action. Derive actor, tenant, author, reviewer, policy authority and audit attribution from trusted context; caller identifiers remain validated selectors. Verify intended-person eligibility separately from organization membership or signature/representation authority. No relationship, shared mailbox, profile name, unverified email, URL, generic role or service credential grants more scope. Preserve stronger source assurance, neutral wrong-account recovery and current revocation; no demo-tenant or profileless-auth fallback may authorize this lane. Where Q01 permits a qualified limited participant without a CRM Party/profile, use that actual path; do not manufacture a Party, profile or membership to fit a helper. System/NHI work uses its actual live qualified authority rather than impersonating the original staff author.

**Q64-C06 — Private, recoverable evidence intake.** Use narrow authorized upload sessions, immutable target identities, actual byte/type/integrity validation, quarantine/processing and source finalization. Do not infer receipt or acceptance from provider/browser success. Resume only the exact current permitted operation; never take the first matching filename/fingerprint from another tenant/account/request. Preserve qualified photos/PDF/assembly and staff supply/reuse, original fidelity and distinct provenance. No public fallback, mutable-key upsert, unsafe scanner override, automatic external viewer, e-sign certification or silent conversion of an evidentiary original. Unsupported encrypted, malformed, oversized, unsafe or unreadable evidence gets the actual supported correction/help path.

**Q64-C07 — Exact review and source completion.** Keep transfer, actual receipt, processing readiness, reviewer decision, item requirement satisfaction, whole-request fulfillment and partner-process outcome distinct. Revalidate current instructions, exact evidence, current required-item set, expected source basis and actor at review/fulfillment commit. A stale reviewer cannot accept a replaced or withdrawn version. Q52 selected acceptance retains its exact transactional semantics; independently accepted prior decisions retain original attribution. Q22's source predicate alone establishes fulfillment. Show scope-specific result copy without an extra routine confirmation or “agreement approved” claim.

**Q64-C08 — Full correction and temporal lifecycle.** Preserve ratified questions/replies, Request changes, evidence withdrawal, staff-assisted correction, acceptance remediation, request cancellation, item additions/removal, instruction changes, Requested by changes and linked recipient replacement at their exact eligible states. None resets prior receipt/F/access clocks or creates automatic renewal. Different recipients use Q49's separate request lineage without evidence/grant transfer. A partner-side change cannot silently reopen a terminal ordinary request. Source handoff/remediation uses current exact reliance and actual owner commands; no fabricated Undo, universal unaccept or unqualified downstream reversal.

**Q64-C09 — Authoritative records and structural integrity.** Use one source write authority per fact and typed same-Tenant/environment/source relationships for request, item, revision, evidence use/version/copy, recipient admission, decisions, policy binding and command results. Enforce required non-null identity, valid state combinations, semantic uniqueness and immutable historical attribution through constraints and the authoritative mutation boundary. A nullable or polymorphic reference must not admit cross-scope or dangling authority; qualify equivalent checks where a single FK is not appropriate. Code-owned global definitions may remain global while assignments and effects remain correctly scoped. Original source dates and issued revisions are facts, not editable UI defaults. Avoid blanket account/partner/request deletion cascades into evidence/history. No financial row, amount, currency or posted fact is changed by this reference.

**Q64-C10 — Supabase and every effective access path.** Preserve source/P12 fine-grained decisions plus coarse Tenant RLS, with current permitted projections. Verify effective grants, table/column/sequence privileges, views, function owners/overloads/search paths, RPC/direct SQL, REST/GraphQL, Storage, service/operator/worker/import and restore paths. Test SELECT visibility and RETURNING, INSERT checks, UPDATE old/resulting rows after relevant triggers, DELETE, UPSERT/MERGE and bulk effects under actual roles. Deny reparenting, actor/policy/status forgery and cross-tenant transformations; zero affected rows is not success. User metadata and stale JWT claims are not live authority. Pool context must agree with the application and reset after error/cancel/retry. A helper callback or collection of SDK calls is not a single transaction; no new permission engine or broad service-role shortcut is introduced.

**Q64-C11 — Atomic business outcomes and replay.** Commit the exact accepted source effect, attribution, required durable history and recoverable original result coherently; durably admit necessary follow-through without claiming external all-or-none execution. Use semantic identity bound to tenant/environment/source occurrence/operation and expected basis, not only a transport request ID. A lost response recovers the original outcome under current disclosure before new work; an old retry cannot approve changed evidence. Changed intent under the same identity conflicts; complete semantic no-ops, genuine factual corrections with equal displayed values and stale ABA bases remain distinct. Expired unverifiable intent cannot be treated as a fresh command. Recheck relevant current source/time facts after waits at the actual action boundary. Event/job delivery can repeat or arrive late; projections and coordination apply only causally valid source receipts. Failed notification/handoff does not undo accepted evidence, and recovery cannot require routine direct database repair or duplicate irreversible effects.

**Q64-C12 — Purpose-specific policy map.** For this reference, explicitly map every actual record/copy class to its real owner, purpose/applicability, policy/version, authoritative typed trigger/date precision, preservation/privacy bounds, permitted choices, hold/stop powers, disposition mode/proof and lawful recovery. Distinguish original evidence, review/history, messages, access/control evidence, temporary uploads/assembly/renditions and shared copies as applicable; this is an investigation map, not a new fixed taxonomy. No agreement title implies expiry/termination as the trigger. Missing or conflicting policy stays an owned affected-cohort gate; it is neither retain-forever permission nor permission to collect/dispose with a guessed default. R-POL and R-LEGACY are closed only by actual evidence, never by selecting the example.

**Q64-C13 — Custody, current use and disposition.** Retain Q63 in full: approved mode, complete current use/hold/writer/copy coverage, exact final dispatch admission, finite immutable targets, possible-attempt fences, per-copy truth, effective Stop across rediscovery and lawful reconciliation/recovery. Ending access, completing/canceling a request, unlinking or finishing partner handoff cannot alone delete bytes or another lawful use. An unknown timeout/expired lease is not nonexecution; a later hold cannot promise recovery after irreversible loss. Preserve P23 manual/no-auto-purge and every other specialist or qualified temporary-copy rule. Database metadata/backups do not establish physical deletion or byte restoration.

**Q64-C14 — Clear, accessible source-context UI.** Use existing source/request/review/history surfaces and shared Base UI/base-maia components. Keep one clear primary action per stage, visible optional actions, specific inline errors, meaningful transfer/receipt/review status and preserved permitted input/focus. Use a real file chooser, keyboard alternatives to drag/reorder, touch targets, visible unobscured focus, screen-reader status without focus theft, 320-CSS-pixel reflow and appropriate reduced motion. Support long/international names, Unicode/RTL, source-language labels and explicit date/time-zone meaning without forcing legal-name splitting or new address fields. Passive or implicit browser events, IME confirmation, selection/blur, navigation and prefetch cannot submit, accept or stop work; deliberate click/tap and focused-button Enter/Space retain their qualified action. Preserve the existing command-origin and CSRF protections where applicable. Weak connectivity uses qualified resumable/recovery paths, not persistent restricted offline storage or an automatic action queue.

**Q64-C15 — Privacy, truthful history and capable operations.** Apply current source field projection before metadata, counts, search, thumbnails, filenames, comments, reminders, history, export, caches/logs and recovery results. Neither knowing a partner nor seeing one use reveals other contacts, private obligations or holds. Keep staff-only display names/internal review and participant-visible messages separate. Store durable business history and protected security evidence with their own purpose/retention; technical telemetry contains no private content, tokens or raw provider URLs. Record real participant/staff/system attribution. Missing reviewer, policy or owner and technical uncertainty reach an actual capable permitted source handler; a queue label alone is not recovery. No new generic notifications, support inbox or public/donor exposure is implied.

**Q64-C16 — Bounded implementation and compatibility.** Reuse capable source/custody/auth/task/communication seams after proof; do not copy financial Tasks assumptions, Eve expiry resets, broad assets metadata or unqualified catalog adapters as ordinary authority. Inventory real retained data and readers/writers before migration; do not manufacture historic consent, policy, acceptance or zero-data assertions. Add compatible safeguards before favorable writers and enable only qualified cohorts. Rollback/kill switches preserve admitted history, current denials and unknown effects; restore adverse control facts before serving/restarting. Define measured finite workloads and owner-approved budgets for records, payloads, bytes/pages/pixels, processing, queries, locks, retry, history and provider copies; apply tenant fairness and stable permitted pagination. No new runtime, e-sign provider, catalog builder, universal limit or full Workflow Studio dependency is required.

**Q64-C17 — Falsifiable proof and trace.** Maintain the evidence/owner map, category findings, analytic traces, composed journeys, P01–P16 proof groups and B01–B04/M01–M05 below. Trace later source requirements, canonical OpenSpec projections, design, tasks, exact tickets, implementation and release evidence without inventing IDs or independently editing generated views. Test native behavior with real permitted/forbidden roles, concurrency/fault/mixed-version/restore and intended-user/AT/mobile cases, not only a polished mock or structural schema. Reference cardinalities do not replace required multi-item/tenant/specialist/public/resource regression coverage. Any measured threshold must name units, workload and owner; missing mandatory proof is a gate, not a monitor-only concern.

Story: US29-E24. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q64-C01, Q64-C02, Q64-C03, Q64-C04, Q64-C05, Q64-C06, Q64-C07, Q64-C08, Q64-C09, Q64-C10, Q64-C11, Q64-C12, Q64-C13, Q64-C14, Q64-C15, Q64-C16, Q64-C17; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

Extend the ordinary Q28 admission meaning to new-to-custody local files on eligible issued unfinished items, while keeping existing reuse and recipient intake. New bytes mean neither freshness nor authenticity. Personal statements/signatures/consent/specialist duties stay independent. Current source authority, not possession/read/reviewer/task/rank, governs without a universal admin/second-reviewer/settings gate.

## REQ29-P03 — Enforce database integrity and authorization through every door

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"ef6af82eccb7c43a6e11","name":"REQ29-P03 — Enforce database integrity and authorization through every door"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Use typed same-scope source, use, object/version/copy, policy, authority, operation and outcome relationships. Required tenant/environment/source identity is non-null; nullable composite references are complete-or-absent. Enforce referenced uniqueness, tenant-aware relationships or an explicitly proved placement equivalent, valid state/time/revision combinations and deliberate delete behavior. Application/database tenant context must be unified and reset through pooled success, error, cancellation and retry. One P12/source decision layer governs fine-grained authority; prescribed coarse Tenant RLS supplies defense in depth. Inspect effective schema/table/column/sequence and named/PUBLIC/default/inherited function grants, actual owners, FORCE/BYPASS roles, invocation mode, overloads/search paths and triggers. Existing-row and resulting-row checks must prevent reparenting, forged attribution, source substitution and forbidden state changes. PostgreSQL inherited predicate semantics apply; a literal omitted WITH CHECK alone is not evidence of a bypass. Immutable source facts and control history cannot be erased by an incidental parent deletion.

Story: US29-P03. Source: proof.md. Decision basis: Founder §§11/14/16/19; Q21/Q41/Q52/Q60 database clauses; Q61-C14; Q63-C16; Q66 database and context clauses; Q67-C13.

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Trusted source commands bind context, target/action and relevant expected basis for profile/control/manual/automatic opportunity work. <a id="contract-efe709275865fc207648"></a>

## REQ29-A03 — Recipient corrections with exact review lineage

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"efe709275865fc207648","name":"REQ29-A03 — Recipient corrections with exact review lineage"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Scope and eligibility.** Enable recipient-initiated correction only for the ordinary native request's exact unaccepted, currently eligible item. Respect source freeze, waiver, closure, deadline, recipient and permission state. Request-open or preview access alone is insufficient; a read does not create a lock.

**Exact target and cardinality.** Bind correction to the exact prior submission occurrence and current source review context. Replace only the targeted admitted use in the relevant review set. Preserve other supporting members and every independent use of the bytes; filenames/digests cannot choose intent.

**Atomic admission boundary.** After immutable byte finalization and current authority checks, commit correction receipt, successor relationship, review-set change and required durable history/effect intent coherently. From that commit, the predecessor cannot be accepted as the current candidate, even while new checks are pending. Local selection or transfer start does not change current review selection or acceptance. Authorized intake reservations, quota accounting and safe audit may occur without admitting the correction.

**Current authority and attribution.** Derive tenant, actor, recipient binding, source purpose and trusted audit attribution from server context; validate permitted selectors. Recheck relevant source revisions and current permission at authoritative admission and result disclosure. Workers use explicit source/service authority and cannot fabricate a human submission.

**Exact review and concurrency.** Acceptance pins exact reviewed occurrences and every relevant source/evidence-set revision. Serialize correction, acceptance and adverse source changes through a shared authoritative boundary; stale operations cannot apply to successor bytes. Use no broader locking scope than the invariant requires.

**New input, new qualification.** Every corrected input receives its own immutable identity, actual byte/length verification and required intake/processing qualification. No safety/preview verdict is inherited from a prior file. Provider response, extension, local preview and transfer completion cannot certify receipt, safety or acceptance.

**Durable idempotency and recovery.** Bind the semantic operation key to tenant, admitted principal, exact target/revisions and canonical intent including byte identity. Same intent returns its durable outcome under current disclosure rights; changed intent conflicts. Lost responses do not create fresh versions, tasks or messages. Expiry of a transport idempotency key cannot allow the same admitted correction effect to be applied twice; its durable intent/result relationship remains authoritative without using content equality as global submission identity.

**No silent fallback or destructive undo.** A received correction failing checks retains truthful history and blocks unqualified acceptance; it does not automatically reactivate the corrected predecessor. Reopening, deliberate source correction, disposal and cancellation remain separate owner commands. No undo-by-delete or source-wide rollback is implied.

**Minimal contextual recipient flow.** Offer the correction action at the exact item, current source instructions, accessible file choice, inline target/selection review and one deliberate send. No mandatory reason, repeated identity entry, extra confirmation modal or new drive/account is added unless the source's established contract requires it.

**Truthful state and cancellation.** Separate selected, transferring, unknown outcome, admitted receipt, checks, preview and business acceptance. Show measured progress or explicit indeterminate work. Confirm result persistently. Stop/close cannot promise rollback of a committed correction; reconcile ambiguous completion before retry.

**Reviewer continuity.** Keep displayed version and notes anchored. Announce a new correction without silently changing contents or stealing focus; direct staff to current evidence before a fresh decision. Counters distinguish files/submissions from required items and satisfied requirements. No receipt or preview event itself satisfies the item.

**Accessibility and localization.** Use shared Base UI/base-maia controls, semantic tokens, native selection alternatives, labels/errors/status relationships, stable focus, touch targets, mobile/zoom/RTL support and reduced motion. Preserve qualified document-content accessibility. Display clear time-zone/deadline context without changing authoritative server time.

**Privacy and disclosure.** Enforce current rights on all metadata, counts, history, processing status, preview and result recovery. Exclude private filenames/text/addresses/provider URLs/tokens from ordinary logs, analytics and executor payloads; use approved opaque references. Retained history, duplicate detection and new corrections cannot widen audience or create a searchable private corpus.

**Database and mutation safety.** Require immutable same-scope occurrence/version relationships, non-null required scope, validated byte/digest fields, unique semantic effects and constrained transitions. Prevent caller-controlled authority/acceptance fields and same-tenant reparenting. Verify effective grants, RLS old/new row checks, views/functions/storage and privileged command/worker paths; service-role mocks are insufficient.

**Processing and storage isolation.** Preserve admitted type/size/work limits, immutable keys, private byte access, isolated parsers, bounded expansion and safe renditions. Registration/promotion/cleanup claims must not race into deleting referenced bytes or promoting deletion-claimed objects. No cropping/reencoding originals, public uploader or external-viewer fallback.

**Owner and retention preservation.** Source domains retain acceptance, financial/legal meaning, publication, retention, holds and disposal. Q03 changes no issued document, claim confirmation, signature, accepted application or public release. Earlier bytes remain under source policy; replacement is not erasure or permission to browse all history.

**Coherent secondary effects.** Record required source history and durable secondary-effect intent with the authoritative correction. Existing workers/owners update projections and any separately authorized communications idempotently. Do not add unconditional mail, duplicate tasks, generic CRM interactions or a new workflow engine.

**Bounded operation.** Use finite source admission/reservation/byte/work budgets, fair tenant scheduling, bounded recovery and paginated authorized history. Preview limits remain preview limits; they do not become arbitrary file-intake limits or a maximum number of human corrections. Repeated opens cannot bypass processing budgets.

**Migration and safe rollout.** Activate only an exact source/audience/profile cohort whose writers, readers, review commands and recovery paths enforce the new revision boundary. Reconcile legacy immutable identities and permissions; do not infer lineage from name/time/hash. Disable affected initiation/acceptance paths safely without restoring superseded candidates or public fallback.

**Proof and operation.** Require the stated positive, negative, real-role, concurrency, failure, migration/restore and accessible-user proof before activation. Assign operators/signals/thresholds/responses. Preserve ratification/publication/implementation/qualification distinctions; passing existing seams or a polished screen does not complete Q03.

Q56 assembly correction admits the complete qualified input/recipe/output unit; raw page staging cannot fence a predecessor. Q60 changes current acceptance applicability through its separate source act while preserving historical acceptance; current unaccepted eligibility does not mean never historically accepted.

Story: US29-A03. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q03, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-f015730c2a2afcd3970a"></a>

## REQ29-P10 — Give truthful failure states and capable recovery

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"f015730c2a2afcd3970a","name":"REQ29-P10 — Give truthful failure states and capable recovery"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Show not attempted, rejected/not accepted, accepted with follow-through pending, known success, conflict, unavailable, partial physical result and outcome unknown according to actual source facts. These are independent facts/views rather than a mandatory giant enum. Preserve permitted input, selection and focus after recoverable errors; current authority loss clears restricted state. A source-specific capable action must exist for expected correction, clarification, containment, replacement, reconciliation and restore—not a powerless queue or routine SQL/provider-console repair. A partial operation is allowed only where the owning contract permits independent targets; never reinterpret Q52 as partial new acceptance. Error/recovery responses must not reveal hidden records or reasons.

Story: US29-P10. Source: proof.md. Decision basis: Founder §§9–11/14–16/19; Q23/Q34/Q49/Q52/Q60/Q61/Q63/Q66/Q67.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

## REQ29-P07 — Preserve source clocks, date precision and boundary conditions

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"f03003fb34850540b225","name":"REQ29-P07 — Preserve source clocks, date precision and boundary conditions"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Keep requested-by dates, source access deadlines, actual fulfillment, retention starts, resource review dates, processor expiry, provider permit validity and recorded timestamps distinct. Preserve every ratified elapsed-day duration and exact source anchor; other profiles may not borrow those numbers. Date-only values remain dates with source calendar semantics; instants use the actual qualified timezone/precision. Display locale/browser clock cannot supply authority or secretly change an interval. Actual occurrence, governing effective date, source acceptance time and later recording time are distinct. Do not backdate from upload/scan/client time or represent a stale transaction-start timestamp as a newly committed occurrence after waits. No read, copy, retry, name edit or policy inspection resets a clock unless the source explicitly defines that event.

Story: US29-P07. Source: proof.md. Decision basis: Founder §§10/14/19; Q06–Q20; Q46/Q48/Q55; Q62-C11; Q65; Q66-C03/C09/C13; Q67-C05.

Target one exact Q21 acceptance occurrence and complete item/requirement/purpose/evidence/review basis, not an arbitrary file/digest or whole Q52 batch. <!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"f2e48c32a10ee50ee4d7","name":"REQ29-C29 — Comment on exact ordinary evidence without requiring a preview"} -->

## REQ29-D02 — Admit only a qualified source and purpose profile

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"f2f85f5e5bf634555265","name":"REQ29-D02 — Admit only a qualified source and purpose profile"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Current source policy, native implementation qualification and provider compatibility are separate prerequisites. Missing material source meaning is an affected capability blocker, not a permissive default.

## REQ29-D23 — Operate finite workloads with accountable recovery

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"f3c4f441bce0f111d83b","name":"REQ29-D23 — Operate finite workloads with accountable recovery"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Use the existing qualified execution, claim, dispatch and recovery infrastructure where its actual semantics satisfy the source contract. Source state and required durable identifier-only follow-through are coherent; remote work is outside short database transactions. No additional queue, generic workflow engine, per-file polling service or mandatory Realtime channel is introduced. A worker's credential is not source authority; actual nonhuman purpose ceilings, context, epoch and current source eligibility remain enforced at the relevant boundary.

Story: US29-D23. Source: custody-and-integration.md. Decision basis: Founder §§15–16,19; Q44/Q53/Q56/Q61-C15/B01–B06/M01–M08; exact P12/P23/P26 and consuming-source budgets.

<a id="contract-f4c6c618075dbd999cfc"></a>

## REQ29-E13 — Replace end and correct partner references deliberately

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"f4c6c618075dbd999cfc","name":"REQ29-E13 — Replace end and correct partner references deliberately"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q65-C10 — Deliberate replacement.** Replace reference acts on one exact selected current use/generation and a separately qualifying candidate evidence basis for the same admitted target/purpose. Preserve the old use and its actual history while preparation occurs; commit the qualified successor and old use's operational end with coherent custody protection and original-result evidence. If replacement fails, do not claim it succeeded or remove a still-lawful predecessor; an already unsafe/restricted predecessor does not become a fallback just because replacement failed. Do not silently follow latest uploads, reaccept evidence or mutate the original use's historical target/basis. Identical bytes with a genuinely different accepted basis are not a semantic no-op. Actual equivalent existing-reference reconciliation must not create duplicate active effects.

**Q65-C11 — End and fresh use again.** End reference deliberately ends only the selected ongoing use under current source management authority and expected basis. It does not delete collection evidence, end unrelated uses, erase original acceptance/F/history or certify physical disposal. End must remain usable through sufficient permitted source metadata when bytes or reuse eligibility are unavailable. End remains independently usable when unrelated replacement or other preparation is invalid or unsaved; urgent authorized containment does not wait for a replacement. An explicit wrong-target correction ends the mistaken use and, only with fresh destination/source authority, admits the intended new use; never reparent history in place. A continuing or newly arising lawful need, including correction of an accidental End, may create a fresh distinct admission linked to prior history under current authority once any operative end cause is resolved; no forced reupload or invented different business need is required. A replay returns its original committed result with any separately qualified current state; it cannot reactivate the ended use or rekey around restrictions, unresolved effects or an operative end cause. No generic Undo or permanent ban on all future lawful uses is selected.

**Q65-C12 — Acceptance correction and changing source restrictions.** Register this use as an affected consumer of the relevant source acceptance/restriction facts. Q60 findings, source withdrawals/restrictions, policy changes and other relevant adverse facts must prevent unsupported current reliance through current source/custody checks, even before an async badge or projection catches up. Preserve the reference's actual admission and original source history; do not automatically erase or universally terminate it. Independently allowed repair/history/body inspection may continue under its exact purpose. Clearing an erroneous blocker can restore eligibility of the same still-admitted exact basis only under current proof and remaining restrictions. A genuinely new acceptance/basis requires deliberate adoption through replacement or fresh admission; identical bytes do not silently retarget old reference history. No stale “accepted/current” presentation may hide the current source consequence.

**Q65-C13 — Atomic effects, idempotency and original-result recovery.** Each admission, replacement, End and fresh re-admission binds tenant/environment/source purpose, target/use generation, exact intent/basis and a durable semantic effect identity. Commit authoritative facts, required history, reference protection and original outcome coherently; durably admit required follow-through. A matching committed operation recovers before new eligibility under current result-disclosure rights. Changed intent under the same identity conflicts; complete semantic no-op, stale ABA basis, genuine equal-display correction and expired unverifiable intent remain distinct. Repeated transport keys, events, workers or native/optional Workflow callers cannot duplicate use or revive ended work. Reconcile unknown source outcomes before new work; notification/projection failure is not rollback of a committed use or permission for another effect.

**Q65-C14 — Actual independent records obligations.** Bind approved policy for the collection record and the ongoing use, including actual class/applicability/authority/version, typed trigger/date precision, preservation/privacy bounds, hold/conflict powers, mode and lawful correction/recovery. Preserve historical assignments while applying qualified current successors. Do not infer a period or clock from “agreement,” handoff, relationship end, reference addition, last read or changed default. A new reference must have a legitimate admitted purpose and cannot manufacture retention to evade privacy/disposal duties. One use ending is not all-copy deletion; another actual required use remains protected. Q62/Q63 current coverage, finite dispatch, possible-effect fences, Stop and physical-outcome/restore rules remain fully binding. Missing/unknown is not none, retain-forever or a guessed default.

Story: US29-E13. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q65-C10, Q65-C11, Q65-C12, Q65-C13, Q65-C14; REQ29-P01–P18.

<!-- END CONTRACT -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-end -->

<!-- prettier-ignore-start -->

<a id="contract-f5821e71728060ef72f0"></a>

## REQ29-A22 — Enter and read an exact private document share

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"f5821e71728060ef72f0","name":"REQ29-A22 — Enter and read an exact private document share"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Adopt a narrow authenticated-human admission through native identity and P12's sole authorization decision point for this outbound purpose without broader organization enrollment. A native Auth identity/session may exist; it grants no share, Party, role or membership by itself. Preserve legitimate existing accounts and memberships. Remove or bypass only through an explicitly qualified trusted shared path the automatic donor/default-tenant effects for this purpose; never fabricate a donor/profile, trust caller-controlled metadata or add a parallel login/JWT/PDP. A bounded pending non-authorizing native identity/reservation is permitted if the provider requires it before proof; failed admission leaves no source access and has supported recovery/cleanup. Cleanup rechecks other genuine uses and cannot delete or reassign an identity merely to undo a failed share.

Invitation possession, an email string, a verified-email flag, ordinary account presence and prior collection proof do not alone admit the reader. Obtain current proof at the fixed source-approved mailbox through the qualified shared confirmation flow when sufficient compatible native proof is absent. Reuse proof only under an explicit qualified rule for the principal, exact recipient/address revision, source/share purpose, method, age and required assurance, with a fresh independently authorized binding to this share. Mailbox confirmation establishes mailbox control, not exclusive human identity, representation, signature, document authenticity, MFA, phishing resistance or a NIST assurance level. Stronger source assurance remains mandatory; an incompatible source cannot downgrade to the ordinary email path. Do not force another challenge for every page or range read.

Use the existing scanner-resistant protected doorway and frozen qualified host. Initial GET/HEAD, passive previews and prefetch disclose no protected metadata, grant no source admission and trigger no confirmation send. Deliberate qualified entry initiates the appropriate confirmation, and the final source admission coherently consumes/applies the exact proof and binds the native principal to the current share. Qualified sibling link/code paths cannot independently consume the same proof application twice. Account switching and same-tenant return preserve the exact intended document without credential-bearing redirects, cross-host cookies or role-home detours. A deliberate POST alone is not proof of a human, and a forwarded invitation alone remains insufficient.

Enforce current source/P12 permission on every protected metadata, filename, thumbnail, rendition, full/range/conditional response and any separately permitted original/download/print/export/recovery operation. Use requester-authorized private application retrieval or an equivalently proved current-authorization mechanism; a raw reusable signed storage URL, public bucket or external viewer is not a substitute. Keep caches, browser state and response ordering scoped to actual tenant/principal/share/source context and private delivery rules. Define the atomic read-admission point against revocation and bound every in-flight operation; stop newly admitted affected reads and additional serving steps as the qualified shared serving contract requires. Report the bounded in-flight/copy limit honestly rather than promise recall or unlimited access from an earlier check.

Keep proof expiry, session expiry, invitation/credential expiry, share end, rendition availability and byte retention distinct. Server time governs the finite interval, with access denied at or after its end. Confirmation, viewing, retry, resend, moving/renaming and future default changes cannot lengthen it. Before issuance a draft can change or be discarded. After issuance, wrong recipient/material/end is corrected by revoking the exact share and, only when still independently lawful, deliberately issuing a new share; old facts are not retargeted, reopened or erased. Expired, revoked or source-ended shares do not revive when identity, settings, source state or backups return. A temporary proof, authorization or technical block that did not end the share may clear only through its qualified owner repair within unchanged terms and all current gates; an unknown/denied result is not automatically a terminal revocation. Q41 cannot recreate the original participant's ended Q05–Q20/Q34 request-reading scope merely under another label.

Keep a small truthful UI: source-context Share review; direct protected document entry; visible permitted actions and exact end; usable account switch, confirmation delay, resend and capable source contact/correction; and a clear way to end the reader's session on a shared device. Use existing shared base-maia/Base UI components. Where a code is used, provide one logical labeled input with whole-code paste/autofill and leading-zero support; preserve safe context through mobile email/browser switching and low bandwidth. Use keyboard, focus, zoom, screen-reader/status and localization behavior that works through authentication and the actual reading rendition. Invitation, confirmation and recovery remain usable in plain text and with images blocked, showing safe minimal identification and the actual code or qualified entry action only through its permitted secret carrier. Do not collect unnecessary profile fields or imply that a preview prevents copying, that served bytes were understood, or that a recipient is legally identity-verified.

Private sharing remains a separate outbound source use. Q65 ongoing-reference readers need their actual current reference-purpose authority, not copied collection/share grants or general access to the original request.

Story: US29-A22. Source: request-access.md. Decision basis: Q41, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

## REQ29-B18 — Prepare a linked request for a different person

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"f61829abc183db721401","name":"REQ29-B18 — Prepare a linked request for a different person"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

For an eligible ordinary Private Document Request that needs a different intended person for continuing legitimate source work, provide a guided, linked replacement request. Preserve the original request identity and history; issue a distinct request for the new recipient. Use this same correction shape even when the predecessor has no response, rather than adding a pristine-request retarget shortcut. An unfinished predecessor may be canceled through Q34 at the handoff. A predecessor already lawfully canceled may support Q34's independently authorized remedy when its actual cancellation and current replacement purpose are proved; a canceled status alone is insufficient. Actual fulfillment, a specialist source or an unrelated purpose does not enter this ordinary replacement path. Same-person mailbox correction/lost-inbox recovery, representation, collaborator addition, ownership transfer and source-specific remediation retain their own qualified paths; neither email/name similarity nor a staff checkbox proves those cases.

Separate the current source/P12 powers to prepare/read/edit a replacement draft, reuse origin definitions, administer responsibility if changed, cancel or restrict the predecessor, issue the new request and repair a blocked result. Generic staff/admin status, reviewer assignment, file Read, possession of a link and the fact that someone created the draft confer none of those powers. Tenants allocate these actual capabilities through existing qualified source/P12 controls and preserve their legitimate narrower policies; staff can prepare now, save and resume, discard preparation, stop old access independently when authorized, or complete the qualified handoff. Add no new universal tenant settings panel, routine second approver, mandatory reason essay or policy bypass. Require a concise source basis from trusted facts or only the necessary staff input where the source needs it.

Keep at most one active source-owned replacement preparation per predecessor, using the existing ordinary draft facilities and a narrow protected origin relationship. Repeated Start returns the existing permitted preparation or a safe actionable result; it does not create another draft or reveal hidden draft contents. Other currently authorized staff may continue it without impersonating its creator, gaining their rights or requiring their live session. Preparation, autosave, opening the form and merely selecting another person have no cancellation, recipient authority, notification or reminder effect. A draft does not reserve future issuance, hold fulfillment open or block the predecessor's safety or normal source operations. Deliberately discarding an unissued preparation leaves an active predecessor unchanged and never reverses an earlier committed stop; a later genuine preparation gets its own truthful identity.

Offer source-qualified reuse of selected current item definitions, not cloning an issued request. This is an explicit narrow amendment beyond Q39's template-to-new-draft capability. The reusable field set consists only of currently compatible ordinary definition content such as the item label, requested type, instructions and source-qualified requiredness, with fresh item identities and recorded origin/version provenance. Apply origin definition-read and destination authoring/disclosure authority. Review free text for prior-person or case-specific facts; an allowlist, matching tenant or AI redaction promise cannot make text safe. Do not automatically copy received files, evidence links, attachments, answers, comments, participant assertions, acceptance, source outcomes, proofs/grants, recipient bindings, reviewer instances, date/access clocks, notification material, reminder plans or personal preferences. Any new item or supporting material must enter through its independently qualified authoring/source path.

Use current source meaning to propose genuinely remaining work. Previously accepted or otherwise source-sufficient material is not automatically recollected from the new person, and optional nonblocking work does not become a new completion hold by copying it. Person-specific material may nevertheless be genuinely required for the new person; old acceptance is neither automatic new satisfaction nor a universal prohibition on a new legitimate requirement. Staff review which legitimate requirements apply now and can adjust permitted ordinary definitions before issuance without rewriting the predecessor's requirements. Old acceptance never becomes new-item acceptance, and the new recipient is not required to attest that they supplied someone else's material. Independently qualified Q28 reuse remains a separate attributable evidence act with its own origin/target authority; it is not an automatic copy or prerequisite for replacement. If no legitimate new recipient work remains, do not manufacture a replacement merely to repair a label or obtain another notification allowance; use the actual source outcome/remedy.

Keep the preparation bound to its actual predecessor, same tenant/environment/requesting source and continuing legitimate purpose, its chosen new recipient and current draft revision. A permitted draft edit may change the proposed new recipient or definitions before issuance, but it is not an update to the predecessor's recipient or global Auth/CRM/contact identity. Use expected relevant basis and conflict-safe draft saves; preserve lawful user edits rather than silently refreshing them from a changed source or template. Re-evaluate relevant source work, disclosure and readiness at final review and commitment. Changes that affect the reviewed recipient, definitions, timing, responsibility or notification choice require review of the actual new proposal; unrelated source changes must not cause pointless universal reconfirmation. Caller-controlled IDs, flags or normalized address guesses cannot provide authority.

Before a normal final handoff, show a concise editable review of the old and new recipient contexts the actor may see, selected safe work, actual review responsibility, requested-by meaning, the new admission's actual end rules and the intended communication/reminder consequences. Reuse qualified Q24 responsibility when it covers the new declared work, but satisfy Q25's actual first-issuance gate; no future-file read, entire-roster disclosure, online presence or mandatory re-selection is required. Verify the new person's legitimate source role, current source-approved address/purpose, usable recipient route and required assurance. A narrower actor may prepare but cannot complete an effect requiring powers they lack; provide capable authorized continuation without promoting them to a reviewer or administrator.

This path requires a different intended person. Q60 same-recipient corrective work has its own one-ever-issued remedial result, automatic reminders unavailable/Off and exact independently qualified manual/issuance notices. Same-request correction retains Q48 counts/stops. Both preserve unaffected evidence and original outcomes.

Story: US29-B18. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q49, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

Finalization shall independently establish exact object/generation identity, actual byte length/type and source-qualified cryptographic integrity, close or fence all remaining write capability and required in-flight writers, and recheck current source admission after waits.
