## REQ29-B03 — Recoverable ordinary item review

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"f94be830b480753d9cd1","name":"REQ29-B03 — Recoverable ordinary item review"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Recoverable scope and real resolution.** The ordinary native source SHALL support Request changes for the exact eligible unaccepted item/evidence basis. It remains an unsatisfied review outcome, not acceptance, waiver, technical failure, deletion, new submission or terminal item rejection. Preserve Q03 self-correction independently of staff initiation and Q21's meaningful acceptance. No generic terminal Not accepted control is added. Existing specialist source dispositions remain unchanged. Each enabled source SHALL provide an accountable qualified route able to resolve genuinely non-remediable or mistaken requests; contact wording without an owner able to act is insufficient. Do not force an endless resubmission loop, arbitrary attempt cap or automatic final rejection. Existing permitted source alternatives may resolve work only through their actual authority; Q23 creates no waiver/cancel/reopen power.

**Current authority and safe recipient guidance.** Require the exact currently qualified source authority for this outcome, purpose and scope; reading, assignment, generic staff labels and recipient-grant administration do not supply it. Do not presume a new permission atom is necessary or existing acceptance capability automatically sufficient. Trusted server context derives tenant/environment, actor/author and decision time; validate all item/recipient/source selectors. Expose only the current source-approved guidance and permitted next step to the exact currently eligible participant. Identify the affected item/member and what needs attention sufficiently to act; concise safe text or already-sufficient instructions may satisfy this without a mandatory free-text essay. Bare ambiguous rejection/try-again wording is insufficient. When detail cannot be disclosed, give approved neutral wording and an actually owned next route. Internal reasoning, other people's data, sealed requirements and raw audit history SHALL NOT be exposed. Q05 Submission History does not grant previous review-note history, and Q23 adds no general discussion or export right.

**Exact basis, honest changes and reassessment.** Bind the outcome to the relevant source/requirement/instruction/purpose, evidence members and current review-state revision. Guidance SHALL NOT silently change requested scope, requiredness, instructions or purpose; a real change uses its own qualified source command/version with its consequences. Preserve unaffected supporting members and independent items unless a real source dependency applies. A newly admitted correction preserves old feedback as history on its actual basis and requires current review; it neither inherits adverse findings automatically nor proves all concerns resolved. A fresh authorized review of the same current evidence may accept it under Q21 when actual conditions are met, explicitly recording resolution/supersession of the prior requested change without requiring fabricated reupload. Do not infer acceptance from equal bytes, a removed UI badge or generic Mark resolved. This adds no new recipient text-only clarification channel, reviewer editing of submitted bytes, generic Undo or post-acceptance reopening.

**Ordering, coherent effects and replay.** Request changes SHALL share the source's qualified currentness boundary with correction, acceptance, relevant requirement/authority changes and Q22 closure. Acceptance/closure first prevents an ordinary return from undoing it; a return first changes the relevant review basis so an earlier acceptance attempt cannot silently ignore it. A later deliberate review may use C03. Correction first fences predecessor review; return first does not itself replace bytes and a subsequent correction uses Q03 admission. Concurrent guidance edits/returns must preserve history and a coherent current instruction rather than silent last-writer overwrite or duplicate competing demands. Coherently persist the decision, required history/current outcome and durable handoff intent; co-located source facts/outbox commit atomically, with providers outside locks. Durable business identity survives transport keys/leases; recover the original known effect under current disclosure, conflict on changed intent, and allow genuinely new review decisions without suppressing them as duplicate bytes. Late replay/task/message projection SHALL NOT reinstate Changes requested after successor/resolution/acceptance/fulfillment. Unknown commitment requires reconciliation, never guessed success/failure or automatic new business work.

**Source satisfaction, access and technical separation.** Required work with unresolved substantive concerns remains unsatisfied until its source lawfully resolves it. Do not drop a returned/negative item from Q22's complete predicate or count it as waived. Genuinely nonblocking optional feedback cannot hold an otherwise fulfilled request open; real aggregate dependencies still apply. Once closure wins, remove any ordinary correction promise while preserving permitted history and owned help. Q06–Q22 deadlines, actual fulfillment occurrence, fresh-access authority and current serving restrictions remain unchanged: staff may perform independently authorized source work after participant expiry but cannot revive participant rights through feedback. No access/due-date reset, grace period, automatic fresh grant or new completion occurrence follows. Technical checking/preview/unavailability remains separate from business review: use actual technical recovery or an existing qualified sufficient inspection route; do not fabricate a business return or force resubmission solely to repair infrastructure.

**Lean and accessible interaction.** Use the existing item review context and one deliberate Request changes action. Show current permitted guidance beside the affected item, accurate receipt/checking/changes-requested/awaiting-staff/accepted/completed distinctions, and the actual available action or owned help. Preserve known successful receipt/review despite an ancillary preview/history/task failure; unknown required authority still fails closed. New receipt can move attention to staff without claiming acceptance or silently resolving every issue. Preserve notes, reading position and focus only while current disclosure permits; discard late responses across user/tenant/request/purpose changes and never silently swap the reviewed version. Use semantic accessible controls, document-content qualification, text beyond color/toast/hover, appropriate status announcements, supported mobile/reflow/touch/localized dates/timezones and low-bandwidth recovery. Staff delay is not a recipient fault. No forced long reason, routine second confirmation/reviewer, per-file click ritual, automatic download, offline private cache, new account or workflow console. Q20 remains fresh-access notice only; Q23 selects no review-result email/reminder or general chat.

**Data, privacy and bounded operation.** Prove tenant/environment-aware keys and relationships for source/item/evidence/review/recipient references, valid current-state combinations, immutable original decision attribution/basis for application roles and unique durable effect identity. A mutable current projection may advance only from authoritative compatible source revisions; it cannot overwrite business history. Use constraints for relational/row invariants and the source boundary for aggregate truth. Verify effective grants, RLS USING and WITH CHECK, views/functions/RPC/storage, trigger transforms and privileged service paths; permitted updates cannot move rows into forbidden scope or forge author/owner/approval/time. Safely render feedback through existing output encoding/sanitization and supported link rules, not executable staff markup. Keep sensitive content, care/location data, credentials and raw reasoning out of ordinary logs/events; enforce current purpose on counts/errors/search/caches and saved outputs. Apply governed retention/redaction/disposition to guidance/history, not indefinite storage or destructive cascading deletion of independent uses. Bound indexed current-item proof, paginated history and recovery batches, with finite measured source workload/latency/recovery budgets before activation; no all-history/tenant scan on each review or copied vendor character limit.

**Qualification, rollout and trace.** Before activation qualify P01–P20 on the actual native paths and bind exact source review/guidance/disposition-help authority, currentness and recovery. Use compatible prospective cohorts, mixed-version protection, scoped operational stopping and roll-forward. Legacy generic rejected/task flags or imported snapshots cannot manufacture native Request changes, safe participant instructions or new outward effects. Restore/rollback must preserve later source decisions, restrictions and effect identity; reconcile with outward effects suppressed before resuming authorized work. A stop never reverses accepted/fulfilled truth or grants new access. Trace the corrected decision/C01–C08 into native source/P12/P9 participant projections and applicable GEN-06 owners, canonical requirements/design/tasks, later authorized tickets and release evidence; read full incorporated OpenSpec sections and regenerate declared views. Preserve specialist owners, Q01–Q22 and all nineteen glossary definitions. No new terminal item mode, routing/bulk engine, notification, waiver/reopen power or generic feedback-history entitlement follows.

Request changes cannot itself return accepted evidence. Q60 is the distinct acceptance-error remedy; eligible unfinished correction permits fresh review of retained evidence/current concerns without replaying old acceptance or resurrecting stale guidance.

Story: US29-B03. Source: collection-review.md. Decision basis: Q23, complete incorporated source clauses and current explicit later refinements; REQ29-P01–P18. Exact paragraph, clause, proof and journey locators are mapped individually.

## REQ29-E12 — Separate reference authority identity and availability

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"fc1aa72458331cf06dbe","name":"REQ29-E12 — Separate reference authority identity and availability"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

**Q65-C05 — Separate admission, reading and management powers.** Admission requires current source permission to reuse the exact material for this destination purpose and current target authority to admit it, plus actual policy/custody eligibility. Reading an admitted reference requires current target-purpose read/field authority and applicable continuing source restrictions; it does not require original request-browsing permission, inherit the original participant's access window or grant the original transcript/internal review/other-use history. Management actions such as End may use sufficient permitted metadata without body-read/reuse eligibility where the source authorizes them. Relationship lead, original reviewer, partner record access, folder membership, URL and service key are not substitutes for these separate powers.

**Q65-C06 — Trusted context and current source facts.** Derive tenant/environment, actor, author, policy/approval attribution and trusted execution context from native validated identity and current source/P12 facts. Caller IDs are validated selectors, not authority. Preserve qualified limited/no-Party paths where applicable; do not fabricate a profile, membership or demo Tenant. Current staff or system/NHI authority, including actual owner ceilings/revocation where relevant, applies at each action and result disclosure. Recheck relevant facts after waits at the owning boundary; stale JWT claims, browser state, user metadata, cached acceptance or a queued role alias cannot authorize an effect.

**Q65-C07 — Coherent admission and custody protection.** Admit the exact source use, required copy/reference protection, trusted history and recoverable original result coherently before making it available for operational reliance. Order admission with current source restrictions/acceptance corrections and custody's hold/use/writer/disposition boundary; deletion-claimed or unresolved possibly-deleting material cannot gain a favorable new use. Preserve all required components of an accepted unit. Use a real short transaction or proved durable fence at the actual source/custody seam; multiple SDK calls or a withTenant callback are not one transaction. No long SQL transaction across provider IO, transient list count or asynchronous best-effort link is acceptable proof. Safe incomplete work is not an available reference.

**Q65-C08 — Flexible identity and duplicate handling.** Distinct source-legitimate documents, versions, periods or needs may coexist; impose no universal one-agreement-per-partner or one-active-reference-per-origin-lineage rule. Prevent duplicate equivalent active uses under the actual target, admitted purpose/need and exact evidence basis using source-defined semantics and enforceable uniqueness or equivalent serialized admission. Do not use a permanent digest/target uniqueness rule that blocks a fresh authorized admission after End, including for a continuing lawful need. An equivalent successful replay/no-op preserves original attribution and time; a different legitimate reference is not merged merely because bytes match. No slot graph, arbitrary caller purpose string or configurable cardinality engine is introduced.

**Q65-C09 — Use lifecycle and truthful availability.** Distinguish inert preparation, durably admitted use, current permitted/restricted/temporarily unknown availability, and deliberately ended/replaced history. Availability is derived from current source rules, not a second legal-validity or acceptance flag. Temporary permission/technical uncertainty can clear under the same still-admitted basis when actual current proof permits; it is not a terminal end. Explicitly ended or replaced uses never revive from restored access, background replay, cache refresh or database restore. Seeing retained history never itself authorizes ongoing operational reliance.

Story: US29-E12. Source: records-policies.md. Decision basis: Q65-C05, Q65-C06, Q65-C07, Q65-C08, Q65-C09; REQ29-P01–P18.

## REQ29-P08 — Preserve immutable history and separately govern current applicability

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"fcb7182f49db99850f80","name":"REQ29-P08 — Preserve immutable history and separately govern current applicability"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

Preserve original submitted/accepted byte versions, receipt and acceptance identities, original actor/time/basis, actual fulfillment and prior governing records. A new upload, correction, derivative, metadata label or provider relocation does not silently replace them. Current applicability, safe display metadata and source-qualified correction/supersession are separate records or projections under the actual source model. Do not introduce a universal event-sourcing platform. Append-only business history remains subject to lawful minimized retention/redaction; it does not justify indefinite private-body storage. Required controls/tombstones remain sufficient to reject obsolete intent and prevent restored permissions or effects. Changes to non-operative display metadata must be proved non-operative; uncertain semantic change uses the owning successor path.

## REQ29-B17 — Versioned ordinary item instructions

<!-- BEGIN CONTRACT {"id":"ffb3736f7dfd24d5fad3","name":"REQ29-B17 — Versioned ordinary item instructions"} -->

The system SHALL satisfy this complete contract and REQ29-P01–P18 common safeguards.

For an eligible issued unfinished ordinary Private Document Request, the requesting source SHALL permit currently authorized staff to deliberately publish a prospective revision of participant-facing instructions for the same outstanding, not-currently-accepted Document Request Item and continuing document need. Keep its intended recipient, legitimate purpose, requested document type, requiredness and independent technical/specialist constraints unchanged. The source qualifies what is the same need; arbitrary prose, a client label or an automatic semantic classifier cannot authorize a different demand, waiver, schema/form edit or stronger assertion. Q30 explanation and Q23 feedback cannot substitute for the actual revision command. Generic reviewer, task, file-read, assignment, administration and Q36 addition powers alone are insufficient.

Preserve three distinct facts: the immutable published instruction basis associated with an earlier receipt or review, the currently authoritative instruction revision, and any later deliberate review against its exact current basis. Publish attributable successor content rather than overwriting the earlier instructions or relabeling submissions. Drafting is inert; it does not alter participant demand, current review selection or fulfillment. Publishing exactly unchanged current material creates no new business revision. Deliberately correcting back to older wording, when currently permitted, is a new prospective act on the current basis, not restoration of old scope, decisions or clocks.

Existing durably received evidence remains received under its actual submitted-under basis. Revision creates no new bytes, receipt, Submission Correction, participant affirmation, Request changes decision, rejection or acceptance. Preserve Q03 current-candidate and corrected-predecessor fences and independently applicable review concerns. Retained current evidence may be deliberately reassessed under the explicit new requirement basis through Q21/Q23 without fabricated reupload; ordinary file evidence still requires qualified staff acceptance. Instruction publication supersedes the instruction criterion only under its source-authoring authority; it does not change historical Q21/Q23 decisions or grant review-resolution power. Determine current guidance applicability through qualified source rules, preserving independent concerns without retaining a concern whose only binding criterion has lawfully ended as a hidden blocker. Any required human resolution or reassessment remains a distinct current act by an authorized reviewer; no automatic concern classifier or new per-concern platform is required. Show the real next work and actor, including staff reassessment when needed, rather than resetting every item status or blaming the recipient by default.

Coherently establish the revision, its actual qualified handling route, relevant review coverage, prospective scope/currentness consequence, required history and durable reevaluation/recovery intent. For required participant work, apply an explicit source/P12/Q01 instruction-scope successor within the valid original or fresh period, retaining its exact end, reviewed subsets, earlier caps and current restrictions. Reconcile Q13/Q15/Q36 lineage rules explicitly for this same-item instruction successor; Q36 does not itself grant revision authority. After ordinary expiry, required participant work needs separately authorized actual fresh admission for the revised basis under existing eligibility and nonoverlap rules. No uncommitted promise of a later grant or hidden inaccessible demand is publication of usable work.

A qualified participant route does not require the recipient to be online, acknowledge the revision, finish proof exchange, receive email or successfully render a page when staff publish. A qualified route means actual admitted authority and a proved capable source handling path, not continuous presentation/provider availability or prior evidence acceptance. A recoverable technical delay may retain known publication and owned reassessment/recovery with truthful availability; missing or unknown required authority or a fundamentally absent route cannot be relabeled a delay. Required Q01 proof/current source authority still gates each protected use, with qualified proof reuse where permitted. Independently lawful current staff evidence/review may proceed without recipient renewal where no participant act or assertion is required. Show actual responsibility and participant availability, preserving independently valid old rights; if participant action later becomes necessary, establish its actual authorized route or capable source remedy before demanding it. Reuse valid Q24 coverage and qualify only the revised declared work under Q25; do not rerun all original issuance or require a roster, presence check or extra approver.

Order publication against relevant current instructions, evidence/correction, prepared review, Q30 context, Q33 removal, source restrictions and actual Q22/Q34 terminal acts through one qualified source boundary or equivalent version/fence protocol. Acceptance, removal, freeze or closure first denies an ineligible ordinary revision; publication first fences materially affected stale decisions and operations for deliberate current review. Not-currently-accepted does not mean never historically accepted, but this action cannot clear acceptance to create eligibility. A transfer or open form is not durable receipt; preserve receipt-first facts, revalidate affected unadmitted work and never retarget an old command to the new revision. Preserve unrelated lawful work. Drafts create no completion hold, optional nonblocking work remains nonblocking, and relevant publication/reassessment durably arranges complete-predicate reevaluation without another routine Complete action.

A revision does not reset any requested-by target or access clock, erase legitimate earlier lateness, fabricate pre-revision fault or create an overlapping/fresh period automatically. An individual narrow admission stays narrow without becoming an invented permanent global ceiling for later separately authorized admissions within their real source universe. Use one clear Update instructions flow in the existing source/item UI. Let staff inspect permitted current wording, review the proposed text and relevant consequence, then deliberately publish. On authorized participant return, make current instructions and the true update time clear alongside preserved progress and the submitted-under basis needed to understand the work. Show truthful published, unchanged, uncommitted, conflict or outcome-unknown states and capable recovery; preserve permitted input/reading context/focus and discard stale cross-user/tenant/request results. No mandatory essay, read receipt, reply, second approval, per-file ritual, account, download, new diff engine or private offline archive follows. Updating a page does not prove awareness or understanding, and this instruction publication grants no transport authority; Q40 alone supplies its separately qualified optional update notice, with no other new default, reminder, channel or fake source event.

Retain original instruction content and references under their actual source retention, hold and privacy rules; retention is not perpetual participant disclosure. Every old/current instruction body, history cue, comparison, export and action result requires current exact permission. Where the source already permits operative linked instruction resources, use their qualified exact reference/version or capture the operative requirement in authoritative text; a mutable external link alone must not be represented as a preserved requirement snapshot. Do not add a crawler, archive, attachment feature, public fallback or restore deleted resources through a prose correction. Authorized redaction/disposition preserves permitted provenance and honest unavailable/unknown history, not fabricated blank text or reconstructed meaning.

Correct mistaken instructions through a new currently qualified source revision while that action remains eligible. Q33 applies only under its actual outstanding/unaccepted conditions; accepted, removed, frozen or terminal cases use an actually capable recorded source/privacy remedy with historical facts and other lawful uses preserved. A new request may address a legitimate separate need, but does not repair unnecessary collection or permit bypassing adverse restrictions. Do not offer universal Undo, silent unaccept, destructive version rollback, predecessor revival or a powerless contact.

Enforce trusted tenant/environment/actor/source context and exact item, predecessor, evidence and scope relationships on every write, protected read and replay. Preserve one authoritative current instruction basis per eligible item, immutable publication/receipt/review provenance, valid states and permanent semantic effect identity. Under current result-disclosure authority, resolve a matching already-committed identity before treating the call as a new revision or current-material no-op; later acceptance/removal/closure cannot erase that original effect. Same intent across transport retries recovers the original result; changed material conflicts and stale replay cannot republish an obsolete revision. Effective grants, RLS USING/WITH CHECK, UPSERT, views/functions/triggers, storage and privileged workers/imports must preserve those boundaries. Co-located source facts and required outbox intent commit atomically; equivalent cross-owner designs must prove coherent admission and recovery. Optional projection/provider failures cannot undo known publication or supply new authority; external work stays outside short source locks. Existing pending communication/work retains its exact source purpose and pinned facts while applying its own current usefulness/action/disclosure rules to affected changes. Neither blanket suppression, a fake new event, sealed-material rewriting, unknown-attempt rekey nor recall is authorized by revision alone.

Adopt prospectively through exact source/P12/Q01/Q03/Q13/Q15/Q21–Q25/Q28/Q30/Q33/Q36 and completed-access owner reconciliation, canonical authoring and declared OpenSpec projections. Qualify real roles, all private doors, controlled races/replays/faults, current evidence reassessment, bounded production-shaped workloads, migration/restore and composed accessible user journeys before activation. Do not invent historical revisions or granted scope from mutable legacy text. A prospective baseline is usable only with proved current source eligibility and truthful capture provenance; unknown earlier basis stays unknown. Gate incompatible writers/readers, stop new revisions without undoing admitted work and use roll-forward when downgrade would lose facts. Reuse existing source, identity, custody and work infrastructure; no general revision engine, configurable approval platform or whole future Studio dependency is required.
